(i-ḷ.) ellā meyyum puḷḷi illā - ellā meykaḷum puḷḷi illiyāmpaṭiyāka, uruvu uruvu āki - tattam muṉṉi vaṭivē iṉṉum vaṭivāka, akaramōṭu uyirttalum - akarattoṭu kūṭi olittalum, ēṉi uyirōṭu uruvu tirintu uyirttalum - oḻinta uyirkaḷoṭu vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭu olittalumākiya, a īr iyala - avviraṇṭu iyal piṉiyuṭiya, uyirttal āṟu - avi olikku muṟimi.
‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, aḷapeṭi uyirōṭum, cārpiṟṟōṟṟattu uyirōṭum kūṭum uyirmeyyum koḷka.
u-m: uruvu uruvāki uyirttal ka ṅa eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. uruvu tirintu uyirttal kā ṅā eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
īṇṭu uyirum meyyum kūṭukiṉṟa uyirmeyk kūṭṭattiṉi ‘ellā meyyu’meṉṟu meymēl vittuk kūṟiyatu, atu muṉ kūṟik kūṟappaṭutal nōkkip pōlum. uyirmey yeṉpataṉi oṟṟumi koḷvuḻi ummit tokip puṟattuppiṟanta aṉmoḻittoki yeṉavum, vēṟṟumi koḷvuḻi ummit tokiyeṉavum koḷka. ‘illāka’ eṉpatu ‘illā’ eṉa niṉṟatu.  uruvu tirintu uyirttal mēlum kīḻum vilaṅkupeṟuvaṉa vilaṅkupeṟṟu uyirttalum, kōṭupeṟuvaṉa kōṭupeṟṟu uyirttalum, puḷḷipeṟuvaṉa puḷḷipeṟṟu uyirttalum, puḷḷiyum kōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟu uyirttalum eṉak koḷka.	  (17)
18.	meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu niliyē.
iḵtu, uyirmeyyuḷ uyirum meyyum niṟkumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir - uyir meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu - meykaḷiṉ piṉṉavām, tōṉṟum nili - tōṉṟum nilimikkaṇ.
‘tōṉṟu nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyirmeykaḷip pirikku miṭattum kūṭṭumiṭattum, avvāṟē muṉṉum piṉṉum ātalik koḷka. meyyum uyirum muṉṉum piṉṉum peṟa niṟku meṉṟamiyāṉ, ak kūṭṭam pālum nīrumpōla uṭaṉ kalantataṉṟi, viralnuṉikaḷ talippeytāṟpōla vēṟuniṉṟu kalantaṉavalla eṉpatu peṟutum. īṇṭu vēṟṟuminayam karutappaṭṭatu. (ē - īṟṟaci)	(18)
19.	valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa.
itu, taṉi meykaḷuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu ōr kuṟiyiṭutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu eṉpa - valleḻuttu eṉṉum                 kuṟiya eṉṟu colluvar, ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa - ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷi.
valleṉṟu icittalāṉum, val eṉṟa talivaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu. moḻikku mutalā meḻuttu nāṉku uḷavākalāṉum, avaṟṟāṉ vaḻakkuppayiṟci peritākalāṉum valliṉam muṉkūṟappaṭṭatu. (ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷ k c ṭ t p ṟ)	(19)
20.	melleḻut teṉpa ṅañaṇa namaṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu eṉpa - melleḻuttu eṉṉum kuṟiya eṉṟu colluvar, ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa - ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷi.
melleṉṟu icittalāṉum, mel eṉṟa mūkkiṉ vaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum, melleḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu. moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu mūṉṟu uḷavākalāṉum, avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkup payiṟciyāṉum, (melliṉam), mutalāmeḻuttuc ciṟupāṉmi vaḻakkiṉavāy iraṇṭākiya iṭiyiṉattiṉ muṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. vaṉmi meṉmi kūṟaliṉ, eḻuttu aruvaṉṟi uruvātal peṟappaṭṭatu. uyirukkum kuṟumi neṭumi kūṟaliṉ, uruveṉpatu peṟutum. (ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷ ṅ ñ ṇ n m ṉ.)	 (20)
21.	iṭiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭiyeḻuttu eṉpa - iṭiyeḻuttu eṉṉum kuṟiya eṉṟu colluvar, ya ra la va ḻa ḷa - ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷi.
iṭinikaravāki olittalāṉum, iṭinikarttāya miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum iṭiyeḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu.  (ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷ y r l v ḻ ḷ)	(21)
22.	ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
	meymmayak kuṭaṉili teriyuṅ kāli.
itu, taṉi mey mayakkattiṟkup peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a mū āṟum - mēṟcollappaṭṭa (mūvāṟu) patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, vaḻaṅku iyal maruṅkiṉ - tammi moḻip paṭutti vaḻaṅkum iyalpu uḷatāmiṭattu, mey mayaṅku - meym mayakkam eṉṟum, uṭaṉnili - uṭaṉnili mayakkam eṉṟum iru vakiya, teriyum kāli - (avi mayaṅku muṟimi) ārāyum kālattu.
uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉiyum uṟaḻcci vakiyāṉ uṟaḻa oṉpatu uḷavāmaṉṟē. avaṟṟuḷ taṉi meyyoṭu taṉi meymayakkam oṉṟē kūṟiya teṉṉeṉiṉ, maṟṟavaṟṟiṟku variyaṟi yiṉmiyiṉ variyaṟiyuṭiya taṉimey mayakkamē kūṟiyoḻintār eṉa uṇarka. ‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, taṉimeyyōṭu uyirmey mayakkamaṉṟi, taṉimeyyoṭu taṉi mey  mayakkamātal koḷka.	 (22)
23.	ṭaṟalaḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark
	kacapa veṉṉu mūveḻut turiya.
itu, meymayakkam āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) ṭaṟa laḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ka ca pa eṉṉum mū eḻuttu uriya - ka ca pa eṉṟu collappaṭum mūṉṟeḻuttum mayaṅkutaṟku uriya.
u-m: kaṭka, kaṟka, celka, koḷka eṉavum; kaṭciṟār, kaṟciṟār, celciṟār, koḷciṟār eṉavum; kaṭpa, kaṟpa, celpa, koḷpa eṉavum varum.
mēl ‘teriyuṅ kāli’ (22) eṉṟataṉāṉ, im meymayakkam kūṟukiṉṟa cūttira mellām palapaṭiyāṉ mayakkam koḷḷac col nōkku uṭiyaveṉiṉum, vaḻakki ṉoṭu porunta oṉṟaṉōṭu oṉṟaṉṟi mayaṅkāteṉpatu koḷka. meymayakkam orumoḻikkum puṇarmoḻikkum potuvāka liṉ, mēṟkūṟum puṇarmoḻic ceykiyellām taliyāya aṟiviṉōri nōkka oruvāṟṟāṟ kūṟiya vāṟāyiṟṟu.	(23)
24.	avaṟṟuḷ
	laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟiya nāṉkaṉuḷḷum, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar - lakāra ḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yavavum tōṉṟum - kacapakkaḷēyaṉṟi yakara vakaraṅkaḷum tōṉṟi mayaṅkum.
u-m: kolyāṉi, veḷyāṉi, kōlvaḷi, veḷvaḷi eṉa varum.	   (24)
25.	ṅañaṇa namaṉa veṉumpuḷḷi muṉṉart
	tatta micika ḷottaṉa niliyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉum puḷḷimuṉṉar - ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar; tattam micikaḷ ottaṉa (neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉuḷ) tattamakku mēl niṟkum eḻuttākiya ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟakkaḷ poruntiṉa, nili - mayaṅki niṟṟaṟkaṇ. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci)
u-m: teṅku, mañcu, vaṇṭu, pantu, kampu, kaṉṟu eṉa varum. 		(25)
26.	avaṟṟuḷ
	ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
	kacañapa mayavav vēḻu muriya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa melleḻuttu āṟaṉuḷ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar- ṇakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ka ca ña pa ma ya va ēḻum uriya - (ṭaṟakkaḷē yaṉṟi) ka ca ña pa ma ya va eṉṟu collappaṭum ēḻum mayaṅkutaṟku uriya e-ṟu.
u-m: veṇkalam, puṉkaṇ; veṇcāntu, puṉcey; veṇ ñāṇ, poṉñāṇ; veṇpali, poṉperitu; veṇ māli, poṉmāli; maṇyātu, poṉyātu; maṇvalitu, poṉ  valitu eṉa varum.	(26)
27.	ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	yaḵkā ṉiṟṟaṉ meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - ña na ma va eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal mey peṟṟaṉṟu - yakaram mayaṅki niṟṟal poruṇmi peṟṟatu.
u-m: uriñyātu, porunyātu, tirumyātu, tevyātu eṉa varum.	(27)
28.	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvun tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ - makaramākiya puḷḷi muṉṉar, va um tōṉṟum - (pakara yakaraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi) vakaramum tōṉṟi mayaṅkum. 
u-m: nilam valitu eṉavarum.	 (28)
29.	yaraḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) yaraḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - yaraḻa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, mutal āku eḻuttu ṅakara moṭu tōṉṟum - moḻikku mutal ām eṉṉappaṭṭa oṉpatu meyyum (mutalākā) ṅakarattoṭu tōṉṟi mayaṅkum e-ṟu.
u-m: vēykaṭitu, vērkaṭitu, vīḻkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu; ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu eṉa varum. vēyṅaṉam, vērṅaṉam, vīḻṅaṉam eṉavum oṭṭuka. vēyyātu eṉpuḻi, uṭaṉili yātalāṉ yakaram oḻittu oṭṭuka.	
(`moḻikku mutal ām eṉṉappaṭṭa oṉpatu mey’ eṉpatu moḻikku mutalāy varum oṉpatu uyir meyyeḻuttukaḷil uyiroḻinta meykaḷik kuṟittu niṉṟatu.)	(29)
30.	meynnilic cuṭṭi ṉellā veḻuttun
	tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭiyē.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē uṭaṉili mayakkam āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) meynnili cuṭṭiṉ - poruḷ nilimik karuttiṉ kaṇ, ellā eḻuttum tammuṉ tām varum - ellā meyyeḻuttum tam muṉṉē tām vantu mayaṅkum, ra ḻa alaṅkaṭi - rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ allāta iṭattu. (ēkāram īṟṟaci.)
u-m: kākki, eṅṅaṉam, pacci, maññi, paṭṭi, maṇṇi, tatti, vennōy, appi, ammi, veyyar, elli, evvī, koḷḷi, koṟṟi, kaṉṉi  eṉa varum. ‘meynnili cuṭṭiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘tammuṉ tām varum’ eṉṟatu, meymmuṉṉar meyyeṉṉum māttiriyaṉṟi, uṭaṉilimey mēlatām eṉpatu koḷka. ‘ellām’ eṉṟatu, mēl ya ra ḻa (29) eṉṟa atikāram māṟṟivantu niṉṟatu.	 (30)
31.	a i uam mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu.
(i-ḷ.) a i u am mūṉṟum cuṭṭu - (kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṉap paṭṭa) a i u eṉṉum am mūṉṟum cuṭṭu eṉṉum kuṟiyavām.
u-m: aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam, uṅṅaṉam eṉa varum.	(31)
32.	ā ē ōam mūṉṟum viṉā.
(i-ḷ.) ā ē ō am mūṉṟum viṉā - (mēl neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉappaṭṭa) ā ē ō eṉṉum am mūṉṟum viṉā eṉṉum kuṟiyavām.
u-m: uṇkā, uṇkē, uṇkō cāttā eṉavarum.
‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ekaramum yakara ākāramum viṉāp peṟumeṉak koḷka. ik kuṟikaḷiyum muṉ kuṟileṉṟum neṭileṉṟum kūṟiya vaḻiyē kūṟuka veṉiṉ, ivi col nilimiyiṟ peṟum kuṟiyākaliṉ, āṇṭu viyātu moḻimarapiṉic cāravittār eṉka. 
ikkuṟi moḻinilimikkēl eḻuttiṉmēl vittuk kūṟiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ivvatikārattup peyar viṉiyallaṉa vaṟṟiṟkuk karuvi ceyyāmiyiṉ eṉka.	(32)
33.	aḷapiṟan tuyirttalu moṟṟici nīṭalum
	uḷaveṉa moḻipa viciyoṭu civaṇiya
	narampiṉ maṟiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, eḻuttukaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiriyiṉ nīṇṭu niṟkum iṭam ituveṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷapu iṟantu uyirttalum - (uyireḻuttuk kaḷellām) tamakkuc coṉṉa aḷaviṉik kaṭantu olit taliyum, oṟṟu ici nīṭalum - oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ tammoli muṉkūṟiya aḷapiṉ nīṭaliyum, iciyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟiya - (in nūluṭ kūṟum viḷiyiṉ kaṇṇēyaṉṟik) kuralmutaliya ēḻiciyoṭu poruntiya narampiṉi yuṭiya yāḻiṉatu icinūṟkaṇṇum, uḷa eṉa moḻipa eṉmaṉār pulavar - uḷa eṉac colluvar av vici nūlāciriyar eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
‘oṟṟici nīṭalum’ eṉṟaṉar, ‘aḷapiṟan tuyirtta’ leṉṟatu atikārattāl niṉṟa uyirmēṟ cēṟaliṉ. ‘uḷa’ veṉṟatu annīṭṭippu oru taliyaṉṟeṉpatu viḷakkiṟṟu. icinūlāciriyarum mutaṉūlāciriyar tāmē yeṉiṉum, ‘moḻipa’, eṉa vēṟoruvar pōlak kūṟiyatu, atuvum vēṟu oru nūlākac ceyyappaṭum nilimi nōkkip pōlum. ‘maṟiyavum’ eṉpataṉ ummi vikārattāl tokkatu. akaram ceyyuḷ vikāram.	(33)
mutalāvatu - nūṉmarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.


moḻi marapu
‘moḻikaḷukku eḻuttāṉvarum marapuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ moḻi marapeṉappaṭṭatu’ eṉa iḷampūraṇarum ‘eḻuttāṉām moḻiyatu marapuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ ivvōttu moḻimarapeṉak kāraṇappeyart tāyiṟṟu’ eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ivviyaliṉ peyarkkāraṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. ivviyaluṭ kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇam taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟkaṉṟi moḻiyiṭi yeḻuttiṟku’ eṉa iḷampūraṇar kūṟuvar. eṉavē eḻuttukkaḷi moḻippaṭut ticikkuṅkāl moḻiyil niṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷukku uḷavām iyalpiṉi yuṇarttuvatu ivviyaleṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ivviyaliṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ 49.
moḻiyiṉic cārntu olittaliyē iyalpākavuṭiya cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟiṟkum ‘avitām kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiya lukaram āytam eṉa muṉṉaruritta muṟiyē ivviyalil ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟār. ammūṉṟum oru moḻi puṇarmoḻi ākiya īriṭattum varumeṉakkoṇṭu avaṟṟi orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, oru moḻiyāytam, puṇar moḻiyāytam eṉa āṟākappirittu muṟiyē 1-mutal 6-vari yuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil uṇartti 7-ām cūttirattil avvoru moḻi yāytattiṟku ōr ilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivi moḻiyiṭic cārttiyuṇarappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum oruvāṟṟāṉ eḻutteṉak koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum nūṉ marapiyaṭuttu moḻimarapiṉ mutaṟkaṇ viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiyaṭuttu uyiraḷapeṭi, moḻi yākkam, meykaḷiṉ iyakkam, īroṟ ṟuṭaṉili, makarak kuṟukkam, eḻuttuppōli, aikārak kuṟukkam, moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ, moḻikkīṟāmeḻuttukkaḷ, eṉpaṉa uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa. iviyellām taṉi yeḻuttukkaḷilaṉṟi moḻikaḷil vittu aṟintukoḷḷutaṟ kuriyaṉavātaliṉ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici - 10, pak. 167-168
iraṇṭāvatu
moḻimarapu
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, moḻikaḷukku eḻuttāṉ varum marapu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ moḻimarapu eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉuḷ kūṟukiṉṟatu taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟku aṉṟi moḻiyiṭi (niṉṟa) eḻuttiṟku eṉa vuṇarka.
34.	kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟal vēṇṭum
	yāveṉ ciṉimici yuriyacik kiḷavik
	kāvayiṉ varūu makara mūrntē.
it talic cūttiram eṉ  nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu eḻuttukaḷiṟ kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalikaram - orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, uriyacik kiḷavikku - uriyacic collākiya miyā eṉpataṟku, āvayiṉ varūum - (ciṉiyāka) ac col taṉṉiṭattu varukiṉṟa, yā eṉ ciṉimiciyā eṉ ciṉimici, makaram ūrntu niṟṟal vēṇṭum - makara oṟṟiṉi ūrntu niṟṟali vēṇṭum (āciriyaṉ).
u-m: kēṇmiyā eṉa varum. miyā eṉṉum col iṭam. makaram paṟṟukkōṭu. yā eṉṉum ciṉiyum makarampōlak kuṟukutaṟku oru cārpu.	(1)
35.	puṇariya ṉiliyiṭik kuṟukalu murittē
	uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum.
itu, kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷum varumeṉṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puṇar iyal nili iṭiyum - irumoḻi tammiṟ puṇartal iyaṉṟa nilimikkaṇṇum, kuṟukal urittu - av vikaram kuṟukutaluṭittu, uṇarak kūṟiṉ - (āṇṭi iṭattiṉiyum paṟṟuk kōṭṭiṉiyum īṇṭu) uṇarak kūṟappukiṉ, muṉṉar tōṉṟum - (atu vēṇṭuvatilli) kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ (avviṭaṉum paṟṟuk kōṭum) tōṉṟum.
‘puṇariya ṉili yiṭiyum’ eṉa moḻimāṟṟi urikka. muṉṉar tōṉṟumāṟu: “yakaram varumvaḻi yikaraṅ kuṟukum, ukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu” (kuṟṟiyalukara puṇariyal 5) eṉpataṉuḷ aṟika. yakaram iṭam; ukaram cārnta valleḻuttup paṟṟuk kōṭu.
u-m: nākiyātu, varakiyātu, teḷkiyātu, eḵkiyātu, kokki yātu, kuraṅkiyātu eṉa varum.	(2)
36.	neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
	kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.
iḵtu, orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttu imparum - neṭṭeḻuttiṉatu piṉṉum, toṭarmoḻi īṟṟum - toṭarmoḻiyatu iṟutiyiṉum, kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntu (niṟṟal vēṇṭum) - kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttu āṟiṉiyum ūrntu (niṟṟali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ).
‘tantu puṇarnturittal’ (marapu. 110) eṉṉum tantiravuttiyāṉ, muṉṉiṉṟa ‘niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉpatu īṇṭum puṇarkkappaṭṭatu.
u-m:  nāku, varaku eṉa varum.
neṭṭeḻuttimparum toṭarmoḻiyīṟum iṭam. val leḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu. ivvāṟu iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟavē, moḻikku īṟātalum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.	(3)
37.	iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉu māruṇṭē
	kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷum varumeṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭippaṭiṉum kuṟukum iṭaṉ uṇṭu - (av vukaram) puṇarmoḻi iṭippaṭiṉum kuṟukum iṭamuṇṭu, (ataṉ iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum yāṇṭō peṟuvateṉiṉ) kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyalāṉ - ataṉ puṇarcci muṟimi aṟiyum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaliṉ kaṇṇē e-ṟu.
‘iṭippaṭiṉum kuṟukum’ eṉa moḻimāṟṟi urikka. ak kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ, “valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻit, tolli yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.” (kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal. 4) eṉpataṉuḷ valloṟṟut toṭar moḻiyum valleḻuttu varuvaḻiyum iṭam. av valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟu valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
u-m:   cekkukkaṇi, cukkukkoṭu eṉa varum.
‘iṭaṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ik kuṟukkam ciṟupāṉmi eṉṟuṇarka.  ār, ē eṉpaṉa - acikaḷ. ‘puṇariyalāṉ’ eṉpatu vēṟṟumi mayakkam. akaram - cāriyi)	(4)
38.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
	uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micittē.
iḵtu, orumoḻi āytam varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āytap puḷḷi - āytamākiya puḷḷi, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar, uyiroṭu puṇarnta val āṟaṉ micittu - uyiroṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttu āṟaṉ mēlatu e-ṟu.
u-m:  eḵku, kaḵcu eṉavarum.
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum valleḻuttu miciyum iṭam. iḵtu uyiraṉmiyiṉ, itaṟkup paṟṟukkōṭu eṉpatilli.
kaḵṟītu eṉpataṉi meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci (puṇariyal - 7) eṉpa vākaliṉ, ‘puḷḷi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ āytatti meyppāṟ paṭuttuk koḷka. īṇṭu uyireṉṟatu, mēl ‘āytat toṭar moḻi’ (kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal - 1) eṉṟōtaliṉ perum pāṉmiyum  kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉi. veḵkāmi mutaliya piṟa uyirvaravu ciṟupāṉmi yeṉak koḷka.	(5)
39.	īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉu micimi tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, av vāytam puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷum varumeṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īṟu iyal maruṅkiṉum - nilimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu naṭakkum iṭattiṉum, icimi tōṉṟum - āyta oli tōṉṟum.
u-m: 	kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu eṉavarum. īṇṭum iṭaṅkaḷ avi.		(6)
40.	uruviṉu miciyiṉu marukit tōṉṟum
	moḻikkuṟip pellā meḻutti ṉiyalā
	āyta maḵkāk kāli yāṉa.
iḵtu, av vorumoḻi āytattiṟku ōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uruviṉum - oru poruḷiṉatu uruvattiṉ kaṇṇum, iciyiṉum - ōciyiṉkaṇṇum, arukit tōṉṟum - ciṟupāṉmiyāyt tōṉṟum, kuṟippumoḻi yellām - kuṟippu moḻikaḷellām, eḻuttiṉ iyalā - āyta eḻuttāṉiṭṭu eḻutappaṭṭu naṭavā, (aḵtu ekkālattumō veṉiṉ, aṉṟu), āytam aḵkāk kāli yāṉa - av vāytam taṉ ari māttiri aḷapāyc curuṅki nillātu (av vuruvum iciyatu mikutiyum uṇarttutaṟku) nīṇṭa kālattu an nīṭcikku.
u-m: ‘kaḵṟeṉṟatu’ eṉpatu uruvu. ‘cuḵṟeṉṟatu’ eṉpatu ici.	(7)
41.	kuṉṟici moḻivayi ṉiṉṟici niṟikkum
	neṭṭeḻut timpa rottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
itu, ‘nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ’ (nūṉmarapu. 6) eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṉṟu ici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟu ici niṟikkum - (aḷapeṭi yōciyākac collā toḻiyiṉ) kuṉṟuvatāṉa ōciyiyuṭiya avvaḷapeṭi eḻuttāṉāya moḻik kaṇṇē niṉṟu avvōciyi niṟikkum, (avi yāvi yeṉṉiṉ,) neṭṭeḻuttu impar otta kuṟṟeḻuttu - neṭṭeḻuttukaḷiṉ piṉṉāka (avaṟṟiṟkup piṟappāṉum puṇarcciyāṉum ōciyāṉum) iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukaḷ.
u-m: āa, īi, ūu, ēe, ōo eṉavarum.
īṇṭu ‘moḻi’yeṉṟatu, av vaḷapeṭi eḻuttu oru poruḷ uṇartti ōreḻut torumoḻiyāy niṟkum nilimiyiṉi. iviyum moḻimēṟ kāṇappaṭutaliṟ cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttu eṉappaṭumāleṉiṉ, perumpāṉmiyum am moḻitāṉē avveḻuttāy varutalāṉum, ammoḻi nilimi oḻiya vēṟeḻuttākavum collappaṭutalāṉum, avvāṟu ākāteṉpatu.
ciṟupāṉmiyum ammoḻitāṉē eḻuttāy vārāteṉak koḷka.
u-m: ‘erutu kāluṟāatu’ eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. 	(8)
42.	aioḷa eṉṉu māyī reḻuttiṟ
	kikara kara miciniṟi vākum.
itu, mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.)  ai oḷa eṉṉum a īr eḻuttiṟku - (otta kuṟṟeḻuttu illā) ai oḷa eṉṟu collappaṭum av viraṇṭeḻuttiṟku muṉ, ikaram ukaram iciniṟi ākum - (īkāra ūkāraṅkaṭku otta kuṟṟeḻut tākiya) ikara ukaraṅkaḷ (ak kuṉṟici moḻikkaṇ niṉṟu) ōciyi niṟippaṉavākum.

ittuṇiyum nūṉmarapiṉ oḻipu.	(9)
43.	neṭṭeḻut tēḻē yōreḻut torumoḻi.
iḵtu, eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻum - neṭṭeḻuttākiya ēḻum, ōr eḻuttu orumoḻi - ōr eḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyām.
u-m: ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, oḷa eṉa varum.
ituvum uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu. oḷakārattil uyirmeyyiṉiyē koḷka. ‘ēḻum’ eṉpataṉ ummi vikārattāṉ tokkatu.	(10)
44.	kuṟṟeḻut tintu moḻiniṟi pilavē.
itu, kuṟṟeḻuttukaḷ ōreḻuttorumoḻi ākā veṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ orumoḻiyāvaṉa uḷaveṉpatum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum - kuṟṟeḻuttākiya aintum, moḻi niṟipu ila - ōreḻuttāy niṉṟu oru moḻiyāy niṟital ila; avaṟṟuṭ cila niṟikkum.
okaram oḻinta nāṉkum cuṭṭāyum viṉāvāyum moḻi niṟikkumaṉṟōveṉiṉ, avi iṭiccollātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟkuk karuvi ceyyār eṉka. ituvum uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu. aintum eṉpataṉ ummi īṇṭu eccappaṭa niṉṟatu.
u-m: tu, no eṉavarum.	(11)
45.	ōreḻut torumoḻi yīreḻut torumoḻi
	iraṇṭiṟan ticikkun toṭarmoḻi yuḷappaṭa
	mūṉṟē moḻinili tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.
iḵtu, eḻuttiṉāṉ ākum moḻikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ōr eḻuttu oru moḻi - ōreḻuttāṉ ākum orumoḻi, īr eḻuttu orumoḻi - iraṇṭeḻuttāṉ ākum orumoḻi, iraṇṭu iṟantu icikkum toṭarmoḻi - iraṇṭiṟantu palavaṟṟāṉ icikkum toṭarmoḻi, uḷappaṭa mūṉṟu - uṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷiṉ nilimi mūṉṟām, tōṉṟiya neṟi - avi tōṉṟiya vaḻakku neṟikkaṇ. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci)
u-m: ā, maṇi, varaku, koṟṟaṉ eṉavarum.	(12)
46.	meyyi ṉiyakka makaramoṭu civaṇum.
itu, taṉi meykaḷic collum muṟinili itu veṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) meyyiṉ iyakkam - taṉi meykaḷiṉatu iyakkam, akaramoṭu civaṇum - akarattoṭu poruntum.
u-m: “ṭa ṟa la ḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi”  (nūṉmarapu 23) eṉavarum.
itu moḻiyiṭi (niṉṟa) eḻuttukaḷ aṉmiyiṉ nūṉmarapiṉ vikka veṉil taṉṉi uṇarttātu vēṟu poruḷ uṇarttum col nilipōla ‘ṭaṟalaḷa’ veṉṟatu uyirmeyyi uṇarttātu taṉi meyyi uṇarttalāṉum, oṟṟiṉi uyirmey pōlac collukiṉṟa vaḻuvamiti yilakkaṇattāṉum moḻimarapiṉkaṇṇa tāyiṟṟeṉa uṇarka.		(13)
47.	tammiyal kiḷappi ṉellā veḻuttum
	meynnili mayakka māṉa milli.
itu, meymmayakkattiṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum tam iyal kiḷappiṉ - ellā mey yeḻuttum moḻiyiṭiyaṉṟit tam vaṭiviṉ iyalpic collumiṭattu, meymayakkanili māṉam illi - mey mayakka niliyiṉ mayaṅkivarutal kuṟṟam illi.
u-m: “valleḻut tiyiyiṉ ṭakāra mākum” (puḷḷimayaṅku -7) eṉa varum.
itaṉi am meymmayakkattu vikkaveṉiṉ, itu vaḻuvamiti nōkki moḻimarapiṉkaṇṇa tāyiṟṟu. 	(14)
48.	yaraḻa veṉṉum mūṉṟumuṉ ṉoṟṟak
	kacatapa ṅañanama vīroṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu, īroṟṟu uṭaṉili āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ya ra ḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟu - yaraḻa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu, muṉ oṟṟa - (kuṟiṟkīḻum neṭiṟ kīḻum) muṉṉē oṟṟāy niṟpa (avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉē), kacatapa ṅañanama īr oṟṟākum - kacatapakkaḷiṉ oṉṟātal ṅañanamakkaḷiṉ oṉṟātal oṟṟāyvara avi īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyām.

u-m: vēykkuṟi, vēyṅkuṟi, vērkkuṟi, vērṅkuṟi, vīḻkkuṟi, vīḻṅkuṟi; ciṟi, tali, puṟam eṉa oṭṭuka.
iv vitimēl īṟṟakattu uṇarntukoḷḷappaṭumā leṉiṉ, iḵtu ‘īrkku’, ‘pīrkku’ eṉa oru moḻiyuḷḷum varutalāṉum, iraṇṭumoḻikkaṇ varutal  vikāramātalāṉum īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. aḵtēl, itaṉi nūṉmarapiṉakattu meymmayakkattukkaṇ kūṟukaveṉiṉ, āṇṭu vēṟṟuminayam koṇṭatākaliṉ īroṟṟu uṭaṉiliyātal nōkki oṟṟuminayampaṟṟi īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(15)
49.	avaṟṟuḷ
	rakāra ḻakāraṅ kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā.
itu, rakāra ḻakāraṅkaṭku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum, rakāram ḻakāram kuṟṟu oṟṟu ākā - rakāramum ḻakāramum kuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟākā. avi neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyām.
u-m: tār, tāḻ eṉa neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟāy niṉṟaṉa. karu, maḻu eṉak kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyāy niṉṟaṉa.
ivvāṟu vilakkiṉamiyiṉ, yakaram poy eṉavum nōy eṉavum iraṇṭiṭattum oṟṟāyiṟṟu. kuṟṟoṟṟu eṉpatu kuṟitākiya oṟṟu eṉap paṇputtoki. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṟṟalāṉ, kuṟiyatu eṉappaṭṭatu. īṇṭuk kuṟil neṭil eṉkiṉṟatu moḻimutal eḻuttiṉi eṉa uṇarka. itu, mēl variyaṟi ila eṉappaṭṭa uyirum meyyum mayaṅkum mayakkattiṟku oru variyaṟi kaṇṭu kūṟiṉavāṟu.	(16)
50.	kuṟumiyu neṭumiyu maḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ
	ṟoṭarmoḻi yellā neṭṭeḻut tiyala.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum aḷaviṉ kōṭaliṉ - uyireḻuttiṟkuk kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum aḷaviṟ koḷḷap paṭutaliṉ, toṭarmoḻi ellām neṭṭeḻuttu iyala - toṭarmoḻik kīḻ niṉṟa rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷellām neṭiṟkīḻ niṉṟa rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpiyuṭiya (eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum).
u-m: akar, pukar, akaḻ, pukaḻ eṉak koḷka.
‘pulavar’ eṉṟāṟpōla iraṇṭu māttiriyi iṟantataṉ piṉṉum varumāleṉiṉ, aviyum ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ ‘neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu’ eṉappaṭum. ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi, piṟa oṟṟukaḷum ‘neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu’ eṉappaṭum. itaṉāṉē, viral tītu eṉpuḻi lakaram ‘neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu’ eṉṟu keṭukkappaṭum.	(17)
51.	ceyyu ḷiṟutip pōlumoḻivayiṉ
	ṉakāra makāra mīroṟ ṟākum.
itu, ceyyuṭkaṇ īroṟṟu uṭaṉili āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuḷ iṟuti pōlum moḻivayiṉ - ceyyuḷ iṟutik kaṇ ‘pōlum’ eṉṉum moḻikkaṇ, ṉakāram makāram īr oṟṟu ākum - ṉakāramum makāramum vantu īroṟṟu uṭaṉiliyāy niṟkum (pōli eṉavum pāṭam)
u-m: ‘emmoṭu tammip porūuṅkāṟ poṉṉoṭu, kūviḷam pūttatu pōṉm’ eṉavarum.	 (18)
52.	ṉakāra muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum.
iḵtu, “ariyaḷapu kuṟukaṉ makara muṭittu” (nūṉmarapu 13)  eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṉakāram muṉṉar makāram kuṟukum - (mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa) ṉakārattu muṉvanta makāram māttiri kuṟuki niṟkum.	 (19)
53.	moḻippaṭut ticippiṉun terintuvē 		ṟicippiṉum
	eḻuttiya  ṟiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, eḻuttukaṭku moḻikkaṇ māttiri kāraṇamākap piṟappatōr aiyam tīrttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻippaṭuttu icippiṉum - moḻikkaṇ paṭuttuc colliṉum, terintu vēṟu icippiṉum - terintukoṇṭu vēṟē colliṉum, eḻuttu iyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar - uyirum meyyumākiya eḻuttukaḷ (perukkam curukkam uṭiyaṉapōṉṟu icippiṉum,) tattam māttiri iyalpiṉ tiriyā eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	aḵkal, a eṉavum, āl, ā eṉavum, kaṭal, ka eṉavum, kāl, kā eṉavum kaṇṭukoḷka.
‘vēṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, eṭuttal paṭuttal mutaliya ōci vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉpatu koḷka. (20)
54.	akara ikara mikāra mākum.
itu, pōli eḻuttu āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ikaram - akaramum ikaramum kūṭac colla, aikāramākum - aikārampōla ākum.
u-m:  aiyar, aiyar eṉavarum. atu koḷḷaṟka. 	(21)
55.	akara ukara mukāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ukaram - akaramum ukaramum kūṭac colla, oḷakāram ākum - oḷakārampōla ākum e-ṟu.
u-m: oḷavi, auvi eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.  atu koḷḷaṟka. 	(22)
56.	akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
	aiye ṉeṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) akarattu impar yakarap puḷḷiyum - akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaramēyaṉṟi yakaramākiya puḷḷiyum, ai eṉ neṭuñciṉi mey peṟat tōṉṟum - ai eṉappaṭṭa neṭṭeḻuttām vaṭivupeṟat tōṉṟum.
u-m: aivaṉam, ayvaṉam eṉavarum.
‘meypeṟat tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, (akarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaramēyaṉṟi,) vakarappuḷḷiyum oḷakārampōla varumeṉak koḷka. 
iccūttiram,
“akarat timpar yavakarap puḷḷiyum
  aiyu neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum”
eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
i-ḷ: akarattu impar yavakarap puḷḷiyum akarattiṉ piṉṉar yakara voṟṟum vakara voṟṟum, ai oḷa neṭuñciṉi - aikāram oḷakāram eṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttām. meypeṟa tōṉṟum. (avi) vaṭivu peṟattōṉṟum.
u-m: aiyavi, ayyavi; oḷavi, avvi 
‘mey peṟattōṉṟum” eṉṟaṉāl, avaṟṟik koḷka eṉṟavāṟu’.
kālap paḻimiyil ēṭu peyarttu eḻutiṉōr `yavakarappuḷḷi’ eṉpataṉi `yavarappuḷḷi’ eṉavum `aibyḷa neṭuñciṉi’ eṉpataṉi `aiye ṉeṭuñciṉi’ eṉavum piḻiyāka eḻutiṉar pōlum. appiḻippāṭattip piḻiyaṟṟa pāṭameṉak karuti uriyāciriyar ataṟkut takkavāṟu uriyeḻutic ceṉṟaṉar pōlum.) eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.	(23)
57.	ōraḷa pāku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
	tēruṅ kāli moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, eṉ nutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, uyirkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku māttiric curukkam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tēruṅkāli moḻivayiṉ ōr aḷapu ākum iṭaṉum uṇṭu - aikāram ārāyuṅkālattu moḻikkaṇ ōr aḷapāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu.
u-m: iṭiyaṉ, maṭiyaṉ eṉavarum.
‘tēruṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutaṟkaṇ curuṅkāteṉpatu koḷka. “iṭaṉ” eṉṟataṉāṉ ik kuṟukkam ciṟupāṉmi yeṉpatu koḷka.	
iccūttirattiṟkup piṉvarumāṟu urittalē porutta muṭittu:-
iḵtu, uyirkaḷuḷ iraṇṭaṟku māttiric curukkam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
i-ḷ: tērum kāli moḻivayiṉ āṉa- ārāyuṅkālattu moḻikkaṇ niṉṟa ākāra ūkāraṅkaḷ, ōr aḷapu ākum iṭaṉum uṇṭu ōr aḷapāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu.
`tērum `kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, taṉiyē  niṉṟa aikāramum oḷakāramum oru māttiriyāku meṉṟukoḷka.
u-m: pi, mi, ki; ku, cu, veḷa.
`iṭaṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ikkuṟukkam ciṟupāṉmi yeṉpatu koḷka. ār eṉpaṉa acikaḷ.
mēl “yakarappuḷḷiyum aiya ṉeṭuñciṉi” eṉṟa piḻip pāṭattip piḻiyaṟṟa pāṭameṉak koṇṭatāl uri yāciriyar īṇṭu oḷakārattik kūṟātu aikāram ōr aḷapāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu eṉṟu urittuc ceṉṟaṉar pōlum.     		(24)
58.	ikara yakara miṟuti viravum.
ituvum oru pōliyeḻuttu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṟuti ikara yakaram viravum - ikaravīṟṟu moḻikkaṇ yakaramum (atupōla) ikaramum viravivarum e-ṟu.
u-m: nāy, nāi eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. (viravum eṉṟataṉāl, aviyiraṇṭum koḷka eṉṟavāṟu.)	  (25)
59.	paṉṉī ruyiru moḻimuta lākum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttu moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉṉīr uyirum moḻimutal ākum - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.
u-m: aṭi, āṭi, ili, īyam, ural, ūrti, eḻu, ēṇi, aivaṉam, oḷi, ōkkam, oḷaviyam eṉavarum.	(26)
60.	uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā.
itu, meyyeḻuttu moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirmey allaṉa moḻi mutal ākā - uyiroṭu kūṭiya meyyallātaṉavākiya taṉimeykaḷ moḻikku mutalākā; uyiroṭu kūṭiya meykaḷ moḻikku mutalām.
īṇṭu uyirmey yeṉpatu vēṟṟuminayam karutiyeṉa vuṇarka. īṇṭu oṟṟumi karutil, “katana pameṉum āvin teḻuttum, ellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē”   (moḻimarapu - 28) eṉac cūttiram curuṅka varuvataṉṟi, itaṉāṟ collappaṭṭa aṟupatu uyirmeyyiṉi eṭuttōta vēṇṭic cūttiram parakka varumeṉpatu.
(`curuṅka ōtumaṉṟi’ eṉavum, `ōtavēṇṭil’ eṉavum muntiya accup piratiyilum ēṭṭup piratikaḷilum kāṇappaṭum (urip) pāṭam ēṭupeyart teḻutiṉōrāl nērnta piḻi pōlum)	 (27)
61.	katana pameṉu māvin teḻuttum
	ellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē.
itu, mēl mutalām eṉṉappaṭṭa uyirmeykaṭku variyaṟi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ta na pa ma eṉum a aintu eḻuttum - ka ta na pa ma eṉṟu collappaṭṭa aintu taṉi meyyeḻuttum, ellā uyiroṭum mutal cellum - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirōṭum moḻikku mutalātaṟkuc cellum.
u-m: kali, kāḷi, kiḷi, kīri, kuṭi, kūṭu, keṇṭi, kēḻal, kiti, koṇṭal, kōṭi, kuvi eṉavum; tanti, tāṭi, tiṟṟi, tīmi, tuṇi, tūṇi, teṟṟi, tēvar, tiyal, toṇṭi, tōṭu, tuvi eṉavum; naṭam, nāri, nilam, nīr, nuḻi, nūl, neytal, nēyam, niki, noyyaṉ, nōkkam, nuvi eṉavum; paṭi, pāṭi, piṭi, pīṭam, pukaḻ, pūmi, peṭi, pēṭi, pital, poṉ, pōtakam, puvam eṉavum; maṭam, māli, miṭaṟu, mīṉam, mukam, mūtūr, melintatu, mēṉi, miyal, moḻi, mōtakam, muval eṉavum varum.
‘mutaṟku’ eṉpataṉ nāṉkām urupu vikārattāṉ tokkatu. (ār eṉpatu  aci. ēkāram - īṟṟaci)	(28)
62.	cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
	aai oḷaveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟu ōr aṟṟu - cakaramākiya eḻuttum mēṟcollappaṭṭavipōla ellā uyiroṭum moḻikku mutalām, a ai oḷa eṉum mūṉṟu alaṅkaṭi - a ai oḷa eṉṉum mūṉṟum allāviṭattu.
u-m: cāli, cili, cīṟuka, curumpu, cūḻka, ceyki, cēvaṭi, coṟika, cōṟu eṉa varum.
cakaṭam eṉavum, ciyam eṉavum vilakkiṉavum varumāleṉiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ āriyacciti vallātaṉa ‘kaṭicol illi’  eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. (ēkāram iraṇṭum īṟṟacikaḷ. innūl mūlattil `ōr aṉṉa,’ `ōr aṟṟu’ eṉṟu varum iṭaṅkaḷil `ōr’ eṉpataṉi aciyeṉṟu koḷḷutal poruttamuṭiyatākat tōṟṟukiṉṟatu.)	 (29)
63.	uū oō veṉṉu nāṉkuyir
	veṉ ṉeḻuttoṭu varuta lilli.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō eṉṉum nāṉku uyir - u ū o ō eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa nāṉku uyirum, va eṉ eḻuttoṭu varutal illi - va eṉṉum meyyeḻuttoṭu moḻi mutalil varutalilli.
piṟa uyirkaḷ varum.
u-m: vaḷi, vāḷi, viḷari, vīṭu, veḷḷi, vēr, viyam, veḷavu eṉa varum.	(30)
64.	ā e
	oeṉu mūvuyir ñakārat turiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ā e o eṉum mū uyir ñakārattu uriya - ā e o eṉṟu collappaṭum mūvuyirkaḷ ñakāra oṟṟoṭu mutalātaṟku uriya; piṟa uyir uriyavalla.
u-m: ñālam, ñekiḻi, ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉavarum.
ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumā leṉiṉ, avi aḻi vaḻakkeṉṟu maṟukka.	  (31)
(pāṭam)  `aḻi vaḻakkeṉṟu maṟukka’ eṉpatu.
65.	āvō ṭallatu yakara mutalātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āvōṭu allatu yakaram mutalātu - ākārattōṭu allatu yakaram mutalākātu.
u-m: yāṉ eṉa varum.
yavaṉar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumāleṉiṉ, avi āriyac citiveṉṟu maṟukka.	(32)
66.	mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum.
itu, moḻikku mutalākātaṉavum orōvaḻi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalā ēṉa - moḻikku mutalākāta oḻinta meykaḷum, tam peyar mutalum - (av veḻuttukaḷ) tam peyar kūṟutaṟku mutalām.
mutalāyiṉa mey katanapamakkaḷum, vakaramum, cakaramum, ñakaramum, yakaramum eṉa ivi. mutalākāta mey ṅakaramum, ṭakaramum, ṇakaramum, rakaramum, lakaramum, ḻakaramum, ḷakaramum, ṟakaramum, ṉakaramum eṉa ivi.
u-m: avi tam peyarkku mutalāmāṟu: ṅakkaḷintār, ṭapperitu, ṇannaṉṟu eṉṟāṟpōla oṭṭik koḷka.
iṉi ‘ēṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutalām eṉṉappaṭṭa oṉpatu uyir meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum tam peyar kūṟum vaḻiyum moḻikku mutalām eṉakkoḷka.
u-m: kakkaḷintār, tapperitu, akkuṟitu, āvalitu eṉṟāṟ pōla oṭṭikkoḷka.	(33)
67.	kuṟṟiya lukara muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ
	oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ - kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟip peyariṭattu, oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum - oṟṟāy niṉṟa nakarattiṉ mēlāya nakarattoṭu moḻikku mutalām.
u-m: nunti eṉa varum.
ivvāṟu mutalākkam kūṟavē, moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiya lukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. iṭam nunti eṉṉum muṟippeyar. paṟṟukkōṭu nakaramici nakaram. (nakaramici nakaram - nakara oṟṟiṉ mēluḷḷa nakara oṟṟu)	 (34)
68.	muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāa
	tappeyar maruṅki ṉiliyiya lāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) muṟṟiyalukaramoṭu poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu - (am mutaṟkaṭ kuṟṟiyalukaram āṇṭu itaḻkuvittuk kūṟum vaḻi) muṟṟukarattoṭu piṟa ukarampōlap poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu; (yāṇṭeṉiṉ,) ap peyar maruṅkiṉ nili iyalāṉ - am muṟippeyariṭattut tāṉ niṟṟaṟkaṇ e-ṟu.
u-m:  nāku eṉavum, naku eṉavum kuṟukiyum, kuṟukātum niṉṟa ukaraṅkaḷpōla, nunti eṉṟu kuṟukkamāya vaḻiyum, itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟak kuṟukātavaḻiyum, poruḷ vēṟupaṭātavāṟu aṟika.
iṉi ‘iraṭṭuṟamoḻital’ eṉpataṉāl, poruḷ eṉṟataṉi iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum ākki, piṟa ukaraṅkaḷpōla īṇṭi ukaraṅkaḷ iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum vēṟupaṭātu eṉpatu koḷka. (īṟṟakaram cāriyi)	(35)

69.	uyir oḷa veñciya viṟuti yākum.
iḵtu, uyir moḻikku īṟāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir oḷa eñciya iṟuti ākum - uyireḻuttu kaḷuḷ oḷakāram oḻintavi moḻikku īṟām, oḷakārantāṉ īṟākātu.
iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu.
u-m: āa, ā; īi, ī; ūu, ū;ēe, ē; aii, ai; ōo, ō eṉa uyir īṟāyiṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟeḻuttintum aḷapeṭi vakiyāṉ īṟāyiṉa.
uyirmeykaḷum, mēlvariyaṟi kūṟātaṉavākiya akara ākāra ikara īkāra aikāraṅkaḷōṭu iyintaṉa īṇṭē koḷka.
u-m: viḷa, palā,kiḷi, kurī, paṉi eṉa varum.	(36)
70.	kavavō ṭiyiyi ṉuvu mākum.
iḵtu, īṟākāteṉṟa oḷakāram iṉṉuḻi ām eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ka va ōṭu iyiyiṉ - kakara vakaramākiya meykaḷoṭu poruntiṉ, oḷum ākum - muṉ īṟākā teṉṟa oḷakāramum īṟām.
u-m: ku, veḷa eṉa varum.	(37)
71.	e eṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākātu.
iḵtu, ekaram tāṉē niṉṟavaḻiyaṉṟi meyyōṭukūṭi īṟākāteṉa variyaṟi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) e eṉa varum uyir - e eṉṟu colla varum uyir, mey īṟu ākātu - tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi meyyōṭu iyintu īṟākātu.	(38)
72.	ovvu maṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭiyē.
ituvum variyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) ovvum aṟṟu - okaramum tāṉē niṉṟu īṟāva taṉṟi meyyōṭu iyintu īṟākātu, na alaṅkaṭi - nakara meyyō ṭallāta viṭattu e-ṟu.
u-m: nu eṉa varum.	(39)
73.	ēō eṉumuyir ñakārat tilli.
ituvum variyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) ē ō eṉum uyir ñakārattu illi - ē ō eṉṟu collappaṭum uyir (tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum īṟāvataṉṟi) ñakarattōṭu īṟāvatilli.
tāmē yātal muṉṉē kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāvaṉavaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa voḻiya iṟavātaṉa vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(40)
74.	uū kāra navavoṭu navilā.
ituvum variyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) u ūkāram na va oṭu navilā - ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ (tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum payilvataṉṟi) nakara vakaraṅkaḷoṭu payilā.
tāmē īṟātal mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāmavaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa vallātaṉa vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka. ‘navilā’ eṉṟataṉāṟ ciṟupāṉmi novvu kavvu eṉṟu (ukaram) vakārattōṭu īṟātal koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmi nakarattōṭu varavu uṇṭēṉum koḷka. 	(41)
75.	ucca kāra mirumoḻik kurittē.
ituvum atu orōvaḻi variyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāram irumoḻikku urittu - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakaram iru moḻikku īṟākum; (palamoḻikku īṟākātu).
u-m: ucu, mucu eṉa varum. pacuveṉpatu āriyac citivu.  (42)
76.	uppa kāra moṉṟeṉa moḻipa
	iruvayi ṉiliyum poruṭṭā kummē.
ituvum moḻivariyaṟiyum moḻiyatu poruḷ pāṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāram oṉṟu eṉa moḻipa - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya pakaram oru moḻikku īṟām eṉṟu colluvar, iruvayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭu ākum - atutāṉ taṉviṉi piṟaviṉi eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattum nilipeṟum poruṇmit tām e-ṟu.
u-m: tapu eṉa varum. itu paṭuttuc colla, nī cā eṉat taṉ viṉiyām. eṭuttuc colla, nī oṉṟaṉic cāvippi eṉap piṟa viṉiyām. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	(43)
77.	eñciya vellā meñcuta lilavē.
itu, moḻikku īṟākā uyirmeyyum orōvaḻi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eñciya ellām eñcutal ila - moḻikku īṟākātu niṉṟa uyirmeykaḷellām tam peyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātaṟku oḻipu ila.
eñciya uyirmeyyāvaṉa: oḷakāram kakāra vakāraṅkaḷi oḻinta meyyōṭu iyinta uyirmeyyum, ekaram ellā meyyōṭum iyinta uyirmeyyum, okaram nakaram oḻinta meyyōṭu iyinta uyirmeyyum, ēkāra ōkāram ñakārattōṭu iyinta uyir meyyum, u ūkāram nakara vakaraṅkaḷōṭu iyinta uyirmeyyum eṉa ivi.
u-m: tam peyarkku (īṟu) āmāṟu: ṅuk kaḷintār eṉavum, kek kaḷintār eṉavum, kok kaḷintār eṉavum, ñēk kaḷintār eṉavum, ñōk kaḷintār eṉavum, nuk kaḷintār eṉavum, nūk kaḷintār eṉavum, vuk kaḷintār eṉavum, vūk kaḷintār eṉavum varum.  ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, moḻikku īṟāy niṉṟa uyir meykaḷum tam peyar kūṟum vaḻiyum īṟām eṉṟu koḷka. kak kaḷintār, kā valitu eṉavum, ak kaḷintār, ā valitu eṉavum ivvāṟē oṭṭik koḷka. taṉṉi uṇara niṉṟavaḻi, moḻikaṭku itu karuviyāka īṟṟakattu muṭipu ‘oṉṟiṉa muṭittalāṟ’ koḷka.	(44)
78.	ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa veṉṉum
	appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti.
itu, taṉimeykaḷuḷ moḻikku īṟāvaṉa kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum ap patiṉoṉṟē - ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ap patiṉoṉṟumē, puḷḷi iṟuti - puḷḷikaḷil moḻikku īṟāvaṉa.
u-m: uriñ, maṇ, porun, tirum, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vēḷ eṉa varum. ṉakāratti īṟṟu viyātu, makarattoṭu vittatu ataṉ mayakka iyipu nōkki eṉṟuṇarka.	 (45)
79.	ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum.
ituvum moḻivariyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāramoṭu nakāram civaṇum - ukarattōṭu kūṭiya cakārattōṭē nakāram porunti aḵtu irumoḻikku īṟāyavāṟu pōlat tāṉum irumoḻikku īṟām. 
u-m: porun, verin eṉa varum.	          (46)
(ukarattōṭu kūṭiya cakaram irumoḻikku īṟāyavāṟu pōla nakaram iru moḻikku īṟām eṉka.)
80.	uppa kāramoṭu ñakāriyu maṟṟē
	apporu ḷiraṭṭā tivaṇi yāṉa.
ituvum moḻivariyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāramoṭu ñakāriyum aṟṟu - ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakarattoṭu ñakāramum attaṉmittāy orumoḻikku īṟām, ivaṇiyāṉa a poruḷ iraṭṭātu - ivviṭattu ataṉ poruḷ av vuppakāram pōla iruporuḷpaṭātu.
u-m: uriñ eṉa varum.
ñakāram orumoḻikku īṟātaliṉ, nakarattiṉpiṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci)	(47)
81.	vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu.
ituvum moḻivariyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu - vakaramākiya eḻuttu nāṉkumoḻi īṟṟatām e-ṟu.
u-m: av, iv, uv, tev eṉa varum.	(48)
82.	makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
	ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi yoṉpaḵ teṉpa
	pukaraṟak kiḷanta vaḵṟiṇi mēṉa.
ituvum orōvaḻi moḻivariyaṟi.
(i-ḷ.) pukar aṟa kiḷanta aḵṟiṇi mēṉa - kuṟṟam aṟac collappaṭṭa aḵṟiṇiyiṭattu, makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta - makaravīṟṟut toṭarmoḻiyoṭu mayaṅkāteṉṟu variyaṟukkappaṭṭa, ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵtu eṉpa - ṉakaravīṟṟut toṭarmoḻi oṉpateṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: nilaṉ nilam, pilaṉ pilam eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa mayaṅkuvaṉa. iṉi mayaṅkātaṉa: ekiṉ, cekiṉ, viḻaṉ, payiṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, kuyiṉ, kaṭāṉ, vayāṉ eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟuḷ tiripuṭiyaṉa kaḷika. oṉpaḵtu eṉṉum āytam ceyyuḷ vikāram. aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟatu - īṇṭu aḵṟiṇip peyariṉi.	 (49)
iraṇṭāvatu - moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.


piṟappiyal
akara mutal ṉakaraviṟutiyākavuḷḷa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatiyum nūṉmarapilum cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟiyum moḻi marapilum vittuṇarttiya āciriyar. ammuppattu mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappu muṟiyiṉi ivviyalāl uṇarttukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ivviyal piṟappiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
untiyilirunteḻukiṉṟa kāṟṟāṉatu tali, miṭaṟu, neñcu eṉṉum mūṉṟaṭaṅkaḷilum nilipeṟṟu avaṟṟuṭaṉ pal, utaṭu, nā, mūkku, aṇṇam (mēlvāy) ākiya uṟuppukkaḷ tammiṟ porunti amitipeṟa vēṟuvēṟuruvākiya eḻuttukkaḷāyp piṟantu pulappaṭa vaḻaṅkutalē eḻuttukkaḷuk kuriya potuvākiya piṟappu muṟiyākum itaṉi ivviyaliṉ mutaṟ cūttirattu āciriyar viritturikkiṉṟār. uyireḻuttukkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum tattam nilitiriyātu miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum. eṉavē uyireḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭam miṭaṟu eṉpatu pulaṉām. ōriṭattē oru muyaṟciyāṟ palaveḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkumeṉap potuvakiyāṟ kūṟiṉum avveḻuttuk kaḷiṭiyē nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉaveṉa āciriyar teḷivupaṭuttukiṉṟār.
ivviyaliṉkaṇ 7-mutal 11-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil kaṅa, caña, ṭaṇa, tana, ākiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷukku neṭuṅ kaṇakku muṟi paṟṟip piṟappuk kūṟukiṉṟār. 12 mutal nāvatikāram paṟṟip piṟappukkūṟat toṭaṅki ṟaṉa, raḻa, laḷa, pama, vaya, eṉṉum meyyeḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. iṅkup piṟappukkūṟiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ melleḻut tāṟum tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkañ colliya iṭattē nili peṟṟaṉavāyiṉum avi mūkkiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya kāṟṟōciyāl iyipupeṟat tōṉṟiyolippaṉavām. ivvāṟu melleḻut tāṟukkum mūkkuvaḷiyiṉ toṭarpuṭimiyiṉic collavē iṭiyeḻut tāṟum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyiyum valleḻuttāṟum talivaḷiyiyum peṟṟu olippaṉaveṉak koṇṭār iḷampūraṇar.
mutaleḻuttukkaḷic cārntutōṟṟiṉallatu taṉit tolikkum iyalpillātaṉa eṉa ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttap paṭṭa cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟum tattamakkuc cārpākiya muta leḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭattiyē tamakkuriya piṟappiṭamākap porunti icippaṉavām. ivvāṟu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟap puṇarttiya tolkāppiyaṉār avveḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiriyiṉik kuṟittukkūṟum viḷakkam ivaṇ karutattakuva tākum. ellāveḻuttukkaḷiyum veḷippaṭac collap paṭṭa iṭattiṉkaṇ eḻuṅkāṟṟiṉālē olikkumiṭattu avveḻuttukkaḷ yāvum koppūḻaṭiyilirunteḻuṅ kāṟṟāṉatu taliyaḷavuñ ceṉṟu mīṇṭum neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟutalākiya tiritaruṅ kūṟu pāṭṭiṉiyuṭiyaṉa. ivveḻuttukkaḷukku iṅṅaṉam uṟaḻcci vārattiṉāl uḷatām akatteḻuvaḷiyiciyiṉi nuṭpamāka ārāyntu māttiri variyaṟiyāl aḷantu koḷḷutal antaṇaratu maṟinūṟ kaṇṇatākiya muṟiyākum. ammuṟiyiṉi innūliṟ collātu ellārkkum pulaṉākap puṟattē veḷippaṭṭicikkum meyterivaḷiyiciyākiya eḻuttuk kaḷukkē yāṉ iṅku māttiri kūṟiṉēṉ eṉpatu āciriyar kūṟiya viḷakkamākum. untiyil eḻuntakāṟṟu muṉṉart talikkaṭ ceṉṟu piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilēvantu ataṉpiṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟal uṟaḻccivāram eṉappaṭum. mūlātārattilirun teḻuṅ kāṟṟōci akatteḻuvaḷiyici yeṉappaṭṭatu. ellārkkum eḻutturuvam naṉku pulaṉāka vāyiliruntu puṟattē veḷippaṭ ṭicikkum kāṟṟiṉālākiya eḻuttōciyē meyterivaḷi yici yeṉappaṭum. antaṇar maṟiyiṟ kūṟumāṟu akat teḻuvaḷiyiciyākiya eḻuttukkaḷukku aḷapukūṟiṉ aḵtu ellārkkum naṉṟāka viḷaṅkāteṉak karutiya tolkāppiyaṉār puṟatteḻunticikkum meyterivaḷi yiciyākiya eḻuttukkaḷukkē māttiri kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
untuyileḻunta kāṟṟiṉik kūṟupaṭutti māttiri kūṭṭikkoḷḷutalum, mūlātāram mutalākak kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟutalum, antaṇar maṟikku uḷateṉṟu kūṟiya ivvāciriyar, avar matam paṟṟip peṟuvatōr payaṉiṉṟeṉa ic cūttirattāl uyttuṇara vittaliṉ, iccūttiram piṟaṉ kōṭkūṟal eṉṉum uttikkiṉam eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
- veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 168-170


mūṉṟāvatu 
piṟappiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ  piṟappiyal  eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉi nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉē vikkaveṉiṉ, cārpiṟṟōṟṟat teḻuttum taṉimeyyum moḻimarapiṭi uṇarttip piṟappu uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ, moḻi marapiṉ piṉṉatāyiṟṟu.

83.	unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
	taliyiṉu miṭaṟṟiṉu neñciṉu niliip
	pallu mitaḻu nāvu mūkkum
	aṇṇamu muḷappaṭa veṇmuṟi niliyāṉ
	uṟuppuṟ ṟamiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
	ellā veḻuttuñ colluṅ kālip
	piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
	tiṟappaṭat teriyuṅ kāṭci yāṉa.
it talic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, eḻuttukaḷatu potup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum neṟippaṭa nāṭi collum kāli - tamiḻeḻuttukaḷellām oruvaṉ muṟippaṭa ārāyntu tammic colluṅkālattu, unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi - koppūḻ aṭiyāka mēlē kiḷarkiṉṟa utāṉaṉ eṉṉum peyariyuṭiya vāyut tōṉṟi, taliyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilii - taliyiṉ kaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum neñciṉkaṇṇum nili peṟṟu, pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ uṟuppu uṟṟu amiya - (taliyum miṭaṟum neñcum eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉōṭum) pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṭṭākiya muṟimiyiyuṭiya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ ōr uṟuppōṭu ōr uṟupput tammiṟ porunti amitipeṟa, piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟu vēṟu iyala - piṟappiṉatu ākkam vēṟu vēṟu pulappaṭṭu vaḻaṅkutaluṭiya, tiṟappaṭat teriyum kāṭciyāṉa - kūṟupāṭuḷatāka ārāyum aṟiviṭattu.
itaḻpōṟalāṉ vāy itaḻeṉappaṭṭatu. ‘ellā eḻuttum’ eṉṉum eḻuvāykkup ‘piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvēṟu iyala’ eṉpataṉi orucoṉ ṉīrmippaṭuttip payaṉili yākkuka. (`mutalāka’ eṉpatu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟatu īṟṟakaram cāriyi) 	(1)
84.	avvaḻip
	paṉṉī ruyirun tannili tiriyā
	miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyi ṉicikkum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttiṟkup potuppiṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a vaḻi - avviṭattu, paṉṉīr uyirum tam nili tiriyā - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum tattam niliyiṉ tiriyāvāy, miṭaṟṟu piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icikkum - miṭaṟṟiṉkaṭ piṟanta vaḷiyāṉ olikkum.
‘tannili tiriyā’ eṉṟataṉāl, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiya lukaram tannili tiriyumeṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
85.	avaṟṟuḷ
	aā vāyiraṇ ṭaṅkān tiyalum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttukaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟappup piṟavi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyir paṉṉiraṇ ṭaṉuḷ, a ā airaṇṭu aṅkāntu iyalum - akara ākāraṅkaḷākiya av viraṇṭum aṅkāntu colla aḵtu iṭamākap piṟakkum.	(3)
86.	iī eē aiyeṉa vicikkum
	appā lintu mavaṟṟō raṉṉa
	avitām
	aṇpaṉ mutaṉā viḷimpuṟa luṭiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) i ī e ē ai eṉa icikkum a pāl aintum - i ī e ē ai eṉṟu collappaṭum ak kūṟṟu aintum, avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - mēṟkūṟiya akara ākāraṅkaḷ pōla aṅkāntu collap piṟakkum, avitām palaṇ nāmutal viḷimpu uṟal uṭiya - avitām (avvāṟu collap piṟakkumiṭattup) palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉi nāviṉatu aṭiyiṉ viḷimpu ceṉṟu uṟutali yuṭiya.	(4)
87.	uū oō oḷeṉa icikkum
	appā lintum itaḻkuvin tiyalum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō oḷa eṉa icikkum a pāl aintum - u ū o ō oḷa eṉac colla icikkum ak kūṟṟu aintum, itaḻ kuvintu iyalum - itaḻ kuvittuc colla naṭakkum.	(5)
88.	tattan tiripē ciṟiya veṉpa.
itu, muṉkūṟiya uyirkkum mēṟkūṟum meykkum ōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tamtam tiripu ciṟiya eṉpa - (eḻuttukaḷ oru tāṉattuk kūṭip piṟakku meṉappaṭṭaṉa; avvāṟu kūṭip piṟappiṉum,) tattam vēṟupāṭukaḷic ciṟiya vēṟupāṭukaḷeṉṟu colluvar. av vēṟupāṭu aṟintukoḷka. (ēkāram - aci).	(6)
89.	kakāra ṅakāram mutaṉā vaṇṇam.
itu, meykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟavi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kakāram ṅakāram nāmutal aṇṇam (mutal) - kakāramum ṅakāramum nāmutalum aṇṇamutalum uṟap 
piṟakkum. 	(7)
90.	cakāra ñakāra miṭinā vaṇṇam.
(i-ḷ.) cakāram ñakāram nāiṭi aṇṇam (iṭi) - cakāramum ñakāramum nāvatu iṭiyum aṇṇattatu iṭiyum uṟap piṟakkum.	(8)
91.	ṭakāra ṇakāra nuṉinā vaṇṇam.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṭakāram ṇakāram nānuṉi aṇṇam (nuṉi) - ṭakāramum ṇakāramum nāvatu nuṉiyum aṇṇattatu nuṉiyum uṟap piṟakkum.	(9)
92.	avvā ṟeḻuttum mūvakip piṟappiṉa.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a āṟu eḻuttum mūvaki piṟappiṉa - mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭa āṟu eḻuttum niraṉiṟi vakiyāṉ (aṟuvakip piṟappiṉa alla) mūvakip piṟappiṉa.	(10)
93.	aṇṇaṉ  naṇṇiya paṉmutal maruṅkiṉ
	nānuṉi parantu meyyuṟa voṟṟat
	tāmiṉitu piṟakkun takāra nakāram.
ituvum meykaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam naṇṇiya pal mutal maruṅkiṉ - aṇṇattip poruntiya palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē, nānuṉi parantu mey uṟa oṟṟa - nāviṉatu nuṉi parantu vaṭivi uṟumpaṭi oṟṟa, tām iṉitu piṟakkum - tām iṉitākap piṟakkum, takāram nakāram - takāramum nakāramum.
muṉṉē ‘uṟuppuṟ ṟamiya’ (83) eṉṟu vittup piṉṉum ‘meyyuṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ellā eḻuttukaḷum meyyuṟṟa pōtē iṉitu piṟappaveṉpatu koḷka.	(11)
94.	aṇari nuṉinā vaṇṇa moṟṟa
	ṟaḵkā ṉaḵkā ṉāyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇari nuṉinā aṇṇam oṟṟa - aṇarntu nuṉinā aṇṇattic ceṉṟu oṟṟa, ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - ṟakāra ṉakāramākiya av viraṇṭum piṟakkum.
iṅkuniṉṟu neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟimi viṭṭu nā atikāram paṭṭatu kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟateṉa vuṇarka.	 (12)
95.	nuṉinā vaṇari  yaṇṇam varuṭa
	rakāra ḻakāra māyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nuṉinā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa - nuṉinā aṇarntu aṇṇattit taṭava, rakāra ḻakāram a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - rakāra ḻakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum. 	(13)
96.	nāviḷimpu vīṅki yaṇpaṉ mutaluṟa
	āvayi ṇaṇṇa moṟṟavum varuṭavum
	lakāra ḷakāra māyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nā viḷimpu vīṅki pal aṇ mutal uṟa - nāviṉatu viḷimpu taṭittup palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattip porunta, a vayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟa lakāramum varuṭa ḷakāramum a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - avviṭattu (mutalnā) av vaṇṇatti oṟṟa lakāramum ataṉit taṭava ḷakāramumāka av viraṇṭum piṟakkum.	 (14)
97.	itaḻiyintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) itaḻ iyintu piṟakkum pakāram makāram - kīḻ itaḻum mēl itaḻum tammil iyiyap piṟakkum pakāramum makāramum.	(15)
98.	pallita ḻiyiya vakāram piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) pal itaḻ iyiya vakāram piṟakkum - mēṟpallum kīḻ itaḻum tammil iyiya vakāram piṟakkum.	(16)
99.	aṇṇañ cērnta miṭaṟṟeḻu vaḷiyici
	kaṇṇuṟ ṟaṭiya yakāram piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam cērnta miṭaṟṟu eḻu vaḷiici kaṇ uṟṟu aṭiya yakāram piṟakkum - aṇṇatti nāc cērnta viṭattu miṭaṟṟiṉiṉṟum eḻum vaḷiyāṉāya ici av vaṇṇatti aṇintu ceṟiya yakāram piṟakkum. 	(17)
100.	melleḻut tāṟum piṟappi ṉākkañ
	colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa vāyiṉum
	mūkkiṉ vaḷiyici yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
itu, melleḻuttiṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappiṉ ākkam colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa āyiṉum - melleḻuttukaḷ āṟum tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkam mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattē nili peṟṟaṉa vāyiṉum, mūkkiṉ vaḷi ici yāppuṟa tōṉṟum - avi mūkkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷatākiya vaḷiyatu iciyāṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
‘yāppuṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṭiyeḻuttiṟku miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyum, valleḻuttiṟkut talivaḷiyum koḷka.	(18)
101.	cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
	tērntuveḷip paṭutta vēṉi mūṉṟun
	tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
	otta kāṭciyiṉ tammiyal piyalum.
itu, cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttiṟkup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cārntuvariṉ allatu tamakku iyalpu ila eṉat tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum - cilavaṟṟic cārntu variṉ allatu tamakkut tāmē varum iyalpu ilaveṉṟu ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭa oḻinta mūṉṟum, tamtam cārpiṉ piṟappoṭu civaṇi otta kāṭciyiṉ tam iyalpu iyalum - tattamakkuc cārpākiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟap piṭattē piṟattaloṭu porunti avviṭattē tamakkuriya iyalpiṉ naṭakkum.
‘otta kāṭci’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, āytattiṟkuk kuṟṟeḻuttuc cārpēyeṉiṉum talivaḷiyāṟ piṟattaliṉ, uyiroṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttuc cārpākavē piṟakkumeṉpatu koḷka. ‘tammiyal piyalum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḷapeṭiyum uyirmeyyum tamakku aṭiyākiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka varumeṉpatu koḷka.	 (19)
102.	ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
	colliya paḷḷi yeḻutaru vaḷiyiṟ
	piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi yuṟaḻcci vārat
	takatteḻu vaḷiyici yariṟapa nāṭi
	yaḷaviṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittē.
iḵtu, ellā eḻuttiṟkum āvatōr puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum - ellā eḻuttukkaḷum, veḷippaṭa kiḷantu colliya paḷḷi - veḷippaṭa vitantu collappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ - eḻukiṉṟa vaḷiyāṉē, piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi - tām piṟakkun toḻiluṭiya vātaloṭu tammic collum iṭattu, uṟaḻcci vārattu akattu eḻuvaḷi ici - tiritaruṅ kūṟṟiyuṭiya uḷniṉṟu eḻum vaḷiyāṉāya iciyi, aril tapa nāṭi - piṇakkamaṟa ārāyntu, aḷapiṉ kōṭal - māttiri variyaṟiyāṟ kōṭal, antaṇar maṟittu - pārppār vētattuk kaṇṇatu.
‘uṟaḻcci vāram’ eṉṟatu, untimutalā eḻum vaḷi talikāṟum ceṉṟu mīṇṭum neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟutali eṉak koḷka. ‘vaḷi’ eṉṉātu ‘vaḷiyici’ eṉṟatu, avvāṟu neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟum aḷavē vaḷi eṉappaṭum; piṉṉi neñciṉiṉṟum eḻuvuḻiyellām vaḷittaṉmi tirintu eḻuttām taṉmiyatām eṉpatu viḷakki niṉṟatu.(ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	(20)
103.	aḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticikkum
	meyteri vaḷiyici yaḷapunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.
itu, mēṟcūttirattiṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu - (‘akatteḻu vaḷiyici aḷapiṟ kōṭalākiya’) ataṉi in nūliṭattuc collātu, eḻuntu puṟattu icikkum - (akattiṉiṉṟu) eḻuntu puṟattup pōntu icikkum, mey teri vaḷi ici aḷapu - poruṇmi terikiṉṟa vaḷiyāṉāya iciyatu māttiriyiṉi, nuvaṉṟiciṉ - yāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉēṉ.
maṟṟu, iḵtu “aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittu” (piṟappiyal 20) eṉavē peṟappaṭṭataṉṟōveṉiṉ, ‘antaṇar maṟittu’ eṉpatu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟutaṟkum, piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟṟu nērntu uṭampaṭutaṟkum oppak kiṭantamiyiṉ av viyam tīrtaṟkuk kūṟiṉāreṉpatu. puṟattu icippataṉ muṉṉar, akattu icikkum vaḷiyiciyi am maṟikkaṇ ōr eḻuttiṟku mūṉṟu nili uḷatākak kūṟum (ataṉ āciriyaṉ); aḵtu āmāṟu aṟintu koḷka. ‘meyteri’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṟku mutaliyaṉa muyaṟciyāṟ piṟakkumeṉiṉum, poruḷ teriyā nilimiya vākaliṉ avaṟṟiṟku aḷapu kūṟārāyiṉāreṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. ‘nuvaṉṟiciṉ’ eṉpatu īṇṭu iṟantakālat taṉmi viṉi.	  (21)
mūṉṟāvatu - piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.


puṇariyal
moḻikaḷ tammiṟ puṇartaṟkuriya karuviyiṉ iyalpiṉik kūṟutaliṉ itu puṇariyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum vitikaḷ piṉvarum iyalkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum ceykipaṟṟiya vitikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭutaliṉ karuvikaḷeṉap paṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiluḷḷa muppattu mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ irupattiraṇṭeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalāmeṉavum, irupattu nāṉkeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku īṟāmeṉavum, ellā moḻikaḷukkum iṟutiyum mutalumāvaṉa mey, uyir eṉṉum ivviruvakiyeḻuttukkaḷeṉavum, moḻiyiṟutiyil niṉṟa meyyellām puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟkumeṉavum, moḻiyiṟutiyi luḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyiṉ taṉmiyiyuṭiyatā meṉavum, moḻiyīṟāy niṉṟa uyirmey uyirīṟṟiṉ taṉmiyiyuṭiyatā meṉavum ivviyalil 1-mutal 4-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nilimoḻiyi niṟuttacol eṉṟum varumoḻiyik kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyeṉṟum tolkāppiyaṉār vaḻaṅkuvar. niṟutta colliṉ īṟṟeḻuttōṭu kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyiṉ mutalaleḻuttup porunta avvirumoḻikaḷum iyintu varutalē puṇarcciyeṉappaṭum. appuṇarcci uyirīṟṟuc col muṉ uyir varumiṭam, uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ meyvarumiṭam, meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyir varumiṭam, meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ mey varumiṭam eṉa eḻuttuvakiyāl nāṉku vakippaṭum; peyaroṭu peyar, peyaroṭu toḻil, toḻiloṭu peyar, toḻiloṭu toḻil eṉac col vakiyāl nāṉkākum. moḻikaḷ puṇaruṅkāl iṭiniṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu maṟṟeṉṟākat tiritali meypiṟitātal eṉṟum, avviṭattup putiyaveḻuttut toṉṟutali mikutal eṉṟum, aṅku muṉṉirunta eḻuttuk keṭutalik kuṉṟal eṉṟum, ivvēṟu pāṭetuvumiṉṟi avvirumoḻikaḷum muṉṉuḷḷavāṟu puṇarntu niṟṟali iyalpeṉṟum, innāṉkiṉiyum moḻipuṇariyalpu eṉṟum āciriyar kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. niṟuttacollum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyum taṉittaṉiyē aṭimoḻi peṟṟuvariṉum nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇartaṟku uriyaṉavām. muṉ piṉṉāka māṟi niṉṟa marūu moḻikaḷum nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇarkkappaṭutaluṇṭu.
moḻippuṇarcci vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyeṉavum alvaḻip puṇarcciyeṉavum poruḷ vakiyāl irutiṟappaṭum ivaṟṟuḷ vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyi vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyeṉṟum alvaḻip puṇarcciyi vēṟṟumiyalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyeṉṟu āciriyar vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. ivviruvakip puṇarmoḻikaḷum eḻuttu mikutalum cāriyi mikutalum ākiya irutiṟattālum naṭappaṉa. vēṟṟumi yurupāvaṉa ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ eṉṉum āṟām eṉat tolkāppiyaṉarkku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻiya ṉūlār vakippaṭuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ. vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ puṇarum nilimikkaṇ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattē niṟṟaṟkuriyaṉa. urupēṟkum peyarkaḷ uyartiṇip peyar, aḵṟiṇip peyar eṉa iruvakippaṭum. avi vēṟṟumiyurupōṭu poruntuṅkāl peyarkkum vēṟṟumiyurupiṟkumiṭiyē cāriyi mikum. cāriyikaḷāvaṉa iṉ, vaṟṟu, attu, am, oṉ, āṉ, akku, ikku, aṉ eṉavarum ivvoṉpatum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum ām. iṉ cāriyi ikaraṅkeṭṭu ṉakaramey maṭṭum niṟṟalum, ṉakaraṅ keṭṭu ikaravuyir maṭṭum niṟṟalum, ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu niṟṟalum ākiya mūṉṟu tiripuṭiyateṉṟum, vaṟṟuc cāriyi mutaṟkaṇuḷḷa vakaramey keṭa aṟṟu eṉattiriyumeṉṟum, iṉ, oṉ, āṉ, aṉ eṉṉum iccāriyikaḷiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramāyt tiritaluṇṭeṉṟum, akaravīṟṟuc colmuṉ varum attuccāriyi akaraṅkeṭa ‘ttu’ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, ikara aikāra vīṟṟuc colmuṉvarum ikkuc cāriyi ikaraṅkeṭa ‘kku’ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl akkuc cāriyi iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaramum ataṉulūrappaṭṭa kakarameyyum akkakara meyyiṉmēl niṉṟa kakarameyyum orucērakkeṭa ‘a’ eṉat tirintu niṟkumeṉṟum, kacata varu moḻiyāy varuṅkālattu am cāriyiyiṉ makaram muṟiyē ṅa ña na eṉat tiritalum, melleḻuttum iṭiyeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattuk keṭutalumākiya nilimit teṉṟum, coṟkaḷ peyarun toḻilumāyp pirintum peyarum peyarumāykkūṭiyum icippa vēṟṟumiyurupu virintu niṟku miṭattum maṟintu niṟkumiṭattum moḻikaḷip pirittuk kāṇuṅkāl peyarkkum vēṟṟumiyurupiṟku miṭiyē vantu niṟṟal cāriyiyiṉ iyalpeṉṟum cāriyiyiṉ ilakkaṇat tiṉi āciriyar teḷivāka variyaṟuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷar.
coṟkaḷi nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap pirittuk kāṇum muṟiyiṉit ‘coṟcitar maruṅku’ eṉavum peyar cāriyi urupu mutaliya colluṟuppukkaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉṉoṉṟāka oṭṭi naṭattaṟkuriya moḻivaḻakkiṉi ‘oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakku’ eṉavum āciriyar kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumākap pirittaṟkēṟṟavāṟu oṭṭi nillātu, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉṟāṟ pōla uṭaṅkiyintu niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakkiṉa allaveṉavuṅ ākavē avi cāriyi peṟā veṉavum iḷampūraṇar kūṟiya viḷakkam ivaṇ cintittuṇarat takuvatām.
kāram, karam, kāṉ eṉpaṉa eḻuttiṉ cāriyikaḷām. avaṟṟuḷ karam, kāṉ eṉṉum iru cāriyikaḷiyum neṭṭeḻuttup peṟutalilli. kuṟṟeḻuttu mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu cāriyikaḷiyum peṟum. neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷil ai, oḷa eṉṉumiraṇṭum kāṉ cāriyiyum peṟum. eṉa ivviyalil iyipuṭimi karuti eḻuttuk cāriyikaḷum uṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉ moḻi varuṅkāl varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa uyir taṉittu nillātu; meykaḷukkuriya puḷḷi peṟutalākiya avviyalpiṉik keṭuttu nilimoḻi yīṟṟiluḷḷa ammeyyuṭaṉ kūṭi niṟkum. iṅṅaṉam kūṭiya uyir pirintu nīṅkiyavaḻi nilimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa mey mīṇṭum taṉ paḻiya vaṭivākiya puḷḷiyip peṟum. ivviyalpiṉi ivviyaliṉ 36, 37-ām cūttiraṅkaḷāl  āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvar. kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōlun taṉmiyattu eṉa ivviyaliṉ mūṉṟām cūttirattāl āciriyar māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār. immāṭṭēṟu puḷḷi peṟutalum uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttalumākiya meyyiṉ taṉmikaḷuḷ puḷḷi peṟutali vilakki uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttalākiya avvaḷavukkuc cellutaliṉ “immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭiccēṟa leṉavuṇarka” eṉa uriyāciriyar viḷakkaṅ kūṟuvar. ‘kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa’ eṉa muṉṉar māṭṭēṟu kūṟiṉamiyāl meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyir taṉittu nillātavāṟu pōṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum uyir taṉittu nillātu akkuṟṟiyalukarattōṭu porunti niṟkum eṉat tol kāppiyaṉār uyttuṇara vittami peṟappaṭum.
uyirīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu uyirōṭu uyirkkuk kalantu niṟkum iyalpiṉmiyāl iru moḻikaḷum oṭṭi nillātu viṭṭicippaṉavām. nilimoḻiyīṟṟilum varu moḻi mutalilum niṟkum avviraṇṭuyirkaḷum uṭampaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟiṭiyē yakara vakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu uṭampaṭu meyyāy varum. uṭampaṭāta iraṇṭuyirkaḷum uṭampaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu iṭiyē tōṉṟum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉa vaḻaṅ kappaṭum. uṭaṉpāṭu, uṭampāṭu eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkiyatu varumuyirkku uṭampāka aṭukkum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉapporuḷ kōṭalumuṇṭu. ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum uyir varumvaḻi avaṟṟiṭiyē uṭampaṭumey peṟutali vilakkār eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟutalāl avar kālattu uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vantu puṇaruṅkāl uṭampaṭumey peṟṟē varavēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟiyilli yeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. uṭampaṭumeyyāka varutaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷ iviyeṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟātu pōyiṉum avariyaṟṟiya iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlākiya it tolkāppiyattilum itaṟkuppiṉ tōṉṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷellāvaṟṟilum yakara vakaraṅkaḷē uṭampaṭumeyyāka āḷappeṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām. “uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāram, uyir mutaṉ moḻi varūuṅkāliyāṉa” eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāṭṭiya paḻañcūttiram yakara vakaraṅkaḷē uṭampaṭumeyyāy varutaṟkuriyaṉa eṉpatit teḷivāka viḷakkutal kāṇka. uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra vīṟukaḷ yakara vuṭampaṭumey koḷḷum eṉavum, ēkāravīṟu yakara vakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟi uṭampaṭumeyyākap peṟṟuvarum eṉavum, ēṉiya vuyirīṟukaḷ yāvum vakara vuṭampaṭu meyyē peṟum eṉavum iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum vakipeṟa viḷakkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
eḻuttāl oṉṟupōlat tōṉṟic collāl vēṟu paṭṭup poruḷ viḷaṅki niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ ōci vēṟu pāṭṭaṟ pirintu puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal colnaṭiyiṉ nili peṟṟa paṇpākum. ivvāṟu varum puṇarmoḻikaḷ kuṟippiṉāluṇarum poruḷiyuṭiyaṉa. eḻuttu vakiyāl ittaṉmiya eṉat teḷivāka vuṇarum muṟimiyi yuṭiyaṉa alla. eṭuttukkāṭṭākac ‘cempoṉ patiṉṟoṭi’ eṉṉum puṇar moḻi poṉṉippaṟṟip pēcciṉkaṇ eṭuttāḷap paṭṭāl cempoṉ + patiṉṟoṭi eṉap pirintum, cempik kuṟittu nikaḻum pēccil eṭuttāḷappaṭṭāl cempu + oṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉap pirintum ōci vēṟupaṭṭu vēṟuvēṟu poruḷuṇartti niṟṟal kāṇalām. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉi ivviyaliṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷālum āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār. eṉavē avar kālattu iṅṅaṉam nuṇṇitākap poruḷuṇarttum puṇarmoḻikaḷ peruka vaḻaṅkiṉami naṉku pulaṉākum.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 170-174


nāṉkāvatu
puṇariyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, moḻikaḷ, (mēṟ ceyki yōttukkaḷuḷ) puṇartaṟkuriya karuviyiṉ iyalpu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ puṇariyal eṉappaṭṭatu. mēl moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttiṉa miyiṉ moḻimarapiṉōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
104.	mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ
	iraṇṭutali yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵ
	taṟunāṉ kīṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalum
	ellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalum
	meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala.
it talic cūttiram eṉnutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṉuḷ mutalum īṟum kūṟiyavaḻi uyirum meyyum uyir meyyum eṉa mūṉṟāy virintu niṉṟataṉi uyirum meyyum eṉa iraṇṭākat tokuttalāṉum, avvaḻi irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu moḻikku mutal, irupattu nāṉku eḻuttu moḻikku īṟu eṉak kūṟaliṉ, muppattu mūṉṟu eṉappaṭṭa eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu āvaṉapōl virintataṉi aviyellām muppattu mūṉṟaṉuḷḷaṉavē eṉat tokuttalāṉum virintatu tokuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟu eḻuttiṉ - mūṉṟaṉi muṭivilē yiṭappaṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttiṉuḷ, iraṇṭu taliyiṭṭa mutal āku irupaḵtu - av viraṇṭaṉi muṭivilē yiṭappaṭṭa moḻikku mutalāya irupatum, aṟunāṉku īṟoṭu neṟi niṉṟu iyalum - irupattu nāṉku īṟṟōṭu vaḻakku neṟikkaṇ niṉṟu naṭakkum, ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum - mūvaki moḻikkum īṟum mutalumāvaṉa, mey uyir eṉṟu a īr iyala - meyyum uyirumākiya av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya.
u-m: maram, ili, āl, viḷa eṉa meyyum uyirum mutalum īṟum āyiṉa.
irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu mutalāvaṉa  paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum oṉpatu uyirmeyyum moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉa ivi. irupattu nāṉku eḻuttu īṟāvaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉa ivi. ‘īṟṟoṭu’ eṉpatu vikārattāṉ tokkatu. mey mutaṟ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, nālvakip puṇarcciyum meykkaṇa nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻāveṉpatu koḷka. (`eṉṟu’ eṉpatu eṇṇiṭi c col.) 	 (1)
105.	avaṟṟuḷ
	meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ taṉimeyyum mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟataṉi vilakkaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēl meyyum uyirum eṉṟu kūṟiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ, īṟu meyyellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal - moḻikku īṟāya meyyellām puḷḷi peṟutaloṭu niṟkam; mutalāyavi yellām puḷḷiyiḻantu niṟkum.
u-m:  maram eṉa varum.
‘moḻimutaṉ mey puḷḷiyoṭu nillātu’ eṉṉātu, ‘īṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal’ eṉa īṟṟiṉmēl vittuk kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, av vīṟṟiṉ mey uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu aḵtu ēṟa iṭamkoṭukkumeṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. (2)
106.	kuṟṟiya lukaramu maṟṟeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku ōr karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟu eṉa moḻipa - īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaramum (puḷḷi yīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum) at taṉmittu eṉṟu colluvar.
immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭic cēṟal eṉa vuṇarka.	(3)
107.	uyirmey yīṟu muyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
itu, mēl “meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala” (puṇariyal - 1) eṉṟataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirmey īṟum uyir īṟṟu iyaṟṟu - uyirmey moḻiyīṟṟil niṉṟatuvum uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpiyuṭittu, iṭiyil niṉṟatuvum uyiriṉ iyalpi yuṭittu e-ṟu.
īṟum iṭiyum uyiruḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavē, mutal meyyuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉpatāyiṟṟu.
itaṉāṉ, viḷa mutaliya uyirmey īṟellām akaravīṟu mutaliya uyirīṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkip puṇarccipeṟuvaṉa vāyiṉa. varaku eṉpuḻi iṭi niṉṟa rakara uyirmey akaramāy uyirt toṭarmoḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
īṇṭu uyirmey oṟṟumi nayattāṉ uyirmeyyeṉa vēṟu ōr eḻuttāvataṉṟi, īṟum iṭiyum uyireṉa ōreḻut tāyum, mutal meyyeṉa ōreḻuttāyum niṉṟatāyiṟṟu.
ittuṇiyum orumoḻi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟaliṉ moḻi marapiṉ oḻipāyiṟṟu.	(4)
108.	uyiriṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyum
	uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉ
	ṟivveṉa vaṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kāli
	niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉ
	ṟāyī riyala puṇarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarcci mummoḻip puṇarcciyākātu, irumoḻip puṇarcciyāmeṉpatūum, avi eḻuttu vakiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir iṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyirmutalmoḻi varum iṭamum, uyir iṟu colmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum - uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ meymmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, mey iṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyirmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, mey iṟu colmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum - meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ meymmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, iv eṉa aṟiya - (eṉṟu ap puṇarccivaki) iviyeṉa aṟiya, kiḷakkum kāli - āciriyar colluṅkālattu, niṟutta col kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi eṉṟu a īr iyala - avi niṟutta collum ataṉ poruṇmiyik kuṟittuvarum collu mākiya av viraṇṭu iyalpiyuṭiya, puṇarnilic cuṭṭu - puṇarum nilimikkaṇ.
u-m: ā ī, ā valitu, āl ili, āl vīḻntatu eṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.
viḷaviṉik kuṟittāṉ eṉpatu av vurupu kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyi nilimoḻiyuḷ aṭakki irumoḻip puṇarcciyāy niṉṟavāṟu aṟika. (avvurupu - `viḷaviṉi’ eṉpataṉ ai urupu. mutal ēkāram pirinili. iraṇṭām ēkāram - īṟṟaci. `eṉṟu’ iraṇṭum - eṇṇiṭiccol.)	(5)
109.	avaṟṟuḷ
	niṟutta colli ṉīṟā keḻuttoṭu
	kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyiyap
	peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	peyaroṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālun
	toḻiloṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālun
	toḻiloṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	mūṉṟē tiripiṭa ṉoṉṟē yiyalpeṉa
	āṅkan nāṉkē moḻipuṇa riyalpē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarcci puṇarccivakiyāṉ nāṉkā meṉpatūum, avi colvakiyāṉ nāṉkā meṉpatūum, puṇarvatu collum collumēyaṉṟi, eḻuttum eḻuttum eṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - nilimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉappaṭṭa vaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟu āku eḻuttoṭu kuṟittu varu kiḷavi mutal eḻuttu iyiya - niṟutta colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttiṉōṭu ataṉik kuṟittu varukiṉṟa colliṉatu mutaleḻuttup porunta, peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarkkum kālum - peyarccolloṭu peyarc collip puṇarkkum kālattum, peyaroṭu toḻili puṇarkkum kālum - peyarccolloṭu viṉiccollip puṇarkkum kālattum, toḻiloṭu peyarip puṇarkkum kālum - viṉic colloṭu peyarccollip puṇarkkum kālattum, toḻiloṭu toḻilip puṇarkkum kālum - viṉiccolloṭu viṉiccollip puṇarkkum kālattum, tiripu mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉa a nāṉkē moḻipuṇar iyalpu - tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟum iyalpu oṉṟum ākiya an nāṉkē moḻikaḷ tammiṟ puṇarum iyalpu e-ṟu.
u-m: cāttaṉ ki, cāttaṉ uṇṭāṉ, vantāṉ cāttaṉ, vantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
iṭiyum uriyum peyar viṉikaḷi aṭintallatu tāmāka nillāmiyiṉ, peyar viṉikaṭkē puṇarcci kūṟappaṭṭatu.	
(iṭi - iṭiccol. uri -uriccol. `āṅku’ eṉpatu aci. mutal mūṉṟu ēkāramum tēṟṟapporuḷil vantaṉa. nāṉkām ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	(6)
110.	avitām
	meypiṟi tātaṉ mikutal kuṉṟaleṉ
	ṟivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya tiripu mūṉṟum āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - at tiriputāṉ, meypiṟitu ātal mikutal kuṉṟal eṉṟu iv eṉa moḻipa tiriyum āṟu - mey piṟitātalum mikutalum kuṉṟalumākiya ivi eṉac colluvar tiriyum neṟiyiṉi. im mūṉṟum allātatu iyalpeṉṟu koḷka.
u-m: poṟkuṭam, yāṉikkōṭu, maravēr eṉavarum. 
kuvaḷi malar eṉpatu iyalpu.
ip puṇarcci nāṉkum oru puṇarccikkaṇṇē nikaḻvu peṟumeṉpatu uriyiṟkoḷka. mēl ‘iṭam’ (puṇar. 6) eṉṟataṉāṉ, tiripu mūṉṟaṉuḷ, yātāyiṉum oṉṟāyatu, oru puṇarccikku iraṇṭum mūṉṟum oruṅku varappeṟum eṉakkoḷka.
‘makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ’ eṉpatu am mikuti iraṇṭu vantatu; ‘nīyir kuṟiyir’ eṉpatu am mikuti mūṉṟu vantatu. piṟavum aṉṉa. (mutal utāraṇattil `attiripu mūṉṟu vantatu’ eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. iraṇṭām utāraṇam piḻi. ippiḻikaḷ ēṭupeyartteḻutiṉōrāl nērntaṉa pōlum. ‘eṉṟu’  eṇṇiṭiccol.)	(7)
111.	niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum
	aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilik kuriya.
itu, “niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi” (puṇar. 5) eṉpataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum - nili moḻiyāka niṟuttiṉa collum ataṉik kuṟittu varucollum, aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilikku uriya- (tāmē vantu puṇarvataṉṟi), avi yiraṇṭiṉum ōrōr collaṭivantu oṉṟit tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilikku uriya.
u-m: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu, āyirattorupaḵtu, patiṉāyirat torupaḵtu eṉa varum.
īṇṭu aṭiyeṉṟatu ummittokiyiṉiyum, iru peyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyiṉiyum eṉa vuṇarka. aviyallāta tokikaḷuḷ viṉittokiyum, paṇput tokiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmiyiṉ, orucol eṉappaṭum. aṉmoḻit tokiyum taṉakku vēṟu oru muṭipu iṉmiyiṉ orucol eṉappaṭum. iṉi oḻinta vēṟṟumit tokiyum uvamit tokiyum ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭu orucol eṉappaṭum.
uṇṭacāttaṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭuvantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum avvāṟē orucol eṉappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.  (8)
112.	maruviṉ ṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyum
	uriyavi yuḷavē puṇarnilic cuṭṭē.
iḵtu, ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi marūukkaḷum puṇarkkappaṭum eṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅku iyal moḻiyum - (ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi) marūuttiraḷākiya talitaṭumāṟāka mayaṅkiṉa iyalpiyuṭiya ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiṉa marūu vaḻakkum, uriyavi uḷa puṇarnilic cuṭṭu - uriyaṉa uḷa puṇarum nilimikkaṇ.
u-m: mīkaṇ, muṉṟil eṉa varum.
‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūu vaḻakkallā marūu vaḻakkum puṇarkkappaṭum eṉakkoḷka.	(9)
113.	vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyum
	eḻuttē cāriyi yāyiru paṇpiṉ
	oḻukkal valiya puṇaruṅ kāli.
itu, nālvakip puṇarcciyuḷ mikka puṇarcci ittaṉmit teṉpatūum, an nālvakip puṇarcciyum vēṟu ōr āṟṟāṉ iruvaki yāmeṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyum - vēṟ ṟumiyatu poruṇmiyiṉik kuṟitta puṇarmoḻiyatu nilimiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi puṇarmoḻi niliyum - vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattup puṇarmoḻiyatu nilimiyum, eḻuttu cāriyi a iru paṇpiṉ oḻukkal valiya - (mikka puṇarccikkaṇ) eḻuttu mikutalum cāriyi mikutalu mākiya av viraṇṭu ilakkaṇattāṉum naṭāttutalit tamakku valiyāka uṭiya, puṇarumkāli - avi puṇaruṅkālattu.
u-m: viḷaṅkōṭu, makaviṉki, viḷakkuṟitu, paṉiyiṉ kuṟi eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
‘oḻukkal valiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, eḻuttum cāriyiyum uṭaṉ peṟutal koḷka.
avaṟṟukkōṭu, kalattukkuṟi eṉavarum.
alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu, vēṟṟumiyallātatu alvaḻiyeṉa vēṇṭaliṉ eṉpatu. muṉṉē ‘puṇarmoḻi’ eṉṟu vittu, ‘puṇaruṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarccikkaṇṇē vēṟṟumi alvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭāvatu allākkāl, vēṟṟumiyeṉavē paṭumeṉpatu koḷka. īṇṭu ‘alvaḻi’ yeṉṟatu perumpāṉmiyum eḻuvāyiṉi.	(10)
114.	aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ ṇeṉṉum
	avvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumi yurupē.
itu, mēl vēṟṟumi yeṉṉappaṭṭa avaṟṟatu peyarum, muṟiyum, tokiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum - ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum. a āṟu eṉpa vēṟṟumi urupu - av vāṟum eṉṟu colluvar vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷi e-ṟu.
iv āṟum allaṉa vellām alvaḻi eṉappaṭum. avi eḻuvāyum, viḷiyum, uvamattokiyum, ummit tokiyum, paṇput tokiyum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyum, muṟṟum, iru vaki eccamum, iṭiyum, uriyum eṉa ivi.	(11)
115.	valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ
	kolvaḻi yoṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu, nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyali nōkkiṟṟōr karuvi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi urupiṟku - valleḻuttu mutalākavuṭiya vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭi mikutal vēṇṭum - poruntiṉa iṭattu valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum iṭikkaṇ mikutal vēṇṭum.
u-m: ūrkku, nīrkku, ūrkkaṇ, nīrkkaṇ eṉa valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. taṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉa melleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
‘olvaḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aracarkaṇ, pārppārkaṇ eṉa oṟṟu mikātaṉavum koḷka. ‘meypiṟi tātal’ (puṇar. 7) mutalāya nāṉku puṇarcciyum urupu puṇarccikkaṇṇum eytaliṉ, meypiṟitātali eṭuttōtātu mikkataṉi eṭuttōtiya vataṉāṉ, mikka puṇarcci yallaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka. poṟku, poṟkaṇ, āṅkaṇ, īṅkaṇ, ūṅkaṇ, avaṟku, ivaṟku; nuṅkaṇ, koṟṟaṟku, cāttaṟku eṉa varum.
avaṉkaṇ, cāttaṉkaṇ eṉpuḻi iyalpum itaṉāṉē koḷka. kuṉṟiya puṇarcci vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka. 	(12)
116.	āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi
	īṟā kakaramuṉik keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, āṟāvataṟkut tokimarapiṉi nōkkiyatoru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ urupiṉ akarak kiḷavi - āṟām vēṟṟumiyākiya atu eṉṉum colliṭattu akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟu āku akaramuṉi keṭutal vēṇṭum - (neṭumutal kuṟukum) moḻiyīṟṟu uḷatākiṉṟa akarattiṉ muṉṉart tāṉ keṭutal vēṇṭum.
u-m: namatu, ematu, taṉatu, eṉatu, niṉatu eṉa varum.
mēl īṟāku akaram itaṟkut tārātu, ivvuru pakaramē ēṟi muṭiya amitaliṉ, atu kēṭātal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ, neṭumutal kuṟuki vikārappaṭṭu niṉṟa moḻiyākaliṉ ataṉmēl urupakara mēṟi muṭiyal vēṇṭārāyiṉār pōlu meṉpatu. 	(13)


117.	vēṟṟumi vaḻiya peyarpuṇar niliyē.
itu, vēṟṟumiyurupu peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi peyar vaḻiya puṇarnili - vēṟṟumi peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉa avaṟṟoṭu puṇarum nilimik kaṇ.
u-m: cāttaṉi, cāttaṉoṭu eṉa varum.
mēl “urupunili tiriyā tīṟupeyark kākum” (vēṟ. 8) eṉṟāraṉṟō veṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyar mutaliya nālvakip puṇarcciyiyum vēṟṟumi alvaḻiyeṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkaliṉ, viṉivaḻiyum urupu varumeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟa tākaliṉ īṇṭu itu kūṟappaṭṭatu. (vēṟṟumi - vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ. ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	 (14)
118.	uyartiṇip peyarē yaḵṟiṇip peyareṉ
	ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu, vēṟṟumiyoṭu puṇarum peyarkaṭkup peyarum, muṟiyum, tokiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇip peyarē aḵṟiṇip peyar eṉṟu a iraṇṭu eṉpa - uyartiṇip peyarum aḵṟiṇip peyarumākiya av viraṇṭumeṉṟu collupa, peyar nilic cuṭṭu - peyarākiya nilimiyatu karuttiṉi.
u-m: āṭūu, makaṭūu eṉpaṉa uyartiṇip peyar. oṉṟu pala eṉpaṉa aḵṟiṇip peyar.
maṟṟu viravuppeyar kūṟārāyatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ,  maṟṟatu cāttaṉ vantāṉ; vantatu eṉap puṇarccikkaṇ perumpāṉmi yum oru tiṇip pāṟpaṭutaliṉ, av viraṇṭāka aṭakkik kūṟiṉā reṉpatu.
(ē, eṉṟu eṉpaṉa - eṇṇiṭiccol iraṇṭām. ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	(15)

119.	avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ cāriyi varumē.
itu, cāriyi varum iṭam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ cāriyi varum - ap peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya iṭattiṉkaṇṇē cāriyi varum.
u-m: āṭūuviṉ ki, makaṭūuviṉ ki, palavaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉa varum. (`avaṟṟuḷ vaḻi’ eṉavum pāṭam.)	(16)
120.	avitām
	iṉṉē vaṟṟē yattē yammē
	oṉṉē yāṉē yakkē yikkē
	aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yuḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa veṉpa cāriyi moḻiyē.
itu, cāriyikaṭkup peyarum muṟiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - mēṟcāriyi yeṉṉappaṭṭavi tām, iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉ eṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭa piṟavum - iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉ eṉṉum collumākiya av voṉpatum uḷappaṭap piṟavum, aṉṉa eṉpa cāriyi moḻi - ac cāriyiyām taṉmiya eṉṟu colluvar cāriyiyākiya moḻikaḷi.
‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tam, nam, num, um, keḻu, ē, ai, ñāṉṟu eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka. ‘eṭutta naṟaviṉ kuliyalaṅ kāntaḷ’ eṉpuḻi alam eṉpatoru cāriyiyum uṇṭāleṉiṉ, ataṉi alaṅku kāntaḷ eṉpataṉ vikāra meṉpa. iṉcāriyi vaḻakkup payiṟciyāṉum nūluṭ palakālum eṭut tōtappaṭalāṉum, vāḷā ōtiyavaḻit tāṉē cēṟalāṉum muṉvikkappaṭṭatu. aṉ cāriyiyum atupōlac ciṟappuṭi miyiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. iṭi niṉṟavaṟṟiyalpum aṟintu koḷka. (mutal eṭṭu ēkāramum - eṇṇiṭiccol. oṉpatām ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	 (17)
121.	avaṟṟuḷ
	iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
	muṉṉark keṭutal urittu mākum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyi mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa cāriyikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram āviṉ iṟutimuṉṉar - iṉcāriyiyiṉatu ikaram ā eṉṉum collīṟṟumuṉṉar, keṭutalum urittākum - keṭāmiyēyaṉṟik keṭutalum urittām e-ṟu.
u-m: āṉi, āviṉi, āṉkōṭu, āviṉkōṭu eṉa varum.
‘muṉṉar’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘mā’ eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉum aviru vitiyum eytum. māṉi, māviṉi, māṉkōṭu, māviṉkōṭu eṉa varum.	 (18)
122.	aḷapāku moḻimuta ṉiliiya vuyirmici
	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉākiya nilittē.
iḵtu, iṉ iṟuti tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) aḷavākum moḻi mutal - aḷavup peyarākum moḻi mutaṟkaṇ, niliiya uyirmici ṉaḵkāṉ - nilipeṟṟa uyirukku mēlāy niṉṟa iṉcāriyiyatu ṉakāram, ṟaḵkāṉ ākiya nilittu - ṟakāramākiya nilimittu.
u-m: patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉpuḻi avvāṟu varutal aṟika.
‘nilittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. patiṟṟoṉṟu, patiṟṟēḻu eṉa varum.    (19)
123.	vaḵkāṉ meykeṭac cuṭṭumuta limmuṉ
	aḵkā ṉiṟṟa lākiya paṇpē.
itu, vaṟṟu mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal aimuṉ - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalāka vuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuc colmuṉṉar, vaḵkāṉ mey keṭa  aḵkāṉ niṟṟal ākiya paṇpu - vaṟṟuccāriyi taṉ vakaramākiya meykeṭa akaram niṟṟalākiya paṇpiṉiyuṭiya.
u-m: aviyaṟṟi, iviyaṟṟi, uviyaṟṟi eṉavum, aviyaṟṟukkōṭu eṉavum varum.
‘ākiya paṇpu’ eṉṟataṉāl, cuṭṭu mutalākiya ai eṉ iṟuti aikārattoṭu nillātavaḻi, vaṟṟiṉ vakaram akaram niṟkak keṭātu vaṟṟāyē niṟṟal koḷka. maṟṟitu, “tirintataṉ tiripu piṟitu” eṉṉum nayattāṟ keṭātē niṟkumākaliṉ, itu kūṟavēṇṭā eṉiṉ, “cuṭṭu mutal ai īṟṟuc colliṉ aimuṉ” eṉa ōtātu, “cuṭṭu mutal aimmuṉ” eṉa ac colmuṉ ellām keṭuvatupōla ōtiṉamiyiṉ, vēṇṭiṟṟeṉpatu.(20)
124.	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉāṉka ṉurupiṟku.
itu, ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyi nāṉkaṟkum īṟu tiripu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ urupiṟku ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ - nāṉkām urupiṟku (ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyiyellām) ṉakaram ṟakaramām.
u-m: viḷaviṟku, kōoṟku, orupāṟku, ataṟku eṉa varum. 		(21)
125.	āṉi ṉakaramu mataṉō raṟṟē
	nāṇmuṉ varūum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkē.
iḵtu, āṉcāriyi poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ īṟu tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷmuṉ varum val mutal toḻiṟku āṉiṉ ṉakaramum - nāṭpeyar muṉṉar varum valleḻutti mutalāka vuṭiya viṉic coṟkaṇ varum āṉcāriyiyiṉ ṉakaramum, ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - an nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉcāriyi yōṭu oru taṉmittāy ṉakāram ṟakaramām.
‘toḻiṟku’ eṉpataṉit ‘toḻiṟkaṇ’ eṉa mayakkamākak koḷka.
u-m: paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
ummiyi iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāṉē etiratu taḻīiya tākki, ataṉāṉ nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟka ṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakāramāyt tirital koḷka. paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tiriyumeṉa, peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tiritalum tiriyāmiyumuṭiya teṉpatu  ñāpakattāṟ koḷḷap paṭum. paṟampiṟ pāri, vaṇṭiṉ kāl eṉa varum.	 (22)
126.	atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli.
iḵtu, attu mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) attiṉ akaram akaramuṉi illi - attuc cāriyiyiṉ akaram akaravīṟṟuc colmuṉṉar illiyākum.
u-m: makattukki eṉa varum.	(23)
127.	ikki ṉikara mikaramuṉi yaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ikkuc cāriyi mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ikkiṉ ikaram ikaramuṉi aṟṟu - ikkuc cāriyiyiṉatu ikaram ikara īṟṟuccol muṉṉar mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu pōlak keṭum e-ṟu.
u-m: āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(24)
128.	aiyiṉ muṉṉaru mavviya ṉiliyum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aiyiṉ muṉṉarum a iyal niliyum - (ikkiṉ ikaram) ikaravīṟṟuc col muṉṉaṉṟi aikāravīṟṟuc col muṉṉarum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa keṭutaliyalpilē niṟkum.
u-m:	cittirikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.         (25)
129.	eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
	akki ṉiṟutimey miciyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	kuṟṟiya lukara muṟṟat tōṉṟātu.
iḵtu, akkīṟu tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) e peyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu - evvakippaṭṭa peyar muṉṉum valleḻuttu varumiṭattu akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiyat tōṉṟātu, mey miciyoṭum keṭum - ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa valleḻuttākiya mey taṉakku mēl niṉṟa meyyoṭum keṭum.
u-m:	kuṉṟakkūki, maṉṟappeṇṇi, īmakkuṭam, aracak kaṉṉi, tamiḻakkūttu eṉavarum.
(‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi maṟṟak kaṇaṅkaṭkum koḷka.) tamiḻanūl, tamiḻayāppu, tamiḻavariyar eṉa varum. (26)
130.	ammi ṉiṟuti kacatak kālit
	taṉmey tirintu ṅañana vākum.
itu, ammiṉ iṟuti tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ammiṉ iṟuti ka ca tak kāli - ammiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu ka ca takkaḷ varumoḻiyāka vanta kālattu, taṉ mey tirintu ṅa ña na ākum - taṉ vaṭivutirintu ṅa ña nakkaḷām.
u-m:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, puḷiyañcetiḷ, puḷiyantōl eṉa varum.
‘taṉmey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ammiṉ iṟuti makaramēyaṉṟit tam nam num um eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ iṟuti makaramum tiriyumeṉpatu koḷka.
ellārtaṅkiyum, ellānaṅkiyum, ellīr nuṅkiyum, ‘vāṉa varivillun tiṅkaḷum’, eṉa varum.	(27)
131.	meṉmiyu miṭimiyum varūuṅ kāli
	iṉmi vēṇṭu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) meṉmiyum iṭimiyum varum kāli - ammiṉ iṟuti meṉmiyum iṭimiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu; iṉmi vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - iṉṟimuṭitali vēṇṭum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	puḷiya ñeri; nuṉi, muri, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉa  varum.
uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, puḷiyavili eṉavum uyir varuvaḻik kēṭum koḷka. puḷiyili eṉa muḻuvatūum kēṭu kūṟalum koḷka.	(28)
132.	iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ
	kiṉṉeṉ cāriyi iṉmi vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, iṉ cāriyi muḻuvatūum keṭum iṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉ eṉa varum vēṟṟumi urupiṟku - iṉ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku, iṉ eṉ cāriyi iṉmi vēṇṭum - iṉ eṉṉum cāriyitāṉ iṉṟi muṭital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
u-m: viḷaviṉ, palāviṉ eṉa varum.
‘avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉiṉ ikaram’ (puṇar. 18) eṉṟataṉ piṉ viyātataṉāl, ciṟupāṉmi iṉ cāriyi keṭātu niṟṟal koḷka. pāmpiṉiṟ kaṭitu tēḷ eṉa varum.	(29)
133.	peyarun toḻilum pirintoruṅ kicippa
	vēṟṟumi yurupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyum
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum
	oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇic
	coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkā
	tiṭiniṉ ṟiyaluñ cāriyi yiyaṟki
	uṭimiyu miṉmiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum.
itu, cāriyikaṭkellām potuvāyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peyarum toḻilum pirintu oruṅku icippa - peyarccollum viṉiccollum peyarum viṉiyumāyp pirintum peyarum peyarumāyk kūṭiyum icippa, vēṟṟumi urupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyum - vēṟṟumiyurupu tokātu nilipeṟṟa iṭattiṉum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum - avi tōṟṟutal vēṇṭāta tokutikkaṇṇum, oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi - tām tām poruntutaṟkēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkoṭu porunti, col citar maruṅkiṉ - cāriyi peṟum puṇarmoḻikaḷip pirittuk kāṇumiṭattu, vaḻi vantu viḷaṅkātu iṭiniṉṟu iyalum - avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vantu viḷaṅkātu avaṟṟiṭiyē niṉṟu naṭakkum, cāriyi iyaṟki - cāriyiyiṉ iyalpu. uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum - avitām uṇṭātalum illiyātalum oṭu urupiṉiṭattu okkum.
u-m:	  viḷaviṉik kuṟittāṉ, viḷaviṉik kuṟittavaṉ eṉavum, nilāttukkoṇṭāṉ, nilāttuk koṇṭavaṉ eṉavum varum.
oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkaṉmiyiṉ, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉpaṉa cāriyi peṟāvāyiṉa.
ellā nammiyum eṉac cāriyi īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ, iṭiniṉṟiyaṟal perumpāṉmi yeṉak koḷka.
pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntaleṉavum, pūvoṭu virinta kūntaleṉavum, uṇmiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ ottavāṟu.
‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ oṭuvurupiṉkaṇ cāriyi peṟutalum peṟāmiyum oḻiya, orōvaḻip peṟṟē varumeṉpatu koḷka. palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	          (30)
134.	attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl
	oṟṟumey keṭuta ṟeṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
	avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, attum vaṟṟum varumiṭattu nilimoḻiyiṉum varumoḻiyiṉum varuñ ceyki kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) attē vaṟṟē a irumoḻimēl oṟṟu mey keṭutal teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟu - attum vaṟṟumākiya av virumoḻi mēlniṉṟa oṟṟut taṉvaṭivu keṭutal teḷiyappaṭṭatu, avaṟṟu muṉvarum valleḻuttu mikum - av virucāriyi muṉṉum varum valleḻuttu mikum. (muntiya ēkāram iraṇṭum eṇṇiṭiccol. pintiya ēkāram iraṇṭum īṟṟaci.)
u-m: kalattukkuṟi, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum.
kalaṉ eṉa, ṉakāramāka niṟuttik keṭukkavē. ‘oṉṟiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, puḷḷiyīṟalvaḻi vikāra vakiyāṉ niṉṟaṉavum avaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭukkappaṭumeṉak koḷka.
avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum. iḵtu aikāra īṟu.
īṇṭu ‘valleḻuttu miku’meṉṟatu, īṟṟu valleḻuttiṉṟit tirintu muṭiyum ṉakaramum ṇakaramum lakaramum ḷakaramum eṉa ivaṟṟi nōkkiyeṉa vuṇarka.
attu muṟkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, attiṉ mici yoṟṟuk keṭātu niṟkavum peṟum. viṇṇattuk koṭkum, veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāl, attiṉ akaram piṟavuyiriṉ muṉṉum keṭutal koḷka. aṇṇāttēri eṉavarum.
‘teṟṟu’ eṉṟataṉāl, attiṉ akaram keṭātu niṟṟalum koḷka. viḷavattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	  (31)
135.	kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
	nērat tōṉṟu meḻuttiṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu, eḻuttuc cāriyikaṭkup peyarum muṟiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu civaṇi - kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu porunti, nērattōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyi - ellā āciriyarāṉum uṭaṉpaṭat tōṉṟum eḻuttuccāriyi yātaṟku.
‘nērat tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nērattōṉṟātaṉa āṉam, ōṉam, eṉa ivi.	 (32)
136.	avaṟṟuḷ
	karamuṅ kāṉu neṭṭeḻut tilavē.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ cila cāriyi cila eḻuttōṭu vārā eṉa eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttu ila - mēṟ collappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṟku ila.
kāram neṭṭeḻuttiṟku uṇṭu eṉṟavāṟu. ākāram, īkāram eṉa varum.	(33)
137.	varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tuṭiya.
itu, neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuc cila cāriyi vilakkiṉamiyiṉ kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum cila vilakkuvatuṇṭukol eṉṉum aiyam tīrttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) varalmuṟi mūṉṟum kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭiya - varalāṟṟu muṟimiyiyuṭiya mūṉṟu cāriyiyum kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭiya.
u-m: akāram, akaram, aḵkāṉ eṉa varum.
‘varaṉmuṟi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi āytamikku muṭiyumeṉpatu koḷka.	   (34)
138.	aikāra oḷakāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuḷ kāramuṅ kāṉum eṉpataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aikāram oḷakāram kāṉoṭum tōṉṟum - neṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷil aikāramum oḷakāramum muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭum tōṉṟum.
u-m: aikāṉ, oḷakāṉ eṉa varum.	(35)
139.	puḷḷi yīṟṟumu ṉuyirtaṉit tiyalātu
	meyyoṭuñ civaṇu mavviyal keṭuttē.
iḵtu, uyirmutalmoḻi puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ varuṅkāṟ piṟappatōr karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi īṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭum civaṇum a iyal keṭuttu - ap puḷḷiyoṭum kūṭum tāṉ taṉiniṉṟa av viyalpiṉik keṭuttu e-ṟu.
u-m: āl + aṭi = ālaṭi eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, iyalpallāta puḷḷimuṉ uyir vantālum iv viti koḷka. ataṉi eṉavum, nāṭuri eṉavum varum.
puḷḷiyīṟṟu muṉṉum eṉa, tokuttu niṉṟa ummiyi virittataṉāṉē kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉum iv viti koḷka.	nākaritu eṉa varum.	(36)
140.	meyyuyir nīṅkiṟ ṟaṉṉuru vākum.
itu, puṇarcciyiṭattu uyirmey uyir  nīṅkiyavaḻip paṭuvatoru viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mey uyir nīṅkiṉ taṉ uru ākum - mey taṉṉoṭu kūṭiya uyir puṇarcciyiṭattu nīṅkiya vaḻit taṉ puḷḷi vaṭivu peṟum.
u-m: ataṉi - ataṉ + ai eṉa varum. 	(37)
141.	ellā moḻikku muyirvaru vaḻiyē
	uṭampaṭu meyyi ṉurupukoḷal variyār.
iḵtu, uyirīṟu uyirmutaṉ moḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻva toru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā moḻikkum - mūvakippaṭṭa moḻikkum, uyir varu vaḻi - uyir mutaṉmoḻi varum iṭattu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvu koḷal variyār - iṭi uṭampaṭumey vaṭivu kōṭali nīkkār.
u-m: puḷiyaṅkōṭu, eruvaṅkuḻi, viḷavattukkoṭkum eṉa varum.
