‘uriyiṟ kōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, uṭampaṭumeyyāvaṉa yakaramum vakaramum eṉak koḷka. ikaravīṟum īkāravīṟum aikāravīṟum yakaravuṭam paṭumey koḷvaṉa; allaṉa vellām vakaramey koḷvaṉa. ‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, vikārappaṭṭa moḻik kaṇṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka. maravaṭi, āyirutiṇi eṉa varum.
‘variyār’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṭampaṭumey kōṭal orutali aṉṟeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. kiḷi aritu, mūṅkā illi eṉa varum.	(38)
142.	eḻuttō raṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci
	iciyiṟ ṟirita ṉiliiya paṇpē.
iḵtu, eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavātal kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eḻuttu ōr aṉṉa poruḷ teri puṇarcci - eḻuttu oru taṉmiyāṉa poruḷ viḷaṅkiniṟkum puṇar moḻikaḷ, iciyiṉ tirital niliiya paṇpu - ōci vēṟṟumiyāṉ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal nilipeṟṟa paṇpu.
u-m:	cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi, kuṉṟēṟāmā eṉa varum. 		          (39)
143.	avitām
	muṉṉap poruḷa puṇarcci vāyiṉ
	iṉṉa veṉṉum meḻuttukkaṭa ṉilavē.
itu, mēlataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - mēṟcolliya puṇarmoḻikaḷ tām, muṉṉap poruḷa - muṉṉattiṉāṉ uṇarum poruṇ miyi yuṭiya; puṇarcci vāyiṉ iṉṉa eṉṉum eḻuttuk kaṭaṉ ila - avi puṇarcci yiṭattu ittaṉmiya veṉṉum eḻuttu muṟimiyi uṭiyavalla e-ṟu.
cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉṟavaḻip poṉṉārāycci uḷ vaḻip poṉṉeṉavum, cempārāycci uḷvaḻic cempeṉavum kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu. maṟṟu, itaṉmēl ‘iciyi ṟirital nilital’ (eḻut. 142) eṉa aṟiyumāṟu kūṟiṉāraṉ ṟōveṉiṉ, ōci eṉṟamiyāṉ aḵtu oliyeḻuttiṟ keṉavum, “iṉṉa veṉṉu meḻuttuk kaṭaṉila” eṉṟataṉāṉ itu varivaṭiviṟkeṉavuṅ koḷka.	(40)
nāṉkāvatu - puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.


toki marapu
uyiriyum meyyiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya coṟkaḷip piṉvarum iyalkaḷil īṟukaḷtōṟum taṉittaṉiyē virittōti muṭikkak karutiya āciriyar, pala vīṟukaḷukkum potuvāṉa vitikaḷi ivviyalil ovvōr cūttiraṅkaḷāl tokuttu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ivviyal tokimarapu eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. nilimoḻi varumoḻip puṇarccikaṭpaṭum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāyt toṉṟutoṭṭu varum ilakkaṇa marapukaḷit tokuttuṇarttutaliṉ tokimara peṉap paṭṭatu eṉiṉumamiyum. nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum mēl puṇariyaliṟ kūṟiya karuvikaḷāl tokkuppuṇarum ceyki kūṟuvatu ivviyalātaliṉ itu puṇariyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
ivviyal muppatu cūttiraṅkaḷiyuṭiyatu. itaṉkaṇ alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya iruvaḻiyilum kacatapa eṉṉum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl mikutaṟkuriya melleḻuttukkaḷ muṟiyē ṅa ña na ma eṉpaṉavām eṉṉum varumoḻik karuviyum, irupattunāṉkīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷukku iruvaḻiyum varumoḻimuṭipum, ṇakara ṉakara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷukku iruvaḻiyum nilimoḻi muṭipum, lakara, ṉakara vīṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉum ṇakara ḷakara vīṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉum varum takara nakaraṅkaḷ muṟiyē ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷākavum ṭakara ṇakaraṅkaḷākavum tiriyu meṉṉum varumoḻik karuviyum, uyiriyum meyyiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya muṉṉili viṉic coṟkaḷ valliṉa mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl iyalpum uṟaḻcciyu mākiya irunilimiyiyuṭiyaṉa veṉpatum, oḷakārattiyum ñanamava eṉṉum meykaḷiyum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyum īṟākavuṭiya muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷ muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟum poruntuvaṉa alla eṉpatum, uyiriyum meyyiyum īṟākavuṭiya uyar tiṇip peyarkaḷ nāṉkukaṇattum iruvaḻiyum iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ ikaravīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyar tirintu muṭiyumiṭaṉumuṇ ṭeṉpatum, aḵṟiṇi viravu peyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavumuḷa veṉpatum, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyilum iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyilum uḷavākun tiripukaḷum, ikara aikāravīṟṟup peyarkaḷukku alvaḻi muṭipum, ikara aikāravīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumiyiṭap poruḷuṇara niṉṟa iṭiccol muṭipum, neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa meyyīṟu keṭutal kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa iṟutimey iraṭṭittal ākiya nilimoḻikkaru viyum, ivaṟṟiṟku urupiyali nōkkiyatōr, varumoḻik karuviyum, ukaramoṭu puṇarum meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy variṉ avvukaram peṟātu iyalpām eṉṉum varumoḻic ceykiyum aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum tammiṟ puṇarumāṟum, aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum mutalātaṟkuriya veḻuttukkaḷ ka ca ta pa na ma va a u eṉṉumiv voṉpatumē yeṉṉam variyaṟiyum, mēlē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vaṟṟiṟkup puṟaṉaṭiyum, yāvar eṉpatu yār eṉavum yātu eṉpatu yāvatu eṉavum varum marūu muṭipum ivviyalil uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 174-176


aintāvatu
tokimarapu
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, mēl akattōttiṉuḷ irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṉum virintu muṭivaṉavaṟṟiyellām tokuttu muṭittaliṉ tokimarapu eṉappaṭṭatu. mēl puṇariyaluṭ kūṟiya karuvikaḷāṟ ceyki kūṟum vaḻi, tokkup puṇaruñ ceyki kūṟaliṟ puṇariyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
144.	kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟ ṟōṉṟum
	melleḻut tiyaṟki colliya muṟiyāṉ
	ṅañanama veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
it talic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya iruvaḻikkaṇṇum, uyirmayaṅkiyaliyum  puḷḷimayaṅkiyaliyum nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ca ta pa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttu iyaṟki - uyirīṟṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum iru vaḻiyum ka ca ta pakkaḷi mutalākavuṭiya moḻikaḷiṉmēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttiṉatu iyalpukūṟiṉ, colliya muṟiyāṉ ṅa ña na ma veṉṉum oṟṟākum - melleḻuttu mēṟcollum muṟimiyāṉ ka ca ta pakkaḷukku niraṉiṟi vakiyāṉē ṅa ña na ma veṉṉum oṟṟākum, aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayiṉ āṉa - at taṉmittākiya muṟimiyiṉi yuṭiya moḻikaḷiṭattu.
u-m: viḷaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāṉ tōṉṟi niṉṟaṉavum avvāṟē tirintu melleḻuttā meṉpatu. maraṅkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum. aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyaṉmiyiṉ, viḷak kuṟumi eṉpuḻi melleḻuttu mikātāyiṟṟu.
(ēkāram - īṟṟaci. `āṉa’ eṉpataṉ akaram aci.)	(1)
145.	ña na ma ya va veṉu mutalāku moḻiyum
	uyirmuta lākiya moḻiyu muḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi yaṉittu mellā vaḻiyum
	niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyalpā kummē.
iḵtu, irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṉ muṉṉum, vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇaṅkaṭku iruvaḻiyum varumoḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma ya va eṉum mutal āku moḻiyum - ña na ma ya va eṉṟu collappaṭum mutaleḻuttu uḷavākum moḻiyum, uyir mutalākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa - uyir mutalākiya moḻiyumāka, aṉṟi aṉittum ellā vaḻiyum - av vaṉittu moḻiyum alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya ellā viṭattum, niṉṟa colmuṉ iyalpākum - irupattu nāṉku īṟṟup peyarākiya nilimoḻi muṉṉar iyalpāki muṭiyum.
īṇṭu, ‘uḷappaṭa’ veṉpatu, āka veṉṉum poruṇmittu.
u-m: viḷa, tāḻ eṉa niṟutti - ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu, aṭintatu, āṭiṟṟu, iṭintatu, īriṟṟu, uṭintatu, ūṟiṟṟu, eḻuntatu, ēṟiṟṟu, aitu, oṭintatu, ōṭiṟṟu, oḷaviyattatu, nuntiyatu eṉavum; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, yāppu, valimi, aṭivu, āṭṭam, iṭipu,  īṭṭam, uṭipu, ūṟṟam, eḻu, ēṇi, aiyam, oṭukkam, ōkkam, oḷaviyam, nunti eṉavum oṭṭikkoḷka.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟiraṭṭalum, uṭampaṭumey kōṭalum, uyirēṟi muṭitalum eṉavarum ikkaruvittiripu mūṉṟum tiripu aṉmiyiṉ tiripeṉap paṭāveṉpatu koḷka. iḵtu irupattu nāṉku īṟṟiṟkum, alvaḻiyiṉum, vēṟṟumi yiṉum, akattōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttirattāṉ muṭivataṉi, īṇṭu oru cūttirattāṉ tokuttu muṭitta tāyiṟṟu. mēlum ivvāṟē tokuttu muṭikkiṉṟa vāṟu aṟika.		(2)

146.	avaṟṟuḷ 
	melleḻut tiyaṟki yuṟaḻiṉum variyār
	colliya toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti yāṉa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku, am muṭipu vilakkip piṟitu viti eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kaṇattiṉum, melleḻuttu iyaṟki uṟaḻiṉum variyār - melleḻuttiṉatu iyalpu iyalpātalēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār, colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟutiyāṉa - collappaṭṭa toṭar moḻi īṟṟukkaṇ e-ṟu.
u-m: katirñeri, katirññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, ōreḻuttorumoḻi yuḷḷum, īreḻuttorumoḻiyuḷḷum cilavaṟṟiṟku uṟaḻcci muṭipu koḷka.
u-m:	pūñeri, pūññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉavum; kāyñeri, kāyññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉavum varum.
‘colliya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ōreḻuttorumoḻi yuḷḷum īreḻuttorumoḻiyuḷḷum cilavaṟṟiṟku mikku muṭital koḷka. kiññerittār; nīṭṭiṉār, maṭittār eṉavum, meyñ ñāṉam, meynnūl, meymmaṟantār eṉavum varum.	(3)
147.	ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
	viṉiyō raṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, yakara ñakaraṅkaḷ mutalāmvaḻi nikaḻvatoru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇa ṉa eṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇa ṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉmuṉ, yāvum ñāvum viṉi ōr aṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - yāvum ñāvum viṉiccoṟkaṇ mutalātaṟku oru taṉmiya veṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: maṇyātta eṉavum, poṉyātta eṉavum, maṇñāttu eṉavum,  poṉñāttu eṉavum varum.
ñā muṟkūṟātu yā muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, yā mutaṉ moḻikkaṇ ñā varumeṉṟu koḷka.	(4)
148.	moḻimuta lāku mellā veḻuttum
	varuvaḻi niṉṟa vāyiru puḷḷiyum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻit tiripiṭa ṉilavē.
itu, ṇakāravīṟṟiṟkum ṉakāravīṟṟiṟkum alvaḻikkaṇ nili moḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻimutal ākum ellā eḻuttum varuvaḻi - moḻikku mutalāmeṉṟu collappaṭṭa irupattiraṇ ṭeḻuttum varumiṭattu, niṉṟa a iru puḷḷiyum vēṟṟumi alvaḻi tiripu iṭaṉ ila - peyarc  colliṉiṉṟa ṇa ṉakkaḷākiya avviru puḷḷiyum vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻi yiṭattut tiriyum iṭam ila.
u-m:  maṇ, poṉ eṉa niṟutti, kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa vaṉkaṇattōṭu oṭṭuka. piṟa kaṇattukkaṇṇum avvāṟē oṭṭuka.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, ṇakārattiṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi tiripum uṇṭeṉak koḷka. cāṭkōl eṉa varum. (ēkāram īṟṟaci).	(5)
149.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum valleḻut talvaḻi
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki yāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, av viraṇṭu īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumikkaṇ nilimoḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum, valleḻuttu alvaḻi - valleḻuttu allāta viṭattu, mēlkūṟu iyaṟki avayiṉ āṉa - mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām av viraṇṭu puḷḷiyiṭattum.
u-m: maṇ, poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṟci, nīṭci eṉa vaṉkaṇam oḻittu ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.	(6)
150.	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	tana veṉavariṟ ṟaṉavā kummē.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) la ṉa eṉa varum puḷḷi muṉṉar - la ṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ta na eṉa variṉ - ta na eṉa varumoḻi variṉ, ṟa ṉa ākum - at takara nakaraṅkaḷ niraṉiṟiyāṉē ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷām.
u-m:	kaḵṟītu, kaṉṉaṉṟu, poṉṟītu, poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum.	(7)
151.	ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇa ḷa eṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇa ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar (atikārattiṉāṉ takāra nakāraṅkaḷ variṉ), ṭa ṇa eṉat tōṉṟum - avi niraṉiṟiyāṉ ṭakāra ṇakaraṅkaḷāyt tōṉṟum.
u-m: 	maṇṭītu, maṇṇaṉṟu, muḵṭītu, muṇṇaṉṟu eṉa varum.	(8)
152.	uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum
	iyalpā kunavum muṟaḻpā kunavumeṉ
	ṟāyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.
itu, muṉṉili viṉiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum puḷḷi iṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum - uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilic coṟkaḷum puḷḷi iṟutiyiyuṭiya muṉṉilic coṟkaḷum, valleḻuttuvariṉ - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi variṉ, iyalpu ākunavum uṟaḻpu ākunavum eṉṟu a īr iyala - iyal pāvaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāvaṉavum eṉa av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉi yuṭiya.
uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu eṉṟamiyāṉ, muṉṉili viṉic col eṉpatu koḷka.
u-m:	eṟikoṟṟā, koṇākoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum; uṇkoṟṟā, tiṉkoṟṟā eṉavum varum. ivi iyalpu. naṭakoṟṟā, naṭakkoṟṟā eṉavum, īrkoṟṟā, īrkkoṟṟā eṉavum varum. ivi uṟaḻcci.	(9)
153.	oḷaveṉa varūu muyiriṟu collum
	ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiṅ
	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟutiyu muḷappaṭa
	muṟṟat tōṉṟā muṉṉili moḻikkē.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku am muṭipu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oḷa eṉa varum uyir iṟu collum - oḷa eṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟuc collum, ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi iṟutiyum - ña na ma va eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum, kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutiyum uḷappaṭa - kuṟṟiyalukara mākiya iṟutiyiyuṭiya collumākiya ivi, muṉṉili moḻikku muṟṟat tōṉṟā - muṉṉili moḻikkuk kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻvumākiya muṭiviṟku muṟṟattōṉṟā.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, īṇṭu vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu oḻittu, oḻintaṉavellām nilimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu, varumoḻi valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭitalum, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
u-m:  kuvukoṟṟā, kuvukkoṟṟā, uriñukoṟṟā, uriñuk koṟṟā, porunukoṟṟā, porunukkoṟṟā, tirumukoṟṟā, tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā, tevvuk koṟṟā eṉavum; kūṭṭukoṟṟā, kūṭṭuk koṟṟā eṉavum varum. 	(10)
154.	uyirī ṟākiya vuyartiṇip peyarum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti yuyartiṇip peyarum
	ellā vaḻiyu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, uyartiṇip peyar nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum, iru vaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir īṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarum - uyir īṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi iṟuti uyartiṇip peyarum - puḷḷi iṟutiyiyuṭiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum, ellā vaḻiyum iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - nāṉku kaṇattilum alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya ellāviṭattum iyalpām eṉṟu colluvar.
u-m:   nampi eṉavum avaṉ eṉavum niṟutti, alvaḻikkaṇ kuṟiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāṉ, nīṇṭāṉ, māṇṭāṉ eṉavum; yāvaṉ, valiyaṉ eṉavum; aṭintāṉ, oḷaviyattāṉ eṉavum; vēṟṟumikkaṇ ki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; yāppu, valimi eṉavum; aṭipu, oḷaviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. oruveṉ eṉa niṟuttu, kuṟiyeṉ, ciṟiyeṉ eṉavum; ki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum taṉmip peyarkkaṇṇum oṭṭuka.
‘uyirīṟu’, ‘puḷḷiyiṟuti’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇip poruḷ iyalpaṉṟi muṭivaṉa vellām koḷka. palcāṉṟār, kapilaparaṇar, iṟivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuva māṇikkar, pallaracar eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu muṭintaṉa.
kōlikakkaruvi, vaṇṇārappeṇṭir, ācīvakap paḷḷi eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
kumarakōṭṭam, kumarakkōṭṭam, pirama kōṭṭam, piramak kōṭṭam eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇi(viṉi)ccol iyalpāyum tirintum muṭivaṉa koḷka. uṇpa cāṉṟār, uṇṭār cāṉṟār, uṇpēṉ pārppēṉ eṉa ivi iyalpu. uṇṭēñ cāṉṟēm, uṇṭēnām eṉa ivi tirintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(11)
155.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	ikaravīṟ ṟuppeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭittē.
iḵtu, uyartiṇip peyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyartiṇip peyaruḷ, ikara īṟṟup peyar tiripu iṭaṉ uṭittu - ikaramākiya iṟutiyi yuṭiya peyar tirintu muṭiyum iṭamum uṭittu.
uriyiṟkōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, itu mikka tiripeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.
u-m:	eṭṭippū, eṭṭippuravu; kāvitippū, kāvitippuravu; nampippū, nampippēṟu eṉa varum.
‘iṭaṉuṭittu’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ikara īṟaṉṟip piṟa īṟum īṟu tiriyātu valleḻuttu mikku varuvaṉa īṇṭukkoḷka. tiṉippū, tiṉippuravu eṉa varum.	 (12)
156.	aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē.
itu, viravuppeyar muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar iyalpum uḷa - uyar tiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravum viravuppeyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa; iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa. iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉa, mēl akattōttiṉuṭ kūṟappaṭum.
u-m:   	 cāttaṉkuṟiyaṉ, cāttaṉkuṟitu eṉavum; cāttaṉ
ki eṉavum; ivvāṟu alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum nāṉku kaṇattōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka.	 (13)
157.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟiyāṟ
	ṟammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ
	meymmi yākalu muṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	ammuṟi yiraṇṭu muriyavi yuḷavē
	vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.
itu, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit tiripu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi iṟutiyum uyir iṟu kiḷaviyum - puḷḷi yīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collum, valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟiyāṉ - valleḻuttiṉatu mikuti mēṟcollum muṟimiyāṉ, tammiṉ ākiya toḻiṟcol muṉvariṉ - am mūṉṟāvataṉ poruḷākiya viṉimutaṟ poruḷkaḷ tammāṉ uḷavākiya viṉic coṟkaḷ tām avaṟṟumuṉvariṉ, meymmiyākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum a muṟi iraṇṭum uriyavi uḷa - iyalpākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟutalumākiya am muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya iraṇṭu ceykiyum uriyaṉa uḷa, vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṉ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum - avaṟṟi mēlē vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu collumiṭattup pōṟṟiyaṟital vēṇṭum .
u-m: nāykōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārap paṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivi iyalpu. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭ paṭṭāṉ, vaḷikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci.
‘puḷḷiyīṟu uyirīṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēeyk kōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ ekarappēṟu koḷka. ‘uriyavi yuḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pāppukkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilimoḻi yoṟṟut tiriyāmiyum tiritalum koḷka.	(14)
158.	melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum
	uyirmika varuvaḻi yuyirkeṭa varutaluñ
	cāriyi yuḷavaḻic cāriyi keṭutaluñ
	cāriyi yuḷavaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyaluñ
	cāriyi yiyaṟki yuṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	uyartiṇi maruṅki ṉoḻiyātu varutalum
	aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyark kavviya ṉiliyalum
	meypiṟi tākiṭat tiyaṟki yākalum
	aṉṉa piṟavun taṉṉiyal maruṅkiṉ
	meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarin ticikkum
	aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumittiripu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum - melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu valleḻuttātaloṭu tōṉṟu talum, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum - valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu melleḻuttātaloṭu tōṉṟu talum, iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum - iyaṟki yiṭattu mikuti tōṉṟutalum, uyir mika varuvaḻi uyir keṭa varutalum - uyir mika varumiṭattu avvuyir keṭa varutalum, cāriyi uḷavaḻi cāriyi keṭutalum - cāriyiyuḷḷa iṭattuc cāriyi keṭutalum, cāriyi uḷavaḻi taṉ urupu niliyalum - cāriyi uḷḷaviṭattuc cāriyiyoṭu taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum, cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum - cāriyi peṟuka eṉṟavaḻic cāriyi peṟātu iyalpākiya moḻikaḷ varumoḻiyiṉum nilimoḻiyiṉum mikkum tirintum varum varumoḻi valleḻuttukkaḷ uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟutalum, uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum - uyartiṇip peyariṭattut taṉṉurupu tokātē virintu varutalum, aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyarkku a iyal niliyalum - uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyarkkum av vurupu av viyalpilē niṟṟalum, meypiṟitu āku iṭattu iyaṟki ākalum - mey piṟitāy muṭiyumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭitalum, aṉṉa piṟavum - attaṉmiyaṉa piṟa muṭipukaḷum, taṉ iyal maruṅkiṉ meypeṟa kiḷantu poruḷ varintu icikkum - taṉakku iyalpākiya kūṟṟāṉ akattōttiṉuṭ poruḷ peṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyatu potu muṭiviṉit tāṉ varintu vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripu eṉa moḻipa - aikāra vēṟṟumi yiṉatu vēṟupāṭṭup puṇarcci eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	viḷakkuṟittāṉ eṉpatu melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi  valleḻuttumikkatu. maraṅkuṟittāṉ eṉpatu valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikkatu. tāyk koli eṉpatu iyalpāmiṭattu mikkatu. palāk kuṟittāṉ eṉpatu uyirmika varuvaḻi uyir keṭṭatu. vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyi uḷavaḻic  cāriyi keṭṭatu. vaṇṭiṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyi uḷavaḻit (421) taṉṉurupu niliyiṟṟu. puḷi kuṟittāṉ, puḷikkuṟittāṉ, pūlkuṟittāṉ, pūṟkuṟittāṉ eṉa ivi  cāriyi yiyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟiṉa. nampiyik koṇarntāṉ  eṉpatu uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu vantatu. koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu viravuppeyarkku avviyal niliyatu. maṇkoṇarntāṉ eṉpatu meypiṟitākiṭattu  iyaṟkiyāy vantatu.
‘aṉṉa piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷvaṉa: kaḻikuṟittāṉ, tiṉipiḷantāṉ eṉpaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
‘oḻiyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ orōvaḻi oḻintum varum. ‘avaṟkaṇṭu’  eṉavum, ‘oṉṉārt teṟalum’ eṉavum ivi uyartiṇiyuḷ oḻintu vantaṉa.  makaṟpeṟṟāṉ, makaṭpeṟṟāṉ eṉa ivi viravup peyaruḷ oḻintu vantaṉa. iv vilēcutaṉṉāṉē, ivaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumiyum koḷka.
‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka. mikoṇarntāṉ, mikkoṇarntāṉ eṉavum; vilkōḷ, viṟkōḷ eṉavum varum. ivvāṟu tirintu muṭiyātu, akattōttiṉ potumuṭipē muṭipāyk kaṭukkuṟittāṉ, ceppuk koṇarntāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa muṭivaṉa aṟintu koḷka. ivvāṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭivatu perumpāṉmiyum iraṇṭāvatu viṉiyoṭu muṭiyumvaḻip pōlum. atu “tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ coṉ muṉvariṉ” (tokimarapu 14) eṉa niṉṟa atikārattāṟ koḷḷa vittār pōlum. ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumi viṉi yoṭu muṭiyumvaḻi iyalpum koḷka. ‘varipāy varuṭi’ (malipaṭu. 503), ‘pulampuk kaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟ kāḷi’ eṉa varum.	 (15)
159.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi i ai yeṉṉum
	īṟṟuppeyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliiya 
	avitām
	iyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum
	uṟaḻā kunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkkum aikāravīṟṟup peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi alvaḻi i ai eṉṉum īṟṟup peyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliya - vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattu i ai eṉṉum īṟṟiyuṭiya peyarccoṟkaḷ mūvaki muṭipu nilimiyuṭiya, avitām iyalpākunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻākunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar - avitām iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum val leḻuttu mikuvaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum iviyeṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	parutti kuṟitu, kari kuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa ivi iyalpu. alikkoṟṟaṉ, pulikkoṟṟaṉ eṉa ivi mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu, kiḷikkuṟitu, tiṉikuṟitu, tiṉikkuṟitu eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci.
‘peyarkkiḷavi mūvaki niliya’ eṉavē, peyarkkiḷavi yallāta kiḷavi mikutiyum iyalpum eṉa iruvakiya eṉak koḷka. ollik koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāravīṟṟu viṉic col mikuti. ikaravīṟṟu mikuti vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.
“tillic collē” (col. 253) “maṉṉic collē” (col. 252) eṉṟatu aikāravīṟ ṟiṭiccol mikuti. ivaṟṟiyalpu vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.
kaṭikā eṉpatu ikaravīṟṟu uriccolliyalpu. iv vīṟṟumikuti vanta vaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. paṇittōḷ eṉpatu aikāravīṟṟu uriccol mikuti. ivaṟṟiyalpu vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(16)
160.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya vikara viṟutiyum
	ekaramutal viṉāvi ṉikara viṟutiyuñ
	cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya viye ṉiṟutiyum
	yāveṉ viṉāviṉiye ṉiṟutiyum
	valleḻuttu mikunavu muṟaḻā kunavuñ
	colliya maruṅki ṉuḷaveṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vikara aikāra vīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭap poruḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ikaravīṟṟu iṭiccollum, ekara mutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum - ekaramākiya moḻimutal viṉāviṉuṭiya ikaravīṟṟu iṭiccollum, cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya aieṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya uṟuppeḻuttu nīṇṭa aikāra vīṟṟu iṭiccollum, yā eṉ viṉāviṉ ai eṉ iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāviṉi mutaṟkaṇṇuṭiya aikāra vīṟṟu iṭiccollum, valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻākunavum colliya maruṅkiṉ uḷa eṉa moḻipa - valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē uḷaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, itōḷikkoṇṭāṉ,  utōḷik koṇṭāṉ, etōḷikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; āṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ, īṇṭik koṇṭāṉ, ūṇṭik koṇṭāṉ, yāṇṭik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivi mikku muṭintaṉa.
avvaḻikoṇṭāṉ, avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ; ivvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, ivvaḻik koṇṭāṉ; uvvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, uvvaḻik koṇṭāṉ; evvaḻikoṇṭāṉ, evvaḻik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkavikoṇṭāṉ, āṅkavik koṇṭāṉ; īṅkivi koṇṭāṉ, īṅkivikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum; ūṅkuvikoṇṭāṉ, ūṅkuvik koṇṭāṉ yāṅkavi koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkavik koṇṭāṉ  eṉavum ivi uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ aikāravīṟṟuḷ uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa tiripuṭiyaṉa. tiripillaṉa peṟṟavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
‘colliya maruṅku’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa aikāravīṟu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka. paṇṭic cāṉṟār, orutiṅkaḷik kuḻavi eṉa varum.	 (17)
161.	neṭiyataṉ muṉṉa roṟṟumey keṭutaluṅ
	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru piraṭṭalum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu mey keṭutalum - neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉmuṉ niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭutalum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar taṉ uruvu iraṭṭalum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu iraṭṭutalum, aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟi iyal eṉpa - ivi aṟiyumpaṭi tōṉṟiya muṟimiyāṉa iyal puṭiyaṉaveṉṟu colluvar.
u-m:	kōṟītu, kōṉaṉṟu eṉa ivi neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭṭaṉa. 
	maṇṇakal, poṉṉakal eṉa ivi kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉurupu iraṭṭiṉa.
mēliccūttirattu  āṟaṉurupu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, oṟṟu iraṭṭutal uyirmutaṉmoḻik kaṇṇateṉṟu koḷka. kuṟiyatu piṉkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟa vaḻiyum kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, kuṟiyatu tirintu neṭiyatāyavaḻi ataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāyk keṭutalum koḷka.
tammi, nammi eṉa ivi neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟu oṟṟu iraṭṭiṉa. maṟṟiyatu vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvatu takāra nakāraṅkaḷ vantu tirintavaḻi yeṉpatūum, āṇṭu ellām keṭāteṉpatūum koḷka. tēṉṟītu eṉpatu āṇṭuk keṭātatu.
‘neṟiyiyal’ eṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟiṉṟip puṇarcciyāṟ peṟṟatum iraṭṭumeṉa vuṇarka. avvaṭi eṉavarum.	(18)
162.	āṟa ṉurupiṉu nāṉka ṉurupiṉuṅ
	kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lilli
	īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu niliyum
	neṭumutal kuṟuku moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, urupiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉum īṟu āku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum - āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉka ṉurupiṉkaṇṇum īṟāku puḷḷikaḷ akarattoṭu nilipeṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻi muṉ kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭal illi - neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttuk kuṟuki muṭiyum moḻik kaṇ mēṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭal (āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum) illi.
u-m: tamatu, tamakku; namatu, namakku eṉavarum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉē neṭumutal kuṟukāta moḻiyum kuṟuku moḻiyum iv viru vitiyum koḷka. ellār tamatum, ellār tamakkum eṉa varum.	
(neṭumutal kuṟukātamoḻikaḷ tam, nam, num eṉṉuñ cāriyi yiṭiccoṟkaḷ. āṉ eṉpatu iṭic col. 
a eṉpatu - aci.)	(19)
163.	numme ṉiṟutiyu mannili tiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ iṟutiyum a nili tiriyātu - num eṉṉum makaraviṟuti mēṟkūṟiya īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyalum kuṟṟoṟṟiraṭṭāmiyumākiya annilimiyil tiriyātu e-ṟu.
u-m: numakku, numatu eṉa varum.	 (20)


164.	ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
	yakaramu muyirum varuvaḻi yiyaṟki.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatoru nilimoḻic ceyki kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi iṟuti - ukarap pēṟṟoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyiṟutikaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki - yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu av vukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: uriñ yāṉā, uriñ aṉantā; porun yāṉā, porun aṉantā; uriñ ātā, porun ātā eṉa oṭṭuka. (21)
165.	uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki
	aḷavu niṟiyu meṇṇuñ cuṭṭi
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa vellāc collun
	tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakap paṭṭa
	mutti varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟiṉ
	otta teṉpa vēyeṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu, aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇup peyarum tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇum cuṭṭi uḷa eṉappaṭṭa ellāc collum - uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāy aḷaviyum niṟiyiyum eṇṇiyum karuti uḷaveṉṟu collappaṭṭa ellāc coṟkaḷum, tam tam kiḷavi tam akappaṭṭa muttivarum kālam tōṉṟiṉ ē eṉ cāriyi ottatu eṉpa - tamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāyt tamakku akappaṭum moḻiyāyuḷḷaṉa tam muṉṉē varum kālam tōṉṟumāyiṉ ē eṉṉuñ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭitalip poruntiṟṟu eṉpa.
u-m:	uḻakkēyāḻākku, kalaṉēpatakku eṉa ivi aḷavup peyar; toṭiyēkaḵcu, koḷḷēaiyavi eṉa ivi niṟip peyar; kāṇiyēmuntiriki, kālēkāṇi eṉa ivi eṇṇup peyar.
‘uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, ē eṉ cāriyi peṟātaṉavum uḷaveṉṟu koḷka. kuṟuṇi nāṉāḻi eṉa varum.		  (22)
166.	ariyeṉa varūum pālvari kiḷavikkup
	puriva taṉṟāṟ cāriyi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ari eṉa varum pālvari kiḷavikkup purivatu aṉṟu cāriyi iyaṟki - ari eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa poruṭkūṟṟi yuṇara niṉṟa colliṟkup poruntuvataṉṟu mēṟkūṟiya ē eṉ cāriyi peṟum iyalpu e-ṟu.
u-m: uḻakkari, toṭiyari, oṉṟari eṉa varum.
iḵtu ‘oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku’ (puṇar. 30) aṉṟu eṉpataṉāṉ vilakkuṇṇātōveṉil, ‘tammakappaṭṭa’ eṉṟu varumoḻiyiyum varintōtiṉamiyiṉ itaṟku av viti cellā teṉpatu. (āl eṉpatu aci)	  (23)
167.	kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kāli
	niṟiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟi eṉ kiḷavi muṉ varu kāli - kuṟi eṉṉum col aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmuṉ varuṅkālattu, niṟiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumi iyaṟki - niṟiyat tōṉṟum mēl (236) vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipiṟkuc collum iyalpu.
u-m: urikkuṟi, kalakkuṟi; toṭikkuṟi, koṭkuṟi; kāṇikkuṟi, kāṟkuṟi eṉa varum.
‘muṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, poruṭpeyaroṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum vēṟṟumi muṭipu eytukaveṉpatu. kalappayaṟu eṉa varum.
‘niṟiya’ eṉpataṉāṉ, kūṟu eṉpataṉkaṇṇum vēṟṟumi muṭipu eytumeṉpatu. nāḻikkūṟu eṉa varum.	 (24)
168.	kuṟṟiya lukarak kiṉṉē cāriyi.
itu, vēṟṟumi muṭipu vilakki iṉ vakuttamiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku - kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu aḷavup peyar mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku, cāriyi iṉ - kuṟiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi varum cāriyi iṉ.
u-m: uḻakkiṉkuṟi, kaḻañciṉkuṟi, oṉṟiṉkuṟi eṉa varum.	
(`kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku’  eṉpatu `kuṟṟiyalukarakku’ eṉa vikārappaṭṭu niṉṟatu. aḵtu ākupeyar, avvīṟṟu aḷavup peyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku āyiṉamiyiṉ. ēkāram - aci.) 	(25)
169.	attiṭi varūuṅ kalameṉ ṉaḷavē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) kalam eṉ aḷavu iṭi attu varum - kalam eṉṉum aḷavup peyar (kuṟiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi) iṭiyil attu varum.
u-m: kalattukkuṟi eṉa varum.
itu, ‘kalaṉ’ eṉṉum ṉakaravīṟēl, nilimoḻi oṟṟukkēṭum, varumoḻi oṟṟuppēṟum ‘attē vaṟṟē’ (puṇariyal - 31) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. ‘kalaṉeṉ aḷavu’ eṉa ōtātatu ceyyuḷiṉpam nōkkip pōlum. cāriyi muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyi peṟṟavaḻi muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāṉ (167) eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	 (26)
170.	paṉiye ṉaḷavuṅ kāve ṉiṟiyum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli yiṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉi eṉ aḷavum kā eṉ niṟiyum - paṉi eṉṉum aḷavup peyarum kā eṉṉum niṟip peyarum, niṉiyum kāli iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - (kuṟi eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu) ārāyuṅ kālattu iṉcāriyiyoṭu poruntum.
u-m: paṉiyiṉ kuṟi, kāviṉkuṟi eṉa varum.
‘niṉiyuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, valleḻuttup pēṟum ciṟu pāṉmi koḷka. (eḻut. 167) paṉikkuṟi, kākkuṟi eṉa varum.	(27)
171.	aḷaviṟku niṟiyiṟku moḻimuta lāki
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa  voṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
	avitām
	kacatapa veṉṟā namava veṉṟā
	akara ukaramō ṭaviyeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum mutalām eḻuttukku variyaṟi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻimutalāki uḷa eṉappaṭṭa oṉpatiṟṟu eḻuttu - aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟip peyarkkum moḻi mutalāy uḷaveṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa oṉpateḻuttu, avitām- avi (yāviyōveṉiṉ), ka ca ta pa eṉṟā na ma va eṉṟā akaramoṭu ukaramoṭu avi eṉa moḻipa - ka ca ta pa kkaḷum na ma va kkaḷum akaramum ukaramumākiya avi eṉṟu colluvar.
u-m:	kalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, cīrakam, toṭi, palam, niṟi, mā, vari, anti eṉavum varum.
‘uḷaveṉap paṭṭa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uḷaveṉap paṭātaṉavum aḷavi uḷaveṉpatu. immi, ōraṭi, ōrāṭi eṉa varum.
maṟṟu iv variyaṟi kūṟip payantatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, mēl akattōttiṉuḷ (390) avaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟum vaḻi, atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēṟ cellātu, oḻinta kaṇattiṉum cēṟaṟku eṉpatu. (`akaramoṭu’ eṉpatu oṭu keṭṭu niṉṟatu. `eṉṟā’ eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum, `oṭu’ eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum - eṇṇiṭiccol. ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	 (28)
172.	īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉiviyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
	kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellām
	meyttalip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
	ottavi yuriya puṇarmoḻi niliyē.
iḵtu, iv vōttu, puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) īṟuiyal maruṅkiṉ - uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yāṉavi varumoḻiyoṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu, ivi ivaṟṟu iyalpu eṉa kūṟiya kiḷavi pal āṟu ellām - im moḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu iviyeṉak kūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉ (avvāṟṟāṉ muṭiyātu niṉṟa) palavaki muṭipukaḷellām, mey talippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi ottavi uriya puṇar moḻinili - uṇmiyit talippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu kūṭip poruntiṉavi uriyavām puṇarum moḻikaḷ nilimikkaṇ.
u-m: viḷa ñāṉṟatu eṉpuḻi nilimoḻip peyaratu iyalpum, ‘ñāṉṟa ñāyiṟu’  eṉa nili moḻi viṉiyāya vaḻi irumoḻi iyalpumākiya ‘ña na ma ya va’ (toki marapu 2) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, maṇ kaṭitu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattu iyalpākiya ‘moḻimutalākum’ (toki marapu 5) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, naṭañeḷḷā eṉṉum iyalpukaṇattu irumoḻi iyalpum, nilkoṟṟā, niṟkoṟṟā eṉa nilimoḻi tirinta uṟaḻcciyum, tukkoṟṟā, tuññeḷḷā eṉṉum mikutiyum, uriñuñeḷḷā eṉṉum iyalpukaṇattu ukarap pēṟum, uriñyāṉā, uriñ aṉantā eṉṉum irumoḻi iyalpum, maṇṇukoṟṟā, maṇṇuk koṟṟā, ñeḷḷā eṉavarum ‘oḷaveṉa varūum’ (toki. 10) eṉpataṉuḷ vilakkappaṭāta ṇakara ṉakara lakara ḷakara mākiya puḷḷiyiṟutikaḷiṉ nilimoḻi ukarap pēṟumākiya “uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷavi” (toki. 9) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, viravuppeyart tiripiṉ mēl eṭuttu ōtappaṭātaṉavāya niṉñāṇ eṉṟāṟpōla varum. ‘aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar’ (toki. 13) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, kāvikkaṇ, kuvaḷikkaṇ eṉṟāṟpōla alvaḻi muṭipākiya ‘vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi’ (toki. 16) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, kuṟuṇi patakku nāṉāḻi, cīrakari, orumāvari eṉṉum “uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki” (toki. 22) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, piṟavaṟṟiṉ oḻipumellām īṇṭē koḷka.	(29)
173.	palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum
	peyariṭi vakaraṅ keṭutalu mēṉi
	oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭi
	oṉṟiya vakaram varutalu miraṇṭum
	maruviṉ pāttiyiṟ ṟiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.
itu, marūu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palar aṟicolmuṉ yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭi vakaram keṭutalum - palari aṟiyum colmuṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭiyil vakaram keṭutalum, ēṉi oṉṟu aṟi colmuṉ yātu eṉ viṉā iṭi oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum - oḻinta oṉṟaṉi aṟiyum colmuṉṉar varum yātu eṉṉum viṉāmoḻiyiṭi uyiroṭu poruntiya vakaram varutalum, iraṇṭum maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ payiṉṟu tiriyum - iraṇṭum marūukkaḷatu muṭipiṉiṭattup payiṉṟu vaḻaṅkum.
u-m: avar yār eṉavum, atu yāvatu eṉavum varum.
‘oṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vakara uyirmey eṉṟu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, yāreṉpatum yāvateṉpatum nili moḻiyāyp piṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. ‘yāryārk kaṇṭē yuvappar’ eṉavum, “yāvatu  naṉṟeṉa vuṇarār māṭṭum” (kuṟun. 78) eṉavum varum.
‘payiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāl, palaraṟi collum oṉṟaṟi collum varumoḻiyāya vaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷḷappaṭum.  yāravar, yāvatatu eṉa varum.	 (30)
aintāvatu - tokimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.


urupiyal
vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ peyarkaḷōṭu poruntum muṟimi yiṉi yuṇarttuvatu urupiyalākum. itaṉkaṇ muppatu cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. peyarum ataṉāl ēṟkappaṭum vēṟṟumiyurupum ākiya avviraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyē varum cāriyikaḷ iviyeṉpatum, vēṟṟumiyurupiṉi yēṟkuṅkāl peyarkaḷ peṟum iyalpum tiripumāvaṉa ivi yeṉpatum ivviyalil vakuttu viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
uyirkaḷuḷ a ā u ū ē oḷa eṉṉum āṟiṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya peyarkaḷum, meykaḷuḷ ñakara nakaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, tev eṉṉum vakaravīṟṟup peyarum, makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ cilavum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum urupēṟ kuṅkāl iṭiyē iṉcāriyi peṟuvaṉa.
paṉmip poruḷik karutiṉa akara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, yā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟu viṉāppeyarum, avi, ivi, uvi, yāvi, av, iv, uv eṉpaṉavum vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟpaṉa. yāvi eṉṉum viṉāppeyar vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟuṅkāl appeyariṉ īṟṟil niṉṟa aikāramum ataṉālūrappaṭṭa vakarameyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. ellām eṉṉum potuppeyar aḵṟiṇikkaṇvariṉ vaṟṟuc cāriyiyum, uyar tiṇikkaṇvariṉ nam cāriyiyum peṟuvatuṭaṉ vēṟṟumi yurupiṉ iṟutiyil um cāriyiyum peṟṟumuṭiyum.
atu, itu, utu eṉavarum ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarum, ēḻ eṉṉum ḻakaravīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarum, kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟuḷ varum ellā eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum, yātu eṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉavarum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟpaṉavām. aṉcāriyi peṟuṅkāl atu, itu, utu eṉpaṉa iṟutiyukaraṅ keṭṭu muṭivaṉa; aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉpaṉa āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭivaṉa. paḵteṉpataṉi yiṟutiyākavuṭiya orupatumutal eṇpatu variyuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ eṭṭum, aṉcāriyiyēyaṉṟi āṉcāriyi peṟutalum uṇṭu. āṉcāriyi peṟuṅkāl nilimoḻiyiluḷḷa paḵteṉpataṉ kaṇ pakarameymaṭṭum niṟka aḵteṉpatu keṭum.
ōkāra vīṟṟuppeyar urupēṟkuṅkāl oṉcāriyi peṟum.
akara ākāra vīṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ ēḻāmurupu peṟumvaḻi attuc cāriyipeṟum. makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ urupēṟkuṅkāl attuccāriyi peṟuvaṉa. aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ eṉpaṉa attum iṉṉum peṟṟu uṟaḻvaṉa.
nī eṉṉum peyar niṉ eṉattirintu urupēṟkum. makara vīṟṟuḷ num eṉpatum tām, nām, yām eṉpaṉavum attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉa. yām eṉṉum peyaril yakarattiṉmēlēṟiya ākāram ekaramākat tiriya aṅku niṉṟa yakaram keṭum. tām, nām eṉpaṉa mutalkuṟuki muṟiyē tam nam eṉa niṟkum. ‘ellārum’ eṉṉum paṭarkkic colliṭattum ‘ellīrum’ eṉṉum muṉṉilic colliṭattum iṟutiyil niṉṟa um eṉpatu keṭa, avvirupeyarum muṟiyē tam cāriyiyum nam cāriyiyum peṟṟu urupēṟkum. avipeṟum urupiṉ piṉṉar um cāriyi vantu poruntum
 ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ tāṉ eṉpatu mutalkuṟukit taṉ eṉṟum yāṉ eṉpatu mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa yakaraṅkeṭṭu ataṉiyūrntu niṉṟa ākāram ekaramāki eṉ eṉṟum tirintu urupēṟkum.
neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷuḷ (ṭaṟa) oṟṟi raṭṭikkuñ coṟkaḷ urupēṟkuṅkāl iṉcāriyipeṟātu iyalpāvaṉa.
kuṟṟukaravīṟṟut ticippeyarkaḷ kaṇṇurupiṉi ēṟkuṅ kāl iṉcāriyi peṟātu iyalpātalumuṇṭu. ivvāṟu iyalpāyavaḻi iṟutiyiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa meyyōṭu cērakkeṭum.
iṅku eṭutturikkappaṭṭa peyarkaḷallāta ēṉiya peyarkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟē urupēṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟiyillātaṉa vām. avi cāriyi peṟṟum peṟātum muṭivaṉavām.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 176-178

āṟāvatu
urupiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, urupukaḷoṭu peyar puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, urupiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. mēl tokuttup puṇartta ceyki īṇṭu niṉṟum virittup puṇark kiṉṟārākaliṉ, tokimarapiṉōṭu iyipu uṭittāyiṟṟu.
174.	aā uū ē oḷa veṉṉum
	appā lāṟa ṉilimoḻi muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ kiṉṉē cāriyi.
it talic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, akara ākāra ukara ūkāra ēkāra oḷakāra īṟukaḷ urupiṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a ā u ū ē oḷa eṉṉum appāl āṟaṉ nilimoḻi muṉṉar - a ā u ū ē oḷa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa ak kūṟṟu āṟaṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya nilimoḻi kaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi urupiṟku cāriyi iṉ - vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ varumoḻiyāy vanta nilimikku iṭivarum cāriyi iṉ cāriyi.
u-m:	viḷaviṉi, viḷaviṉoṭu, viḷaviṟku, viḷaviṉ, viḷaviṉatu, viḷaviṉkaṇ eṉavum; palāviṉi, palāvi ṉoṭu eṉavum; kaṭuviṉi, kaṭuviṉoṭu eṉavum; kaḻūuviṉi, kaḻūuviṉoṭu eṉavum; cēviṉi, cēviṉoṭu eṉavum; veḷaviṉi veḷaviṉoṭu eṉavum karuvi aṟintu oṭṭuka.	 (1)
175.	pallavi nutaliya vakara viṟupeyar
	vaṟṟoṭu civaṇa lecca miṉṟē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya īṟṟuḷ akarīṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pallavi nutaliya peyar iṟu akaram - paṉmip poruḷik karutiṉa peyarkaḷiṉ iṟuti akaram, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal eccam iṉṟu - vaṟṟuccāriyiyoṭu poruntutali oḻital illi.
u-m: pallavaṟṟi, pallavaṟṟoṭu; uḷḷavaṟṟi, uḷḷavaṟ ṟoṭu; illavaṟṟi, illavaṟṟoṭu; cillavaṟṟi, cillavaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘ecca miṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭu mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇ cāriyi peṟṟē muṭiyumeṉṟu koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mēl iṉpeṟṟaṉa piṟacāriyiyum peṟumeṉak koḷka. makatti, nilatti eṉa varum.	(2)
176.	yāveṉ viṉāvu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
iḵtu, ākāra vīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yā eṉ viṉāvum a iyal tiriyātu - yā eṉṟu collappaṭum ākāravīṟṟu viṉāp peyarum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa vaṟṟup peṟum av viyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
u-m: yāvaṟṟi, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(3)
177.	cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu civaṇi
	oṭṭiya meyyoḻit tukaraṅ keṭumē.
iḵtu, ukaravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal ukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukaravīṟṟuc col aṉcāriyiyoṭu porunti, oṭṭiya mey oḻittu ukaram keṭum - tāṉ poruntiya meyyi oḻittu ukaram keṭum.
u-m:	ataṉi, ataṉoṭu; itaṉi, itaṉoṭu; utaṉi, utaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(4)

178.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya viye ṉiṟuti
	vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalu murittē.
iḵtu, aikāravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku muṭipukūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya ai eṉ iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāravīṟṟuc col, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittu - vaṟṟuccāriyiyoṭu porunti av vīṟṟu aikāram niṟṟalu murittu; (nillāmiyu murittu.)
u-m: aviyaṟṟi, aviyaṟṟoṭu; iviyaṟṟi, iviyaṟ ṟoṭu; uviyaṟṟi, uviyaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
aikāram keṭṭavaḻi, niṉṟa vakarattiṉi vaṟṟiṉmici oṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu avaṟṟi, ivaṟṟi, uvaṟṟi eṉa oṭṭuka.
maṟṟu, im muṭipu cuṭṭumutal vakara vīṟṟōṭu ottamiyiṉ, īṇṭu itu kūṟal mikipaṭak kūṟalām piṟa eṉiṉ, aḵtu okkum; ivvāṟu kūṟuvaṉa mēlum uḷa; avaṟṟiṟkellām āciriyaṉ karuttu aṟintu koḷḷappaṭu meṉpatu.	(5)
179.	yāveṉ viṉāvi ṉiye ṉiṟutiyum
	āyiya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar
	āvayiṉ vakara miyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) yā eṉ viṉāviṉ ai eṉ iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāviṉiyuṭiya aikāravīṟṟuc collum, a iyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - mēṟkūṟiya cuṭṭumutal aikāram pōla vaṟṟup peṟum iyalpiṉ tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; a vayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭum keṭum - avviṭattu vakaram aikārattoṭukūṭak keṭum.
u-m: yāvaṟṟi, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
vakaram vaṟṟiṉmici oṟṟeṉṟu (134) keṭuvataṉi, īṇṭuk kēṭōtiyavataṉāl piṟa aikāramum vaṟṟuppeṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu: kariyavaṟṟi, ceyyavaṟṟi eṉa varum.	(6)
180.	nīye ṉorupeyar neṭumutal kuṟukum
	āvayi ṉakara moṟṟā kummē.
iḵtu, īkāravīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar neṭumutal kuṟukum - nī eṉṉum oru peyar neṭitākiya mutal kuṟukum; a vayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟākum - avviṭattu varum ṉakaram oṟṟākum.
u-m: niṉṉi, niṉṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘orupeyar’ eṉṟatu, niṉ eṉpatum vēṟoru peyar pōṟali vilakkiṟṟu. ‘peyarkuṟukum’ eṉṉātu ‘mutalkuṟukum’ eṉṟatu, ap peyariṉ neṭṭeḻuttu niliyatu ak kuṟukka meṉṟaṟ keṉpatu. ‘neṭumutal’ eṉṟatu am moḻimutaliṉ nakaram kuṟukutali vilakkiṟṟu. īṇṭu, uyirmey yoṟṟumi paṟṟi neṭiyatu mutalāyiṟṟu. cāriyippēṟṟiṭi eḻuttup pēṟu kūṟiyatu, mūṉṟām urupiṉ kaṇ cāriyi peṟṟē vanta atikāram māṟṟi niṉṟatu.	(7)
181.	ōkāra viṟutik koṉṉē cāriyi.
iḵtu, ōkāra īṟu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟutikkuc cāriyi oṉ - ōkāravīṟṟukku iṭivarum cāriyi oṉcāriyi e-ṟu.
u-m: kōoṉi, kōoṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(8)
182.	a ā veṉṉu marappeyark kiḷavik
	kattoṭuñ civaṇu mēḻa ṉurupē.
iḵtu, akara ākāravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku, urupiṉkaṇ eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a ā eṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavikku - a ā eṉṟu collappaṭum maratti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarākiya coṟku, attoṭum civaṇum ēḻaṉ urupu - muṉkūṟiya iṉṉōṭaṉṟi (174) attōṭum poruntum ēḻām urupu.
u-m: viḷavattukkaṇ, palāvattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	(9)
183. ña na veṉ puḷḷik kiṉṉē cāriyi.
itu, puḷḷiyīṟṟu ñakāravīṟum nakāravīṟum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na eṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyi - ña na eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟukaṭku varum cāriyi iṉcāriyi.
u-m:uriñiṉi, uriñiṉoṭu; poruniṉi, poruni ṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. (ēkāram aci.)	           (10)
184.	cuṭṭumutal vakara miyu meyyum
	keṭṭa viṟuti yiyaṟṟiri piṉṟē.
itu, vakaravīṟu nāṉkaṉuḷḷum cuṭṭumutal vakaravīṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭumutal vakaram - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalāka vuṭiya vakaravīṟṟuccol, aiyum meyyum keṭṭa iṟuti iyal tiripu iṉṟu - aikāramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyum keṭṭu vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭinta cuṭṭumutal aikāravīṟṟiyalpiṉ tiripiṉṟi vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	avaṟṟi, avaṟṟoṭu; ivaṟṟi, ivaṟṟoṭu; uvaṟṟi, uvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(11)
185.	ēṉi vakara miṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
itu, vakaravīṟṟuḷ oḻinta vakaravīṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - oḻinta vakara vīṟu iṉcāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum.
u-m: tevviṉi, tevviṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
maṟṟu itu uriccol laṉṟōveṉiṉ, uriccollē yeṉiṉum paṭuttalōciyāṟ peyarāyiṟṟeṉak koḷka.  (12)
186.	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimu ṉattē cāriyi.
itu, makarīṟu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ attu cāriyi - makaramākiya puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ varum cāriyi attuc cāriyi.
u-m: maratti, marattoṭu eṉa varum.	(13)
187.	iṉṉiṭi varūu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu, makaravīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉ iṭivarum moḻiyum uḷa - makaravīṟu attuccāriyi yoḻiya iṉcāriyi iṭivantu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa.
u-m:  urumiṉi, urumiṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
(ār eṉpatu aci. ēkāram īṟṟaci.)	(14)
188.	numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟki yākum.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ iṟuti iyaṟki ākum - num eṉṉum makaravīṟu mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum .
u-m: nummi, nummoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(15)
 189.	tāmnā meṉṉu makara viṟutiyum
	yāme ṉiṟutiyu mataṉō raṉṉa
	ā e ākum yāme ṉiṟuti
	āvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum
	ēṉi yiraṇṭu neṭumutal kuṟukum.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ muṉkūṟiya muṭipu ovvātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tām nām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum yām eṉ iṟutiyum ataṉ ōr aṉṉa - tām nām eṉṟu collappaṭum makaravīṟum yām eṉṉum makaravīṟum mēṟkūṟiya num eṉṉum makaravīṟupōla attum iṉṉum peṟātu muṭitali yuṭiyavām; yām eṉ iṟuti ā e ākum - yām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟu moḻi ākāram ekāramām; a vayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu yakaramākiya mey keṭutal vēṇṭum; ēṉi iraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum - oḻinta iraṇṭum neṭiyavākiya mutal kuṟukiniṉṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: tammi, tammoṭu; nammi, nammoṭu; emmi, emmoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘āvayiṉ mey’ eṉṟataṉāl, piṟavayiṉ meyyum keṭumeṉak koḷka. taṅkaṇ, naṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉa varum.  (16)
190.	ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
	vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum
	ummi niliyu miṟuti yāṉa.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellām eṉṉum iṟutimuṉṉar - ellām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟuc colliṉmuṉpu, vaṟṟu eṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum - mēṟkūṟiya attum (186) iṉṉumiṉṟi (187) vaṟṟu eṉṉum cāriyi muṭiyattōṉṟi muṭiyum, ummi niliyum iṟutiyāṉa - um eṉṉum cāriyi nilipeṟum iṟutikkaṇ.
u-m:	ellāvaṟṟiyum, ellāvaṟṟoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka. 
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉkām urupiṉkaṇṇum āṟām urupiṉkaṇṇum ummiṉ ukarakkēṭu koḷka. iṟutiyiṉ eṉpatu vēṟṟumi mayakkam. akaram cāriyi)	 (17)
191. uyartiṇi yāyi ṉammiṭi varumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇiyāyiṉ nam iṭivarum - ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar aḵṟiṇip peyaraṉṟi uyartiṇip peyarāy niṟkumāyiṉ, nam iṭivarum - nam eṉṉum cāriyi iṭivantu puṇarum.
nilimoḻiyoṟṟum mēl ‘vaṟṟumici yoṟṟu’ eṉṟu keṭṭuniṉṟa atikārattāṟ keṭukka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē ummup peṟutalum, ataṉkaṇ ukaram cila urupiṉkaṇ keṭutalum koḷka.
u-m: ellānammiyum, ellānammoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka. (18)
192.	ellāru meṉṉum paṭarkki yiṟutiyum
	ellīru meṉṉu muṉṉili yiṟutiyum
	oṟṟu mukaramuṅ keṭumeṉa moḻipa
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakarap puḷḷi
	ummi niliyu miṟuti yāṉa
	tammiṭi varūum paṭarkki mēṉa
	nummiṭi varūu muṉṉili moḻikkē.
itu, makara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkki iṟutiyum ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉili iṟutiyum - ellārum eṉṟu collap paṭum paṭarkkiyiṭattu makaravīṟṟuc collum ellīrum eṉṟu collap paṭum muṉṉiliyiṭattu makaravīṟṟuc collum, oṟṟum ukaramum keṭum eṉa moḻipa - ivaṟṟiṉ makaravoṟṟum ataṉmuṉ niṉṟa ukaramum keṭṭu muṭiyu meṉṟu colluvar, rakarappuḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - av vukaram keṭuvaḻi ataṉāl ūrappaṭṭa rakarappuḷḷi keṭātu niṟṟal vēṇṭum, iṟutiyāṉa ummi niliyum - av viru moḻikkum iṟutikkaṇ um eṉṉum cāriyi nilipeṟum, paṭarkki mēṉa tam iṭi varum - paṭarkkiyiṭattut tammuc cāriyi iṭi varum, muṉṉili moḻikku num iṭivarum - muṉṉili moḻikku nummuccāriyi iṭivarum.
u-m:	ellār tammiyum, ellārtammoṭum; ellīr nummiyum, ellīrnummoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘paṭarkki’, ‘muṉṉili’ eṉṟa mikutiyāṉ, makaravīṟṟut taṉmip peyariṭikkaṇ nammuccāriyiyum, īṟṟukkaṇ ummuc cāriyiyum peṟṟumuṭivaṉa koḷka. kariyēnammiyum, kariyēnammoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka.
paṭarkkippeyar muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, rakāravīṟṟup paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilip peyarum iṟutikkaṇ ummoṭu tammuc cāriyiyum nummuccāriyiyum (iṭiyē) peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka. kariyārtammiyum, cāṉṟīrnummiyum eṉa oṭṭuka. ukaramum oṟṟum eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, am mūṉṟu urupiṉkaṇ (eḻut. 190) ummiṉ ukarakkēṭu eṭuttōtiyavaṟṟiṟkum ilēciṉāṟ koṇṭa vaṟṟiṟkum koḷka. (mēl eṉpatu mēṉ eṉat tirintu akaraccāriyi ēṟṟuniṉṟatu.)	(19)
193.	tāṉyā ṉeṉṉu māyī riṟutiyum
	mēṉmup peyaroṭum vēṟupā ṭilavē.
itu, ṉakara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉ yāṉ eṉṉum a iru iṟutiyum - tāṉ yāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭum avviraṇṭu ṉakara vīṟum, mēl muppeya roṭum vēṟupāṭu ila  mēl makaravīṟṟuṭ collappaṭṭa (189) mūṉṟu peyaroṭum vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukiyum yāṉ eṉpataṉkaṇ ākāram ekāramāy yakaraṅkeṭṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: taṉṉi, taṉṉoṭu; eṉṉi, eṉṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
(`iru’ eṉpatu taṉmuṉṉar uyirvantamiyāl `īr’ eṉṟāyiṟṟu. akarac cuṭṭu nīṇṭu yakara uṭumpaṭumey peṟṟatu. ēkāram īṟṟaci.)	(20)
194.	aḻaṉē puḻaṉē yāyiru moḻikkum
	attu miṉṉu muṟaḻat tōṉṟal
	otta teṉpa uṇaru mōrē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aḻaṉ puḻaṉ a iru moḻikkum - aḻaṉ puḻaṉākiya av viru moḻikkum, attum iṉṉum uṟaḻat tōṉṟal ottatu eṉpa uṇarumōr - attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum māṟivarat tōṉṟu talip poruntiṟṟeṉpa uṇaruvōr.
u-m:	aḻatti, aḻattoṭu; aḻaṉiṉi, aḻaṉiṉoṭu;  puḻatti, puḻattoṭu; puḻaṉiṉi, puḻaṉiṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘tōṉṟal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, evaṉ eṉṟum, eṉ eṉṟum niṟutti, vaṟṟukkoṭuttu vēṇṭum ceykiceytu evaṟṟi, evaṟṟoṭu eṉavum, eṟṟi, eṟṟoṭu eṉavum muṭikka.
‘ottatu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ekiṉ eṉa niṟutti, attum iṉṉum koṭuttuc ceyki ceytu ekiṉatti, ekiṉattoṭu; ekiṉiṉi, ekiṉiṉoṭu eṉavum muṭikka.
attu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, attup peṟṟavaḻi iṉitu icikku meṉak koḷka.	
(aḻaṉ - piṇam. muṉ ēkāram iraṇṭum eṇṇiṭic col. piṉ ēkāram īṟṟaci. `uṇarumōr’ eṉpatu tollivaḻakku.)	 (21)
195.	aṉṉeṉ cāriyi yēḻa ṉiṟuti
	muṉṉart tōṉṟum miyaṟkit teṉpa.
itu, ḻakaravīṟṟu orumoḻikku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉ eṉ cāriyi ēḻaṉ iṟuti muṉṉart tōṉṟum iyaṟkittu eṉpa - aṉ eṉṉum cāriyi ēḻeṉṉum colliṟutiyiṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟum iyalpiṉi yuṭitteṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
u-m: ēḻaṉi, ēḻaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, piṟavum aṉ peṟuvaṉa koḷka. pūḻaṉi, pūḻaṉoṭu; yāḻaṉi, yāḻaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	 (22)
196.	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟuti muṉṉar
	muṟṟat tōṉṟu miṉṉeṉ cāriyi.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutimuṉṉar muṟṟat tōṉṟum iṉ eṉ cāriyi - kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar muṭiyat tōṉṟum iṉ eṉ cāriyi.
u-m: varakiṉi, varakiṉoṭu; nākiṉi, nākiṉoṭu eṉa  oṭṭuka.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa cāriyi peṟuvaṉavum koḷka. vaḻakkattāṟ pāṭṭārāyntāṉ, kariyataṉi, kariyataṉoṭu eṉa varum.	(23)
197.	neṭṭeḻut timpa roṟṟumikat tōṉṟum
	appāṉ moḻika ḷalvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttu impar oṟṟu mikat tōṉṟum - neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉāka (iṭi) iṉavoṟṟu mikat tōṉṟum, appāl moḻikaḷ avvaḻiyāṉa - ak kūṟṟu moḻikaḷ allāta iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē.
av vīṟu iṉcāriyi peṟuvatu; āṇṭāyiṉ peṟātu.
u-m:	yāṭṭi, yāṭṭoṭu, yāṟṟi, yāṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.
appāṉ moḻikaḷāvaṉa: ka ca ta pa kkaḷ. ivi iṉavoṟṟu mikāveṉṟu koḷka.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyirttoṭar moḻiyum iṉpeṟātu iṉavoṟṟu mikutal koḷka. pakaṭṭi, pakaṭṭoṭu; muyiṟṟi, muyiṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	(24)
198.	avitām
	iyaṟkiya vākuñ ceyaṟkiya veṉpa.
itu, mēṟ cāriyi vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - mēṟ cāriyi peṟāveṉa vilakkap paṭṭa avitām, iyaṟkiya ākum ceyaṟkiya eṉpa - iyalpāy muṭitali yuṭiyavākum ceytiyiyuṭiya veṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: yāṭṭi, yāṭṭoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘ceyaṟkiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉavoṟṟu mikkaṉa ciṟupāṉmi iṉpeṟutalum koḷka. yāṭṭiṉi, yāṭṭi ṉoṭu; muyiṟṟiṉi, muyiṟṟiṉoṭu eṉa varum.	 (25)
199.	eṇṇi ṉiṟuti yaṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇiṉ iṟuti aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉatu kuṟṟukara vīṟu aṉcāriyiyoṭu poruntum.
u-m:	oṉṟaṉi, oṉṟaṉoṭu; iraṇṭaṉi, iraṇṭa ṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(26)
200.	oṉṟu mutalākap pattūrntu varūum
	ellā veṇṇuñ colluṅ kāli
	āṉiṭi variṉu māṉa milli
	aḵteṉ kiḷavi yāvayiṟ keṭumē
	uytal vēṇṭum paḵkāṉ meyyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalākap pattu ūrntu varum ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyar mutalākap pattu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarāṉ ūrappaṭṭu varum ellā eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, colluṅ kāli - muṭipu colluṅ kālattu, āṉ iṭivariṉum māṉam illi - av vīṟṟiṟku mēṟkūṟiya aṉṉēyaṉṟi āṉcāriyi iṭivariṉum kuṟṟamilli, a vayiṉ aḵtu eṉ kiḷavi keṭum - av vāṉcāriyi peṟṟavaḻi aḵtu eṉṉum col keṭum, paḵkāṉmey uytal vēṇṭum - atu keṭuvaḻi av vakarattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa pakaramākiya mey keṭātu niṟṟali vēṇṭum.
u-m: orupāṉi, orupāṉoṭu; orupaḵtaṉi, orupaḵtaṉoṭu; irupaḵtaṉi, irupaḵtaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘colluṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇup peyarum āṉ peṟṟu, avvīṟṟiṉ atu eṉṉum coṟkeṭṭu muṭital koḷka. oṉpāṉi, oṉpāṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(27)
201.	yāte ṉiṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
	āyta viṟutiyu maṉṉoṭu civaṇum
	āytaṅ keṭuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum, ak kuṟṟukara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yātu eṉ iṟutiyum - yātu eṉa varum kuṟṟukara īṟum, cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - aṉcāriyiyoṭu poruntum, ā vayiṉāṉa āytam keṭutal - avviṭattu āytam keṭuka.
u-m: yātaṉi, yātaṉoṭu; ataṉi, ataṉoṭu; itaṉi, itaṉoṭu; utaṉi, utaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.  
(akaraccuṭṭu nīṇṭu niṉṟatu. `āṉ’ eṉpatu - vēṟṟumi mayakkam. īṟṟakaram - cāriyi.)	    (28)
202.	ēḻa ṉurupiṟkut ticippeyar muṉṉarc
	cāriyik kiḷavi yiyaṟkiyu mākum
	āvayi ṉiṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvum kuṟṟukaravīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku ēḻaṉ urupoṭu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēḻaṉ urupiṟku - ēḻām vēṟṟumikku, ticip peyar muṉṉar - ticiyi uṇaraniṉṟa peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, cāriyikkiḷavi iyaṟkiyum ākum - iv vīṟṟiṟku muṉkūṟiya iṉcāriyiyākiya col niṉṟu muṭitalēyaṉṟi nillātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum; a vayiṉ iṟuti meyyoṭum keṭum - iyalpāyavaḻip peyar iṟutik kuṟṟukaram taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyoṭum keṭum.
u-m: vaṭakkiṉkaṇ, kiḻakkiṉkaṇ, teṟkiṉkaṇ, mēṟkiṉkaṇ eṉavum; vaṭakkaṇ, kiḻakkaṇ, teṟkaṇ, mēṟkaṇ eṉavum varum.
urupu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, kīḻcār, kīḻpuṭi; mēlcār, mēlpuṭi; teṉcār, teṉpuṭi; vaṭacār, vaṭapuṭi eṉa ivvāṟu cāriyi peṟātu tirintu muṭivaṉavellām koḷka.	 (akaraccuṭṭu nīṇṭu niṉṟatu. ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	(29)
203.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	colliya valla vēṉiya vellām
	tēruṅ kāli yurupoṭu civaṇic
	cāriyi niliyuṅ kaṭappā ṭilavē.
iḵtu, iv vōttiṟkellām puṟaṉaṭi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi iṟutiyum uyir iṟu kiḷaviyum colliya alla ēṉiya ellām - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collumeṉa muṭipu colliyavi allāta oḻintavi yellām, tērum kāli - ārāyuṅkālattu, urupoṭu civaṇi cāriyi niliyum kaṭappāṭu ila - urupukaḷoṭu porunti iṉ cāriyi niṉṟu muṭiyum muṟimiyi yuṭiya valla (niṉṟum nillātum muṭiyum).
puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa aintu; uyirīṟṟuḷ oḻintatu oṉṟu. iviyum eṭuttōtiya īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉavumellām īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭum.
u-m: maṇṇiṉi, maṇṇi; vēyiṉi, vēyi; nāriṉi, nāri; kalliṉi, kalli;  muḷḷiṉi, muḷḷi   eṉavum; kiḷiyiṉi, kiḷiyi eṉavum; poṉṉiṉi, poṉṉi   eṉavum; tāḻiṉi, tāḻi; tīyiṉi, tīyi; kaḻiyiṉi, kaḻiyi eṉavum  oṭṭuka.
puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa palavākaliṉ, atu muṟkūṟap paṭṭatu. ‘tēruṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupukaḷ nilimoḻi yāka niṉṟu tam poruḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi muṭiyum muṭipu vēṟṟumi yellām koḷka.
maṇṇiṉik koṇarntāṉ, nampiyik koṇarntāṉ, koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉ; maliyoṭu porutatu mālyāṉi; marattāṟ puṭittāṉ; marattukkup pōṉāṉ; kākkiyiṟ karitu; kākkiyatu pali; marattukkaṇ kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka.
iḵtu urupiyalākaliṉ, ‘urupoṭu civaṇi’ eṉa vēṇṭāvaṉṟē, ataṉāṉ, urupupuṇarccikkaṇ ceṉṟa cāriyikaḷellām īṟṟup potumuṭipu uḷvaḻip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum cellu meṉpatu koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, uyirīṟum meyyīṟum cāriyi peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉa koḷka. nampiyi, koṟṟaṉi eṉa varum.	          (30)
āṟāvatu - urupiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.


uyir mayaṅkiyal
uyirīṟu niṉṟu valleḻuttōṭum ciṟupāṉmi ēṉi yeḻuttukkaḷōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟuvatu uyir mayaṅkiya lākum. mayaṅkutal - kalattal. uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya iru vaḻiyilum iyalpāmeṉavum, oru cila viṭaṅkaḷil aḵṟiṇi viravup peyar iyalpātalu muṇṭeṉavum toki marapil vitantu kūṟiya āciriyar, aviyoḻinta uyirīṟṟu aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiyum ēṉi viṉimuṟṟu viṉiyeccam peyarec caṅkaḷiyum akaravīṟumutal oḷakāravīṟumuṭiya neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyil vittu uṇarttukiṉṟār. ivviyalil 93-cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ pala māṭṭēṟṟu muṟiyil amintaṉavākum. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷ yāvum valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iyalpāvaṉa, melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, uyirmika varuvaṉa, nīṭavaruvaṉa kuṟukivaruvaṉa, cāriyipeṟuvaṉa, piṟavāṟu tirivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkuvaṉavākum.
a, i, u. eṉṉum mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉum uyirum yakaramumvariṉ vakaravoṟṟum kacatapañanamava eṉpaṉavariṉ vanta oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum mikumeṉpatum ceyyuḷuḷ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu muṭiyumeṉpatum 3-6, 34, 35, 53-ām cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ collap paṭṭaṉa.
ā, ā, ī, u, ū, ē, ō, ōḷa eṉpavaṟṟi īṟākavuṭiya peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya īriṭattum valleḻuttu mikappeṟumeṉṟum, ikara aikāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikappeṟu meṉṟum 1, 14, 19, 22, 33, 47, 57, 62, 64, 72, 74, 78, 87, 90, 93-ām, cūttiraṅkaḷil āciriyar virittukkūṟiyuḷḷār.
alvaḻikkaṇ akaravīṟṟuḷ aṉṉaveṉṉum uvamavurupum, aṇmicuṭṭiya uyartiṇi viḷippeyarum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum viṉiccollum, ēvalik karutiya viyaṅkōḷ viṉiyum, ceyta eṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccamum, ceyyiya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccamum, amma eṉṉum uriyaciyum, akara vīṟṟup palavaṟicollum, ākāravīṟṟuḷ ā, mā eṉṉum peyarc collum, uyartiṇi viḷippeyarum, yā viṉāvum muṟṟuviṉiyum, miyā eṉṉum acic collum, taṉ toḻilik kuṟittuvarum ākāra viṉāviyuṭiya viṉiccollum, īkāra vīṟṟuḷ nī, mī eṉpaṉavum pakaravīkāramum, ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum, tēṟṟap poruḷil varum ekaravīṟum, ciṟappup poruḷil varum okaravīṟum, māṟukōḷeccap poruḷilum viṉāp poruṇmiyilum eṇṇupporuṇmiyilum aiyap poruṇmiyilum varum ēkāra ōkāra vīṟṟiṭic coṟkaḷum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāvaṉa.
akaravīṟṟu marappeyarum ā, i, u, ē, ai ākiya īṟukaḷil eṭuttōtiya piṭā, taḷā, mā (maram) ā, mā, (vilaṅku) uti, oṭu, cē, vici, ñemi, nami eṉṉum peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavām. yā, piṭā, taḷā, puḷi (cuvi) eṉpaṉa valleḻuttup peṟutalumuṇṭu.
ummittokikkaṇvarum ākāravīṟṟuppeyarum vēṟṟumik kaṇ kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ākāravīṟṟuppeyarum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyākiya ākāra vīṟṟuppeyarum akaram mikku muṭiyum. irā eṉṉuñ collukku akaramikutal illi. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ūkāravīṟum ōreḻuttuttoru moḻiyākiya ūkāra vīṟum vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukaram mikkumuṭiyum. ē yeṉṉum peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ ekaram mikku muṭiyum. ōkāravīṟu vēṟṟumik kaṇ okaram mikum. oḷakāravīṟṟup peyar iruvaḻiyilum ukarammikum.
ceyyuṭkaṇvarum akaraccuṭṭum, amma eṉṉum iṭic colliṟutiyum, pala cila eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti akaramum, ḻakarameyyiyūrnta iṟuti ukaramum nīṇṭu muṭivaṉavām.
ā muṉvarum pakara vīkāram kuṟukum. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa iṟuti ākāram kuṟuki ukaram peṟum.
maka, āṭūu, makaṭūu cē eṉpaṉa iṉcāriyipeṟum. nilā attuccāriyipeṟum. paṉi (kālam), vaḷi (pūtam), maḻi eṉpaṉa attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum peṟuvaṉa. puḷi, eru, ceru, paṉi, ari, āviri eṉpaṉa amcāriyipeṟuvaṉa. ikara aikāra vīṟṟu nāṭ peyarmuṉ toḻiṟcolvariṉ iṭiyē āṉcāriyi varum. avviraṇṭīṟṟut tiṅkaṭ peyar muṉ toḻiṟcolvariṉ iṭiyē ikkuccāriyi varum. ūveṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi ṉakaravoṟṟum akkuc cāriyiyum peṟum.
a, ī, u, ai, ō eṉṉum īṟukaḷukku urupiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa cāriyikaḷi ivviyaliluḷḷa 18, 51, 61, 79, 92-ām cūttiraṅkaḷāl avvīṟṟup poruṭ puṇarccikkum māṭṭeṟintu viti kūṟuvar āciriyar.
‘cāva’ eṉpataṉ vakaravuyirmeyyum ‘vāḻiya’ eṉpataṉ yakaravuyirmeyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. nāḻi eṉpataṉmuṉ uri yeṉṉuñcol vantupuṇariṉ nilimoḻiyīṟṟiṉ ikaram tāṉ ēṟiya ḻakara meyyuṭaṉkeṭa avviṭattu ṭakaramey tōṉṟi muṭiyum. ceru eṉpataṉ muṉvarum amcāriyiyiṉ makaraṅkeṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. paṉi, āviri eṉpaṉa amcāriyi peṟuṅkāl iṟuti aikāraṅkeṭṭu muṭivaṉa. paṉi eṉṉuñcolmuṉ aṭṭu eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ nili moḻiyiṟuti aikāraṅkeṭa avviṭattu ākāram tōṉṟum. pala cila eṉpavi tam muṉṉart tāmvariṉ iṟuti niṉṟa lakara vuyirmey lakaravoṟṟākat tiriyum. iṉṟiyeṉpataṉ iṟutiyikaram ceyyuḷuḷ ukaramākattiriyum. cuṭṭup peyarīṟṟu ukaram aṉṟu eṉpataṉōṭu puṇaruṅkāl ākāramākat tiritalum aiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇaruṅkāl keṭutalum ceyyuḷiṭat tuṇ ṭākun tiripukaḷām. vēṭki yeṉṉuñcolmuṉ avā eṉpatu vantu puṇariṉ nilimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa aikāram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa kakarameyyoṭuṅkeṭa aṅkuḷḷa ṭakaram ṇakara māyt tirital ceyyuṭkaṇvarun tiripākum.
- ka.veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 178-181
ēḻāvatu
uyirmayaṅkiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, uyirīṟu vaṉ kaṇattoṭum ciṟupāṉmi piṟa kaṇattoṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttaliṉ uyirmayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. mēl urupu puṇarcci kūṟi īṇṭup poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, mēlata ṉōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu.
204.	akara viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ
	tatta motta voṟṟiṭi mikumē.
it taliccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, akara vīṟṟup peyarkku vaṉkaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akara iṟuti peyarnili muṉṉar - akaramākiya iṟutiyiyuṭiya peyarccol muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi ka ca ta pa tōṉṟiṉ - vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻikkaṇ ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiṉ, tam tam otta oṟṟu iṭimikum - tattamakkup poruntiṉa ak ka ca ta pakkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikum.
u-m: viḷakkuṟitu, makakkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu 
eṉa varum.
‘otta’ oṟṟeṉṉātu ‘tattam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaravīṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkum melleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum muṭipum, iṭiccoṟkaḷuḷ eṭuttōtātavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum, akaram taṉṉi uṇaraniṉṟavaḻi muṭiyum muṭipum koḷka.
taṭakki, tavakkoṇṭāṉ eṉa ivi uriccol valleḻuttuppēṟu. taṭañcevi, taṭantōḷ eṉa ivi melleḻuttuppēṟu.  “maṭava maṉṟa taṭavu nilik koṉṟi” eṉpatu iṭiccol muṭipu. akkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉpatu taṉṉi uṇara niṉṟavaḻi valleḻuttu mikuti. avyātu eṉpatu iṭiyeḻuttu mikuti. avvaḻakitu eṉpatu uyirkkaṇattu muṭipu.	 (1)
205.	viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvamak kiḷaviyum
	eṉave ṉeccamuñ cuṭṭi ṉiṟutiyum
	āṅka veṉṉu muriyacik kiḷaviyum
	ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, akarīṟṟu viṉiccol muṭipum, iṭiccol muṭipum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum - akara vīṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya collum uvamac collum, eṉa eṉ eccamum cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum - eṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa eccac collum cuṭṭākiya akaravīṟum, āṅka eṉṉum uriyacik kiḷaviyum - āṅka eṉṟu collappaṭum uriyaci yākiya collum, ñāṅkar kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum - mēlic cūttirattuc coṉṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: uṇakkoṇṭāṉ, tiṉakkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉpaṉa - viṉiyeccam.  mēlaṉavellām iṭiccol. pulipōlak koṉṟāṉ eṉpatu - uvamam. koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu - eṉa eṉ eccam. akkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉpaṉa - cuṭṭiṉ iṟuti. āṅkak koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu - uriyacik kiḷavi.	(2)
206.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ña na mat tōṉṟiṉ
	oṭṭiya voṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, akaraccuṭṭu meṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉar ña na ma tōṉṟiṉ - akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamakkaḷ mutalākiya moḻi varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiṉ, oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭimikutal vēṇṭum - tattamakkup poruntiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇṇē mikutali vēṇṭum.
u-m: aññālam, nūl, maṇi eṉa varum.	(3)
207.	yavamuṉ variṉē vakara moṟṟum.
itu, cuṭṭu iṭikkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ya va muṉ variṉ vakaram oṟṟum - yakara vakaram mutal moḻiyākac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉē variṉ iṭikkaṇ vakaram oṟṟām.
u-m:avyāḻ, avvaḷi eṉa varum.	(4)
208. uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
iḵtu, accuṭṭu uyirkkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir muṉ variṉum a iyal tiriyātu - uyir mutal moḻi akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar variṉum mēṟkūṟiya vakaram mikku muṭiyum av viyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
u-m: avvaṭi, avvāṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.
iṭimikka vakarattiṉi ‘neṟiyiyal’(161) eṉṟa ilēciṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atu nilimoḻit toḻileṉpatuvē kūṟappaṭṭatu. ‘tiriyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, mēṟ cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi vakarakkēṭu koḷka.	(5)
209.	nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.
iḵtu, accuṭṭuc ceyyuḷuḷ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nīṭavarutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - akarac cuṭṭu nīḷumpaṭiyāka varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
u-m: “āyiru tiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉa collē”
(kiḷavi. - 1) eṉa varum.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, im muṭipu vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇamellāvaṟṟoṭum ceṉṟatu. utāraṇam peṟṟavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.	(6)
210.	cāva veṉṉuñ ceyave ṉeccat
	tiṟuti vakaraṅ keṭutalu murittē.
itu, mēl ‘viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum’ (uyirmayaṅkiyal 2) eṉṟa muṭipiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cāva eṉṉum ceya eṉ eccattu iṟuti vakaram keṭutalum urittu - cāva eṉṟu collappaṭum ceya eṉ eccattu iṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa vakara voṟṟum keṭātu niṟṟalē yaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭitalum uriya.
u-m:	cākkuttiṉāṉ, cīṟiṉāṉ, takarttāṉ, puṭittāṉ  eṉa varum.
itaṉi ‘viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum’ (uyirmayaṅkiyal - 2) eṉṟataṉpiṉ viyāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, iyalpu kaṇattum an nilimoḻik kēṭu koḷka. cāñāṉṟār eṉa varum.	(7)
211.	aṉṉa veṉṉu muvamak kiḷaviyum
	aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinilik kiḷaviyum
	ceymmaṉa veṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum
	ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum
	ceyta veṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum
	ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	amma veṉṉu muripporuṭ kiḷaviyum
	palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi yuḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi yaṉittu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, akaravīṟṟuḷ orucārp peyarkkum viṉikkum iṭikkum muṉ eytiyatu vilakkiyum, eytātatu eytuvittum muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. aṉṉaveṉpatum ceyyiya veṉpatum palavaṟṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi yeṉpatum eytiyatu vilakkiṉa. maṟṟiyaṉa eytātatu eytuvittaṉa.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṉa eṉṉum uvamakkiḷaviyum - aṉṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum uvama urupākiya iṭiccollum, aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinilik kiḷaviyum - aṇiyārik karutiya viḷiyākiya nilimiyi yuṭiya uyartiṇip peyarc collum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum toḻil iṟu collum - ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiccollākiya akaravīṟṟuc collum, ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ kiḷaviyum-  ēvalik karutiya viyaṅkōḷākiya viṉiccollum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccamākiya viṉiccollum, ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉi eñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyyiya eṉṟu collap paṭum viṉi yeccamākiya viṉic collum, amma eṉṉum urip poruḷ kiḷaviyum - amma eṉṟu collappaṭum uri yacip poruṇmiyiyuṭiya iṭiccollum, palavaṟṟu iṟutip peyarkkoṭi uḷappaṭa aṉṟi aṉittum - paṉmip poruḷkaḷiṉ akara vīṟṟup palaveṉṉum peyarc collumākiya avvaṉittuc collum, iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar (āciriyar).
u-m: poṉ aṉṉa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum;  ūra koḷ, cel, tā, pō eṉavum; uṇṭaṉa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; celka kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; uṇṭa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; itaṉ etirmaṟi uṇṇāta kutiri eṉavum; itaṉ kuṟippu nalla kutiri, cennāy eṉavum; uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; amma koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum;  pala kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum varum.
‘ūra koḷ’ eṉpatu ‘uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇip peyar’ (154) eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātōveṉiṉ, ūraṉ eṉpatu ūra eṉa īṟu tirintamiyiṉ āṇṭu aṭaṅkātāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu.
viḷinilik kiḷaviyākiya peyari muṉvittataṉāl, ceyta veṉpataṉ maṟiyaṉṟic ceyyum eṉpataṟku maṟiyākiya ceyyāta eṉpatum av viyalpu uṭitteṉak koḷka. vārāta koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum.
‘uriyiṟkōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, viyaṅkōḷ muṉ vikkaṟ pālataṉi muṉviyātu ceymmaṉa eṉpataṉi muṉvittataṉāṉ, iv viyalpu muṭipiṉkaṇ ceymmaṉa eṉpatu ciṟappuṭitteṉap peṟappaṭṭatu. ataṉāṉ, ‘ēvalkaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷum’ iv viyalpu muṭipu uṭitteṉak koḷka. “maṉṉiya perumanī” (puṟam. 6) eṉa varum.
‘palavaṟṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi’ eṉpataṉi amma eṉpataṟku muṉ viyātataṉāṉ, pala eṉpatu mēṟkūṟum ceyyuḷ muṭipiṉ (214) tirintu muṭital īṇṭi iyalpiṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉpatūum, akara vīṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟāta muṟṟuviṉiyum viṉikkuṟippum iv viyalpiṉaveṉpatūum peṟappaṭum. uṇṭaṉa kutiri eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉi. kariya kutiri eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉik kuṟippu.
‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ (poruḷ.999 uri) eṉpataṉāṉ, pala eṉpataṉ iṉamākiya cila eṉpataṟkum av viyalpu koḷka.
cila kutiri eṉavarum.	(8)
212.	vāḻiya veṉṉuñ cēyeṉ kiḷavi
	iṟuti yakaraṅ keṭutalu murittē.
iḵtu ēval kaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷkaḷiṉ oṉṟaṟku eytiya iyalpu vilakki vikāram kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vāḻiya eṉṉum cēy eṉ kiḷavi - vāḻiya eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa, av vāḻuṅkālam aṇmiyataṉṟic cēymiyatu eṉṟu uṇarttum col, iṟuti yakaram keṭutalum urittu - taṉṉiṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa yakaravoṟṟum keṭātu muṭitalēyaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭitalum urittu.
u-m: vāḻikoṟṟā eṉa varum.
‘cēyeṉ kiḷavi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, am muṭipu iv ‘vāḻiya’ eṉpataṟku ēval kaṇṇāta niliya teṉpatu viḷakkiya niṉṟatu. ‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ (poruḷ - 999 uri) eṉpataṉāṉ, piṟa kaṇattukkaṇṇum iv viti koḷka. vāḻiñeḷḷā eṉa varum.	(9)
213.	uripporuṭ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum variyār.
iḵtu, amma eṉpataṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uripporuḷ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum variyār - uriyacip poruṇmiyiṉiyuṭiya amma eṉṉum iṭiccol taṉ īṟṟakaram akaramāy niṟṟalēyaṉṟi ākāramāy nīṇṭu muṭitaliyum variyār.
u-m:ammākoṟṟā eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ (poruḷ - 999 uri) eṉpataṉāṉ, an nīṭci iyalpukaṇattum koḷka. ammāñeḷḷā eṉa varum. 	(10)
214.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
	ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa.
itu, mēṟ “palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi” (uyir mayaṅkiyal 8) eṉṟu ōtiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa - pala eṉṉum colliṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; (yāṇṭu uḷaveṉiṉ), ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭar moḻiyāṉa- ceyyuḷātalik karutiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarccippaṭum ceyyuḷ muṭipuṭiya moḻikaḷiṉ kaṇṇē.
‘ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya moḻi’ eṉṉātu ‘toṭarmoḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ip palaveṉpatu nīḷumvaḻi varumoḻiyāvatu cilaveṉpatē eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. ‘ceyyuḷāṉa’ eṉṉātu ‘ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pala eṉṉum moḻiyīṟu nīṇṭavaḻi nilimoḻi akarap pēṟum varumoḻi ñakāra melleḻuttup pēṟum, varumoḻiyīṟu nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟum makaramākiya melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka. ‘uṇṭu’ eṉṉātu ‘uḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, cilaveṉṉum varumoḻi yiṟuti nīṭalum koḷka.
u-m:	‘palāañ cilāa meṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉavarum.  itaṉ colnili palacila eṉṉum cevveṇ. (akaram cāriyi.)	(11)
215.	toṭara liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ
	lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟātalu murittē.
itu, pala cila eṉpavaṟṟiṟku iyalpēyaṉṟit tiripum uṇṭeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toṭaral iṟutit tammuṉ tāmvariṉ - toṭar moḻiyallāta īreḻut torumoḻiyākiya pala cila eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuc col tammuṉṉē tāmvariṉ, lakaram ṟakara oṟṟu ātalum urittu - tam īṟṟil niṉṟa lakaravoṟṟu ṟakara voṟṟāyt tirintu muṭitalum urittu.
u-m:	paṟpala koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ciṟcila vitti eṉavum varum.
‘taṉmuṟṟāṉ’ eṉa orumiyāṟ kūṟātu ‘tammuṟṟāmvariṉ’ eṉṟa paṉmiyāṉ, mēṟ pala cila eṉa niṉṟa iraṇṭum taḻuvap paṭṭaṉa. ‘toṭara liṟuti tammuṉ variṉ’ eṉṉātu ‘tām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, im muṭipiṉkaṇ palaveṉpataṉmuṉ pala varuka, cilaveṉpataṉ muṉ cila varuka eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
‘lakaram ṟakaramām’ eṉṉātu ‘lakaram ṟakara voṟṟām’ eṉṟa ñāpakattāṉ, akaram keṭumeṉṟārākak koḷka. aruttāpatti mukattāl, tammuṉ tāmvariṉ lakaram ṟakara voṟṟā meṉavē, tammuṉ piṟa vantaviṭattu lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākātu akaram keṭumeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. ‘palpaṭi’ palyāṉi, cilpaṭi, cilvēḷvi eṉa varum.
‘toṭara liṟuti’ eṉpatu cuṭṭallatu ōreḻuttoru moḻi akaramiṉmiyiṉ, ōreḻut torumoḻimēṟ cellā tāyiṟṟu. uritteṉṟatu, akaravīṟṟorumi paṟṟi. sēkāram īṟṟaci]	(12)
216.	valleḻut tiyaṟki yuṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
ituvum, mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṟkum iṉṉum oru muṭipu vēṟṟumi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) val eḻuttu iyaṟki uṟaḻattōṉṟum - mēṟkūṟiya pala cila eṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum akaravīṟṟup potuvitiyiṟ kūṟiya valleḻuttu mikutiyatu iyalpu, mikutalum mikāmiyumāki uṟaḻntuvarat tōṉṟum.
u-m: palappala; cilaccila; palapala, cilacila eṉa varum.
īṇṭum tammuṉ tām varutal koḷka. ‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ muṉkūṟiya paṟpala, ciṟcila eṉṉum muṭipōṭu palpala, cilcila eṉṉum muṭipupeṟṟu uṟaḻcciyātal koḷka.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaram keṭa lakaram āytamum mel leḻuttumāyt tirintu muṭital koḷka.
u-m: paḵṟāṉi, paṉmaram; ciḵṟāḻici, ciṉṉūl eṉa varum.	 (13)
217.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, akara īṟṟup peyarkku vaṉkaṇattoṭu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - akara vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum mēṟkūṟiya (204) alvaḻiyōṭu oru taṉmittāyk ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi vantavaḻi tattam oṟṟu iṭimikku muṭiyum.
u-m: iruviḷakkoṟṟaṉ; cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉa varum. viḷakkuṟumi eṉak kuṇavēṟṟumik kaṇṇum koḷka. (iruviḷa - ōli, vēṇāṭṭakattu ōr ūr)	   (14)
218.	marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, akaravīṟṟu marappeyark kiḷavikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum - akaravīṟṟu marappeyarākiya col melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: viḷaṅkōṭu, viḷañcetiḷ; tōl, pū eṉa varum. (15)
219.	makappeyark kiḷavik kiṉṉē cāriyi.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makappeyark kiḷavikku iṉcāriyi - akaravīṟṟu maka eṉṉum peyarccolliṟku vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ varum cāriyi iṉ.
u-m: makaviṉki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
cāriyippēṟu variyātu kūṟiyavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattuk kaṇṇum cellumeṉpatākaliṉ, makaviṉñāṇ eṉa iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum koḷka.	  (16)
220.	attavaṇ variṉum varinili yiṉṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) attu avaṇ variṉum vari nili iṉṟu - mēṟkūṟiya iṉṉēyaṉṟi attu eṉṉum cāriyi iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu am maka eṉṉum colliṭattu vantu muṭiyiṉum nīkkum nilimi iṉṟu.
u-m:  makattukki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉac ceyki aṟintu muṭikka.
‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makappālyāṭu eṉa valleḻuttup pēṟum, makaviṉki eṉa mēl iṉcāriyi peṟṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ vantavaḻi viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka. ‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makampāl yāṭu eṉa melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka. 	 (17)
221.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti yurupiya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu, akaravīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟu iṟuti urupu iyal niliyum - pala eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuc col urupupuṇarccikkaṇ (175) vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarnta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṟkum.
u-m: palavaṟṟukkōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.   (18)
222.	ākāra viṟuti yakara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ākāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ākāra vīṟṟup peyar (al vaḻikkaṇ) akara vīṟṟu  alvaḻiyatu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: tārāk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.      (19)
223.	ceyyā veṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	avviya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu viṉiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyā eṉṉum viṉi eñcu kiḷaviyum - (peyarē yaṉṟi) ceyyā eṉṟu collappaṭum ākāravīṟṟu viṉiyeccac collum, a iyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum avviyalpiṉ tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	uṇṇāk koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘tiriyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘ceyyā’ eṉṉum peyareccamum avvāṟu muṭiyumeṉak koḷka. uṇṇāk koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum.	(20)
224.	ummi yeñciya virupeyart tokimoḻi
	meymmi yāka vakara mikumē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummittoki muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ummi eñciya irupeyart tokimoḻi - ummi tokka irupeyarākiya tokiccol, meymmi yāka akaram mikum - meyyāka nilimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: uvāap patiṉāṉku.
‘meymmiyāka’ eṉṟataṉāṉ valleḻuttuk koṭukka. ‘ummi tokka’ eṉṉātu ‘eñciya’ eṉṟa vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumiyāṉ, im muṭipu irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokikkum koḷka. arāap pāmpu eṉavarum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉ, eḻuvāy muṭipiṟkum peyareccat tiṟkum akarappēṟu koḷka. uvāak koṭitu; uvāak kākki eṉavarum.
nilimoḻi eḻuttuppēṟu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum cellumākaliṉ, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka. iṟāa vaḻutuṇaṅ kāy eṉavarum.	 (21)
225.	āvu māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum
	yāveṉ viṉāvum palavaṟ ṟiṟutiyum
	ēval kuṟitta vuriyaci miyāvum
	taṉṟoḻi lurikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyō
	ṭaṉṟi yaṉittu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku iyalpukūṟi eytiyatu vilakkutalum, eytātatu eytuvittalum nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āvum māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum - ā eṉṉum peyarccollum mā eṉṉum peyarccollum viḷittaliyuṭiya peyarākiya uyartiṇiccollum, yā eṉ viṉāvum palavaṟṟu iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāp peyarum aḵṟiṇip paṉmip poruḷi uṇarttum ākāravīṟṟu muṟṟuviṉic collum, ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum - muṉṉiliyil ēval viṉiccollik kuṟittu varum uriyaciyākiya miyā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟu iṭic collum, taṉtoḻil urikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyōṭu aṉṟi aṉittum - taṉatu toḻiliṉic collum ākāra viṉāviṉiyuṭiya viṉic collumākiya avvaṉittum, iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	ā kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum;  mā kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; ūrā koḷ, cel, tā, pō eṉavum;  yā kuṟiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya eṉavum; uṇṇā kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; kēṇmiyā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum; uṇkā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum varum.
‘viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum’, ‘palavaṟṟiṟutiyum’, ‘ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum’, ‘taṉtoḻi lurikkum viṉāvum’ eytātatu eytuvikkappaṭṭaṉa. ūrā koḷ eṉpatu ‘uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇip peyareṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātō veṉiṉ, muṉ (uyirmayaṅkiyal  8) kūṟiṟṟē kūṟuka.	 (22)
226.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu vēṟṟumi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - (ākāra vīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum akaravīṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum.
u-m: tārākkāl;  ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉavarum.  	(23)

227.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉaru mōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟu makarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōr eḻuttu moḻikkum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa ākāravīṟṟiṟkum ōreḻut torumoḻi ākāravīṟṟiṟkum, akarakkiḷavi aṟiyat tōṉṟum - (nilimoḻikkaṇ) akaramākiya eḻuttu aṟiyat tōṉṟum.
u-m: palāakkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; kāakkuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
ōreḻuttorumoḻi muṟkūṟātavataṉāṉ, ataṉkaṇ akarappēṟu ciṟupāṉmi yeṉak koḷka. ‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, av viruvaḻiyum akaram poruntiya vaḻiyē varutalum, avvīṟṟu vēṟṟumiyuḷ eṭuttōtātavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum, ivvīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟa vaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum, iv vuyirīṟṟil varum urupīṟṟuc ceykiyum koḷka.
u-m: aṇṇāattuk kuḷam, uvāattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, uvāattāṟ koṇṭāṉ, yāvaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa ivi - piṟamuṭipu. mūṅkāviṉṟō eṉpatu - valleḻuttu vīḻvu. iṭāviṉuṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu - urupīṟṟuc ceyki.		(24)
228.	irāveṉ kiḷavik kakara milli.
iḵtu, av akarappēṟṟiṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) irā eṉ kiḷavikku akaram illi - irā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟuc colliṟku muṉkūṟiya akarappēṟu illi.
u-m: irākkoṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(25)
229.	nilāveṉ kiḷavi yattoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ oruvaḻi akaram vilakki attuvarutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nilā eṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum - nilā eṉṉum col attuc cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum.
u-m: nilāattuk koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
nilimoḻit toḻil  varumoḻit toḻili vilakku mākaliṉ, attu vakuppa akaram vīḻntatu.	   (26)
230.	yā marak kiḷaviyum piṭāvun taḷāvum
	āmup peyaru melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yāmarak kiḷaviyum piṭāvum taḷāvum a muppeyarum - yā eṉṉum maratti uṇaraniṉṟa collum piṭā eṉṉum collum taḷā eṉṉum collumākiya am mūṉṟu peyarum, melleḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:	yāaṅkōṭu, piṭāaṅkōṭu, taḷāaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
melleḻuttuppēṟu varumoḻit toḻilātaliṉ, varumoḻi valleḻutti vilakkiṟṟu.	  (27)
231.	valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttum ciṟupāṉmi mikku muṭiyum eṉa iṟantatu kāttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi - mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarum melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illi.
u-m: yāakkōṭu, piṭāakkōṭu, taḷāakkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘māṉamilli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, yā mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
u-m: yāaviṉkōṭu, piṭāaviṉkōṭu, taḷāaviṉ kōṭu eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, yāattukkōṭu eṉac ciṟupāṉmi attuppēṟuṇṭēṉum koḷka. av vakarap pēṟṟōṭu valleḻuttup peṟutaliṉ. ‘yāmarak kiḷavi’ eṉpataṉik ‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum’ eṉpataṉpiṉ viyāta itaṉāṉ, irāviṟ koṇṭāṉ, nilāviṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. nilāviṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉpataṟku nilāattuk koṇ ṭāṉ eṉpatu īṟṟup potu muṭipāyiṉa vāṟu aṟika.	 (28)
232.	māmarak kiḷaviyu māvu māvum
	āmup peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa
	akaram valleḻut taviyava ṇiliyā
	ṉakara moṟṟu māvu māvum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) māmarakkiḷaviyum āvum māvum a muppeyarum avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - māmaramākiya collum ā eṉṉum collum mā eṉṉum collumākiya am mūṉṟu peyarccollum mēṟkūṟiya (230) yā mutaliya mūṉṟaṉoṭum oru taṉmiyavāy valleḻuttup peṟātu melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitalumuṭiya, āvum māvum akaram valleḻuttu avi avaṇ niliyā ṉakaram oṟṟum - avaṟṟuḷ āvum māvum muṉpeṟṟu niṉṟa akaramum (227) valleḻuttumākiya avi avviṭattu nilipeṟāvāy ṉakaram oṟṟākap peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: māaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; āṉkōṭu, māṉkōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘aṟiya’ (uyir. 24) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ciṟupāṉmi māṅkōṭu eṉa akaramiṉṟiyum varum. iṉi ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ kāyāṅkōṭu, āṇāṅkōṭu, nuṇāṅkōṭu eṉṟāṟpōlap piṟavum melleḻuttup peṟutalum; aṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, iṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, uṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, eṅkāk koṇṭāṉ eṉa ivvīṟṟu ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ valleḻuttup peṟutalum; āviṉkōṭu, māviṉkōṭu eṉac ciṟupāṉmi iṉpeṟutalum, peṟṟavaḻi valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka. ‘avaṟṟōraṉṉa’ eṉṟa māṭṭēṟṟāṉ peṟṟuniṉṟatu mel leḻuttākaliṉ, ‘akaram valleḻut taviyava ṇiliyā’ eṉṟu ōtaṟ pālataṉṟeṉiṉ, mēl “ūveṉ ṉorupeya rāvoṭu civaṇum” (uyirmayaṅkiyal 67) eṉṟavaḻi, am māṭṭēṟṟāṉē ataṉ valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷal vēṇṭit ‘tirinta taṉṟiripatu’ eṉṉum nayattāṉē mel leḻutti valleḻuttāka ōtiṉāreṉak koḷka.	(29)
233.	āṉoṟ ṟakaramoṭu niliyiṭa ṉuṭittē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ ā eṉṟataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉ oṟṟu akaramoṭum niliiṭaṉ uṭittu - muṉpu peṟṟuniṉṟa āṉ eṉṉum colliṉatu ṉakaravoṟṟu akarattoṭum niṟkum iṭaṉuṭittu.
‘iṭaṉuṭittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇattu im muṭipeṉak koḷka.
u-m: ‘āṉaney teḷittu nāṉa nīvi’ eṉa varum.
‘akaramoṭum’ eṉṟa ummiyāṉ, akaramiṉṟi varutalē perumpāṉmi eṉak koḷka.	 (30)
 234.	āṉmuṉ varūu mīkāra pakaram
	tāṉmikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalu murittē.
iḵtu, iṉṉum āṉ eṉpataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal  nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉmuṉ varum īkārap pakaram - āṉ eṉṉum colmuṉṉar varumoḻiyāy varukiṉṟa īkārattoṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalum urittu - ap pakaramākiya tāṉ mikat tōṉṟi av vīkāram ikaramākak kuṟuki muṭitaliyum uṭittu.
‘tōṉṟi’ eṉṟataṉāl, nilimoḻip pēṟākiya ṉakara voṟṟiṉ kēṭu koḷka.
u-m: āppi eṉa varum.
ummiyāṉ, āṉpī eṉpatē perumpāṉmiyeṉak koḷka.	 (31)
235.	kuṟiyata ṉiṟutic ciṉikeṭa vukaram
	aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉi keṭa ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ākārattiṉatu ciṉiyākiya akāram māttiri keṭa āṇṭu ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu.
u-m:	 “iṟavup puṟattaṉṉa piṇarppaṭu taṭavumutal” (naṟ. 19) eṉa varum.
‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukarampeṟātu ciṉikeṭutalum koḷka. aravaṇi koṭi eṉa varum.
‘piṇavunāy muṭukkiya’ eṉṟāṟpōla varum alvaḻimuṭipu ‘kiḷanta valla’ (kuṟṟiyalukara puṇariyal - 77) eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭiya teṉak koḷka.	  (32)
236.	ikara viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu toki marapiṉuṭ kūṟi  niṉṟamiyiṉ ataṉ vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ikara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - ikaravīṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar atikārattāṟ ka ca ta pa mutal moḻi vantavaḻi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: kiḷikkāl; ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉa varum. 	(33)
237.	iṉiyaṇi yeṉṉuṅ kāliyu miṭaṉum
	viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyuñ cuṭṭu maṉṉa.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟuḷ cila iṭiccollum viṉic collum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉi aṇi eṉṉum kāliyum iṭaṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum cuṭṭum aṉṉa - iṉi eṉṟum aṇi eṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟa kālattiyum iṭattiyum uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccollum, iv vikaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya collum, ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭākiya iṭiccollum, mēṟkūṟiya vāṟē valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum taṉmiya.
u-m: iṉikkoṇṭāṉ, aṇikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; tēṭikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; ikkoṟṟaṉ; cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉavum varum.	(34)
238.	iṉṟi yeṉṉum viṉiyeñ ciṟuti
	niṉṟa vikara mukara mātal
	toṉṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṟ ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu viṉiyeccak kuṟippiṉuḷ oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu iṟuti niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal - iṉṟi eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiyeccattu iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ikaram ukaramāyt tirintu muṭital, toṉṟu iyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - paḻaka naṭanta kūṟṟiyuṭiya ceyyuḷkaḷiṭattu urittu.
u-m:	“uppiṉṟu puṟki yuṇkamā koṟki yōṉē” eṉa varum.
‘niṉṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṉ peṟṟuniṉṟa valleḻuttu vīḻkka. ‘toṉṟiyaṉ maruṅku’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aṉṟi eṉpatum ceyyuḷuḷ im muṭipiṟṟātal koḷka. ‘nāḷaṉṟu pōkip puḷḷiṭi taṭpa’ (puṟam - 124) eṉa varum.	  (35)
239.	cuṭṭi ṉiyaṟki muṟkiḷan taṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar iyalpukaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟki muṉkiḷanta aṟṟu - ikara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉatu iyalpu iyalpukkaṇam varumvaḻiyum uyirkkaṇam varum vaḻiyum muṉ akaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṟkuc collappaṭṭa taṉmittām.
eṉṟatu meṉkaṇam varumvaḻi am melleḻuttu mikkum (uyirmayaṅkiyal 3), iṭikkaṇam varumvaḻiyum uyirkkaṇam varum vaḻiyum nilimoḻi vakaram peṟṟum (uyirmayaṅkiyal 4,5), ceyyuṭ kaṇ vakaramkeṭṭuc cuṭṭunīṇṭum (uyirmayaṅkiyal - 6) muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
u-m:	iññāṉam, innūl, immaṇi eṉavum; ivyāḻ, ivvaṭṭu eṉavum; ivvaṭi, ivvāṭi, ivveḷaviyam eṉavum; īvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum.	(36)
240.	patakkumuṉ variṉē tūṇik kiḷavi
	mutaṟkiḷan teṭutta vēṟṟumi yiyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷil aḷavuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku mēl tokimarapiṉuḷ (cūt. 22) eytiya ē eṉ cāriyi vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) patakku muṉ variṉ tūṇikkiḷavi mutal kiḷantu eṭutta vēṟṟumi iyaṟṟu - patakku eṉṉum col taṉmuṉ variṉ tūṇi eṉṉum col muṉpu  vitanteṭutta vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: tūṇippatakku eṉa varum.
