varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, irutūṇippatakku eṉa aṭiyaṭuttu vantavaḻiyum ivviti koḷka. ‘kiḷanteṭutta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tūṇimuṉṉarp piṟa poruṭ peyar vantavaḻiyum, āṇṭu nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttu vantavaḻiyum, taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantavaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. iṉṉum, ataṉāṉē, taṉmuṉṉart tāṉ vantavaḻi ikkuc cāriyippēṟum koḷka.
u-m: tūṇikkoḷ, tūṇiccāmi eṉavum; iru tūṇikkoḷ eṉavum; tūṇittūṇi eṉavum; tūṇikkut tūṇi eṉavum varum.	   (37)
241.	urivaru kāli nāḻik kiḷavi
	iṟuti yikara meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	ṭakāra moṟṟu māvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uri varu kāli nāḻikkiḷavi iṟuti ikaram mey yoṭum keṭum - uri eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy vanta kālattu nāḻi eṉṉum col taṉ īṟṟil niṉṟa ikaram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa mey yoṭum keṭum, a vayiṉ ṭakāram oṟṟum - avviṭattu ṭakaram oṟṟāy varum.
 u-m: nāṭuri eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, nilimoḻi aṭi yaṭuttu ‘iru nāṭuri’ eṉṟa vaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. ‘ikaram’ eṉṉātu ‘iṟuyikaram’ eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭi nili moḻiyum varumoḻiyum nilimoḻikaḷāy niṉṟu piṟa poruṭ peyaroṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum koḷka. 
nāḻikkāyam, urikkāyam eṉa varum.	 (38)
242.	paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumik
	kattu miṉṉuñ cāriyi yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumiyuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻutti ṉoṭu cāriyi peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉi eṉa varum kāla vēṟṟumikku attum iṉṉum cāriyi ākum - paṉi eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa paṉikkālatti uṇara niṉṟa vēṟṟumi muṭipuṭiya peyarkku varum cāriyi attum iṉṉum ākum.
u-m: paṉiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘vēṟṟumi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻutti vīḻkka.	(39)
243.	vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum
	avviya ṉiliyal cevvi teṉpa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) vaḷi eṉavarum pūtak kiḷaviyum a iyal niliyal cevvitu eṉpa - vaḷi eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa iṭakkarallāta aimperum pūtaṅkaḷil oṉṟi uṇara niṉṟa collum mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟum av viyalpiṉkaṇ niṟṟal cevviteṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: vaḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, vaḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘cevvitu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	 (40)
244.	utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyar oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum - uti eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa col valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:  utiṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. 	(41)
245.	puḷimarak kiḷavik kammē cāriyi.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyar oṉṟaṟku mēl eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷimarak kiḷavikku cāriyi am - puḷi eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa colliṟku varum cāriyi ammuc cāriyi.
u-m: puḷiyaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
cāriyippēṟṟiṭi eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ammup peṟṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu (236) vīḻkka.       (42)
246.	ēṉip puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, am marappeyarallāta puḷippeyarkku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikum - am marap peyaraṉṟi oḻinta cuvippuḷi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: puḷiṅkūḻ; cōṟu, tayir, pāḷitam eṉa varum.	(43)
247.	valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli
	olvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, mēlataṟku valleḻuttu mikumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi - ēṉip puḷippeyarmuṉ eytiya melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illi. olvaḻi aṟital vaḻakkattāṉa - poruntum iṭamaṟika vaḻakkiṭattu.
u-m: puḷikkūḻ; cōṟu, tayir, pāḷitam eṉa varum.
‘olvaḻi yaṟital’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, puḷiccōṟu eṉṟatupōla maṟṟiyavi vaḻakkup payiṟci ilaveṉpatum koḷka. ‘vaḻakkat tāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟuḷ eṭuttōttum ilēcum illātaṉavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumiyellām koḷka. kūtāḷaṅkōṭu eṉavum, kaṇaviraṅkōṭu eṉavum, tuḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, paruttikkuc ceṉṟāṉ eṉavum, kappi tanti kappinti eṉavum, kaṭṭi akal kaṭṭakal eṉavum, kuḷikuṟumi kuḷikkuṟumi eṉavum, iṉṉiṉik koṇṭāṉ aṇṇaṇik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ‘puḷiṅkāy’  (aiṅkuṟu. - 51) eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, ivvīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻtalum koḷka. kiḷiyiṉkāl eṉa varum.	 (44)
248.	nāṇmuṟ ṟōṉṟun toḻiṉilik kiḷavik
	kāṉiṭi varuta liya miṉṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc cāriyi vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷ muṉ tōṉṟum toḻilnilik kiḷavikku - ikaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum viṉic colliṟku, āṉiṭi varutal aiyam iṉṟu - āṉcāriyi iṭi vantu muṭital aiyam illi.
u-m: paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
`aiya miṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.			(45)
249.	tiṅkaṇ muṉvari ṉikkē cāriyi.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷmuṉ variṉ cāriyi ikku - tiṅkaḷi uṇara niṉṟa ikaravīṟṟup peyarmuṉṉart toḻilnilik kiḷavi variṉ varum cāriyi ikkuccāriyi.
u-m āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	(46)
250.	īkāra viṟuti yākāra viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, īkāra vīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻiyiṉkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - īkāravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāravīṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu varumvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: tīkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum. 	(47)
251.	nīyeṉ peyaru miṭakkarp peyarum
	mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvari kiḷaviyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ peyarum iṭakkarp peyarum mī eṉa marīiya iṭamvari kiḷaviyum - nī eṉṉum peyarum iṭakkarp peyarākiya (pī eṉṉum) īkāravīṟṟup peyarum mī eṉṟu colla varūum maruvāy vaḻaṅkiṉa, ōr iṭattiṉi varintuṇarttum collum, a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - mēl iv vīṟṟuṭ kūṟiya valleḻuttup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	nī kuṟiyi, ciṟiyi, tīyi, periyi eṉavum;  pī kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; mīkaṇ, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘nī kuṟiyi’ eṉpatu mēl “aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumāruḷavē” (toki. 13) eṉṟavaḻi aṭaṅkātō veṉiṉ, mēl (254) vēṟṟumikkaṇ niṉki eṉat tirintu muṭitaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟ ṟeṉka. mīkaṇ eṉpatu alvaḻi muṭipaṉ ṟeṉiṉum iyalpātal nōkki uṭaṉkūṟappaṭṭatu. (48)
252.	iṭamvari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum
	uṭaṉili moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ mī eṉpataṟku vēṟoru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭamvari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum uṭaṉ nilimoḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa - iṭattiṉi varintuṇarttum mī eṉṉum colmuṉ iyalpāy muṭitalēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum tammil iyintu niṟṟaliyuṭiya moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: mīkkōḷ,  mīppal eṉa varum.
‘uṭaṉili’ eṉṟataṉāl, mīṅkuḻi, mīntōl eṉa mel leḻuttup peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka. 	   (49)
253.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - īkāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra vīṟṟu  alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: īkkāl, ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	 (50)
254.	nīye ṉorupeya rurupiya ṉiliyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar urupu iyal niliyum - nī eṉa niṉṟa orupeyar urupupuṇarccikkaṇ neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakara voṟṟup peṟṟu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum, a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - avvāṟu muṭintaviṭattu iyipu valleḻuttu mikātu.
u-m: niṉki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	          (51)
255.	ukara viṟuti yakara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ukaravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ukara vīṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ akarīṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: kaṭukkuṟitu;  ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.     	(52)
256.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉaru mattoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuc cuṭṭu vaṉkaṇattoṭu kūṭi muṭiyu māṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum a toḻiṟṟu ākum - ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varumvaḻi av akaravīṟṟu al vaḻiyiṉ toḻiṟṟāy (205) valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:  ukkoṟṟaṉ; cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉa varum.  (53)
257.	ēṉavi variṉē mēṉili yiyalpē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuc cuṭṭu oḻinta kaṇattoṭu muṭiyu māṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉavi variṉ mēl nili iyalpu - ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ muṉ oḻinta kaṇam varumoḻiyāka variṉ mēl akaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭu muṭintu niṉṟa nilimiyiṉ iyalpi yuṭiyatāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	uññāṇ, unnūl, ummaṇi eṉavum; uvyāḻ, uv vaṭṭu eṉavum; uvvaṭi, uvvāṭi, uvveḷaviyam eṉavum; ūvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum.	(54)
258.	cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yiyalpā kummē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti iyalpākum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara vīṟṟuppeyar mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: atukuṟitu, itukuṟitu, utukuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	 (55)
259.	aṉṟuvaru kāli yāvā kutalum
	aivaru kāli meyvarintu keṭutalum
	ceyyuṇ maruṅki ṉuritteṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuc cuṭṭumutaṟ peyarkku oru ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṟu varukāli ā ākutalum - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa cuṭṭumutal ukaravīṟṟup peyar aṉṟu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu av vukaram ākāramākit tirintu muṭitalum, ai varukāli mey varintu keṭutalum - ai eṉṉum cāriyi iṭivantu muṭiyuṅ kālattu avvukaram tāṉ ūrnta meyyi oḻittuk keṭṭu muṭitalum, ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ urittu eṉa moḻipa - av viru muṭipum ceyyuṭkaṇ uritteṉṟu colvar.
u-m: atāaṉṟamma, itāaṉṟamma, utāaṉṟamma eṉavum; atimaṟṟamma, itimaṟṟamma, utimaṟṟamma eṉavum varum.	(56)
260.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ukaravīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum av vakara vīṟṟu (204) alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: kaṭukkāy; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. 	(57)
261.	eruvuñ ceruvu mammoṭu civaṇit
	tiripiṭa ṉuṭiya teriyuṅ kāli
	ammiṉ makarañ ceruvayiṟ keṭumē
	tammoṟṟu mikūum valleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vitiyum, oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyi vitiyum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eruvum ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇi tiripu iṭaṉ uṭiya teriyum kāli - eru eṉṉum collum ceru eṉṉum collum ammuccāriyiyoṭu porunti muṉ coṉṉa vēṟṟumip potu vitiyiṉ vēṟupaṭṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭiya ārāyuṅ kālattu; ammiṉ makaram ceruvayiṉ keṭum - av vammuc cāriyiyatu īṟṟiṉ makaram ceru eṉṉum colliṭattuk keṭṭu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu iyaṟki tam oṟṟu mikūum - avvāṟu keṭṭaviṭattuc ceru eṉpatu valleḻuttākiya iyalpiyuṭiya taṉatu oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: eruvaṅkuḻi; cēṟu, tātu, pūḻi eṉavum; ceruvakkaḷam, cēṉi, tāṉi, paṟi eṉavum varum.
‘teriyuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, eru eṉpataṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi melleḻuttuppēṟum, valleḻuttuppēṟum, ceru eṉpataṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi valleḻuttuppēṟum koḷka. eruṅkuḻi, erukkuḻi, cerukkaḷam eṉa varum.
‘valleḻuttiyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi av virumoḻikkum atikāra valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka. eruviṉ kaṭumi, ceruviṉ kaṭumi eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, “ammoṭu civaṇit tiripiṭa ṉuṭiya” eṉa valleḻuttiṉkaṇṇatāka varintu kūṟiṉami yiṉ, ammuc cāriyi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ peṟuvaṉa koḷka. eruva ñāṟci, ceruva ñāṟci eṉa varum. 	          (58)
262.	ḻakara vukara nīṭiṭa ṉuṭittē
	ukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷuḷ eytiyataṉ mēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ḻakara ukaram nīṭu iṭaṉ uṭittu - iv vīṟṟu moḻikaḷuḷ ḻakarattoṭu kūṭiya ukaravīṟṟu moḻi av vukaram ūkāramāy nīṇṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉ uṭittu; a vayiṉ ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram vantu muṭika.
‘iṭaṉuṭittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, itu ceyyuḷiṭatteṉak koḷka.
u-m: “paḻūuppal laṉṉa paruvukirp pāvaṭi” eṉa varum.			 (59)
263.	oṭumarak kiḷavi yutimara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oṭumarak kiḷavi utimara iyaṟṟu - oṭu eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa col uti eṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy  melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: oṭuṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. 	  (60)
264.	cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yurupiya ṉiliyum
	oṟṟiṭi mikāa valleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vakutl nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti urupu iyal niliyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ urupu puṇarcciyiṟ coṉṉa iyalpilē  niṉṟu aṉcāriyi peṟṟu ukaram keṭṭu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu iyaṟki oṟṟu iṭimikā - avviṭattu valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikā.
u-m: ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘oṟṟu iṭi mikā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, cāriyi vakuppa valleḻuttu mikāteṉpatu peṟṟām. ‘valleḻut tiyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kaṭuviṉkuṟi, oṭuviṉpuṟam eṉa varum.	 (61)
265.	ūkāra viṟuti yākāra viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ūkāravīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - ūkāravīṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ ākāravīṟṟu  alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: koṇmūkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.(62)
266.	viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikku muṉṉili moḻikkum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli yavvaki variyār.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu viṉiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikkum muṉṉili moḻikkum - ūkāravīṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya colliṟkum, muṉṉili moḻi yākiya viṉiccolliṟkum, niṉiyumkāli avvaki variyār - ārāyuṅ kālattu av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum kūṟṟiṉi nīkkār e-ṟu.
u-m: uṇṇūk koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; kitūk koṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum varum.
‘niṉiyuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyar alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka. āṭūukkuṟiyaṉ, makaṭūukkuṟiyaṉ eṉa varum.	 (63)
267.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, ūkāravīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ūkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum av vākāra vīṟṟu al vaḻiyōṭu otta iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: koṇmūkkuḻām; celavu, tōṟṟam, paṟivu eṉa varum.	 (64)
268.	kuṟṟeḻut timparu mōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	niṟṟal vēṇṭu mukarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum ōr eḻuttu moḻikkum ukarak kiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉpiṉ niṉṟa ūkāravīṟṟu moḻikkum ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya ūkāra vīṟṟu moḻikkum ukaramākiya eḻuttu niṟṟali vēṇṭum.
u-m:	uṭūukkuṟi, tūukkuṟi; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
‘niṟṟal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇippeyar vēṟṟumik kaṇ valleḻuttu mikuvaṉavum koḷka.  āṭū ukki, makaṭūukki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.  	(65)
269.	pūve ṉorupeya rāyiyal piṉṟē
	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakkip perumpāṉmi melleḻuttum ciṟupāṉmi valleḻuttum peṟumeṉa eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pū eṉ oru peyar a iyalpu iṉṟu - pū eṉṉum ūkāra vīṟṟiyuṭiya oru peyar mēṟcoṉṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum anta iyalpillāmiyi uṭittu; eṉavē, vēṟu ōr iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
a vayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - avviṭattu ām melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu.
melleḻuttup peṟumeṉṟatu, uriyiṟ kōṭalāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
u-m: pūṅkoṭi, pūkkoṭi; ceyki, tāmam, pantu eṉa varum.	  (66)
270.	ūve ṉorupeya rāvoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, iṉṉum av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki nilimoḻi ṉakaram peṟumeṉa eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ū eṉ oru peyar āvoṭu civaṇum - ū eṉṟu collappaṭum ūkāravīṟi yuṭiya oru peyar ākāra vīṟṟiṉ ā eṉṉum colloṭu porunti, ukaramum  valleḻuttum   peṟātu nilimoḻi ṉakara voṟṟup peṟṟu  muṭiyum.
u-m: ūṉkuṟi; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.  	 (67)

71.	akkeṉ cāriyi peṟutalu murittē
	takkavaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, iṉṉum ataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akku eṉ cāriyi peṟutalum urittu - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa ū eṉṉum peyar mēṟkūṟiya ṉakarattōṭu akku eṉṉum cāriyi peṟṟu muṭitalum urittu; vaḻakkattāṉ  takkavaḻi aṟital - vaḻakkiṭattu am muṭipu takka iṭam aṟika.
‘takkavaḻi yaṟital’ eṉṟataṉāl, cāriyi peṟṟavaḻi nilimoḻi ṉakaram vilakkuṇṇātu niṟṟalum, muṉ (270) māṭṭēṟṟāṉ vilakkuṇṭa valleḻuttuk keṭātu niṟṟalum koḷka.
u-m: ūṉakkuṟi; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. koṇmūviṉ kuḻām eṉa varum.	(68)
272.	āṭūu makaṭūu vāyiru peyarkkum
	iṉṉiṭi variṉu māṉa milli.
iḵtu av vīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkku muṉ eytiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi, cāriyiyum peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṭūu makaṭūu a iru peyarkkum - āṭūu makaṭūu vākiya avviraṇṭu peyarkkum, iṉ iṭivariṉum māṉam illi - mēl eytiya (“kuṟṟeḻut timparum’ uyirmayaṅkiyal - 65) eṉṉum cūttirattiṉ ‘niṟṟal’ eṉpataṉāṉ vanta valleḻuttēyaṉṟi iṉcāriyi iṭivariṉum kuṟṟam illi.
u-m:  āṭūuviṉki, makaṭūuviṉki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
‘māṉa milli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟavaḻi mēl ilēciṉā ṉeytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	  (69)
273.	ekara vokaram peyarkkī ṟākā
	muṉṉili moḻiya veṉmaṉār pulavar
	tēṟṟamuñ ciṟappu malvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu, ekaravīṟṟiṟkum okaravīṟṟiṟkum īṟākāta niliyil vēṟupāṭu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ekaram okaram peyarkku īṟu ākā - ekaramum okaramum peyarccoṟku īṟu ākā, muṉṉili moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - viṉiccolluḷ muṉṉili moḻiyiṭat taṉaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar, tēṟṟamum ciṟappum alvaḻiyāṉa - tēṟṟap poruṇmiyiṉ varum iṭiccol ekāra vīṟum ciṟappup poruṇmiyiṉ varum iṭiccol okāra vīṟum allātaviṭattu.
u-m:	ēe eṉavum, ōo eṉavum varum. ivi muṉṉili viṉi. ēe koṇṭāṉ, ōo koṇṭāṉ. ivi iṭic col.	  (70)
274.	tēṟṟa vekaramuñ ciṟappi ṉovvum
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki valleḻut tumikā.
itu, muṉ īṟām eṉṉappaṭṭa ekara okara īṟṟu iṭiccoṟkum, av vīṟṟu muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkum muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ ovvum - tēṟṟap poruṇ miyil varum ekaravīṟṟu iṭiccollum ciṟappup poruṇmiyil varum okaravīṟṟu iṭiccollum, mēl kūṟu iyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā - mēli muṉṉili viṉic coṟkuk kūṟappaṭum iyalpuṭiya valleḻuttu mikāvāy iyalpāy muṭiyum.
‘mēṟkūṟiyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘vantatu (muṉṉili viṉi) koṇṭu vārātatu uṇarka’ eṉṉum tantiravutti vakiyāṉ valleḻuttu mikumeṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭatāyiṟṟu.
u-m: yāṉēe koṇṭēṉ, nīyēe koṇṭāy, avaṉēe koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; ōokoṇṭēṉ, ōo koṇ ṭāy, ōokoṇṭāṉ eṉavum varum. ivi iṭiccol. ēekkoṟṟā, ōokkoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉa ivi muṉṉiliviṉi.
‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, am muṉṉili viṉikaḷi aḷapeṭiyāka niṟīik koḷka.	 (71)
275.	ēkāra viṟuti yūkāra viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ēkāravīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēkāra iṟuti ūkāra iyaṟṟu - ēkāravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ ūkāravīṟṟu alvaḻi iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: cēkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	 (72)
276.	māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu meṇṇum
	kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum eṇṇum - māṟupāṭu kōṭaliyuṭiya eccap poruṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭiccollum, viṉāpporuṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭiccollum, eṇṇup poruṇmik kaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭic collum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: yāṉē koṇṭēṉ, ceṉṟēṉ, tantēṉ, pōyiṉēṉ eṉavum; nīyē koṇṭāy, ceṉṟāy, tantāy, pōyiṉāy eṉavum; koṟṟaṉē cāttuṉē tēvaṉē pūtaṉē eṉavum varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pirinilip poruṇmikkaṇṇum, īṟṟacikkaṇṇum varum ēkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpu muṭipum koḷka.
avaṉē koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu - pirinili. ‘kaṭalē pāṭeḻun tolikkum’  eṉpatu - īṟṟaci.          (73)
277.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ēkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum avvūkāra vīṟṟu (265) alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:  vēkkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉa varum.      (74)
278.	ēye ṉiṟutik kekaram varumē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ē eṉ iṟutikku ekaram varum - av vēṟṟumikkaṇ ē eṉṉum iṟutikku ekaram varum.
u-m:	ēekkoṭṭil; cāli, tuḷi, puḻi eṉa varum.
‘uriyiṟkōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, av vekarappēṟu poruntiṉa vaḻik koḷka.	 (75)
279.	cēveṉ marappeya roṭumara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki mel leḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cē eṉ marappeyar oṭu mara iyaṟṟu - cē eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar oṭumarattiṉ (263) iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:	cēṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.     (76)
280.	peṟṟa māyiṉ muṟṟaviṉ vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, am marappeyarallāta cē eṉpataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peṟṟam āyiṉ muṟṟa iṉ vēṇṭum - mēṟkūṟiya cē eṉpatu peṟṟattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa poḻutāyiṉ muṭiya iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiya vēṇṭum.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ic cē eṉpatu eṭuttōttāṉ iṉ peṟṟavaḻiyum, atuvē marappeyarāy urupiṟku eytiya cāriyi (iṉ) peṟṟavaḻiyum, piṟacol avvāṟu iṉ peṟṟavaḻiyum iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
u-m: 	cēviṉkōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; cēviṉ kōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum;  ēviṉ kaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum varum.	 (77)
281.	aikāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, aikārīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu tokimarapiṉuṭ 
(e. 159) kūṟi niṉṟamiyiṉ, ataṉ vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) aikāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar - aikāra īṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi āyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - atikārattāṟ ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi vantavaḻi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyil tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: yāṉikkōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.     (78)
282.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal niliyum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cuṭṭu mutaṟpeyarkku valleḻut toṭu vaṟṟu vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti urupu iyal niliyum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāravīṟṟuppeyar urupu puṇarcciyiṟ (178) kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aikāram keṭātum keṭṭum vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: aviyaṟṟukkōṭu, iviyaṟṟukkōṭu, uvi yaṟṟuk kōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; avaṟṟuk kōṭu, ivaṟṟukkōṭu, uvaṟṟukkōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.	 (79)
283.	vicimarak kiḷaviyum ñemiyum namiyum
	avimup peyaruñ cēmara iyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ marappeyar cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vicimarakkiḷaviyum ñemiyum namiyum avi muppeyarum - vici eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum ñemi eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum nami eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum ākiya am mūṉṟu peyarum, cēmara iyala - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu cē eṉṉum marattiṉatu iyalpiṉavāy (279) melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: viciṅkōṭu, ñemiṅkōṭu, namiṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(80)
284.	paṉiyu mariyu māvirik kiḷaviyum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli yammoṭu civaṇum
	aiye ṉiṟuti yarivarintu keṭumē
	meyyava ṇoḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyum ariyum āvirik kiḷaviyum - paṉi eṉṉum collum ari eṉṉum collum āviri eṉṉum collum, niṉiyuṅkāli ammoṭu civaṇum - ārāyuṅ kālattu mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu, am eṉṉum cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum, ai eṉ iṟuti ari varintu keṭum - avviṭattu ai eṉṉum īṟu ari eṉṉum colli nīkkik (ēṉiyiraṇṭilum) keṭum, mey avaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - taṉṉāṉūrappaṭṭa mey ac colliṭattē oḻiyaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: paṉaṅkāy, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; ariyaṅ kōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; āviraṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum varum.
‘niṉiyuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ piṟavum tūtuṇi, vaḻutuṇi, tilli, ōli eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum ammuk koṭuttu aikāram keṭuttut tūtuṇaṅkāy, vaḻutuṇaṅkāy, tillaṅkāy, ōlampōḻ eṉṟu muṭikka.	     (81)
285.	paṉiyiṉ muṉṉa raṭṭuvaru kāli
	niliyiṉ ṟāku miye ṉuyirē
	ākāram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭu varukāli - mēṟkūṟiya vaḻiyēyaṉṟip paṉi eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉar aṭṭu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nili iṉṟākum ai eṉ uyir - niṟṟal iṉṟām ai eṉṉum uyir; a vayiṉāṉa ākāram varutal - avviṭattu ākāram vantu am meymēl ēṟi muṭika.
u-m: paṉāaṭṭu eṉa varum.
‘āvayiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, viccāvāti eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vaṟṟatu vēṟṟumi muṭipu koḷka.	(82)
286.	koṭimuṉ variṉē piyava ṇiṟpak
	kaṭinili yiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) koṭimuṉ variṉ - koṭi eṉṉum col paṉi eṉṉum colmuṉṉar variṉ, ai avaṇ niṟpa valleḻuttu mikuti kaṭinili iṉṟu mēṟkeṭum eṉappaṭṭa aikāram āṇṭuk keṭātē niṟpa, valleḻuttu mikuti nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu.
u-m: paṉikkoṭi eṉa varum.
‘kaṭinili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟuḷ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēciṉāṉum ammuccāriyiyum piṟa cāriyiyum peṟṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkuc ceṉṟavaḻiyum iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. u-m: paṉiyiṉ kāy, ariyiṉkōṭu, āviriyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum; viciyiṉkōṭu, ñemiyiṉ kōṭu, namiyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum; tūtuṇiyiṉkāy, vaḻutuṇiyiṉkāy eṉavum; vaḻiyiṉ kōṭu, vaḻiyiṉ pū eṉavum varum. ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṉittiraḷ eṉa valleḻuttup pēṟum paṉintiraḷ eṉṟa melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka.	 (83)

287.	tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa.
iḵtu, iyipu valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyippēṟum, valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyippēṟum kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu vitiyum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu vitiyum vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷum nāḷum muntu kiḷantaṉṉa - aikāra vīṟṟut tiṅkaḷi uṇaraniṉṟa peyarum av vīṟṟu nāḷi uṇara niṉṟa peyarum muṉ ikara īṟṟut tiṅkaḷum  nāḷum kiḷanta taṉmiyavāy ikkum āṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: cittirikkuk koṇṭāṉ; kēṭṭiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
nāḷ muṉkūṟātu tiṅkaḷ muṉkūṟiyavataṉāl, kariyavaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉavum; aviyattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; vaḻiṅ kōṭu, vaḻik kōṭu eṉavum; kaliṅkōṭu, kalikkōṭu eṉavum ivvīṟṟu muṭiyā taṉavellām koḷka.	(84)
288.	maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu, iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu attuppēṟum, valleḻuttu vilakki iṉṉum vakukkiṉṟamiyiṉ, eytiya taṉmēṟ ciṟappum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituvitiyum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maḻi eṉ kiḷavi vaḷi iyal niliyum - maḻi eṉṉum aikāravīṟṟuccol, ikaravīṟṟu vaḷi eṉṉum col (243) attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: maḻiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, maḻiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	    (85)
289.	ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki yeṉṉum
	aiye ṉiṟuti yavāmuṉ variṉē
	meyyoṭuṅ keṭuta leṉmaṉār pulavar
	ṭakāra ṇakāra mātal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇ ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki eṉṉum ai eṉ iṟuti - ceyyuḷiṭattu alvaḻikkaṇ vēṭki eṉṉum aikāra vīṟṟuccol, avā muṉvariṉ - avā eṉṉum col taṉakku muṉvariṉ, meyyoṭum keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - av vikāram tāṉ ūrnta meyyoṭuṅkūṭak keṭukaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar; ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu niṉṟa ṭakāravoṟṟu ṇakāra voṟṟāyt tirintu muṭital vēṇṭum.
u-m: “vēṇavā naliya veyya vuyirā” (naṟ. 61) eṉa varum. itaṉi ummittokiyākak koḷka. avāveṉpatu av vēṭkiyiṉ mikuti.
iv valvaḻiyi vēṟṟumi muṭipiṟku muṉ kūṟātataṉāṉ, viccāvāti eṉṟāṟpōla varum ummittoki alvaḻi muṭipum, pāṟaṅkal eṉa irupeyaroṭṭu alvaḻi muṭipum koḷka.	 (86)
290.	ōkāra viṟuti yēkāra viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ōkāravīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟu - ōkāravīṟṟu  alvaḻip peyarccol ēkāravīṟṟu (275) alvaḻi iyal piṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum.
u-m: ōkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum. 	(87)
291.	māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu miyamum
	kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu iṭiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum aiyamum - māṟu pāṭṭiṉik koṇṭa eccapporuṇmiyiṉiyuṭiya ōkāramum, viṉāp poruṇmiyi yuṭiya ōkāramum, aiyapporuṇmiyiṉiyuṭiya ōkāramum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - muṉ peyarkkuk kūṟiya valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum.

u-m: yāṉōkoṇṭēṉ eṉavum, nīyō koṇṭāy eṉavum, pattō patiṉoṉṟō eṉavum varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piriniliyum, teriniliyum, ciṟappum, eṇṇum, īṟṟaciyum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. avaṉōkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, naṉṟō tītōvaṉṟu eṉavum, ōo koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, “kuṉṟuṟaḻnta kaḷiṟeṉkō koyyuḷiya māveṉkō” (puṟam. 387) eṉavum ‘yāṉō tēṟēṉ’ (kuṟun. 21) eṉavum varum.	 (88)
292.	oḻintata ṉiliyu moḻintavaṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oḻintataṉ niliyum oḻintavaṟṟu iyaṟṟu - oḻiyici ōkārattiṉatu niliyum mēṟcolliyoḻinta ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: koḷalō koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(89)

293.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē
	okaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ōkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum av vōkāravīṟṟu (290) alvaḻiyō ṭottu valleḻuttup peṟṟup puṇarum; a vayiṉāṉa okaram varutal - avviṭattu okaram varuka. 
u-m: ōokkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉa varum.  (90)
294.	illoṭu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟki ākum - ōkāra vīṟṟuk kō eṉṉum moḻiyiṉi il eṉṉum varu moḻiyoṭu colliṉ ōkāram mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: kōyil eṉa varum.
nilimoḻi okaraveḻuttuppēṟu variyātu kūṟiṉavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum cellumeṉpatu itaṉāṟ peṟṟām.
kōveṉṟatu uyartiṇip peyaraṉṟōveṉiṉ, kōvantatu eṉa aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭitaliṉ aḵṟiṇippāṟ paṭṭatu pōlum.	(91)
295.	urupiya ṉiliyu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttamiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) urupuiyal niliyum moḻiyumār uḷa - av vīṟṟuṭ cila urupu puṇarcciyatu iyalpilē niṉṟu oṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki ākum - avviṭattu valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: kōoṉki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
itaṉāṉum peṟṟām, cāriyippēṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu vilakkāmi.	  (92)
296.	oḷakāra viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	alvaḻi yāṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	valleḻuttu mikutal varinili yiṉṟē
	avviru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal
	cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē.
iḵtu, oḷakāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oḷakāra iṟuti peyarnili muṉṉar alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu mikutal varinili iṉṟu - oḷakāravīṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi variṉ avi alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu mikku muṭital nīkkum niliyiṉṟām; a iru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal cevvitu eṉpa ciṟanticiṉōr - av virukūṟṟu muṭipiṉkaṇṇum nili moḻik kaṇ ukaram vantu muṭital cevviteṉṟu colluvar ciṟantōr.
u-m:	kuvukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; kuvuk kaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum varum.
‘cevvitu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum iṭik kaṇattukkaṇṇum iruvaḻiyum ukarappēṟu koḷka. u-m: kuvu ñāṉṟatu, kuvuñāṟci eṉavum; kuvu valitu, kuvu valimi eṉavum varum.
‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuviṉ kaṭumi eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvu koḷka.	 (93)
ēḻāvatu - uyirmayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.



puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal
meyyīṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum piṟakaṇattōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟuvatu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalākum. meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niṟkumātaliṉ puḷḷiyeṉṟār. meyyīṟṟuḷ ukarampeṟuvaṉa, iṟuti keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iṟutikeṭātu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, valleḻuttum melleḻuttum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉa, iyalpāy varuvaṉa, cāriyi peṟuvaṉa, tirintu muṭivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakiyiṉuḷ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷyāvum aṭaṅku vaṉavām.
ña, na, ṇa, ma, la, ḷa eṉṉum meykaḷi iṟutiyākavuṭiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar valleḻuntu mutaṉmoḻi variṉ iru vaḻiyum varumoḻi valleḻuttu mika nilimoḻiyīṟu ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyum. nakara vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukarampeṟātu akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum, īm, kam, urum, miṉ, piṉ, kaṉ, val, tev, puḷ, vaḷ eṉa eṭuttōtiya peyarkaḷum toḻiṟpeyarpōla iruvaḻiyum ukarampeṟṟu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉavām. avaṟṟuḷ kaṉ eṉṉuñcol vēṟṟumikkaṇ akaram peṟṟu valleḻuttu mikappeṟum. val eṉṉuñ colliṉmuṉ nāy, palaki eṉpaṉa varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avvaḻi ukaramiṉṟi akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum.
verin eṉṟa col, iṟuti nakaravoṟṟum accol peṟṟa akaramumkeṭa valleḻuttu varumvaḻi avvalleḻut tāyiṉum ataṉ kiḷiyeḻuttākiya melleḻuttāyiṉum mikku muṭiyum, makaravīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṟuti makaramkeṭa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. iṅṅaṉam makara vīṟukeṭṭa viṭattu varumoḻi valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum cila uḷa. āyiram eṉpataṉmuṉ aḷavuppeyar vantu puṇarumiṭattu vēṟṟumiyiṟpōla iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum. illam eṉṉum marappeyar iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu melleḻuttu mikum. aḻaṉ eṉpataṉ iṟutikeṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum.
vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇvarum yaraḻa vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu avval leḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa. tāy eṉṉuñcolmuṉ makaṉ viṉivariṉ valleḻuttu mikum. ār, vetir, cār, pīr, kumiḻ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ valleḻuttu varuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa. vēṟṟumikkaṇ yakara vīṟṟuṭ cilavum cār, pāḻ eṉpaṉavum valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttuppeṟṟu uṟaḻvaṉavām. kīḻ eṉṉuñcol valleḻuttuppeṟṟum peṟātum uṟaḻum.
āṇ, peṇ, umaṇ, muraṇ, kuyiṉ, ekiṉ, tāṉ, pēṉ, kōṉ eṉpaṉavum neṭṭaḻuttiṉ piṉvarum lakāra, ḷakāra vīṟṟuppeyarkaḷ cilavum tiriyātu iyalpāvaṉavām. tāy eṉṉum peyarum alvaḻikkaṇvarum yakāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāvaṉa. alvaḻik kaṇvarum ellārum, tām, nām, yām, tāṉ eṉpaṉa kuṟukalun tiritalumiṉṟi iyalpāvaṉa. nūṟāyiram, tāmari, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉpavaṟṟōṭum ēṉi uyir mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarum, uyir mutaṉ moḻikaḷōṭum yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum vakara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷum kuṟukalun tiritalumiṉṟi iyalpāvaṉavām.
āṇ, ekiṉ, pīr, pūl, vēl, āl, kumiḻ eṉpaṉa amcāriyiyum, vēṟṟumikkaṇvarum īm, kam eṉpaṉavum kōl eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum tāḻ eṉṉuñ collum, tamiḻ eṉṉum collum akkuccāriyiyum, veyil, iruḷ eṉpaṉa attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum, makaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyar ikara vīṟṟu nāṭpeyarpōla āṉcāriyiyum ataṉmēl attuc cāriyiyum, ceyyuḷiṭattuvarum viṇ eṉṉuñcol viṉic col varumoḻiyāka varumiṭattu attuccāriyiyum, taṉittum aṭiyaṭuttum varum āyiram eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar piṟa eṇkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu attuccāriyiyum peṟuvaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkippeyarum, ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilippeyarum, neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyumiyal puṭiya tām, nām, yām eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ tāṉ, yāṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum cāriyipeṟuvaṉa īṟukeṭṭu iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum cāriyipeṟṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukiyum, urupu puṇarccikkaṇ muṭintavāṟē vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyiṉum muṭiyumeṉa āciriyar māṭṭēṟṟu muṟiyāṉ vitikūṟiyuḷḷār. ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ iṭiyē vaṟṟuccāriyiyum iṟutiyil umcāriyiyum peṟum. appeyar alvaḻikkaṇ cāriyi peṟātu. uyartiṇikkaṇ iṭiyē nam cāriyiyum iṟutiyil umcāriyiyumpeṟum. vakara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar vaṟṟuccāriyipeṟum. ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṉcāriyi peṟum.
ṇakara ḷakara vīṟukaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl ṭakaramākavum, ṉakara lakara vīṟukaḷ ṟakaramākavum tirivaṉa. eḷḷi yuṇarttum eṇ eṉṉum peyariṉ ṇakaram alvaḻiyilum ṭakara mākattiriyum. nel, cel, kol, col eṉpavaṟṟiṉ lakaram alvaḻiyilum ṟakaramāyttiriyum. alvaḻikkaṇvarum ḷakaravīṟu tirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻntu muṭiyum. vēṟṟumiyiṟpōla alvaḻiyilum ṭakaramākattiriyum ḷakara vīṟukaḷ cilavuḷa.
maṉ, ciṉ, āṉ, īṉ, piṉ, muṉ eṉpaṉavum ceyiṉ eṉṉum viṉiyeccamum avvayiṉ, ivvayiṉ, uvvayiṉ evvayiṉ eṉavarum ēḻām vēṟṟumi yiṭapporuḷuṇarttum iṭic coṟkaḷum ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākattiriyum. mīṉ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākattirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻum.
lakara ḷakara vīṟṟuccoṟkaḷ takaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ āytamākattiriyum. melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl muṟiyē lakaram ṉakaramākavum ḷakaram ṇakaramākavum tirivaṉa.
makaravīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ valivariṉ ēṟṟamelleḻuttākat tiriyum. akam eṉṉuñ colliṉmuṉ ki eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ nilimoḻiyiṉ naṭuveḻuttākiya kakaravuyir meykeṭa melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. num eṉpataṉi alvaḻikkaṇ kūṟuṅkāl nakaravoṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa ukaram keṭa avvoṟṟiṉmēl īkāram ūrntu nī eṉṟāki ōr ikaram iṭiyilvara iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu rakara voṟṟut tōṉṟi nīyir eṉattiriyumeṉpar tolkāppiyar. eṉavē num eṉpatē tiripil colleṉṟum nīyir eṉpatu ataṉ tiripeṉṟuṅ koḷḷutalvēṇṭum.
tēṉ eṉṉuñcol, valivariṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻtalum, iṟuti ṉakaraṅkeṭṭu valimeli mikutalum, melivariṉ iṟutikeṭṭum keṭātum uṟaḻtalum peṟum. iṟāl eṉpatu varumoḻiyākavariṉ tēṉ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram keṭṭut takaram iraṭṭittu muṭiyum.
cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ mutaliya iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantiyeṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ, tanti yeṉpataṉ mutaliluḷḷa takaraṅkeṭṭu, ataṉmēlēṟiya akaraṅkeṭātu niṟpa, nilimoḻiyiyaṟpeyariluḷḷa aṉkeṭṭu, aṅku niṉṟa meyyiṉmēl varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa akaram ēṟimuṭiyum. muṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉṉum irupeyarkaḷum varumoḻiyākiya tantiyeṉṉum muṟippeyaroṭu puṇaruṅkāl ivviyaṟ peyarkaḷiṉ iṟutiniṉṟa aṉkeṭa eñciya takaravoṟṟum, tanti eṉpatil muṉkūṟiyapaṭi takaraṅkeṭa eñciya akaravuyirum cērakkeṭṭu, ānti, pūnti eṉa muṭivaṉavām. ippeyarkaḷ ciṟappup paṇpaṭuttuvaruṅkāl aṅṅaṉantiritaliṉṟi iyalpāvaṉa eṉpar. mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ iṉṉāṟku makaṉ iṉṉāṉ eṉṉum muṟiyil oṭṭi niṟkuṅkāl nilimoḻip peyarīṟṟiṉ aṉkeṭṭu amcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartalumuṇṭu.
muṉ eṉṉum colmuṉ il eṉṉuñcol vantu puṇariṉ iṭiyē ṟakaravoṟṟut tōṉṟi muṉṟil eṉamuṭital toṉṟu toṭṭu maruvivaḻaṅkum ilakkaṇa muṭipām. poṉ eṉṉuñcol, īṟṟil ṉakaramkeṭa lakaravuyirmeyyum makara meyyumpeṟṟup polam eṉat tirintuvaḻaṅkum. il eṉṉum col, vīṭṭi yuṇarttātu illāmiyeṉṉum poruḷi yuṇarttuṅkāl, aikāram peṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum, mikāmiyum, iyalpātalum, ākārampeṟṟu valleḻuttu mikutalum ākiya nāṉkumuṭipiṉi yuṭiyatākum.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 181-185

eṭṭāvatu
puḷḷimayaṅkiyal
ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, puḷḷiyīṟu vaṉkaṇat tōṭum ciṟupāṉmi piṟa kaṇattōṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ puḷḷimayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarttu.
297.	ñakāri yoṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar
	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	valleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē
	ukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
it talic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, ñakāra īṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭu iruvaḻikkaṇṇum puṇarumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ñakāri oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar - ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṟṟāka niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyariṉ muṉṉar, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - alvaḻiyic collu miṭattum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ av veḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāy iyiyiṉ valleḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇ mikku muṭiyum, āvayiṉāṉa ukaram varutal - āṇṭu nilimoḻik kaṇ ukaram varuka.
u-m:	uriñuk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; uriñuk kaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum varum.	 (1)
298.	ñanamava viyiyiṉu mukara niliyum.
iḵtu, av vīṟu meṉkaṇattōṭum iṭikkaṇattu vakarat toṭum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma va iyiyiṉum ukaram niliyum - anta ñakāravīru vīṉkaṇamaṉṟi ña na ma va mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi yāy iyiyiṉum nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	 uriñu ñāṉṟatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; uriñu ñāṟci;  nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; uriñuvalitu, valimi eṉavum varum.
iṭikkaṇattu yakarattōṭum uyirōṭum puṇarumāṟu tokimarapiṉuḷ “ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti” (cūt. 21) eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
299.	nakara viṟutiyu mataṉō raṟṟē.
itu, nakaravīṟu mēṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu oruvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nakara iṟutiyum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - nakarīṟṟup peyarum mēṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi añ ñakāra īṟṟōṭu ōr iyalpiṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m: porunuk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; porunu ñāṉṟatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; porunu valitu eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(3)
300.	vēṟṟumik kukkeṭa vakara niliyum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ nilimoḻi muṭipu vēṟāy muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikku u keṭa akaram niliyum - an nakara vīṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ eytiya nilimoḻi ukaram keṭa akaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	porunak kaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum; poruna ñāṟci; nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; poruna valimi eṉavum varum.
‘akaram niliyum’ eṉṉātu ‘ukaraṅ keṭa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, av vīr īṟṟiṉ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum ciṟupāṉmi ukarappēṟum koḷka. poruniṉ kuṟi, uriñiṉ kuṟi eṉavum, “uyaval yāṉi porunucceṉ ṟaṉṉa” (akam. 65) eṉavum varum.	(4)
301.	verine ṉiṟuti muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi
	varumiṭa ṉuṭittē melleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu, an nakaravīṟṟu orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) verin eṉ iṟuti muḻutum keṭuvaḻi - verin eṉṟu collappaṭum nakaravīṟṟumoḻi taṉ īṟṟu nakaram muṉ peṟṟa akarattōṭu eñcāmaṟ keṭṭaviṭattu, melleḻuttu iyaṟki varum iṭaṉ uṭittu - melleḻuttup peṟum iyalpu vantu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu.
u-m: veriṅ kuṟi; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(5)
302.	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.
iḵtu, iṉṉum am moḻikku eytiyatu oru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a vayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - av verin eṉṉum col avvāṟu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟaviṭattu melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭitalum urittu.
u-m: verik kuṟi; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
ñakāravīṟṟoṭu nakāravīṟu otta muṭipiṟṟātaliṉ uṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(6)
303.	ṇakāra viṟuti valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
	ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
itu, ṇakāravīṟu vēṟṟumipporuṭkaṇ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - ṇakāra vīṟṟuppeyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi iyiyiṉ, ṭakāram ākum vēṟṟumip poruṭku - ṭakāramāy muṭiyum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ.
u-m: maṭkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉa varum.	(7)
304.	āṇum peṇṇu maḵṟiṇi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇum peṇṇum aḵṟiṇi iyaṟki - āṇ eṉṉum peyarum peṇ eṉṉum peyarum mēl toki marapiṉuḷ ‘moḻimuta lākum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aḵṟiṇip peyar muṭinta iyalpupōlavē vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m:	āṇki, peṇki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
maṟṟitu tokimarapiṉuḷ “aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar” (cūttiram 13) eṉpataṉuḷ iyalpāy muṭintataṉṟōveṉiṉ, ivi āṇṭu muṭintaṉapōlat tattam marapiṉ viṉiyāṟ pālaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa vaṉṟi, irutiṇikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭitaliṉ av vaḵṟiṇip peyaratu iyalpoṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭittār eṉak koḷka. ivvāṟātaliṉ, āṇkaṭitu peṇ kaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻiyum ‘moḻimuta lākum’ (toki. 5) eṉpataṉuṭ koḷḷappaṭum. 			(8)
305.	āṇmarak kiḷavi yarimara viyaṟṟē.
itu, tiripu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttamiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇmarak kiḷavi arimara iyaṟṟu - āṇ eṉṉum maratti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarccol arimaram ammuppeṟum iyalpiṟṟāyt (284) tāṉum ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: āṇaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(9)

306.	viṇṇeṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ
	uṇmiyu murittē yatteṉ cāriyi
	ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṟ ṟoḻilvaru kāli.
itu, ceyyuḷuḷ tiripu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṇ eṉa varum kāyap peyarvayiṉ - viṇ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa ākāyatti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarkkaṇ, attu eṉ cāriyi uṇmiyum urittu - attu eṉṉum cāriyi uṇṭātalum urittu illiyātalum urittu, ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻil varu kāli - ceyyuḷiṭattu viṉivarum kālattu.
u-m: viṇṇattuk koṭkum eṉavum, ‘viṇkuttu nīḷvari’ (nālaṭi. 226) eṉavum varum.	(10)
307.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki ukaramum valleḻuttum vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyarellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ṇakāra vīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarellām alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyaratu iyalpāy vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi valleḻuttum ukarappēṟum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum vantavaḻi ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	maṇṇukkaṭitu eṉavum, maṇṇukkaṭumi eṉavum; maṇṇu ñāṉṟatu; ñāṟci eṉavum; maṇṇuvalitu; valimi eṉavum iruvaḻiyum oṭṭuka.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, toḻiṟpeyarallaṉavum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟuvaṉa koḷka. veṇṇukkari, eṇṇuppāṟu, maṇṇuccōṟu eṉa varum.	(11)
308.	kiḷippeya rellāṅ koḷattiri pilavē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷippeyar ellām koḷat tiripu ila - ṇakāra vīṟṟuḷ ōr iṉatti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarellām tiripuṭiya veṉṟu karutum paṭiyākat tiritaluṭiyavaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: umaṇkuṭi; cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟuc cāriyi peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
u-m:  maṇṇakkaṭi, eṇṇanōli eṉavum; paraṇkāl, kavaṇkāl eṉavum varum.
`koḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu ēḻām vēṟṟumip poruṇmi uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccol tirintu muṭivaṉa koḷka.
u-m: aṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, iṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, uṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; āṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, īṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ, ūṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; avaṭ koṇṭāṉ, ivaṭ koṇṭāṉ, uvaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum oṭṭuka.
(`maṇṇakkaṭi’ eṉpatil akkuccāriyiyum `vaṇṇa nōli’ eṉpatil amccāriyiyum vantaṉa.)	(12)
309.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yeṇṇe ṉuṇavuppeyar
	vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟu alvaḻiyuḷ vēṟṟumi muṭipu pōlaṭ tirintu muṭivatu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi alvaḻi - vēṟṟumi yallāta viṭattu, eṇ eṉ uṇavuppeyar - eṇ eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa uṇaviṉi yuṇarttum peyar, vēṟṟumi iyaṟki niliyalum urittu - vēṟṟumiyatu tirintu muṭiyum iyalpu niṟṟalum urittu.
u-m: eṭkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.
ummiyāṉ, eṇkaṭitu eṉṟu iyalpātalē (148) perum pāṉmi.	(13)
310.	muraṇeṉ ṟoḻiṟpeyar mutaliya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar oṉṟaṟkut toḻiṟ peyar muṭipu vilakki, iv vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipum vēṟṟumi muṭipum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) muraṇ eṉ toḻiṟpeyar mutal iyal niliyum - muraṇ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum toḻiṟpeyar iv vīṟṟiṟku iruvaḻiyum muṉkūṟiya iyalpum (148), tiripumākiya (303) iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	muraṇkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; muraṭ kaṭumi, cēṉi, tāṉi, paṟi eṉavum varum.
itaṉi ‘toḻiṟpeya rellām’ (cūttiram 11) eṉpataṉpiṉ viyāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, muraṇkaṭumi eṉṉum iyalpum; araṇ kaṭumi, araṭkaṭumi eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyum koḷka.	 (14)
311.	makara viṟuti vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ
	tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.
itu, makaravīṟṟiṟku mēṟkūṟiya ṇakaravīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipōṭu iyiya vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makara iṟuti vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ - makaravīṟṟup peyarc col vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ, tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum - am makaram muṟṟak keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: marakkōṭum; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum uyar tiṇip peyarkkaṇṇum viravuppeyarkkaṇṇum makarakkēṭu koḷka. 
u-m: marañāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭi, āṭi eṉavum; naṅki, eṅki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; nuṅki, taṅki eṉavum varum.	  (15)
312.	akara ākāram varūuṅ kāli
	īṟṟumici yakara nīṭalu murittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu muṭipu vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ākāram varuṅkāli - akara mutal moḻiyum ākāra mutalmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkāli, īṟṟumici akaram nīṭalum urittu - nilimoḻikkaṇ īṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa akaram nīḷātu niṟpatēyaṉṟi nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu.
u-m: marāaṭi, kuḷāampal eṉavum; maravaṭi, kuḷavāmpal eṉavum varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟuṭ piṟavum vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉa koḷka. kōṇākōṇam, kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉa varum.
muṉṉarc ‘celvaḻi yaṟital’ (cūt-17) eṉpataṉāl, kuḷā ampal eṉpuḻi varumoḻi ākārakkuṟukkamum, kōṇā kōṇam eṉpuḻi varumoḻi valleḻuttukkēṭum koḷka.
(kōṇā kōṇam, kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉpaṉavaṟṟuḷ kōṇattuṭ kōṇam kōṇattuḷ vaṭṭam eṉa ēḻaṉurupu virikka.)	 (16)
313.	melleḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyum uḷa - melleḻuttōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; vaḻakkattāṉa celvaḻi aṟital - vaḻakkiṉkaṇ avi vaḻaṅkum iṭam aṟika.
u-m:	kuḷaṅkari, kuḷakkari; cēṟu, tātu, pūḻi eṉa varum.
‘celvaḻi yaṟital’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuḷaṅkari, kuḷakkari eṉṟatu pōla, allaṉa otta uṟaḻcciyalla veṉpatu koḷka. ‘vaḻakkat tāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyātaṉa vellām muṭittuk koḷka. ilavaṅkōṭu eṉavum, ‘pulampuk kaṉaṉē’ eṉavum, nilattuk kiṭantāṉ eṉavum varum.	  (17)
314.	illa marappeyar vicimara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) illam marappeyar vici mara iyaṟṟu - illam eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar viciyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: illaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
itaṉkaṇ makarakkēṭu muṉṉar ‘ellām’ (cūttiram 19) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.	   (18)
315.	alvaḻi yellā melleḻut tākum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām melleḻuttu ākum - makaravīṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: maraṅkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipiṉ muṭiyātaṉa vellām koḷka. 
u-m: marañāṉṟatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; vaṭṭat taḻi, vaṭṭappalaki eṉavum; kalakkoḷ, kalanel eṉavum; nīlakkaṇ, pavaḷavāy eṉavum; nilanīr eṉavum; kolluṅ koṟṟaṉ, paṟakkunāri eṉavum varum.	     (19)

316.	akameṉ kiḷavikkuk kimuṉ variṉē
	mutaṉili yoḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum
	varinili yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka
	melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ marūu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akam eṉ kiḷavikku ki muṉvariṉ - akam eṉṉum colliṟku ki eṉṉum col muṉvariṉ, mutal nili oḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum - muṉ ‘makaraviṟuti’ (cūttiram -15) eṉṟataṉāṉ makaram keṭṭu niṉṟa nilimoḻi mutal niṉṟa akaram oḻiya ataṉmuṉ niṉṟa akaramum akarattāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa kakarameyyum keṭṭu muṭitalum avi keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum, varinili iṉṟu āciriyarkku - nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu āciriyarkku; a vayiṉṉāṉa melleḻuttu mikutal - av viraṇṭiṭattum melleḻuttu mikku muṭika.
u-m: aṅki, akaṅki eṉa varum. (oḻiya-tavira)	(20)
317.	ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kāli
	niliyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu uriccol oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ilam eṉ kiḷavikkup paṭu varukāli - ilam eṉṉum colliṟkup paṭu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, niliyalum urittuc ceyyuḷāṉa - muṉ ‘makaraviṟuti’ (cūttiram -15) eṉpataṉāṟ keṭṭa īṟu keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittuc ceyyuṭkaṇ.
u-m:	“ilampaṭu pulava rēṟṟaki niṟiya” (malipaṭu. 576) eṉa varum.
uriccollākalāṉ urupu viriyāteṉiṉum ilattāṟ paṟṟappaṭum pulavareṉṉum poruḷ uṇara niṟṟaliṉ, vēṟṟumi muṭipāyiṟṟu. ummi makaravīṟu eṉṉum cātiyorumipaṟṟi vanta etirmaṟi.	(21)
318.	attoṭu civaṇu māyirat tiṟuti
	otta veṇṇu muṉvaru kāli.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut toki marapiṉuḷ eytiya ē eṉ cāriyi vilakki, attu vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āyirattu iṟuti - āyiram eṉṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ makaramey, otta eṇṇu muṉ varukāli - taṉakku akappaṭumoḻiyāyp poruntiṉa eṇṇuppeyar taṉmuṉ varuṅkālattu, attoṭu civaṇum - tokimarapiṟ kūṟiya ē eṉ cāriyi oḻittu attuccāriyi porunti muṭiyum.
u-m:	āyirattoṉṟu, āyirattiraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku eṉa oṭṭuka.
nilimoḻi muṟkūṟātu cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, itaṉ muṉṉark kuṟi, kūṟu, mutal eṉpaṉa vantavaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. āyirattukkuṟi; kūṟu, mutal eṉa oṭṭuka. 	(22)
319.	aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, av veṇṇuppeyar aṭiyaṭuttavaḻi muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - av vāyiram eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṭiyaṭutta moḻiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum mēṟcoṉṉataṉōṭu orutaṉmittāy attup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu; iraṇṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
mēl ilēciṉāṉ vantaṉavum aṭiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka. patiṉāyirattukkuṟi; kūṟu, mutal eṉa varum.	 (23)
320.	aḷavu niṟiyum vēṟṟumi yiyala.
iḵtu, av veṇṇiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyar niṟippeyar vantāl muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum vēṟṟumi iyala - āyirattu muṉ aḷavum niṟiyum vantāl iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumi iyalpāy (311) makaramkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m:	āyirakkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum; āyirak kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum oṭṭuka.
immāṭṭēṟṟāṉē, mēl ‘tuvara’ (cū. 15) eṉṟa ilēciṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ kēṭu eytiya makaram īṇṭum keṭuttuk koḷka. 
u-m: āyiranāḻi; vaṭṭi, akal eṉa varum.
patiṉāyirakkalam eṉṟāṟpōla aṭiyaṭuttu vantavaḻiyum oṭṭuka.	(24)
321.	paṭarkkip peyaru muṉṉilip peyarum
	toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum peyarnilik kiḷaviyum
	vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉurupiya ṉiliyum
	melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cila uyartiṇippeyarum viravup peyarum urupipuṇarcci muṭintavāṟē īṇṭup poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭiyumeṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṭarkkippeyarum muṉṉilip peyarum toṭakkam kuṟukum peyarnilikkiḷaviyum - ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkip peyarum ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarum (kiḷit toṭarccip poruḷavāy) neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum tām nām yām eṉṉum peyarum, vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ urupu iyal niliyum - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇāyiṉ urupupuṇarccikkaṇ coṉṉa iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu, cāriyi peṟuvaṉa īṟukeṭṭu iṭiyum īṟum cāriyi peṟṟum (192), neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukiyum (189) muṭiyum, avayiṉāṉ melleḻuttu mikutal āṉa - an neṭumutal kuṟukumoḻikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikum.
u-m: ellārtaṅkiyum, ellīrnuṅkiyum; ceviyum, taliyum, puṟamum eṉavum; taṅki, naṅki, eṅki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum oṭṭuka.
‘vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṭarkkippeyarkkum muṉṉilippeyarkkum iyalpukaṇattu ñakaramum nakaramum vantavaḻi tammuccāriyiyum nummuccāriyiyum īṟu keṭutal koḷka.
‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṭarkkippeyarkkum muṉṉilip peyarkkum ñakaramum nakaramum vantuḻi avi mikutalum, toṭakkam kuṟukum peyarkkum añ ñakaramum nakaramum vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭṭu avi mikutaluṅ koḷka. ellār taññāṇum, ellīr nuññāṇum, nūlum eṉavum; taññāṇ, naññāṇ, eññāṇ, nūl eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṭarkkippeyarum muṉṉilippeyarum cāriyi peṟātu iṟuti ummup peṟutalum koḷka. 
u-m: ellārkiyum, ellīrkiyum; ceviyum, taliyum, puṟamum eṉavarum. 
u-m: iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, urupīṟṟuc ceyki yellām koḷka. tamakāṇam eṉavarum. (iḵtu akara urupukoṭuttu muṭittavāṟu kāṇka).	  (25)
322.	allatu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟki yākum.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟki ākum - mēṟkūṟiya paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilip peyarum toṭakkam kuṟukum peyar nilikkiḷaviyum alvaḻiyic collumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
īṇṭu iyalpeṉpatu cāriyi peṟāmi nōkki. ivaṟṟiṉ īṟutirital ‘alvaḻi yellām’ (cūttiram 19) eṉṟataṉuḷ ‘ellām’ eṉṉum ilēciṉāṟ koḷka.
u-m:	ellāruṅ kuṟiyar; ciṟiyar, tīyar, periyar eṉavum; ellīruṅ kuṟiyīr, ciṟiyīr, tīyīr, periyīr eṉavum; tāṅ kuṟiyar;ciṟiyar, tīyar, periyar eṉavum; nāṅ kuṟiyam; ciṟiyam, tīyam, periyam eṉavum; yāṅ kuṟiyēm; ciṟiyēm, tīyēm,  periyēm eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ‘ellām’ eṉṉum ilēciṉāṉē, iv vīṟṟukkaṇ meṉkaṇattu makaram oḻintaṉa vantavaḻi makaram avvoṟṟāyt tiritalum koḷka. 
u-m: ellāruñ ñāṉṟār, nīṇṭār eṉavum; ellīruñ ñāṉṟīr, nīṇṭīr eṉavum; tāññāṉṟār, nīṇṭār eṉavum; nāññāṉṟām, nīṇṭām eṉavum; yāññāṉṟēm, nīṇṭēm eṉavum koḷka.        	  (26)
323.	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	ellā meṉumpeya rurupiya ṉiliyum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻic cāriyi niliyātu.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum urupiyaloṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - al vaḻiyic collumiṭattum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci yiṭattum, ellām eṉum peyar urupu iyal niliyum - ellām eṉṉum viravup peyar urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu (190) vaṟṟuccāriyiyum iṟuti ummuccāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi cāriyi niliyātu - ap peyar vēṟṟumiyallāta iṭattuc cāriyi peṟutal niliyātāyē muṭiyum.
māṭṭēṟu ēlāta alvaḻiyiṉiyum urupiyaloṭu māṭṭeṟintu vilakkiya mikutiyāṉ, alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇat tiṟuti ummuppēṟum, nilimoḻi makarakkēṭum, varumoḻi valleḻuttuppēṟum, meṉ kaṇattu makarakkēṭum, paṇput tokikkaṇ makarakkēṭṭōṭu iṟuti ummuppēṟum koḷka.
u-m: ellākkuṟiyavum; ciṟiyavum, tīyavum, periyavum eṉavum; ellāvaṟṟukkōṭum; ceviyum, taliyum, puṟamum eṉavum; ellāñāṉṟaṉa; nīṇṭaṉa, māṇṭaṉa eṉavum; ellāñāṇum; nūlum, maṇiyum, yāppum, valimiyum, aṭivum, āṭṭamum eṉavum; ellāvaṟṟuñāṇum; nūlum, maṇiyum, yāppum, valimiyum, aṭivum, āṭṭamum eṉavum varum.
ellāk kuṟiyarum; ciṟiyarum, tīyarum, periyarum eṉa uyar tiṇikkaṇṇum oṭṭuka.
īṇṭuc cāriyi peṟṟavaḻi, makarakkēṭu vaṟṟiṉmici oṟṟāyk keṭṭatu. itu viravuppeyarākaliṉ, īṟṟup potu muṭipiṟku ēlāteṉṟu cāriyi valleḻuttuk koḷḷap paṭṭatu.	 (27)
324.	melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya ellām eṉpataṟku alvaḻikkaṇ eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi - av vellāmeṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ mēl ilēciṉāṟ kūṟiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illi e-ṟu.
mēṟkūṟiya ceykimēlē itu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, makarak kēṭum iṟuti ummuppēṟuṅ koḷka.
u-m: ellāṅkuṟiyavum; ciṟiyavum, tīyavum, periyavum eṉavum; ellāṅkuṟiyarum; ciṟiyarum, tīyarum, periyarum eṉavum oṭṭuka.
iṉi, uriyiṟkōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, iṟuti ummiṉṟi ellāṅ kuṟiya, ellāruṅkuṟiyar eṉavum varum.
mēl ilēciṉāṟ kūṟiniṉṟa valleḻuttiṉoṭu mel leḻuttu vakuttamiyiṉ, iḵtu alvaḻiyāyiṟṟu.
325.	uyartiṇi yāyi ṉurupiya ṉiliyum.
itu, mēlataṟku uyartiṇi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇiyāyiṉ urupu iyal niliyum - av vellāmeṉpatu aḵṟiṇippeyarāyaṉṟi uyartiṇip peyarāy niṉṟa nilimiyāyiṉ urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu āṇṭuk kūṟiya nammuccāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum.
vaṟṟu vakutta ceyki mēl vakuttamiyiṉ, makarakkēṭu koḷka. iṟuti ummiyum ac ceyki mēlē vakuttamiyiṟ koḷka.
u-m: ellānaṅkiyum; ceviyum, taliyum, puṟamum eṉa varum.
mēl māṉamilli (cūt. 28) eṉṟataṉāṉ, alvaḻik kaṇ vaṉkaṇattu makaramkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku iṟuti ummuppeṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaram keṭṭu iṟuti ummuppeṟṟu muṭitalum koḷka. 
u-m: ellāk kollarum, cēvakarum, taccarum, pulavarum eṉavum; ellā ñāyiṟum, nāyakarum, maṇiyakārarum vaṇikarum, aracarum eṉavum varum. 	(29)
326.	numme ṉorupeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
ituvum, av vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ oru peyar melleḻuttu mikum - num eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa oru viravuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: nuṅki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
makaram ‘tuvara’ (cūt. 15) eṉṟa ilēciṉāṟ keṭṭatu. ‘orupeyar’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ñakara nakaraṅkaḷ vanta iṭattum avvoṟṟu mikutal koḷka. nuññāṇ, nunnūl eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, uṅki eṉa um eṉpataṉ muṭipum iv vīṟṟatākak koḷka.	 (30)
327.	allataṉ maruṅkiṟ colluṅ kāli
	ukkeṭa niṉṟa meyvayi ṉīvara
	iiṭi nilii yīṟukeṭa rakaram
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntē
	appāṉ moḻivayi ṉiyaṟki yākum.
itu, mēlataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) allataṉ maruṅkiṉ collum kāli - an num eṉ oru peyar taṉṉi alvaḻiyiṭattuc colluṅkāli, u keṭa niṉṟa meyvayiṉ ī vara i iṭinilii īṟu keṭa rakaram puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntu niṟṟal vēṇṭum - nakarattu ukaraṅkeṭa an niṉṟa meyyiṭattu īkāram vara, ōr ikaram iṭiyilē peṟa makaram keṭa avviṭattu oru rakaram puḷḷiyoṭu porunti niṟṟal vēṇṭum; appāl moḻivayiṉ iyaṟki yākum - varumoḻiyiṭattu am moḻitāṉ ivvāṟu tiriyātu iyalpātal vēṇṭum.
u-m: nīyīrkuṟiyīr, ciṟiyīr, tīyīr, periyīr eṉa varum. 
ñāṉṟīr, nīṇṭīr, māṇṭīr eṉa iyalpu kaṇattōṭum oṭṭuka.	 (31)
328.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyarellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - makaravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarellām alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṉavāy  vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, iyalpukaṇattu ukaram peṟṟum varum.
u-m:	cemmukkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; cemmu ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; cemmuk kaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum; cemmu ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukarampeṟātu alvaḻikkaṇ nāṭṭaṅ kaṭitu eṉa melleḻuttāyt tirivaṉavum, vēṟṟumik kaṇ nāṭṭak kaṭumi eṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.	          (32)
329.	īmuṅ kammu murumeṉ kiḷaviyum
	āmup peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa.
itu, poruṭpeyaruṭ cila alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum toḻiṟpeyarōṭu ottu muṭiyumeṉak kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īmum kammum urum eṉ kiḷaviyum a muppeyarum- īm eṉṉum collum kam eṉṉum collum urum eṉṉum collumākiya am mūṉṟu peyarum, avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum at toḻiṟ peyarōṭu (297, 298) orutaṉmiyavāy vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, iyalpukaṇattu ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m:	īmukkaṭitu, kammukkaṭitu, urumukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, eṉavum; īmuñāṉṟatu, kammuñāṉṟatu, urumuñāṉṟatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; īmuk kaṭumi, kammukkaṭumi, urumukkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum; īmuñāṟci, kammu ñāṟci, urumuñāṟci; -nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉavum varum.
(īm-iṭukāṭu, kam-kammiyaratutoḻil, urum-iṭi.)     (33)
330.	vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉēṉi yiraṇṭum
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭu makkeṉ cāriyi.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṟku vēṟṟumikkaṇ vēṟu oru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi āyiṉ - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcci yāyiṉ, ēṉi iraṇṭum akku eṉ cāriyi tōṟṟam vēṇṭum - iṟuti urum oḻinta iraṇṭum `akku’ eṉṉum cāriyi tōṟṟi muṭital vēṇṭum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ukaram nīkkutal vēṇṭumeṉka.
u-m: īmakkuṭam, kammakkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi 
eṉa varum.
mēl vēṟṟumi kūṟiya muṭipu kuṇavēṟṟumik kaṇṇa teṉṟum, īṇṭuk kūṟiya muṭipu poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇa teṉṟum koḷka.	(34)
331.	vakāra miciyu makāraṅ kuṟukum.
itu, paruntuviḻukkāṭāy ‘ariyaḷapu kuṟukal’ (nūṉmarapu 13) eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vakāram miciyum makāram kuṟukum - mēl oru moḻikkaṇ kūṟiya ‘ṉakāri muṉṉar’ (moḻimarapu. cūttiram. 19) aṉṟi, īṇṭup puṇarmoḻikkaṇ vakarattiṉmēlum makaraṅ kuṟukum.
u-m: nilam valitu eṉa varum.	(35)
332.	nāṭpeyark kiḷavi mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa
	attu māṉmici varinili yiṉṟē
	oṟṟumey keṭuta leṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷpeyark kiḷavi mēl kiḷanta aṉṉa - makaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyarccol mēl (248) ikaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyariṟ kiḷanta taṉmiya vāy āṉ peṟṟu muṭiyum; attu āṉ miciyum varinili iṉṟu - attuccāriyi av vāṉcāriyimēlum piṟa cāriyimēlum nīkkum nilimi yiṉṟām; oṟṟumey keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - avviṭattu makaravoṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭuka veṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ; makattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘oṟṟu’ eṉṉātu ‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nāṭpeyarallāta poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇum am muṭipu koḷka.
u-m: marattāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. (ñāṉṟu eṉpatu -  cāriyi.)	(36)
333.	ṉakāra viṟuti valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
	ṟakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
itu, ṉakāra vīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - ṉakāra vīṟṟuppeyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyiyiṉ, ṟakāram ākum vēṟṟumip poruṭku - ṟakāram ākum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ.
u-m: poṟkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉa varum.     (37)
334.	maṉṉuñ ciṉṉu māṉu mīṉum
	piṉṉu muṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	aṉṉa viyala veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu acinili iṭiccollum, ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷum, viṉiyeccamum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maṉṉum ciṉṉum āṉum īṉum piṉṉum muṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - maṉ eṉṉum collum ciṉ eṉṉum collum āṉ eṉṉum collum īṉ eṉṉum collum piṉ eṉṉum collum muṉ eṉṉum collum viṉiyeccamākiya collum, aṉṉa iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar- mēṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉavāy ṉakaram ṟakaramāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	“atumaṟ koṇkaṉ ṟērē,” “kāppum pūṇṭiciṟ kaṭiyum pōkali” eṉavum; āṟkoṇṭāṉ, īṟkoṇṭāṉ,  piṟkoṇṭāṉ, muṟkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; variṟkoḷ ḷum; cellum, tarum, pōm eṉavum varum.
peyarān taṉmiyavākiya āṉ, īṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi muṟ kūṟātataṉāṉ, āṉkoṇṭāṉ, īṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉat tiriyātu muṭitalum koḷka.
piṉ, muṉ eṉpaṉa peyarniliyum viṉiyeccaniliyum urupu niliyum paṭum. avaṟṟuḷ viṉiyeccanili īṇṭu ‘viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum’ eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum. urupunili urupiyaluḷ muṭiyum. īṇṭup peyar kūṟukiṉṟatu. ap peyari muṉ kūṟātataṉāṉ, piṉ koṇṭāṉ, muṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉat tiriyāmiyum koḷka.
‘iyala’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ūṉ eṉṉum cuṭṭu ūṉ koṇṭāṉ eṉa iyalpāy muṭital koḷka.	          (38)
335.	cuṭṭumutal vayiṉu mekaramutal vayiṉum
	appaṇpu niliyu miyaṟkiya veṉpa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ orucār ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇartti niṉṟa iṭiccolliṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭumutal vayiṉum ekaramutal vayiṉum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vayiṉ eṉṉum collum ekaramākiya mutaliyuṭiya vayiṉ eṉṉum collum, a paṇpu niliyum iyaṟkiya eṉpa - mēl ṟakaramāy muṭiyumeṉṟa ap paṇpu nili peṟṟu muṭiyum iyaṟkiyiyuṭiya eṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: avvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ, ivvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ, uvvayiṟ koṇṭāṉ, evvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘iyaṟkiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka. kāṉkōḻi eṉa varum.	(39)
336.	kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuyiṉ eṉ kiḷavi iyaṟki yākum - kuyiṉ eṉṉum col tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	kuyiṉkuḻām;  celavu, tōṟṟam, maṟivu eṉa varum. kuyiṉ eṉpatu mēkam.	 (40)
337.	ekiṉmara māyi ṉāṇmara viyaṟṟē.
itu, tiripu vilakki ammu vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ekiṉ maram āyiṉ āṇmara iyaṟṟu - ekiṉ eṉṉum collum marappeyarāyiṉ āṇmarattiṉatu iyalpiṟṟāy  ammup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: ekiṉaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.       (41)
338.	ēṉi yekiṉē yakaram varumē
	valleḻut tiyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi ekiṉ akaram varum - oḻinta maramallā ekiṉ nilimoḻikkaṇ akaram vantu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu iyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu varumoḻi valleḻuttu iyalpu mikku muṭital vēṇṭum.
u-m: ekiṉakkāl, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattōṭu itaṉi oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka. ekiṉañāṟci, yāppu, aṭivu eṉa varum.
`iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ akarappēṟṟōṭu melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka. 
um:ekiṉaṅkāl, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavarum. 	(42)
339.	kiḷippeya rellāṅ kiḷippeya riyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷippeyar ellām kiḷippeyar iyala - ṉakāra īṟṟuk kiḷippeyarellām ṇakāra īṟṟuk kiḷippeyar pōlat (308) tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: eyiṉkuṭi; cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akkuccāriyiyum valleḻuttum peṟṟu eyiṉakkaṉṉi eṉa muṭitalum; pārppaṉakkaṉṉi eṉa nilimoḻi tirintu akkum valleḻuttum peṟutalum; iṉic cāriyi peṟātu īṟu tirintu vēḷāṇkumari, vēḷāṇ vāḻkki eṉa muṭitalum koḷka. 	    (43)
340.	mīṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tuṟaḻvē.
ituvum, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mīṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu uṟaḻvu - mīṉ eṉṉum col taṉ tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
u-m: mīṉkaṇ, mīṟkaṇ; ciṉi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum. (44)
341.	tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
	mēṉili yottalum valleḻuttu mikutalum
	āmuṟi yiraṇṭu murimiyu muṭittē
	valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟuti yilli.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - tēṉ eṉṉum col valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻiyāy vantu poruntiṉ, mēlnili ottalum valleḻuttu mikutalum ā muṟi iraṇṭum urimiyum uṭittu - mēl mīṉ eṉṉum colliṟkuc coṉṉa tiri puṟaḻcciyiṉ nilimiyiyottu muṭitalum varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalumākiya am muṟiyiyuṭiya iraṇṭaṉiyum urittātaliyum uṭittu, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi iṟuti illi - valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu nilimoḻiyiṟuti ṉakaravoṟṟu niliyiṉṟik keṭum.
‘urimiyum’ eṉṟa vummi ‘melleḻuttu mikiṉum’ (342) eṉa mēlvarukiṉṟa muṭiviṉi nōkki niṉṟatu.
u-m: tēṉkuṭam, tēṟkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum; tēkkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum varum.	          (45)
342.	melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi - tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu vantāl valleḻuttu mikutalē yaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illi.
ṉakarakkēṭu atikārattāṟ koḷka.
u-m: tēṅkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉa varum.        (46)
343.	melleḻut tiyiyi ṉiṟutiyō ṭuṟaḻum.
itu, mēlataṟku meṉkaṇattukkaṇ tokimarapiṟ kūṟiya muṭipu oḻiya vēṟumuṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ iṟutiyōṭu uṟaḻum - at tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mutalmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ nilimoḻiyiṟuti ṉakāravoṟṟuk keṭutalum keṭāmiyumākiya uṟaḻcciyāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	tēṉñeri, tēñeri; nuṉi, muri eṉak koḷka.
mēl ‘māṉa milli’ (cūttiram 49) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘iṟuti yōṭu uṟaḻum’ eṉṟatu īṟukeṭṭu varumoḻi melleḻuttu mikkum mikātum uṟaḻtaṟkum, varumoḻi mikātu iṟuti keṭṭum keṭātum uṟaḻtaṟkum, av viraṇṭaṟkum urittāyc ceṉṟataṉi vilakki varumoḻi mikātē niṟpa avvīṟē keṭṭum keṭātum niṉṟu uṟaḻumeṉpatu koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
ataṉmēl ‘āmuṟi’ (cūttiram 45) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ciṟupāṉmi īṟukeṭṭut tēññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉa melleḻuttu mikutalum koḷka.	         (47)
344.	iṟāaṟ ṟōṟṟa miyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, iṉṉum ataṟku uyirkkaṇattu orumoḻikkaṇ muṭiyum vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṟāal tōṟṟam - tēṉ eṉṉum col iṟāl eṉṉum varumoḻiyatu tōṟṟattukkaṇ, iyaṟki ākum - nilimoḻi ṉakāraṅkeṭātē niṉṟu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: tēṉiṟāl eṉa varum.	(48)
345.	oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalu murittē.
iḵtu, ataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalum urittu - attēṉ eṉpatu iṟāl eṉṉum varumoḻikkaṇ piṟitum oru takaravoṟṟu uṭaṉmiku takarattoṭu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittu.
mēl “valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟutiyilli” (puḷḷi. 45) eṉṟataṉāṉ nilimoḻi yīṟu keṭukka, ‘takara mikum’ eṉṉātu, ‘oṟṟumiku takaram’ eṉṟataṉāṉ īroṟṟākkuka.
u-m: tēttiṟāl eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattōṭu itaṉi oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāl, piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum im muṭipukoḷka. tēttaṭi, tēttī eṉa varum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tēṉaṭi, tēṉī eṉṉum iyalpum koḷka.	 (49)

346.	miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉuṅ kaṉṉum
	annāṟ collun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟumuṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum kaṉṉum a nāl collum - miṉ eṉṉum collum piṉ eṉṉum collum paṉ eṉṉum collum kaṉ eṉṉum collumākiya an nāṉku collum, toḻiṟ peyar iyala - vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum alvaḻikkaṇṇum ñakaravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla (297, 298) vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum iṭik kaṇattukkaṇ ṇum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: miṉṉukkaṭitu, piṉṉukkaṭitu, paṉṉukkaṭitu, 
kaṉṉuk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu, yātu eṉavum; miṉṉukkaṭumi, piṉṉukkaṭumi, paṉṉuk kaṭumi, kaṉṉuk kaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi, yāppu eṉavum varum.
“toḻiṟpeyarellān toḻiṟpeyariyala” eṉṟu ōtātu ivvāṟu eṭuttōtiyavataṉāṉ, im muṭipiṉit toḻiṟpeyarkkum poruṭpeyarkkum uṭaṉ koḷka. miṉ eṉpatu oru toḻilumuṇṭu; poruḷumuṇṭu. piṟavum aṉṉa.		  (50)
347.	vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉēṉi yekiṉoṭu
	tōṟṟa mokkuṅ kaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu oru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaṉ eṉ kiḷavi vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ - kaṉ eṉṉum col vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ, ēṉi ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam okkum - oḻinta maramallā ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam ottu (338) akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	kaṉṉakkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉa varum.
ciṟupāṉmi kaṉṉakkaṭumi eṉak kuṇavēṟṟumik kaṇṇum im muṭipu koḷka.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāl, alvaḻikkaṇ akaramum, vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ melleḻuttum koḷka. 
u-m kaṉṉaṅkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ciṟupāṉmi kaṉṉaṅ kaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉak kuṇavēṟṟumik kaṇṇum akaramum melleḻuttum koḷka.
ik ‘kaṉ’ eṉpataṉ vēṟṟumi muṭipiṟku, mēṟkūṟiyatu (346) kuṇavēṟṟumikku eṉavum, īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu poruṭpeyarkku eṉavum koḷka.	(51)
348.	iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tanti muṟivariṉ
	mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa vakara niliyum
	meyyoḻit taṉkeṭu mavviyaṟ peyarē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ iyaṟpeyarkkut toki marapiṉuḷ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tanti muṟivariṉ - ṉakāra vīṟṟu iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tanti eṉṉum muṟippeyar varumoḻiyāy variṉ, mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa akaram niliyum - mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa ataṉmēl ēṟiniṉṟa akaram keṭātu nilipeṟum, a iyaṟpeyar mey oḻittu aṉkeṭum - nilimoḻiyākiya iyaṟpeyar avvaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ akaram ēṟiniṉṟa meyyi oḻittu avvaṉtāṉ keṭṭu muṭiyum.
u-m: cāttanti, koṟṟanti eṉa varum.
‘mutaṟkaṇ mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, cāttaṉṟanti, koṟṟaṉ ṟanti eṉṉum iyalpu muṭipum koḷka.	(52)
349.	ātaṉum pūtaṉuṅ kūṟiya viyalpoṭu
	peyaroṟ ṟakaran tuvarak keṭumē.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ātaṉum pūtaṉum - mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉum pūtaṉum eṉṉum iyaṟpeyarkaḷ, kūṟiya iyalpoṭu peyar oṟṟu akaram tuvarakkeṭum - mēṟkūṟiya ceykiyoṭu nilimoḻip peyaruḷ aṉ keṭaniṉṟa oṟṟum varumoḻiyuḷ oṟṟukkeṭa niṉṟa akaramum muṟṟakkeṭṭu muṭiyum.
u-m: ānti, pūnti eṉa varum.
‘iyalpu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, peyaroṟṟum akaramum keṭātē niṉṟu  muṭintavāṟē muṭitalum koḷka. ātanti, pūtanti eṉavarum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḻāṉ, puḻāṉ eṉa niṟuttip poruntiṉa ceyki ceytu aḻānti, puḻānti eṉa muṭikka.		  (53)
350.	ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi yiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, eytiyatu oruvaḻi vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi iyaṟki ākum - av viyaṟpeyar paṇpaṭuttu varuvaḻi muṉkūṟiya irumoḻic ceykiyum tavirttu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	peruñcāttaṉṟanti, peruṅkoṟṟaṉṟanti eṉa varum.	   (54)
351.	appeyar meyyoḻit taṉkeṭu vaḻiyē
	niṟṟalu murittē yammeṉ cāriyi
	makkaṇ muṟitokūu maruṅki ṉāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku vēṟu oru varumoḻikkaṇ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) a peyar makkaḷ muṟi tokum maruṅkiṉ āṉa mey oḻittu aṉkeṭu vaḻi - avviyaṟpeyar makkaḷ eṉṉum muṟippeyar vantukūṭum iṭattiṉkaṇṇum piṟitiṭattum, tāṉ ēṟiya meyyi oḻittu aṉkeṭum avvaḻi, am eṉ cāriyi niṟṟalum urittu - am eṉṉum cāriyi niṟṟalum urittu.
u-m:cāttaṅkoṟṟaṉ, koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum; cāttaṅkuṭi, koṟṟaṅkuṭi eṉavum varum.	(55)
352.	tāṉum pēṉuṅ kōṉu meṉṉum
	āmuṟi yiyaṟpeyar tiripiṭa ṉilavē.
itu, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉum pēṉum kōṉum eṉṉum a muṟi iyaṟpeyar - av viyaṟpeyaruḷ tāṉum pēṉum kōṉum eṉṉum muṟimi yiṉiyuṭiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tanti makkaḷ eṉṉum muṟippeyaroṭu puṇarumvaḻi, tiripu iṭaṉ ila - mēṟkūṟiya tiripukaḷ iṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum .
u-m: tāṉṟanti, pēṉṟanti, kōṉṟanti eṉavum; 
tāṉ koṟṟaṉ, pēṉkoṟṟaṉ, kōṉkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum varum.  		(56)
353.	tāṉyā ṉeṉumpeya rurupiya ṉiliyum.
itu, viravuppeyaruḷ tāṉ eṉpataṟkum, uyartiṇip peyaruḷ yāṉ eṉpataṟkum, vēṟṟumikkaṇ tokimarapiṉuḷ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉ yāṉ eṉum peyar urupu iyal niliyum - tāṉ eṉṉum viravuppeyarum yāṉ eṉṉum uyartiṇip peyarum mēl tokimarapiṉuṭ kūṟiya iyalpukaḷ oḻittu urupu puṇarcciyiṟ kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē  nilipeṟṟut tāṉ eṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukit taṉ eṉṟāyum, yāṉ eṉpatu yakaramkeṭṭu ākāram ekāramāy eṉ eṉṟāyum muṭiyum.
u-m: taṉki, eṉki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; 
taṉ ñāṇ, eṉñāṇ; nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭi, āṭi eṉavum varum.	(57)
354.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kuṟukalun tiritalum
	tōṟṟa milli yeṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, mēlavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi alvaḻi - mēṟkūṟiya tāṉ yāṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumippuṇarcciyallātaviṭattu, kuṟukalum tiritalum tōṟṟam illi eṉmaṉār pulavar - tāṉ eṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukalum yāṉ eṉpatu avvāṟu tiritalum tōṟṟamiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: tāṉkuṟiyaṉ; ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ, ñāṉṟāṉ, nīṇṭāṉ, māṇṭāṉ eṉavum; yāṉkuṟiyēṉ, ciṟiyēṉ, tīyēṉ, periyēṉ, ñāṉṟēṉ, nīṇṭēṉ, māṇṭēṉ eṉavum varum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumikkaṇ avvāṟaṉṟit tiritalum koḷka.
u-m: 	 taṟpukaḻ, taṟpaki eṉavum; eṟpaki, eṟpāṭi eṉavum varum.	   (58)
355.	aḻaṉeṉ ṉiṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḻaṉ eṉ iṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikum - aḻaṉ eṉṉum col taṉ ṉakaravīṟu keṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: aḻakkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉa varum. 
aḻaṉ eṉpatu piṇam.	 (59)
356.	muṉṉeṉ kiḷavi muṉṉart tōṉṟum
	illeṉ kiḷavimici ṟakara moṟṟal
	tolliyaṉ maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūukkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉ eṉ kiḷavi muṉṉart tōṉṟum il eṉ kiḷavi mici ṟakaram oṟṟal - muṉ eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum il eṉṉum colliṉmēl ṟakara voṟṟu vantu muṭital, tol iyal maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu - paḻiyatākiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiya viṭattu maruvi vanta ilakkaṇa muṭipu.
u-m: muṉṟil eṉa varum.
itu kaṭikkaṇ eṉṟāṟpōla varūum marūu muṭipu pōlaṉṟi, muṉṉil eṉa oṟṟiraṭṭi muṭiyaṟpālatu, iru moḻikkum iyalpilatōr oṟṟu mikku muṭinta marūu muṭipu.		   	   (60)
357.	poṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yīṟukeṭa muṟiyiṉ
	muṉṉart tōṉṟum lakāra makāram
	ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṟ ṟoṭariya lāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) poṉ eṉ kiḷavi īṟu keṭa muṉṉar muṟiyiṉ lakāram makāram tōṉṟum - poṉ eṉṉum col pakara mutaṉmoḻi vanta viṭattut taṉ īṟṟiṉ ṉakaramkeṭa ataṉ muṉṉar muṟiyāṉēlakaramum makaramum tōṉṟi muṭiyum; ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toṭar iyalāṉa - (yāṇṭeṉil,) ceyyu ḷiṭattuc coṟkaḷ tammil toṭarccippaṭum iyalpiṉkaṇ.
‘muṟiyiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, lakaram uyirmeyyākavum makaram taṉimeyyākavum koḷka.
u-m: “polampaṭip polinta koycuvaṟ puravi” (malipaṭu. 574) eṉa varum.
‘toṭariyalāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pakaram allāta vaṉkaṇattuk kaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi īṟukeṭṭu lakaramum valleḻuttiṟkēṟṟa melleḻuttum mikkumuṭital koḷka. polaṅkalam, polañcuṭar, polantēr eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ ‘polanaṟun teriyal’ (puṟam. 29) ‘polamala rāviri’ (kali. 138) eṉṟāṟpōla varum piṟa kaṇattu muṭipum koḷka.	(61)
358.	yakara viṟuti vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ
	valleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē.
itu, yakāravīṟṟiṟku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yakara iṟuti vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ - yakāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ a eḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:	nāykkāl; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.       (62)
359.	tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāy eṉ kiḷavi iyaṟki yākum - tāy eṉṉum col valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: tāy ki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
ivviyalpu mēl iṉṉavaḻi mikum eṉkiṉṟami yiṉ, ‘aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar’ eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. (63)
360.	makaṉviṉi kiḷappiṉ mutaṉili yiyaṟṟē.
itu, mēlataṟku aṭiyaṭuttu vantavaḻi iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉa eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makaṉ viṉi kiḷappiṉ mutalnili iyaṟṟu - at tāy eṉṉum col makaṉatu viṉiyik kiḷantu collu miṭattu, iv vīṟṟu mutaṟkaṇ (358) kūṟiya nilimiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: makaṉṟāyk kalām; ceru, tār, paṭi eṉa varum.
‘makaṉviṉi’ eṉṟatu, makaṟkut tāyāṟ payaṉpaṭum nilimiyiṉṟi, avaḷoṭu pakitta nilimiyi.	64)
361.	melleḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumār uḷa - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttiṉoṭu melleḻuttu mikkum uṟaḻntum muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa.
u-m:	vēyṅkuṟi; vēykkuṟi; ciṟi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(65)
362.	alvaḻi yellā miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - yakara vīṟṟu alvaḻiyellām iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:  nāykaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāl, iv vīṟṟu urupu vārātu, urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vanta iṭiccol muṭipum, viṉiyecca muṭipum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtoki muṭipum, alvaḻi yuṟaḻcci muṭipum koḷka. 
u-m: avvāyk koṇṭāṉ, ivvāyk koṇṭāṉ, uvvāyk koṇṭāṉ, evvāyk koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; tāyk koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; poyccol, meyccol eṉavum; vēykaṭitu, vēykkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum varum.	 (66)
363.	rakāra viṟuti yakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, rakāra vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) rakāra iṟuti yakāra iyaṟṟu - rakāra vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ yakāra vīṟṟu iyal piṟṟāy (358) valleḻuttu vantavaḻi mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: tērkkāl; ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.       (67)
364.	ārum vetiruñ cārum pīrum
	melleḻuttu mikutaṉ meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ārum vetirum cārum pīrum - ār eṉṉum collum vetir eṉṉum collum cār eṉṉuñ collum pīr eṉṉum collum, melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟa tōṉṟum - melleḻuttumikku muṭital meymmipeṟat tōṉṟum.
u-m: ārṅkōṭu, vetirṅkōṭu, cārṅkōṭu, pīrṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavum melleḻuttu mikutal koḷka. 
u-m: kutirṅkōṭu, tuvarṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, pīr eṉpatu mēl ammup peṟṟa vaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka.	(68)
365.	cāreṉ kiḷavi kāḻvayiṉ valikkum.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟuṭ cār eṉpataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cār eṉ kiḷavi kāḻvayiṉ valikkum - cār eṉṉum col kāḻ eṉṉum colloṭu puṇarumiṭattu valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.
u-m: cārkkāḻ eṉa varum.	(69)
366.	pīreṉ kiḷavi yammoṭuñ civaṇum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ pīr eṉpataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pīr eṉ kiḷavi ammoṭum civaṇum- pīr eṉṉum col melleḻuttēyaṉṟi ammuccāriyiyum peṟṟu vantu muṭiyum.
u-m: pīraṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.  	 (70)
367.	lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, lakāra īṟṟiṟku ṉakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumiyōṭu iyiya vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra iyaṟṟu - lakāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉ ṉakāravīṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy (333) lakāram ṟakāramāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: kaṟkuṟi; ciṟi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum. 	 (71)
368.	melleḻut tiyiyi ṉakāra mākum.
iḵtu, av vīṟu meṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṉakāram ākum - avvīṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyiyiṉ lakāram ṉakāramāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: kaṉñeri; nuṉi, muṟi eṉa varum.
ic cūttirattiṉi vēṟṟumiyatu īṟṟukkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttukkōṭaṟkaṇ  ciṅkanōkkāka  vittamiyāṉ, alvaḻikkum im muṭipu koḷka. kaṉñerintatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉa varum.	(72)
369.	alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām uṟaḻ eṉa moḻipa - av vīṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām tantiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: kalkuṟitu, kaṟkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kalkuṟumi, kaṟkuṟumi eṉak kuṇam paṟṟi vanta vēṟṟumikkum ivvuṟaḻcci koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, ivvīṟṟu viṉiccollīṟu tirintaṉavum koḷka. vantāṉāṟ koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, akkāṟkoṇṭāṉ, ikkāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ukkāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ekkāṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum īṇṭē koḷka.
itaṉāṉ, piṟavum uḷḷavāṟu aṟintu oṭṭik koḷka.	(73)
370.	takaram varumvaḻi yāyta niliyalum
	pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulami yōrē.
itu, mēlataṉuḷ oru kūṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) takaram varumvaḻi - avvāṟu ṟakāramāyt tirinta lakāram takara mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantavaḻi, āytam niliyalum pukar iṉṟu eṉmaṉār pulamiyōr - avvāṟu ṟakāramāyt tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamāyt tirintu niṟṟalum kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉṟu colluvar āciriyar.
u-m: kaḵṟītu, kaṟṟītu eṉa varum.
‘pukariṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘neṭiyata ṉiṟuti’ (puḷḷi. 75) eṉpataṉuḷ, vēṟītu, vēṟṟītu eṉṉum uṟaḻcci muṭipuṅ koḷka.	  (74)
371.	neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpumā ruḷavē.
itu, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpumār uḷa - neṭiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa lakāravīṟu kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa lakāram pōlat tirintu uṟaḻtalēyaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa.
u-m: pālkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
iyalpākātu tirintaṉa vēlkaṭitu, vēṟkaṭitu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.	  (75)
372.	nelluñ celluṅ kolluñ collum
	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumi yiyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu alvaḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nellum cellum kollum collum - nel eṉṉum collum cel eṉṉum collum kol eṉṉum collum col eṉṉum collum ākiya ivi nāṉku collum, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumi iyala - avvaḻiyic collumiṭattum  tam vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṉavāy lakāram ṟakāramāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: neṟkaṭitu, ceṟkaṭitu, koṟkaṭitu, coṟkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.	(76)
373.	illeṉ kiḷavi yiṉmi ceppiṉ 
	valleḻuttu mikutalu miyiṭi varutalum
	iyaṟki yātalu mākāram varutalum
	koḷattaku marapi ṉākiṭa ṉuṭittē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu viṉikkuṟippuc col oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) il eṉ kiḷavi iṉmi ceppiṉ - il eṉṉum col pukkuṟiyum illi uṇarttātu oru poruḷatu illāmiyi uṇarttumiṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum ai iṭi varutalum iyaṟki ātalum ākāram varutalum - valleḻuttu mikutalum ai iṭi varutalum iyalpātalum ākāram mikku muṭitalum ākiya im muṭipu nāṉkum, koḷattakum marapiṉ āku iṭaṉ uṭittu - ic colliṟku muṭipākak koḷattakum marapāṉē itaṉ muṭipu ākum iṭaṉ uṭittu.
‘koḷattaku marapu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, valleḻuttu mikkavaḻi aikāram varutalum, ākāram mikkavaḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum koḷka. iyalpu muṟkūṟātu oḻintataṉāṉ, valleḻuttu mikuti ākāram vantavaḻi mikkē muṭitaliṉ ciṟappuṭittātalum, aikāram vantavaḻi mikutalum mikāmiyu muṭimiyiṉ ciṟappiṉṟātalum koḷka.
u-m:	illikkal, illikal; ilkal; illākkal; cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi eṉa varum.
