illikkal, illikal eṉpaṉa illi eṉṉum aikāra vīṟṟuc col muṭipaṉṟō veṉṟum, illākkal eṉpatu uḷḷa eṉṉum peyareccattu etirmaṟiyākiya ākāravīṟṟuc collatu muṭipaṉṟō veṉṟum, ilkal eṉpatu paṇput toki muṭipaṉṟō veṉṟum kūṟiṉ, am muṭipukaḷōṭu im muṭipukaḷ eḻuttoppumiyaṉṟi, ivi ōci vēṟṟumi yuṭiya veṉpatu pōlum karuttu. avvōci vēṟṟumi yāvaṉa, aikāra vīṟāyavaḻi av vikārattu mēl oliyūṉṟiyum, lakāravīṟu iyalpu muṭipāyavaḻip paṇputtoki pōla oru tiraṇmiyāka oliyātu iṭiyaṟṟu lakārattumēl oli yūṉṟiyum, ākāravīṟāyavaḻi, oru tiraṇmiyāl av ākārattumēl oliyūṉṟiyum, āṇṭu lakāra vīṟāya vaḻi oru tiraṇmiyāka oliyātu anta ākārattumēl oliyūṉṟiyum varum vēṟupāṭukaḷ pōlum.
il eṉpatu etirmaṟi viṉikkuṟippumuṟṟu viravuviṉi.		 (77)
374.	valleṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) val eṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu - val eṉṉum col alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy (297, 298) vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: vallukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; vallukkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉavum varum.      			(78)
375.	nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli
	āvayi ṉukaraṅ keṭutalu murittē
	ukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi yakara niliyum.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttup piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāyum palakiyum varum kāli - mēlniṉṟa val eṉpataṉmuṉ nāy eṉṉum collum palaki eṉṉum collum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, a vayiṉ ukaram keṭutalum urittu - avviṭattu ukaram keṭātē niṉṟu muṭitalēyaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭiyavum peṟum, ukaram keṭuvaḻi akaram niliyum - av vukaram keṭumiṭattu akaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: vallanāy, vallappalaki eṉa varum.
‘akaram niliyum’ eṉṉātu ‘ukaram keṭum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum av vakarappēṟu koḷka.
u-m: vallakkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉa  varum.		(79)
376.	pūlvē leṉṟā varaleṉ kiḷaviyō
	ṭāmup peyarkku mammiṭi varumē.
ituvum, avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pūl vēl āl eṉ kiḷaviyōṭu a 
muppeyarkkum - pūl eṉṉum collum vēl eṉṉum collum āl eṉṉum collum ākiya am mūṉṟu peyarkkum, am iṭi varum - vēṟṟumikkaṇ tiripiṉṟi ammuccāriyi iṭi vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m:	pūlaṅkōṭu, vēlaṅkōṭu, ālaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. (eṉṟā eṉpatu - eṇṇiṭiccol) (80)
377.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - lakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarellām ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyariṉ iyalpiṉavāy alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: kallukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; kallukkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvāṟu muṭiyātu piṟavāṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka. 
u-m: piṉṉalkaṭitu, tuṉṉalkaṭitu; piṉṉaṟ kaṭumi, tuṉṉaṟ kaṭumi eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, meṉkaṇam vantavaḻi, piṉṉaṉ ñāṉṟatu; piṉṉaṉ ñāṟci eṉa melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭivaṉavum koḷka.	(81)
378.	veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiya ṉiliyum.
ituvum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) veyil eṉ kiḷavi maḻi iyal niliyum - veyil eṉṉum kiḷavi maḻi eṉṉum colliyalpiṉkaṇṇē (288) nilipeṟṟu attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: veyilattuk koṇṭāṉ; veyiliṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	(82)
379.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya vakara viṟuti
	muṟpaṭak kiḷanta vurupiya ṉiliyum.
itu, vakāravīṟṟup peyar nāṉkiṉuḷ cuṭṭumutal vakaram mūṉṟaṟkum vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya vakara iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻutti mutalākavuṭiya vakara vīṟṟuccol, muṟpaṭak kiḷanta urupu iyal niliyum - vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṟpaṭac coṉṉa urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpu nilipeṟṟu vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarum.
u-m:	avaṟṟukkōṭu, ivaṟṟukkōṭu, uvaṟṟuk kōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
‘muṟpaṭak kiḷanta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṟṟiṉōṭu iṉṉum peṟutal koḷka. 
u-m: avaṟṟiṉkōṭu, ivaṟṟiṉkōṭu, uvaṟṟiṉ kōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	        (83)
380.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yāyta mākum.
itu, mēlavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi alvaḻi āytam ākum - ac cuṭṭu mutalākiya vakaravīṟu vaṉkaṇam vantāl vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyallāta alvaḻikkaṇ avvakaram āytamāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: aḵkaṭiya, iḵkaṭiya, uḵkaṭiya; ciṟiya, tīya, periya eṉa varum.	   (84)
381.	melleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻut tākum.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku meṉkaṇattu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ a eḻuttu ākum - accuṭṭu mutalākiya vakaravīṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyinta viṭattu avvakaram avvam melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m:	aññāṇ, iññāṇ, uññāṇ; nūl, maṇi eṉa varum.	 (85)
382.	ēṉavi puṇari ṉiyalpeṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku iṭikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇattum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉavi puṇariṉ iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - ac cuṭṭu mutalākiya vakaravīṟu oḻinta iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantu puṇariṉ av vakaram tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: avyāḻ, ivyāḻ, uvyāḻ; vaṭṭu, aṭi, āṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.	(86)
383.	ēṉi vakaran toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oḻinta oṉṟaṟkum alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi vakaram toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu - oḻinta vakaravīṟu ñakara vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, allaṉa vaṟṟukkaṇ ukaramē peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: tevvukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; tevvukkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉavum varum.        			(87)
384.	ḻakāra viṟuti rakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, ḻakāravīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ḻakāra iṟuti rakāra iyaṟṟu - ḻakāra īṟṟup peyar vaṉkaṇam vantāl vēṟṟumikkaṇ rakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: pūḻkkāl; ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	 (88)
385.	tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ
	akkiṭi varuta lurittu mākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāḻ eṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ - tāḻ eṉṉum col kōl eṉṉum colloṭu puṇarum iṭattu, akkuiṭi varutalum urittu - valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyi iṭi vantu muṭitalum urittu. 
u-m: tāḻakkōl eṉa varum.	(89)
386.	tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tamiḻ eṉ kiḷaviyum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - tamiḻ eṉṉum collum ataṉōṭu oru taṉmittāy valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi akkum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: tamiḻakkūttu; cēri, tōṭṭam, paḷḷi eṉa varum.(90)
387.	kumiḻeṉ kiḷavi marappeya rāyiṉ
	pīreṉ kiḷaviyō ṭōriyaṟ ṟākum.
ituvum, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kumiḻ eṉ kiḷavi marappeyar āyiṉ - kumiḻ eṉṉum col kumiḻttal eṉṉum toḻilaṉṟi marappeya rāyiṉ, pīr eṉ kiḷaviyōṭu ōr iyaṟṟu ākum - pīr eṉ kiḷaviyōṭu ōr iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttum ammuc cāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: kumiḻṅkōṭu; kumiḻaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū
eṉavarum.
‘ōr iyaṟṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavaṟṟiṟkum im muṭipu koḷka. makiḻṅkōṭu eṉa varum.	(91)
388.	pāḻeṉ kiḷavi melleḻut tuṟaḻvē.
ituvum, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pāḻ eṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu uṟaḻvu - pāḻ eṉṉum col valleḻuttoṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
u-m:	pāḻkkiṇaṟu; pāḻṅkiṇaṟu; cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum. (`ēkāram’ - īṟṟaci.)	  (92)
389.	ēḻeṉ kiḷavi yurupiya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarkku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēḻ eṉ kiḷavi - ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇup peyaratu iṟuti, urupu iyal niliyum - urupu puṇarccikkaṇ coṉṉa iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē (195) nilipeṟṟu (aṉpeṟṟu) muṭiyum.
u-m: ēḻaṉ kāyam; cukku, tōri, payaṟu eṉa varum.
iyalpu valleḻuttu (384) iv vōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ (406) vīḻkka. (`kiḷavi’ āku peyar.)	 (93)
390. 	aḷavu niṟiyu meṇṇum varuvaḻi
	neṭumutal kuṟukalu mukaram varutalum
	kaṭinili yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka.
itu, mēlataṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇum varuvaḻi - (av ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar) aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇup peyarum (varumoḻiyāy) varumiṭattu, neṭumutal kuṟukalum ukaram varutalum āciriyarkku kaṭinili iṉṟu - neṭumutal kuṟukutalum (āṇṭu) ukaram varutalum āciriyarkku nīkkum nilimi iṉṟu.
u-m: eḻukalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; eḻukaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉavum; eḻumūṉṟu, eḻunāṉku eṉavum varum.
‘niliyiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇattup poruṭ peyarkkum im muṭipu koḷka.
u-m: eḻukaṭal; cili, tici, piṟappu eṉa varum.
(`ēkāram’ - aci. `āciriyarkku’ eṉpatu `āciriyarkka’ eṉṟāyiṟṟu.)	 (94)
391.	patteṉ kiḷavi yoṟṟiṭi keṭuvaḻi 
	niṟṟal vēṇṭu māytap puḷḷi.
itu, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pattu eṉ kiḷavi iṭi oṟṟu keṭuvaḻi āytap puḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - (av) ēḻ eṉpataṉoṭu pattu eṉpatu (puṇarum iṭattu appattu eṉ(ṉum) kiḷavi(yiṉ) iṭi oṟṟuk keṭuvaḻi āytamākiya puḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum.)
u-m: eḻupaḵtu eṉa varum.	(95)
392.	āyiram varuvaḻi yukaraṅ keṭumē.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram varuvaḻi ukaram keṭum - (av ēḻ) eṉpatu āyiram eṉpatu varumiṭattu (muṉpeṟṟa) ukaram peṟātu muṭiyum.
u-m: eḻāyiram eṉa varum. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci)	(96)
393. 	nūṟūrntu varūu māyirak kiḷavikkuk
	kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukka miṉṟē.
ituvum, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu muḻuvatum vilakku kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu ūrntu varum āyirak kiḷavikku - (av ēḻ) eṉpatu nūṟu eṉṉum col ūrntu varukiṉṟa āyirak kiḷaviyākiya nūṟāyiram eṉpataṟku, kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukkam iṉṟu - (muṉ) kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutal iṉṟu.
u-m: ēḻ nūṟāyiram eṉa varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟuki, ukaram peṟṟu eḻu nūṟāyiram eṉṟum ām. iv vilēciṉāṉē ēḻāyiram eṉṟum ām.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, iyalpukaṇattu muṭipu koḷka. eḻuñāyiṟu, eḻunāḷ eṉa varum. (`ēkāram’ - īṟṟaci)  	(97)
394.	aiyam palleṉa varūu miṟuti
	alpeya reṇṇu māyiya ṉiliyum.
ituvum, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu muḻuvatum vilakku kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti alpeyar eṇṇum  -  (avvēḻ eṉpataṉmuṉ)  ai eṉṟum, am eṉṟum, pal eṉṟum varukiṉṟa iṟutikaḷiyuṭiya (poruṭpeyar) allāta eṇṇup peyarākiya tāmari veḷḷam āmpal eṉpaṉavum (vantāl); a iyal niliyum -  (neṭumutal kuṟukkam iṉṟi ukaram peṟātu) avviyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu  muṭiyum.
u-m:	ēḻtāmari, ēḻveḷḷam, ēḻāmpal eṉa varum.	 (`ākāram’ ceyyuḷ vikāram.)	(98)
395. 	uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
ituvum, mēlataṟku eytiyatu oruvaḻi vilakkip piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir muṉ variṉum - (av vēḻ eṉpatu aḷavu mutaliya peyarkaḷuḷ) uyir mutalmoḻi (muṉ) variṉum, a iyal tiriyātu - (neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram varātu) av viyalpil tiriyātu muṭiyum.
u-m:	ēḻakal, ēḻuḻakku, ēḻoṉṟu, ēḻiraṇṭu eṉa varum. (`ākāram’ ceyyuḷ vikāram)	(99)
396. 	kīḻeṉ kiḷavi yuṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, iv īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟṟumikkaṇ vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kīḻ eṉ kiḷavi uṟaḻa tōṉṟum - kīḻ eṉṉum col uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟi muṭiyum.
u-m: kīḻkuḷam, kīḻkkuḷam eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟukātu ukaram varutalum koḷka. (āṅku) iyipu valleḻuttu iv vōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ (406) vīḻkka.
u-m: kīḻukuḷam; cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum.      (100)
397.	ḷakāra viṟuti ṇakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, ḷakāra īṟṟiṟku ṇakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipōṭu iyiya vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ḷakāra iṟuti ṇakāra iyaṟṟu - ḷakāra īṟṟup peyar (vēṟṟumikkaṇ) ṇakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy (303) (vaṉkaṇam vantāl ḷakāram ṭakāramāyt tirintu) muṭiyum.
u-m: muṭkuṟi; ciṟi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum. 	(101)
398. 	melleḻut tiyiyiṉ ṇakāra mākum.
itu, mēlataṟku meṉkaṇattu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṇakāram ākum - (ḷakāra īṟu) melleḻuttu (varumoḻiyāy vantu) iyiyiṉ ṇakāramāy(t tirintu) muṭiyum.
u-m: muṇñeri; nuṉi, muri eṉa varum.
itaṉi vēṟṟumi iṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttuk kōṭaṟ kaṇ ciṅkanōkkāka vittaliṉ, alvaḻikkum im muṭipu koḷka.
u-m: muṇñerintatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉa varum. (102)
399. 	alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēlataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām uṟaḻ eṉa moḻipa - (ḷakāra īṟṟu) alvaḻikaḷellām (tirintum tiriyātum) uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: muḷkaṭitu, muṭkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuṇavēṟṟumikkaṇṇum iv vuṟaḻcci koḷka.
u-m: muḷkuṟumi, muṭkuṟumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum; kōḷkaṭumi, kōṭkaṭumi eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum koḷka.
u-m: atōṭkoṇṭāṉ, itōṭkoṇṭāṉ, utōṭ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	   (103)
400.	āyta niliyalum varinili yiṉṟē
	takaram varūuṅ kāli yāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āytam niliyalum varinili iṉṟu - (ḷakāram ṭakāramāyē tiriyātu) āytamāy(t tirintu) niṟṟalum variyum nilimi iṉṟu, takaram varum kāli - takara mutalmoḻi varum kālattu.
u-m: muḵṭītu, muṭṭītu eṉa varum. 	(104)
(ēkāram - īṟṟaci. ukara nīṭṭam ceyyuḷ vikāram. ‘āṉ’ iṭiccol.‘akaram’ cāriyi. varinilimi - kaṭiyum nilimi)
401.	neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpā kunavum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi vēṟṟumi niliyalum
	pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpākunavum - (ḷakāram) neṭiyataṉ iṟuti tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavaṟṟiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi vēṟṟumi niliyalum - vēṟṟumi allāta alvaḻiyiṭattu vēṟṟumi(yiṉ iyalpuṭiyaṉa vāyt tirintu) muṭitaliyum, pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumār uḷa - pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum moḻikaḷum uḷa.
u-m: vāḷkaṭitu, kōḷkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; tōṭkaṭitu; nāṭkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum varum.
‘pōṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, utaḷaṅkāy, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa ammuppeṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka.
(`ār’, `ē’ acikaḷ. ammu - am cāriyi.)	(105)
402.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟ peyariyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkku vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum alvaḻikkaṇṇum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - (ḷakāra īṟṟut) toḻiṟpeyarellām vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum alvaḻik kaṇṇum ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṉa vāy (297, 298) vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi valleḻuttum ukaramum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum vantavaḻi ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: tuḷḷuk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; tuḷḷukkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ iruvaḻiyum ivvā ṟaṉṟip piṟavāṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
u-m: kōḷkaṭitu, kōṭkaṭitu; vāḷkaṭitu, vāṭkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; kōḷ kaṭumi, kōṭkaṭumi; vāḷ kaṭumi, vāṭkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum varum. 
īṇṭu, vāḷ eṉṟatu - collutali.	(106)
403.	iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliya ṉiliyum.
ituvum, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip 
piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iruḷ eṉ kiḷavi veyil iyal niliyum - iruḷ eṉṉum col (vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇ) veyil eṉṉum colliṉ iyalpilē (niṉṟu, attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum).
u-m:	iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ, iruḷiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
404. 	puḷḷum vaḷḷun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
ituvum, av vīṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷum vaḷḷum toḻiṟpeyar iyala - puḷ eṉṉum collum vaḷ eṉṉum collum (vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum alvaḻik kaṇṇum) ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟavāy (vaṉ kaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭik kaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum) muṭiyum.
u-m:	puḷḷukkaṭitu, vaḷḷukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; puḷḷuk kaṭumi, vaḷḷukkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉavum varum.
itaṉit ‘toḻiṟpeyarellām’ eṉṟataṉpiṉ viyāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, iruvaḻiyum vēṟṟumit tiripu eyti muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
u-m:	puṭkaṭitu, vaṭkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu; ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; puṭkaṭumi, vaṭkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, paḷ eṉpataṉkaṇṇum, kaḷ eṉpataṉ kaṇṇum iruvaḻiyum ivviru muṭipu peṟṟamiyum koḷka.
paḷḷukkaṭitu, kaḷḷukkaṭitu; paḷḷukkaṭumi, kaḷḷuk kaṭumi eṉavum; paṭkaṭitu, kaṭkaṭitu; paṭkaṭumi, kaṭkaṭumi eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(108)
405. 	makka ḷeṉṉum peyarnilik kiḷavi
	takkavaḻi yaṟintu valittalu murittē.
itu, makkaḷ eṉṉum uyartiṇip peyarukku ‘uyirīṟākiya vuyartiṇip peyarum’ (toki. 11) eṉpataṉuḷ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarnili kiḷaviyum - makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarccolliṉ iṟuti, takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalum urittu - (iyalpēyaṉṟit) takka iṭam aṟintu valloṟṟāy(t tirintu) muṭitalum ām.
‘takkavaḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, am makkaḷ uṭampu, uyir nīṅkiya kālattu im muṭipu eṉak koḷka.
u-m: makkaṭki; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.	(109)
(‘kiḷavi’ ākupeyar. ‘ēkāram’ īṟṟaci)
406. 	uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
	kaṇṭuceyaṟ kuriyavi kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē.
iḵtu ivvōttiṟkup puṟanaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uṇarak kūṟiya - uṇarak kūṟappaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu(kaḷ), puṇar iyal maruṅkiṉ - (varumoḻiyoṭu) puṇarum iyal piṭattu, kaṇṭu ceyaṟku uriyavi kaṇṇiṉar koḷal - (mēl muṭitta muṭipukaḷēyaṉṟi vaḻakkiṉuḷ) kaṇṭu muṭittaṟkuriya piṟa muṭipukaḷik karutikkoṇṭu muṭikka.
u-m:  viḻaṉ kuḷam; cēṟu, taṟi, paḻaṉam eṉa ivi, ṉakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. poṉṉappattam eṉpatu avvīṟu akkuppeṟṟu muṭintatu.
nīr kuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu (eṉpatu) rakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu.
vēr kuṟitu; vērkkuṟitu (eṉpatu) av vīṟṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci.
amparkkoṇṭāṉ, imparkkoṇṭāṉ, umpark koṇṭāṉ, empark koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟuḷ urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu.
takarkkuṭṭi eṉpatu av vīṟṟu irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokiyākiya alvaḻi muṭipu.
vaṭacārkkūri, teṉcārkkūri eṉpaṉa avvīṟṟu ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūu muṭipu.
ucilaṅkōṭu, eliyālaṅkōṭu eṉpaṉa lakāra īṟṟu ammup pēṟu.
kallampāṟi eṉpatu av vīṟṟu alvaḻi ammuppēṟu.
aḻalattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu avvīṟṟu attuppēṟu.
aḻukkaṟpōr eṉpatu av vīṟṟu alvaḻit tiripu.
yāḻkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉpaṉa ḻakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu.
vīḻkuṟitu, vīḻkkuṟitu eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci.
tāḻappāvi eṉpatu av vīṟṟu alvaḻi akkuppēṟu.
iṉi ‘uṇarak kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuḷattiṉpuṟam, marat  tiṉpuṟam eṉa urupiṟku eytiya attōṭu iṉpeṟutalum koḷka.
iṉik ‘kaṇṇiṉar’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, orunāḷik kuḻavi eṉa ḷakara īṟu ai eṉṉum cāriyiyum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa koḷka.
piṟavum, iv vōttiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉ akattu muṭittuk koḷka.	(110)
eṭṭāvatu - puḷḷimayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal
kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟu niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum iyal piṉi yuṇarttuvatu ivviyalākum. neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟum kuṟṟiyalukaram varum eṉa moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiṉār. toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟukaratti ayaliluḷḷa eḻuttu vakiyāl uyirttoṭar, iṭittoṭar, āytat toṭar, vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar eṉa aintākappakuttu, avaṟṟoṭu neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉvaruṅ kuṟṟukarattiyuṅ kūṭṭi aṟuvakippaṭuttu viḷakkukiṉṟār.
yaraḻamuṉṉark kacatapaṅañanama īroṟṟākivaruṅ kuṟṟukaraṅka ḷ oruvāṟṟāṉ iṭiyoṟṟukkaḷōṭu toṭarntuvariṉum kuṟṟiya lukarattiyaṭuttu nērē toṭarntaṉa allavātaliṉ avi iṭit toṭarākak koḷḷappaṭāveṉṟum, āṟīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum iruvaḻiyilum keṭātu niṟintē niṟkumeṉṟum, vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi varumiṭattu muṉkūṟiya (arimāttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟukum) iyalpil niṟṟalumuritteṉṟum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟiṉmuṉ yakara mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkeṭa aṅku ōr ikaram vantu kuṟuki niṟkumeṉṟum ivviyaliṉ mutal aintu cūttirattāṉum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyal piṉi āciriyar potuvakiyāṟ kūṟippōntār. 6-mutal 17-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟiṉār. 18-mutal kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu alvaḻippuṇarcciyiṉit toṭaṅkik kūṟukiṉṟār.
uṇmit taṉmiyi yuṇarttum uṇṭu eṉṉuñcol valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ iṟutiniṉṟa ukaram mey yoṭuṅkeṭa ṇakaram ḷakaramākat tiriyumeṉṟum, kuṟṟukara vīṟṟut ticippeyarkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu perunticiyum tammiṟ puṇarumiṭattu ēyeṉṉuñcāriyi iṭiyē vantumuṭiyu meṉṟum, kuṟṟukaravīṟṟup perunticikaḷōṭu kōṇat ticikaḷ puṇarumiṭattu iṟutiyukaram meyyoṭuṅ koṭumeṉṟum avvaḻi teṟku eṉpataṉ ṟakaram ṉakaramākat tiriyumeṉṟum kūṟuvar āciriyar.
kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇup peyariyalpiṉi 28-ām cūttira mutalākat toṭaṅkik kūṟukiṉṟār. pattu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ muṉ oṉṟumutal eṭṭuvariyuḷḷa eṇkaḷum āyirameṉṉum eṇṇum niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum vantu puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 28-mutal 30-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ uṇarttuvaṉa oṉṟu mutal eṭṭuvariyuḷḷa kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇum peyarkaḷiṉmuṉ pattu vantu puṇartali 31-mutal 38-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvaṉa. oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉmuṉ pattu vantu puṇaruṅkāl nilimoḻiyākiya oṉpatil okarattiṟku mēlākat takara meytōṉṟit toṉpatu eṉṟākip patu keṭṭu ṉakaram ṇakaramākat tirintu iraṭṭittut toṇṇ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, varumoḻiyākiya paḵteṉpatil pakaravuyirmeyyum āytamumkeṭṭu ūkāram tōṉṟi iṟutiyiluḷḷa ‘tu’ eṉpataṉ takaram ṟakaramākat tirintu ūṟu eṉṟākit toṇṇuhṟu eṉa muṭiyumeṉṟum, ivviyal 39-ām cūttirattāl āciriyar viritturippar. mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpatu variyuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum vantu puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 40-mutal 53-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum, nūṟeṉṉuñcol varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarvataṉi 54, 55, 56, 57-ām cūttiraṅkaḷālum, āyiram eṉṉum eṇ taṉittum nūṟeṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttum puṇartali 58-mutal 65-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum kūṟuvar āciriyar. oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ nūṟu eṉṉum eṇ vantupuṇariṉ muṟkūṟiyavāṟu nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟa oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉ okarattiṉ muṉṉē takaramey oṉṟu tōṉṟit toṉpatu eṉṟākip patukeṭṭu ṉakaramey ḷakara meyyāka iraṭṭittut toḷḷ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉpataṉ mutalil niṉṟa nakaramey keṭa ammeyyiṉ mēlēṟiya ūkāram ākāramākattirintu ataṉi yaṭuttu ikaravuyirum rakaravuyir meyyum iṭiyētōṉṟi īṟṟiluḷḷa ṟakara vukaraṅkeṭṭu makarameytōṉṟi āyiram eṉṟākit toḷḷāyiram eṉa muṭiyumeṉṟum 57-ām cūttirattāl tolkāppiyaṉār virittukkūṟiyuḷḷār. toṇṇuhṟu, toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum puṇarmoḻikaḷukku āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟum puṇarcci muṟikaḷ valintu kūṟuvaṉavākavē amintuḷḷaṉa veṉṟum oṉpatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭat tolkāppiyarē toṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarit tam nūlil āṇṭiruttalāl toṇṭu+pattu = toṇṇuhṟu eṉṟum, toṇṭu+āyiram = toḷḷāyiram eṉṟum nilimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu puṇarcciviti kūṟuvatē moḻiyiyalpukku ottatākumeṉṟum paritimāṟkaliñar kūṟum koḷki iṅku cintikkat takkatākum.
nūṟu eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka ataṉ muṉ oṉṟu mutal oṉpatum aviyūr pattum aḷavum niṟiyum vantu puṇarum muṟiyiṉi 66-mutal 68-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ viḷakku vaṉavām. ōṉṟu mutal eṭṭiṉiyūrnta paḵteṉṉum eṇṇup peyaruṭaṉ oṉṟu mutal oṉpateṇkaḷum āyiramum aḷavum niṟiyum puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 69, 70, 71-ām cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭ peyarkaḷ puṇarumiyalpiṉi 72, 73-ām cūttiraṅkaḷālum iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu variyuḷḷa eṇkaḷiṉmuṉ aḷavu, niṟi, eṇ eṉpavaṟṟukkuriya mā eṉṉuñcol puṇartali 74-ām cūttirattālum āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvar. eṇṇuppeyar kaḷuḷ mikka eṇṇōṭu kuṟinta eṇ vantu puṇartali ummittokiyākavum, kuṟinta eṇṇōṭu mikka eṇ varutalip paṇput tokiyākavumkoṇṭu āciriyar viti kūṟiyuḷḷami ivaṇ karutaṟkuriyatākum. lakāra ṉakāra vīṟṟuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟuvatu 75-ām cūttiram. iv vatikārattu nilimoḻi varumoḻiceytu puṇarkkappaṭā moḻikaḷ iviyeṉat tokutturippatu 76-ām cūttiramākum. ivviyaliṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa 77-ām cūttiram eḻuttatikārattiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyākum.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 16-166


oṉpatāvatu
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ  kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal eṉappaṭṭatu.
407.	īreḻut torumoḻi yuyirttoṭa riṭittoṭar
	āytat toṭarmoḻi vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭar
	āyiru mūṉṟē yukaraṅ kuṟukiṭaṉ.
it taliccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, ik kuṟṟiyalukaram varum iṭattiṟkup peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īr eḻuttu oru moḻi - iraṇṭu eḻuttāṉākiya oru moḻiyum, uyirttoṭar - uyirttoṭar moḻiyum, iṭittoṭar - iṭittoṭar moḻiyum, āytattoṭar - āytattoṭar moḻiyum, vaṉtoṭar - vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum, meṉtoṭar - meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum, a iru mūṉṟu - ākiya av vāṟu eṉṟu collappaṭum, ukaram kuṟuku iṭaṉ - ukaram kuṟuki varum iṭaṉ.
u-m: nāku, vāku, teḷku, eḵku, kokku, kuraṅku eṉa varum.
(nāku - iḷamaram. teḷku - oru pūcci, eḵku - vēl, `toṭar’ nāṉkum ākupeyar. cuṭṭiṉ nīṭṭamum vakara uṭampaṭumey yakara uṭampaṭumeyyāṉatum ceyyuḷ vikāram. `ēkāram’ tēṟṟēkāram. naṉṉūlār pōla innūlār valloṟṟiṉ mītu ēṟiya ukaramē kuṟukum eṉṟu kūṟālami kāṇka.)	 (1)
408. 	avaṟṟuḷ
	īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi yiṭittoṭa rākā.
iḵtu, avvāṟaṉuḷ ōr īṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ īr oṟṟu toṭarmoḻi - avaṟṟuḷ īr oṟṟut toṭarmoḻi(kaḷil), iṭittoṭar ākā - iṭittoṭar ākā, (vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum ām.)
u-m: īrkku; moympu eṉa varum.	
sa(iṭiyil) iraṇṭu oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ toṭarntu varum (mūvaki) moḻikaḷil iṭittoṭar moḻikaḷ ukaram kuṟukum iṭaṉ ākā eṉpatē nēr uri.
u-m: veyytu eṉavarum. 
ivviṭit toṭarmoḻikaḷ “alarpeyytu vūrāraḻippiṉu meṉṉoṭu, malarkoyytu vūṭu makiḻntu” eṉṟatu pōṉṟa ceyyuḷiṉ kaṇṇē nikaḻum. iraṇṭoṟṟu iṭiviṭāmiyik kuṟittu niṉṟatu.]	(2)
409. 	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	ellā viṟutiyu mukara niṟiyum.
iḵtu, eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉum - alvaḻiyic collum iṭattum, vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum, ellā iṟuti ukaramum niṟiyum - āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukara mum niṟintē niṟkum.
u-m	nāku kaṭitu; nāku kaṭumi; varaku kaṭitu; varaku kaṭumi eṉa varum.
(`niliyum’ eṉṟa pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum (oṟṟukkaḷ iraṇṭu toṭarntuvarum iṭit toṭarmoḻikaḷ allāta) aṟuvaki moḻikaḷiṉ iṟutikkaṇṇum kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟkum eṉṟu urittalē poruttam uṭittu.)	(3)
410.	valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu 	varuvaḻi
	tolli yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.
iḵtu, eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi - (av vāṟīṟṟuḷḷum) valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varum iṭattu, tolli iyaṟki niliyalum urittu - muṉ (eḻut. 12) kūṟiya iyaṟki niṟṟalum urittu.
u-m: kokkuk kaṭitu, kokkuk kaṭumi eṉa varum. 	
(`ēkāram’ - īṟṟaci. `tolli iyaṟki’ eṉpataṟku īṇṭuk kūṟum ukaram (atāvatu kuṟṟiyalukaram) taṉatu muntiya taṉmiyil (atāvatu muṟṟukaramāka) niṟṟalum urittu, nillāmiyum urittu eṉṟu urittalē poruttam uṭittu.
u-m: kokku kaṭitu; kokku kaṭumi eṉpavaṟṟil muṟṟukaram olittalum, kokkukkaṭitu; kokkukkaṭumi eṉpavaṟṟil kuṟṟukaram olittalum kāṇka.)	(4)
411. 	yakaram varuvaḻi yikaraṅ kuṟukum
	ukarak kiḷavi tuvarattōṉ ṟātu.
itu, kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyuḷ varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. (itu, kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalikaram ām iṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟutal mikapporuttam uṭittu.)
(i-ḷ.) yakaram varuvaḻi ukarakkiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu - yakara mutalmoḻi varum iṭattu nilimoḻi ukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu; ikaram kuṟukum - (āṇṭu) ōr ikaram (vantu) kuṟukum.
u-m: nākiyātu, varakiyātu, teḷkiyātu, eḵkiyātu, kokkiyātu, kuraṅkiyātu eṉa varum.
itaṉāṉē, ākāra īṟu akaram peṟṟāṟ (uyir. mayaṅ. cūt. 24) pōla āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum ikaramum peṟṟu yakara mutalmoḻiyoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟiṟṟāyiṟṟu.	(5)

412.	īreḻuttu moḻiyu muyirttoṭar moḻiyum
	vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉoṟṟiṭi yiṉamikat
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya āṟaṉuḷḷum muṉ niṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) īr eḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum - īr eḻuttu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum uyirt toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, vēṟṟumi āyiṉ - vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci yāyiṉ, iṉa oṟṟu iṭimika valleḻuttu mikuti tōṟṟam vēṇṭum - iṉamākiya oṟṟu iṭiyilē mika valleḻuttu mikuti tōṉṟi muṭital vēṇṭum.
u-m: yāṭṭukkāl; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; muyiṟṟukkāl; ciṉi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv viraṇṭaṟkum iyalpu kaṇattu muṭipum koḷka.
u-m: yāṭṭu ñāṟci, muyiṟṟu ñāṟci; nīṭci, māṭci, yāppu, valimi, aṭivu, āṭṭam eṉa varum. (moḻi iraṇṭum ākupeyar.)	(6)
413. 	oṟṟiṭi yiṉamikā moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	attiṟat tilli valleḻuttu mikalē.
iḵtu, eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉa oṟṟu iṭi mikā moḻiyumār uḷa - iṉa oṟṟu iṭi(yil) mikku muṭiyāta moḻikaḷum uḷa; valleḻuttu mikal a tiṟattu illi - valleḻuttu mikku muṭital ak kūṟṟuḷ illi.
u-m:	nākukāl; ciṉi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; varakukatir; ciṉi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘attiṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupiṟku eytiya cāriyi poruṭku eytiyavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. 
u-m: yāṭṭiṉ kāl, muyiṟṟiṉkāl, nākiṉkāl, varakiṉkāl eṉa varum.
(`ār’ - aci. ēkāram iraṇṭum - īṟṟaci.)	(7)
414.	iṭiyoṟṟut toṭaru māytat toṭarum
	naṭiyā yiyala veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, iṭiniṉṟa iraṇṭiṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭi oṟṟuttoṭarum āytattoṭarum naṭi - iṭi oṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum naṭakkum iṭattu, a iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar - mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipiṉi uṭiya eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	teḷkukāl; ciṟi, tali, puṟam eṉavum; eḵku kāl; ciṟi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum. (cuṭṭiṉ nīṭṭam ceyyuḷ vikāram.)	(8)
415. 	vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
	vanta valleḻut toṟṟiṭi mikumē
	melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi melloṟ 	ṟellām
	valloṟ ṟiṟuti kiḷiyoṟ ṟākum.
itu, piṉ niṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - vaṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum meṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiya lukara īṟum, vanta valleḻuttu oṟṟu iṭi mikum - (varumoḻiyāy) vanta valloṟṟu iṭiyilē mikku muṭiyum; melloṟṟu toṭar moḻi melloṟṟu ellām - (av viraṇṭu īṟṟiṉuḷ) melloṟṟut toṭar moḻi(kkaṇ niṉṟa) melloṟṟellām, iṟuti valloṟṟu kiḷi oṟṟu ākum - iṟuti valloṟṟum kiḷi valloṟṟum āy muṭiyum.
u-m:	kokkukkāl;ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉavum; eṭkuk kuṭṭi; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘vanta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivviraṇṭaṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭku eytiyavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. 
u-m: kokkiṉkāl, kuraṅkiṉkāl eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṟampiṟpāri eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa melloṟṟut tiriyāmiyum koḷka.
oṟṟu eṉṟa mikutiyāṉ iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum kurakkuñāṟci; nīṭci, māṭci, yāppu, valimi, aṭivu, āṭṭam eṉa melloṟṟut tiritalum koḷka.
(ēkāram - īṟṟaci, `moḻiyum’ eṉṟa iraṇṭum ākupeyar.)	 (9)
416.	marappeyark kiḷavik kammē cāriyi.
iḵtu, av vīṟu iraṇṭaṟkum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) marappeyar kiḷavikkuc cāriyi am - (vaṉṟoṭariṉ kaṇṇum meṉṟoṭariṉkaṇṇum) marap peyarākiya colliṟku (varum) cāriyi am(muc cāriyi.)
u-m: kuruntaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; vēppaṅ kōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum varum.	           (10)
417. 	melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyaru muḷavē.
itu, meṉṟoṭar moḻikku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuk kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyarum uḷa - mel loṟṟu valloṟṟāyt tiriyātu muṭiyum marappeyarum uḷa.
u-m:	kuruntaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; puṉkaṅ kōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum varum.
maṟṟu, itu nilimoḻit toḻili nilimoḻi vilakku mātaliṉ (eḻut. 229) cāriyi vakuppavē muṭiyum piṟa eṉiṉ, itu nilimoḻiyiṉ uḷ toḻilākaliṉ avvāṟu vilakkuṇṇā teṉpatu karuttu. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	(11)
418.	īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟut toṭarum
	ammiṭi varaṟku muriyavi yuḷavē
	ammara poḻuku moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, īreḻuttu orumoḻikkum vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkum eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īr eḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟut toṭarum - īreḻuttu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaramum vaṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaramum, am iṭi varaṟkum uriyavi uḷa - muṉ muṭittup pōnta muṭipukaḷaṉṟi ammuccāriyi iṭivantu muṭittaṟku uriyaṉavum uḷa. yāṇṭeṉiṉ, am marapu oḻukum moḻivayiṉ āṉa - av vilakkaṇam naṭakkum moḻiyiṭattu.
u-m:	ēṟaṅkōḷ (cīvaka. 489), vaṭṭampōr eṉa varum.
‘uḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, teṅkaṅkāy, payaṟṟaṅkāy eṉa vaṉṟoṭar allaṉavaṟṟiṟku am(mup)pēṟu koḷka.
‘ammara poḻukum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aracakkaṉṉi, muracakkaṭippu eṉa akkuppēṟum koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, iruṭṭattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉṉum attuppēṟum koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, mayilāppiṟ koṟṟaṉ, paṟampiṟpāri eṉa iṉpēṟum koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, kariyataṉkōṭu eṉa aṉpēṟum koḷka. 
(ēkāram - īṟṟaci. `āṉ’ iṭiccol; akaram cāriyi. `moḻiyum’ `toṭarum’ ākupeyar. `am’ cāriyiyi `ammu’c cāriyiyeṉakkūṟuvatu vaḻakku.)	 (12)
419. 	oṟṟunili tiriyā takkoṭu varūum
	akkiḷi moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu, meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkuta lum eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṟṟu nili tiriyātu akkoṭu varum a kiḷi moḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa - oṟṟu muṉ niṉṟa nili tiriyātu akkuc cāriyiyoṭum piṟa cāriyiyoṭum varum ak kiḷi yeḻuttu moḻiyum uḷa eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar.
u-m: kuṉṟak kūki, maṉṟap peṇṇi eṉa varum.
ummiyāṉ, koṅkattuḻavu, vaṅkattu vāṇikam eṉa attup peṟṟaṉa.
‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟunili tiriyā atikārattuk kaṇ iyipu valleḻuttu vilakkuka.
‘akkiḷimoḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pārppaṉakkaṉṉi, pārppaṉac cēri eṉa aṉṉum akkum vantaṉa koḷka.
(ukara nīṭṭam ceyyuḷ vikāram. `akkiḷi - ākupeyar.)		  (13)
420.	eṇṇup peyarkkiḷavi yurupiya ṉiliyum.
itu, kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyaroṭu poruṭpeyar muṭikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇuppeyar kiḷavi urupu iyal niliyum - eṇṇuppeyarākiya coṟkaḷ urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē (199) niṉṟu (aṉpeṟṟu) muṭiyum.
u-m: oṉṟaṉkāyam, iraṇṭaṉkāyam; cukku, tōri, payaṟu eṉa varum.	 (14)
421.	vaṇṭum peṇṭu miṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
itu, meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟa muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉ oṭu civaṇum - vaṇṭu eṉṉum collum peṇṭu eṉṉum collum iṉcāriyi yoṭu porunti muṭiyum.
u-m: vaṇṭiṉkāl, peṇṭiṉkāl eṉavarum. 	(15)
422. peṇṭeṉ kiḷavik kaṉṉum variyār.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peṇṭu eṉ kiḷavikku aṉṉum variyār - peṇṭu eṉṉum colliṟku (iṉṉēyaṉṟi) aṉ cāriyiyum variyār (āciriyar).
u-m: peṇṭaṉki eṉa varum. 	(16)
423. 	yāte ṉiṟutiyuñ cuṭṭu mutalākiya
	āyta viṟutiyu murupiya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu, īreḻuttu orumoḻiyuḷ oṉṟaṟkum, cuṭṭu mutal āytat toṭarmoḻikkum vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yātu eṉ iṟutiyum cuṭṭu mutal ākiya āyta iṟutiyum - yātu eṉṉum īṟum cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, urupu iyal niliyum - urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉ(kaṇṇē) (201) niṉṟu cuṭṭu mutal moḻikaḷ (aṉpeṟṟum) āytam keṭṭum muṭiyum.
u-m: yātaṉkōṭu, ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉkōṭu eṉa varum. 	(17)
424. 	muṉṉuyir varumiṭat tāytap puḷḷi
	maṉṉal vēṇṭu malvaḻi yāṉa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya īṟṟuḷ cuṭṭumutal īṟṟu ukarattiṟku oruvaḻi alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉ uyir varum iṭattu - (avaṟṟuḷ cuṭṭu mutal āytat toṭarmoḻi ukara īṟu taṉ) muṉṉē uyir varum iṭattu, āytappuḷḷi maṉṉal vēṇṭum - āytap puḷḷi (muṉpu pōlak keṭātu) nilipeṟṟu muṭital vēṇṭum, alvaḻiyāṉa - alvaḻik kaṇ.
u-m:	aḵtaṭi, iḵtaṭi, uḵtaṭi; āṭi, ili eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘muṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum uyir mutal moḻi vanta iṭattu aḵtaṭipu, aḵtāṭṭam eṉa āytam keṭāmi koḷka.
(`āṇ’ eṉpatu - vēṟṟumi mayakkam. akaram - cāriyi.)		(18)
425.	ēṉimuṉ variṉē tāṉili yiṉṟē.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkup piṟa kaṇattoṭu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi muṉvariṉ tāṉ niliyiṉṟu - (accuṭṭu mutal ukara īṟu uyirkkaṇam oḻinta) piṟa kaṇaṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ av vāytam niliyiṉṟi muṭiyum.
u-m: atu kaṭitu, itu kaṭitu, utu kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu eṉa varum. (muṉṉiya ēkāram - aci, piṉṉiyatu - īṟṟaci.)	           (19)
426. 	allatu kiḷappi ṉellā moḻiyum
	colliya paṇpi ṉiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu, āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum, alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉ - alvaḻiyic collum iṭattu, ellā moḻiyum - āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum, colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟki ākum - mēṟcolliya paṇpiṉiyuṭiya iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	nāku kaṭitu, varaku kaṭitu, teḷku kaṭitu, eḵku kaṭitu, kuraṅku kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellā moḻiyum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, viṉiccollum viṉik kuṟippuc collum iyalpāy muṭintaṉa koḷka. 
u-m: kiṭantatu kutiri, karitu kutiri eṉa varum.
‘colliya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput toki muṭipukoḷka. u-m: kāṭṭukkāṉam, kuruṭṭerutu eṉavarum
‘paṇpiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ai eṉṉum cāriyi peṟṟu varum alvaḻi muṭipum koḷka. u-m: aṉṟik kūttaṉ, paṇṭic cāṉṟār, ōr yāṭṭi yāṉi, maṟṟi yāṉi eṉa varum.  (20)
427. 	valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu 	mikumē.
iḵtu, av oṟṟuḷḷum oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi - valloṟṟut toṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, valleḻuttu mikum - (valleḻuttu varuvaḻi) valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: kokkukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
(`valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi’ eṉpatu ākupeyar. `ēkāram’ - īṟṟaci.)	  (21)
428. 	cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
	yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
	āyiya ṟiriyā valleḻut tiyaṟki.
ituvum, av īṟṟuḷḷum oṉṟaṉkaṇ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu ciṉi nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - cuṭṭākiya ciṉiyeḻuttu nīṇṭa meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, yā viṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉā mutalākiya meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, valleḻuttu iyaṟki a iyal tiriyā - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākiya av viyalpil tiriyā(tu muṭiyum.)
u-m:	āṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, īṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, ūṅkuk koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkukkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ak kuṟṟukara īṟṟu viṉiyecca muṭipu koḷka. cettukkiṭantāṉ, iruntukoṇṭāṉ eṉa varum. 	 (22)
(meṉṟoṭar moḻi iraṇṭum - ākupeyar. akaranīṭṭam - ceyyuḷ vikāram.)
429. 	yāviṉā moḻiyē yiyalpu mākum.
itu, mēlavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉmēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yā viṉā moḻi iyalpum ākum - (avaṟṟuḷ) yā viṉā moḻi (mēṟkūṟiya vikāramēyaṉṟi) iyalpāyum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: yāṅku koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	 (23)
430. 	annāṉ moḻiyun tannili tiriyā.
itu, mēlavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻic ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) a nāl moḻiyum - (cuṭṭu mutal mūṉṟum yā mutal moḻiyumākiya) an nāṉku moḻiyum, tam nili tiriyā - tam melloṟṟu nili tirintu valloṟṟu ākātu muṭiyum.
‘tannili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, melloṟṟut tiriyātu mikku muṭivaṉa piṟavum koḷka.
u-m:	aṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, iṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, uṅkuk koṇṭāṉ, eṅkukkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘yāmoḻi’ eṉṉātu ‘viṉā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka. muntu koṇṭāṉ, paṇṭu koṇṭāṉ, iṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, aṉṟu koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.  (`ēkāram’ aci.)	(24)
431.	uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi yuṇmi ceppiṉ
	munti yiṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum
	mēṉili yoṟṟē ḷakāra mātalum
	āmuṟi yiraṇṭu murimiyu muṭittē
	valleḻuttu varūuṅ kāli yāṉa.
itu, meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuḷ oru viṉikkuṟippu moḻikku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uṇṭu eṉ kiḷavi uṇmi ceppiṉ - uṇṭu eṉṉum col (uṇṭu eṉṉum toḻil oḻiya) uṇmi eṉṉum paṇpu uṇara niṟkum iṭattu, munti iṟuti meyyoṭum keṭutalum, mēlnili oṟṟu ḷakāram ātalum - muṟpaṭṭa īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram (tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa) meyyoṭum keṭutalum ataṟku mēl niṉṟa ṇakāra oṟṟu ḷakāram ātalumākiya, a muṟi iraṇṭum urimiyum uṭittu - am muṟimiyuṭiya iraṇṭaṉiyum urittātalum uṭittu, valleḻuttu varuṅkāli - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi varuṅkālattu.
valleḻuttatikāram vārā niṟpa, (‘valleḻuttu varūuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ) iv irumuṭipum uḷḷatu, pakara mutalmoḻi vantāl; maṟṟi mūṉṟu eḻuttiṉkaṇṇum īṟu keṭātē niṉṟu muṭiyum eṉṟu koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, iyalpukaṇattu iṟuti keṭātē muṭital koḷka.
u-m:  uḷ poruḷ, uṇṭu poruḷ eṉavum; uṇṭu kāṇam, uṇṭu cākkāṭu, uṇṭu tāmari eṉavum; uṇṭu ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭi, āṭi eṉavum varum.
‘uḷporuḷ’ eṉpatu paṇputtoki muṭipu aṉṟō eṉiṉ, aḵtu ōci oṟṟumippaṭac collum vaḻiyatu; iḵtu, ōci iṭiyaṟavupaṭac collum vaḻiyatu pōlum. 
(uṇmippaṇpāvatu, oru poruḷ tōṉṟuṅkāl tōṉṟi atu keṭuntuṇiyum uṇṭāy niṉṟa taṉmi. `ēkāram’ aci. akara nīṭṭamum ukara nīṭṭamum ceyyuḷ vikāram. `āṉ’ iṭiccol; akaram - cāriyi.)	          (25)
432.	irutici puṇari ṉēyiṭi varumē.
itu, kuṟṟukara īṟṟut ticippeyarkku alvaḻikkaṇ vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) irutici puṇariṉ ē iṭi varum - iraṇṭu perun ticikaḷ (tammiṟ) puṇariṉ ē eṉṉum cāriyi iṭivantu puṇarum.
u-m:  vaṭakkē teṟku, kiḻakkē mēṟku eṉa varum. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci).	   (26)
433. 	tiripuvēṟu kiḷappi ṉoṟṟu miṟutiyum
	keṭutal vēṇṭu meṉmaṉār pulavar
	oṟṟumey tirintu ṉakāra mākum
	teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅ kāli yāṉa.
iḵtu, ap perunticiyoṭu kōṇattici puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tiripu vēṟu kiḷappiṉ - (ap perunticikaḷoṭu) kōṇatticikaḷi vēṟākap puṇarkkum iṭattu, oṟṟum iṟutiyum keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - (av vukaram ēṟi niṉṟa) oṟṟum av īṟṟu ukaramum keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, teṟkoṭu puṇarum kāli - teṟku eṉṉum ticiyoṭu puṇarum kālattu, oṟṟu mey tirintu ṉakāram ākum - (ataṉkaṇ niṉṟa) ṟakāra oṟṟu (taṉ vaṭivu) tirintu ṉakāramāy muṭiyum.
‘tirintu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṭakku eṉpataṉkaṇ iṭi niṉṟa kakara oṟṟuk keṭukka.
u-m:	vaṭakiḻakku, vaṭamēṟku; teṉkiḻakku, teṉmēṟku eṉa varum.
‘vēṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ticippeyaroṭu poruṭ peyarkkum iv viti koḷka. vaṭakaṭal, vaṭavari eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, atticippeyaroṭu poruṭ peyar puṇarumiṭattu iṟutiyum mutalum tirintu muṭivaṉa vellām koḷka. 
u-m: kīḻkūri, mēl kūri eṉa varum.  	        (27)
434. 	oṉṟu mutalāka veṭṭa ṉiṟuti
	ellā veṇṇum pattaṉ muṉvariṉ
	kuṟṟiya lukara meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	muṟṟaviṉ varūu miraṇṭalaṅ kaṭiyē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu eṇṇup peyarukku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭu eṉ iṟuti ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟu eṉṉum col mutalāka eṭṭu eṉṉum col iṟutiyākavuḷḷa ellā eṇṇuppeyarum, pattaṉ muṉvariṉ - pattu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyar muṉvariṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum keṭum - (ap pattu eṉṉum colliṟ) kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyoṭum keṭṭu muṭiyum, iraṇṭu alam kaṭi muṟṟa iṉ varum - iraṇṭākiya eṇṇup peyar allāta eṇṇuppeyariṭattu muṭiya iṉ vantu puṇarum.
u-m:	patiṉoṉṟu, patiṉmūṉṟu, patiṉāṉku, patiṉintu, patiṉāṟu, patiṉēḻu, patiṉeṭṭu eṉa varum.
nilimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ, piṟamoḻiyum av iṉ pēṟu koḷka. oṉpatiṉpāl, oṉpatiṉkūṟu eṉa varum.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēl eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum iṉ peṟṟavaḻi, patiṟṟoṉṟu patiṟṟiraṇṭu eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭipukaḷ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām koḷka.
(`ēkāram’ iraṇṭum - īṟṟaci. ukara nīṭṭam - ceyyuḷ vikāram.)	     (28)
435. 	pattaṉoṟ ṟukkeṭa ṉakāra miraṭṭal
	otta teṉpa viraṇṭuvaru kāli.
itu, mēl iṉ peṟāteṉṟu vilakkiya ataṟkup piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pattaṉ oṟṟu keṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal - pattu eṉṉum colliṉ niṉṟa takara oṟṟukkeṭa ṉakara oṟṟu vantu iraṭṭutal, ottatu eṉpa - poruntiṟṟu eṉṟu collupa (pulavar), iraṇṭu varu kāli - iraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇ varum kālattu.
u-m: paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉa varum. 	(29)
436. 	āyiram variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
ituvum, eṇṇuppeyarkku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram variṉum - (mēṟkūṟiya pattu eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar muṉṉar, oṉṟu mutalākiya eṇṇup peyarēyaṉṟi) āyiram eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar vantālum, a iyal tiriyātu - mēl (434) īṟu keṭṭu iṉ peṟṟa iyalpiṉ tiriyātē muṭiyum.
u-m: patiṉāyiram eṉa varum. (akara nīṭṭam - ceyyuḷ vikāram)	(30)
437. 	niṟiyu maḷavum varūuṅ kāliyum
	kuṟiyā tāku miṉṉeṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu, eṇṇup peyaroṭu niṟippeyarum aḷavup peyarum puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) niṟiyum aḷavum varūum kāliyum - (mēl niṉṟa patteṉpataṉmuṉ) niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varum kālattum, iṉ eṉ cāriyi kuṟiyātu ākum - (av) iṉ eṉṉum cāriyi kuṟiyātu vantu muṭiyum.
u-m:	patiṉkaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉavum; kalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum varum.
‘kuṟiyātākum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pattu eṉpataṉ muṉṉarp poruṭpeyarkku varumuṭipum koḷka.
u-m: patiṉṟiṅkaḷ, patiṟṟumuḻam, patiṟṟuvēli patiṟṟitaḻ eṉa varum.	           (31)
438.	oṉṟumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
	niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa vāytam
	vantiṭi niliyu miyaṟkit teṉpa
	kūṟiya viyaṟkik kuṟṟiya lukaram
	āṟa ṉiṟuti yalvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu, eṇṇup peyarōṭu eṇṇup peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar niṉṟa pataṉ oṟṟu keṭa āytam vantu iṭiniliyum iyaṟkittu eṉpa - oṉṟu mutalāka oṉpatu īṟākac collappaṭukiṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar (varumoḻiyāy vantu) niṉṟa pattu eṉṉum colliṉatu takara oṟṟuk keṭa āytamāṉatu vantu iṭiyil nilipeṟum iyalpiyuṭit teṉṟu colluvar pulavar, āṟaṉ iṟuti alvaḻi kuṟṟiyalukaram kūṟiya iyaṟki - avaṟṟuḷ āṟu eṉṉum īṟu allāta iṭattuk kuṟṟiya lukaram mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟkiyāy meyyoṭum keṭṭu muṭiyum.
u-m: orupaḵtu, irupaḵtu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘vanta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, āytamāyt tiriyātu keṭṭu orupatu eṉṟumām. (`āṉ’ - iṭiccol. akaram - cāriyi.)          (32)
439. 	mutalī reṇṇi ṉoṟṟurakara mākum
	ukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟku uriyatoṉṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mutal īr eṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakaram ākum - (avaṟṟuḷ) mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa iraṇṭu eṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakāra oṟṟām, a vayiṉ ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram varuka.
u-m:	orupaḵtu eṉa varum.  (`āṉ’ - iṭiccol. akaram - cāriyi.)	          (33)
440. 	iṭinili rakara miraṇṭe ṉeṇṇiṟkum
	naṭimaruṅ kiṉaṟē poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) iraṇṭu eṉ eṇṇiṟku iṭinili rakaram poruḷ vayiṉ naṭi maruṅku iṉṟu - av iraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇiṟku iṭi niṉṟa rakaram am moḻi poruḷāmiṭattu naṭakkum iṭam iṉṟik keṭum.
u-m: irupaḵtu eṉa varum. (`ēkāram’ - aci. `āṉ’ eṉpati mēṟkūṟi yāṅkuk koḷka.)	 (34)
441.	mūṉṟu māṟu neṭumutal kuṟukum
	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē pakāra mākum.
ituvum atu. 
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟum āṟum neṭu mutal kuṟukum - mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇum āṟu eṉṉum eṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum, mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu pakāram ākum - mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟup pakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m: muppaḵtu, (aṟupaḵtu) eṉa varum (ēkāram aci.)  (35)
442. 	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē ṟakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ oṟṟu ṟakāram ākum - nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟu ṟakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m: nāṟpaḵtu eṉa varum. (ēkāram - aci.)	(36)
443. 	ainta ṉoṟṟē makāram ākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉ oṟṟu makāram ākum - aintu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakāra oṟṟu makāra oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m: aimpaḵtu eṉa varum.
āṟaṉ neṭumutal kuṟukiyavāṟē niṉṟu aṟupaḵtu eṉa varum. ‘ēḻ’ kuṟṟukara īṟu aṉṟām.	(37)
444. 	eṭṭa ṉoṟṟē ṇakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) eṭṭaṉ oṟṟu ṇakāram ākum - eṭṭu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṭakāra oṟṟu ṇakāra oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
e-ṭu: eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum. (ēkāram - aci.)	(38)
445. 	oṉpāṉ ṉokaramicit takara moṟṟum
	munti yoṟṟē ṇakāra miraṭṭum
	patteṉ kiḷavi yāyta pakaraṅkeṭa
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum mūkārak kiḷavi
	oṟṟiya takaram ṟakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ okara mici takaram oṟṟum - (nilimoḻi yākiya) oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉ okarattiṟku mēlākat takaram oṟṟāy mikku varum, munti oṟṟu ṇakāram iraṭṭum - muṉ coṉṉa okarattiṉ muṉṉar ṉakara oṟṟu iraṇṭu ṇakāra oṟṟāy mikku varum, pattu eṉ kiḷavi pakaram āytam keṭa ūkārak kiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - (varumoḻi yākiya) pattu eṉṉum col taṉkaṇ pakaramum āytamum keṭa (nili moḻiyil iraṭṭiya ṇakarattiṉ piṉṉar) ūkāramākiya eḻuttu niṟṟal vēṇṭum; oṟṟiya takaram ṟakāram ākum - (varumoḻiyākiya pattu eṉpataṉ īṟṟataṉmēl ēṟiya ukaram keṭātu pirintu niṟpa) oṟṟāy niṉṟa takaram ṟakāra oṟṟākum.
iḵtu, oṉpatum pattum eṉa niṉṟāl muṭiyaṟpāla (iṉṉa) veṉpatu. ‘pakara āytam’ eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, nili moḻikkaṇ pakarakkēṭum koḷka. kuṟṟiyalukaramum aḵtu ēṟiya meyyum muṉṉar (438) māṭṭēṟṟāṟ keṭṭaṉa. (`ēkāram’ - aci. iccūttiram “ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampal” ām.)
u-m: toṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. 	(39)
446. 	aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyu niṟiyeṉ kiḷaviyum
	kiḷanta viyala tōṉṟuṅ kāli.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷantu aṟi kiḷaviyum niṟi eṉ kiḷaviyum tōṉṟum kāli - (mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar) aḷantu aṟiyappaṭum aḷavup peyarccollum niṟi eṉṉum peyarccollum tōṉṟuṅkālattu, kiḷanta iyala - av voṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ mēl pattu eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumvaḻik kiḷanta (438 - 444) iyal piṉavāy muṭiyum.
u-m: oru kalam, iru kalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi eṉavum; orukaḻañcu, irukaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘tōṉṟuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, av veṇkaḷiṉmuṉ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟa eṇṇup peyari yellām ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eytuvittu muṭittuk koḷka.
u-m: ōroṉṟu, ōriraṇṭu; īroṉṟu, īriraṇṭu; oru muntiriki, irumuntiriki; ōrarikkāl, īrarikkāl; oru kāl, irukāl; ōrari, īrari; oru mukkāl, iru mukkāl eṉa oṭṭik koḷka. 	 (40)
447. 	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum.
itu, māṭṭēṟu eytātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu vantatu okkum - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟu varumoḻiyāy vanta aḷavuppeyar niṟippeyariṉ muta(li)l vanta oṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	mukkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum; mukkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
māṭṭēṟṟāṉē nāṟkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum; nāṟkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. (ēkāram aci.)     (41)
448.	ainta ṉoṟṟē melleḻut tākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉ oṟṟu melleḻuttu ākum - aintāvataṉ kaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu (mēl varukiṉṟa varumoḻi mutal valleḻuttiṟku ēṟṟa) melleḻuttāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	 aiṅkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum;  aiṅkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. (ēkāram - aci.)	(42)
449. 	kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūuṅ kāli.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum varumoḻi variyaṟuk kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi varum kāli - mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu vantatu oppatūum  aintaṉ oṟṟu melleḻuttāvatūum (av aḷavuppeyar niṟippeyar oṉpatiṉum vaṉkaṇamākiya) ka ca ta pa mutal moḻikaḷ vanta iṭattu.
u-m: mēl māṭṭēṟṟāṉē, aṟukalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum; aṟukaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
ēḻu kuṟṟukara īṟaṉmiyiṉ, māṭṭēṟu ēlātāyiṟṟu. (muntiṉa iraṇṭu cūttiramum ituvum oru cūttiramāyiruntu kāla aḷavil mūṉṟāyiṉa pōlum.)	          (43)
450. 	namava veṉṉu mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi
	akaram variṉu meṭṭaṉmu ṉiyalpē.
itu, vēṇṭā kūṟi vēṇṭiyatu muṭittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) na ma va eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi akaram variṉum - (aḷavuppeyar niṟippeyarkaḷil meṉkaṇattu iraṇṭum iṭik kaṇattu oṉṟumākiya) na ma va eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉōṭum porunti, (uyirk kaṇattu) akara mutalmoḻi variṉum (ummiyāṉ av vuyirkkaṇattu oḻinta ukaramum kūṟāta valleḻuttukkaḷum variṉum), eṭṭaṉmuṉ iyalpu - eṭṭeṉpataṉ muṉ (mēṟkūṟiya niṉṟa vikāramē vikāramāka vēṟoru vikāramiṉṟi) iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	eṇkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; eṇkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
iv vēṇṭā kūṟalāṉ, eṇṇakal eṉat toṭar moḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷḷappaṭṭatu.  (`ēkāram’ - īṟṟaci.)	  (44)
451. 	aintu mūṉṟu namavaruṅ kāli
	vanta tokku moṟṟiya ṉiliyē.
ituvum, mēlmāṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvā vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) aintum mūṉṟum nama varum kāli - aintu eṉṉum eṇṇum mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇum nakara mutal moḻiyum makara mutal moḻiyum varuṅkālattu, oṟṟu iyal nili vantatu okkum - taṅkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟu naṭakkum nilimi (colliṉ) av varumoḻi mutalil vanta oṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	munnāḻi, mummaṇṭi; ainnāḻi, aimmaṇṭi eṉa varum.
mūṉṟum aintum eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, nāṉāḻi eṉṉum muṭipiṉkaṇ vikāramākiya ṉakarattiṉ muṉṉar varumoḻi nakarat tiripum, atu kāraṇamāka nilimoḻi ṉakarak kēṭum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.  (`ēkāram’  -īṟṟaci.)	          (45)
452. 	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāram varuvaḻit
	tōṉṟiya vakārat turuvā kummē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu vakāram varu vaḻi - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉ kaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu vakara varumoḻi varum iṭattu, tōṉṟiya vakārattu urupu ākum - varumoḻiyāya vakarattu uruvāy muṭiyum.
u-m: muvvaṭṭi eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟiṉṟi mūvaṭṭi eṉṟumām.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, mutal nīḷātu oṟṟiṉṟi muvaṭṭi eṉṟumām. (makara oṟṟu mikuti ceyyuḷ vikāram, ēkāram īṟṟaci).	   (46)
453. 	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ oṟṟu lakāram ākum - nāṉkām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟu vakaram vantāl lakāra oṟṟāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: nālvaṭṭi eṉa varum.  (`ēkāram’ - aci.)	(47)
454.	ainta ṉoṟṟē muntiyatu keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉ oṟṟu muntiyatu keṭum - aintām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu vakaram vantāl muṉ niṉṟa vaṭivu keṭṭu muṭiyum.
u-m: aivaṭṭi eṉa varum.
‘munti’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nakara oṟṟukkeṭātu av vakaramāyt tirintu aivvaṭṭi eṉṟum ām. (mutal ēkāram aci. iraṇṭām ēkāram - īṟṟaci)	(48)
455. 	mutalī reṇṇiṉmuṉ ṉuyirvaru kāli
	tavaleṉa moḻipa vukarak kiḷavi
	mutaṉili nīṭa lāvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mutal īr eṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir varu kāli - muṟpaṭṭa iraṇṭu eṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir mutalmoḻi varuṅkālattu, ukarak kiḷavi taval eṉa moḻipa - ukaramākiya eḻuttuk keṭum eṉṟu colluvar (pulavar). mutalnili a vayiṉ nīṭal - (av veṇṇiṉ) mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ avviṭattu nīṇṭu muṭiyum.
u-m:	ōrakal, īrakal; ōruḻakku, īruḻakku eṉa varum. (`āṉ’  - iṭiccol -akaram - cāriyi.) 	       (49)
456. 	mūṉṟu nāṉku mintaṉ kiḷaviyum
	tōṉṟiya vakarat tiyaṟki yākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟum nāṉkum aintu eṉ kiḷaviyum - mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇum nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇum aintu eṉṉum eṇṇuc collum, tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkiyākum - (mēl) tōṉṟi muṭinta vakarattu iyaṟkiyāy mūṉṟaṉkaṇ vakara oṟṟāyum nāṉkaṉkaṇ  lakara oṟṟāyum aintaṉkaṇ oṟṟuk keṭṭum muṭiyum.
u-m:	muvvakal, muvvuḻakku eṉavum; nālakal, nāluḻakku eṉavum; aiyakal, aiyuḻakku eṉavum varum.
‘tōṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēl mūṉṟu eṉpatu mutal nīṇṭa iṭattu nilimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭuttuk koḷka. 		 (50)
457. 	mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalu murittē
	uḻakkeṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkat tāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ mutalnili uḻakku eṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa - mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ muta(li)l niṉṟa eḻuttu uḻakku eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyatu vaḻakkiṭattu, nīṭalum urittu - kuṟukātu nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu.
u-m: mūvuḻakku eṉa varum.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akal eṉpataṉkaṇṇum ic ceyki koḷka.
mūvakal eṉa varum. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci. `vaḻakkattāṉ’ - vēṟṟumi mayakkam. akaram - cāriyi.)	(51)
458. 	āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutaṉī ṭummē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟu eṉ kiḷavi mutal nīṭum - āṟu eṉṉum col (uyir mutalmoḻi vantāl muṉ kuṟuki niṉṟa) mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭiyum.
u-m:	āṟakal, āṟuḻakku eṉa varum. (makara oṟṟu mikuti - ceyyuḷ vikāram, ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	 (52)
459. 	oṉpā ṉiṟuti yurupunili tiriyā
	tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi moḻiyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ iṟuti uruvu nili tiriyātu cāriyi moḻi iṉ peṟal vēṇṭum - oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟuti taṉ vaṭivu nili tiriyātu cāriyi moḻiyākiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭital vēṇṭum.
u-m: oṉpatiṉkalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; oṉpatiṉkaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘uruvu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṉpatiṟṟakal eṉpuḻi iraṭṭiya ṟakaramākak koḷka. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci.)	(53)
460.	nūṟumuṉ variṉuṅ kūṟiya iyalpē.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu nūṟu eṉpataṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu muṉ variṉum kūṟiya iyalpu - nūṟu eṉpatu (oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaṇ) muṉvariṉum (mēl patteṉpata ṉoṭu puṇarum vaḻi) kūṟiya iyalpu eyti muṭiyum.
u-m: orunūṟu, irunūṟu, aṟunūṟu, eṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. ivi māṭṭēṟṟāṉē muṭintaṉa. 	(54)
461.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāra mākum.
itu, māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu nakāram ākum - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu nakara oṟṟākum.
u-m: munnūṟu eṉa varum. (`ēkāram’ - aci)	          (55)
462. 	nāṉkum mintu moṟṟumey tiriyā.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā - nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇum aintu eṉṉum eṇṇum tam oṟṟukkaḷ nili tiriyātu muṭiyum.
u-m: nāṉūṟu, ainnūṟu eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nāṉūṟu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi oṟṟākiya nakarakkēṭu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, oṟṟiṉṟi ainūṟu eṉa varum muṭipum koḷka. (muntiya iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷum ituvum oṉṟāyiruntu kāla aḷavil mūṉṟāyiṉa pōlum.)	  (56)
463. 	oṉpāṉ mutaṉili muntukiḷan taṟṟē
	munti yoṟṟē ḷakāra miraṭṭum
	nūṟeṉ kiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa
	ūā vāku miyaṟkit teṉpa
	āyiṭi varuta likāra rakāram
	īṟumey keṭuttu makāra moṟṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ mutal nili muntu kiḷantu aṟṟu - oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉ mutal niṉṟa okaram mēl (patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇarum vaḻik) kūṟiyavāṟu pōla (445) okaramicit takara oṟṟu mikum, munti oṟṟu ḷakāram iraṭṭum - av vokarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa (ṉakara) oṟṟu iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟām, nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi nakāra mey keṭa ū ā ākum iyaṟkittu eṉpa - (varumoḻiyākiya) nūṟu eṉṉum collum nakāra mākiya meykeṭa (ataṉmēl ēṟiya) ūkāram ākāramām iyalpi yuṭittu eṉpar (pulavar). a iṭi ikāram rakāram varutal - am moḻiyiṭi ōrikaramum rakāramum varum, īṟu mey keṭuttu makāram oṟṟum - itaṟku īṟākiya kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉiyum aḵtu ēṟi niṉṟa ṟakāra oṟṟiṉiyum keṭuttu (ōr) makaram oṟṟāy vantu muṭiyum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nilimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa pakaram keṭukka.
u-m: toḷḷāyiram eṉa varum. 
(`akaram’ ceyyuḷ vikārattāṟ keṭṭatu. ēkāraṅkaḷ acikaḷ. akaraccuṭṭiṉ nīṭṭam ceyyuḷ vikāram.) (iccūttiramum “ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampal” ām.)	    (57)
464. 	āyirak kiḷavi varūuṅ kāli
	mutalī reṇṇi ṉukaraṅ keṭumē.
iḵtu, av voṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷmuṉ āyiram eṉpatu varuṅkāl muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyirak kiḷavi varum kāli mutal īr eṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭum - āyiram eṉṉum col (oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ) muṉ varuṅkālattu mutal īr eṇṇiṉkaṇ peṟṟu niṉṟa ukaram keṭṭu muṭiyum.
u-m:	orāyiram, irāyiram eṉa varum. (`ēkāram’ - īṟṟaci. mutal īr-mutal iraṇṭu. aviyāvaṉa oru, iru eṉpaṉa.)  (58)
465. 	mutaṉili nīṭiṉu māṉa milli.
iḵtu, eytiyataṉ mēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalnili nīṭiṉum māṉam illi - ammutal īr eṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa okara ikaraṅkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illi.
u-m: ōrāyiram, īrāyiram eṉa varum. 	(59)
466. 	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāra mākum.
ituvum, māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvā muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu vakāram ākum - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟu vakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m: muvvāyiram eṉa varum.
‘mutaṉili’ (465) eṉṟataṉāṉ mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟuk keṭṭu mūvāyiram eṉṟum varum. (ēkāram - aci.)	          (60)
467. 	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ oṟṟu lakāram ākum - nāṉkām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m: nālāyiram eṉa varum. (ēkāram - aci).	 (61)
468. 	ainta ṉoṟṟē yakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉ oṟṟu yakāram ākum - aintām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu yakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m: aiyāyiram eṉa varum. muṉṉar ivvāṟu ōtāmiyāṉ, aiyakal aiyuḻakku (eṉpavi) uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟatu. (ēkāram - aci.)	  (62)
469. 	āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaram
	īṟumey yoḻiyak keṭutal vēṇṭum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ kuṟṟiyalukaram īṟu mey oḻiya keṭutal vēṇṭum - āṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram (tāṉ ēṟiya) meyyākiya ṟakara oṟṟuk keṭātu niṟpa (ukaramākiya avvīṟu tāṉē) keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭum.
u-m: aṟāyiram eṉa varum.
‘tirintataṉ tiripatu’ eṉṉum nayattāṉ, ‘āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ’ eṉṟu ōtappaṭṭatu. āṟu eṉpatu aṟu eṉak kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaramāka ōtappaṭṭu niṉṟamiyiṉ av vukarakkēṭu ōtappaṭṭatu.
‘īṟu’ eṉavum ‘mey’ eṉavum av vuyirmeyyip pirittuc ceyki ōtiṉamiyāṉ, av vuyirmeyyiṉi oṟṟumi nayattāṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟu ōtiṉārākak koḷka.
‘āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ īṟu mey yoḻiyak keṭum’ eṉṉātu ‘kuṟṟiyalukaram’ eṉṟōtiṉamiyāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟukātē niṉṟu āṟāyiram eṉṟum ām.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē ‘āṟā kuvatē’ (col. 79) eṉṟāṟ pōlap poruṭpeyarkkaṇ varumuṭipum koḷka.
mēl māṭṭēṟṟāṉē (460) eṇṇāyiram eṉa muṭintatu.		 (63)
470. 	oṉpā ṉiṟuti yurupunili tiriyā
	tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi marapē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ iṟuti urupu nili tiriyātu cāriyi marapu iṉ peṟal vēṇṭum - oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram taṉ vaṭivu nili tirintu keṭātu cāriyiyākiya marapiṉiyuṭiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭital vēṇṭum.
u-m: oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉa varum.
‘urupu’ eṉṟum, ‘nili’ eṉṟum, ‘cāriyi marapu’ eṉṟum kūṟiya mikutiyāṉ, āyiramallāta piṟa eṇṇiṉkaṇṇum poruṭpeyariṭattum, iṉṉum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum muṭipum koḷka.
oṉpatiṟṟukkōṭi, oṉpatiṟṟoṉṟu; oṉpatiṟṟut taṭakki (paripāṭal 3) oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu (toki. 28) eṉa varum.
iṉṉum av vilēcāṉē mēl eṇṇāyiram eṉṟavaḻi oṟṟiraṭṭuk koḷka. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci)	 (64)
471. 	nūṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ kāli
	nūṟa ṉiyaṟki mutaṉilik kiḷavi.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu nūṟu eṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttu āyiram eṉpatu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟāyiram muṉvaru kāli mutalnili kiḷavi nūṟu eṉ iyaṟki - nūṟāyiram eṉṉum aṭiyaṭutta moḻi (oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ) muṉvaruṅ kālattu mutaṉilik kiḷaviyākiya oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇ (mēl oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu muṭinta) nūṟu eṉṉum col av voṉṟaṉoṭu muṭinta vikāra iyaṟki eyti muṭiyum.
vaḻinilik kiḷaviyākiya iraṇṭu mutal eṇkaḷ vikāram eytiyum eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.
u-m:	orunūṟāyiram eṉa varum. irunūṟāyiram, iraṇṭu nūṟāyiram; munnūṟāyiram, mūṉṟu nūṟāyiram; nāṉūṟāyiram, nāṉku nūṟāyiram; ainnūṟāyiram, aintu nūṟāyiram; aṟunūṟāyiram, āṟu nūṟāyiram; eṇṇūṟāyiram, eṭṭu nūṟāyiram; oṉpatu nūṟāyiram eṉa varum.
uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, toḷḷāyiram eṉṟa muṭipiṉoṭu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟatēṉum avvāṟu muṭiyā teṉṟu koḷka.
‘muṉ’ eṉpataṉāṉ iṉcāriyi peṟṟu oṉpatiṉū ṟāyiram eṉṟum ām.
‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mūṉṟum āṟum iyalpāy muṭiyum muṭipiṉkaṇ neṭumutal kuṟukāmi kaṇṭu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē nāṉūṟāyiram eṉpuḻi varumoḻi oṟṟākiya nakarakkēṭu koḷka. 	 (65)
472. 	nūṟeṉ kiḷavi yoṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ
	kīṟuciṉi yoḻiya viṉavoṟṟu mikumē.
itu, nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku īṟu ciṉi oḻiya iṉa oṟṟu mikum - nūṟu eṉṉum col oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu puṇarumiṭattu īṟākiya kuṟṟiya lukaramum av vukaram ēṟiya meyyākiya ciṉiyum keṭātu niṟpa (ciṉikku) iṉamākiya iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟoṉṟu; iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, oṉpatu eṉa varum.
‘īṟuciṉi’ eṉṟu ōtiya mikiyāṉē nūṟu eṉpataṉoṭu piṟa eṇṇum piṟa poruṭpeyarum iv vitiyum piṟa vitiyum peṟṟu muṭiyumāṟu koḷka. nūṟṟukkōṭi, nūṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟuttoṇṇūṟu eṉavum; nūṟṟukkuṟi, nūṟṟaṭukku eṉavum varum.	  (66)
473. 	aviyūr pattiṉu mattoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu, an nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ aṭiyaṭuttuvaḻip puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avi ūrpattiṉum atoḻiṟṟu ākum - (an nūṟu eṉpatu) oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷāṉ ūrappaṭṭa pattiṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu attoḻiṟṟāy iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:	nūṟṟorupaḵtu; irupaḵtu, muppaḵtu, nāṟpaḵtu, aimpaḵtu, aṟupaḵtu, eḻupaḵtu, eṇpaḵtu eṉavarum.
‘ākum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nilimoḻi yaṭiyaṭuttu varum muṭipum koḷka. orunūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa oṭṭuka. 	          (67)
474. 	aḷavu niṟiyu māyiya ṟiriyā
	kuṟṟiya lukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkiyum
	muṟkiḷan taṉṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, an nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum a iyal tiriyā - (an nūṟu eṉpata ṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu) aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum mēṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉ tiriyātē niṉṟu iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭiyum, kuṟṟiyalu karamum valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyum muṉ kiḷanta aṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar - avviṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭāmiyum (iṉa oṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyāy niṉṟamiyāṉ varumoḻi) valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum mēl (vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkuk) kūṟiya taṉmiyaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	nūṟṟukkalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘tiriyā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nūṟu eṉpatu aṭiyaṭutta vaḻiyum iv viti koḷka.
u-m:	orunūṟṟukkalam, irunūṟṟukkalam eṉa oṭṭuka. (ākāram - ceyyuḷ vikāram. kiḷantu eṉpatu - viṉiyeccat toki.)	   (68)
475. 	oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
	oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭi mikumē
	niṉṟa vāytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭiyaṭutta pattiṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi - oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākap pattu eṉṉum eṇ ūrappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku - av voṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varumoḻi yāy vantu puṇarumiṭattu, niṉṟa āytam keṭutal vēṇṭum - āṇṭu niṉṟa āytam keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭum, oṟṟu iṭi mikum - (keṭṭa vaḻi iṉavoṟṟāy oru takara) oṟṟu iṭi mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: orupattoṉṟu, irupattoṉṟu, orupattiraṇṭu, irupattiraṇṭu, orupattumūṉṟu, irupattumūṉṟu eṉa oṭṭik koḷka.
‘niṉṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēl (476)  orupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉum muṭipiṟku iṉ pēṟum koḷka. (ēkāram - īṟṟaci.) 	(69)
476. 	āyiram variṉē yiṉṉāñ cāriyi
	āvayi ṉoṟṟiṭi mikuta lilli.
iḵtu, av vaṭiyaṭutta pattiṉōṭu āyirattiṉip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram variṉ cāriyi iṉ ām - (av voṉṟu mutalākiya pattūr kiḷavi muṉ) āyiram eṉpatuvariṉ iṭi vantu puṇaruñcāriyi iṉ ām, a vayiṉ oṟṟu iṭi mikutal illi - avviṭattu muṉ kūṟiya oṟṟu iṭi mikutal iṉṟi muṭiyum.
u-m: orupatiṉāyiram, irupatiṉāyiram eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘āvayiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, orupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉum muṭipiṟku ukaramum valleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka. (ēkāram  -aci. ākāram - ceyyuḷ vikāram.)	   (70)
477. 	aḷavu niṟiyu māyiya ṟiriyā.
iḵtu, av voṉṟu mutalākiya pattūr kiḷavi muṉ aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum a iyal tiriyā - (avvoṉṟu mutalākiya pattūrkiḷavi muṉ) aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum mēl āyirattoṭu puṇarumvaḻi muṭinta iyalpiṉ tiriyātē niṉṟu iṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: orupatiṉkalam, irupatiṉkalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; oru patiṉ kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum koḷka.
‘tiriyā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, orupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉum muṭipiṟku iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram iraṭṭi ṟakaramākalum, orupatiṉāḻi eṉṉum muṭipiṉ kaṇ varumoḻi nakaram tirintavaḻi nilimoḻiyiṉ ṉakarakkēṭum koḷka. (ākāram ceyyuḷ vikāram.)	(71)
478. 	mutaṉili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum
	ña na ma tōṉṟiṉum yavavan tiyiyiṉum
	mutaṉili yiyaṟki yeṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu poruṭpeyarip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalnili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum ña na ma tōṉṟiṉum ya va vantu iyiyiṉum - mutaṉili eṇṇākiya oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum ñanamak kaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum ya vakkaḷākiya iṭi yeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu poruntiṉum, mutalnili iyaṟki eṉmaṉār pulavar - av voṉṟu muṉ eytiya muṭipunili eyti muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
eṉavē, vaḻinili eṇṇākiya iraṇṭumutalākiya eṇkaḷ am (mutaṉili muṭipākiya) muṟkūṟiya vikāram eytiyum eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.
u-m:	 orukal; cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi, ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉa varum. irukal, iraṇṭukal; cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi, ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉavum oṉpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka.
oṉpatiṉkal eṉac ceṉṟatēṉum vaḻakkiṉmiyiṉ oḻikka.
‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, māṭṭēṟṟukku (171) ēlāta ñakara yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṭipu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. 	  (72)
479. 	ataṉili yuyirkkum yāvaru kāliyum
	mutaṉili yokara mōvā kummē
	rakarat tukaran tuvarak keṭumē.
iḵtu, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi muṭiyumāṟum mēṟkūṟiya yakaram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ataṉ nili uyirkkum yā varukāliyum mutal nili okaram ō ākum - av voṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu puṇarumiṭattu uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattum yā mutal moḻi vanta iṭattum mutalnili eṇṇākiya oṉṟu eṉpataṉkaṇ okaram ōkāram ām, rakarattu ukaram tuvarakeṭum - (avviṭattu) rakarattu ukaram muṟṟak keṭṭu muṭiyum.
eṉavē, vaḻinili yeṇkaḷuḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu muṉkūṟiyavāṟē iruvāṟṟāṉum muṭiyum.
u-m:	ōraṭi ōrāṭi eṉavum; iruvaṭi, iruvāṭi iraṇṭaṭi, iraṇṭāṭi eṉavum uyir mutal moḻikaḷi oṭṭik koḷka.
yā mutalmoḻi ōr yāḻ eṉa varum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇum mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇum ceyyuḷakattu īraci eṉavum mūvaci eṉavum mutal nīṇṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟu koḷka.
‘ataṉili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutaṉili nīḷātē niṉṟu ukaram keṭṭu oraṭi, orāṭi, oryāḻ eṉa varum muṭipum koḷka.  (ēkāraṅkaḷ - īṟṟacikaḷ.)	          (73)
480. 	iraṇṭumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
	vaḻaṅkiyaṉ māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
	makara vaḷapoṭu nikaralu murittē.
iḵtu, iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉar aḷavu mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum uriya mā eṉpatu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar - iraṇṭu mutalāka oṉpatu īṟākac collappaṭukiṉṟa eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vaḻaṅku iyal mā eṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ - vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇē kiṭakkiṉṟa (vilaṅku maram mutaliya allāta aḷavu mutaliya vaṟṟiṟkuriya) mā eṉṉum col tōṉṟiṉ, makara aḷavoṭu nikaralum urittu - (iyal pāy muṭitalēyaṉṟi) mēṟkūṟiya (446) maṇṭi eṉṉum aḷavuppeya rōṭu ottu vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉavum peṟum.
u-m:iraṇṭumā, irumā, mūṉṟumā, mummā eṉa oṉpatiṉ kāṟum ivvāṟu oṭṭuka.
‘iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ’ eṉṟu eṭuttamiyiṉ, oṉṟaṟku orumā eṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi oṉṟumā eṉṉum muṭipu illiyāyiṟṟu. ivaṟṟuḷ, mikka eṇṇoṭu kuṟinta eṇ varuṅkāl ummit tokiyākavum, kuṟinta eṇṇōṭu mikkatu variṟ paṇputtoki yākavum muṭittār eṉka. 
(ēkāram - īṟṟaci)	(74)
481. 	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
	ummuṅ keḻuvu muḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic
	ceyyuṭ ṭoṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa niliyum
	vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
itu, lakāra ṉakāra īṟṟuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) la ṉa eṉa varum puḷḷi iṟutimuṉ - la ṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ, ummum keḻuvum uḷappaṭa piṟavum - um eṉṉum cāriyiyum keḻu eṉṉum cāriyiyum uḷappaṭap piṟa cāriyiyum, aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic ceyyuḷ toṭar vayiṉ mey peṟa niliyum - ap peṟṟippaṭṭa marapiṉiyuṭiya moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic ceyyuḷ moḻikaḷit toṭarntu collum iṭattu meymmi peṟa nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum, vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷ vayiṉāṉa - vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ.
u-m: ‘vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum’ ‘kalkeḻu kāṉavar nalkuṟu makaḷē’  eṉavum, ‘mānitik kiḻa vaṉum pōṉm’  eṉavum, ‘kāṉkeḻu nāṭu’  eṉavum varum.
‘moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟi’ eṉṟa mikiyāṉ, piṟa īṟṟuḷḷum ic cāriyi peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka. ‘tuṟikeḻu mānti’ ‘vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar’ eṉa varum.
‘aṉṉa marapiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, cāriyi kāraṇamāka valleḻuttup peṟutalum, atu kāraṇamāka nilimoḻiyīṟu tiritalum, cāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum, ekara nīṭciyum koḷka. 
u-m. pūkkēḻūraṉ, vaḷaṅkēḻ tirunakar eṉṟu avvāṟu vantami yaṟika.
‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ic cāriyippēṟṟiṉkaṇ īṟṟu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.  (`āṉ iṭiccol akaram cāriyi.)	 (75)
482. 	uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yākik
	kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉu miciyiṉun tōṉṟi
	neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟiccoṟ kiḷaviyum
	uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi yāyiru maruṅkiṉ
	aimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum
	ceyyuñ ceyta veṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ
	meyyoruṅ kiyalun toḻiṟoku moḻiyum
	tammiyal kiḷappiṟ ṟammuṟ ṟāmvarūum
	eṇṇiṉ ṟokuti yuḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉavi yellā maruviṉ pāttiya
	puṇariya ṉiliyiṭi uṇarat tōṉṟā.
iḵtu, iv vatikārattup puṇarkkappaṭā moḻikaḷ iviyeṉa avaṟṟi eṭuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākik kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum iciyiṉum tōṉṟi neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟiccol kiḷaviyum - uyireḻuttum puḷḷiyeḻuttum īṟākik kuṟippiṉkaṇṇum paṇpiṉ kaṇṇum iciyiṉ kaṇṇum tōṉṟi oru neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟic coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi a iru maruṅkiṉ aimpāl aṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum - uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyākiya av viraṇṭu tiṇiyiṭattum uḷavāya aintu pāliṉiyum aṟiya varum paṇputtoki moḻikaḷum, ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum kiḷaviyiṉ mey oruṅku iyalum toḻiltoku moḻiyum -  ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum peyareccac coṟkaḷiṉpaṭi oruṅku naṭakkum viṉic col tokka viṉittokiyum, tam iyal kiḷappiṉ - (eṇkaḷ) tam iyalpu kiḷakkum iṭattu, tam muṉ tām varum eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭa piṟavum aṉṉavi ellām - (niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy vārātu) tammuṉ tām varum eṇṇiṉ tokutiyum uṭpaṭṭa piṟavum at taṉmiyavellām, maruviṉ pāttiya - vaḻakkiṭattu maruvi naṭanta iṭattuḷḷaṉa (ākalāṉ avvāṟē koḷḷappaṭum). puṇar iyal niliyiṭi uṇarat tōṉṟā - puṇarcci iyaṉṟa nilimikkaṇ (avaṟṟiṉ muṭipu) viḷaṅkat tōṉṟā.
u-m: viṇviṇittatu, kārkaṟuttatu, ollolittatu: ivi kuṟiccoṟ kiḷavi āyiṉamiyiṉ muṭikkap paṭāvāyiṉa. viṇṇeṉa vicittateṉa iṭiccolloṭu kūṭiya vaḻip puṇarkkappaṭum.
karuñcāṉṟāṉ eṉṟatu paṇputtoki. itu karum eṉap paṇpuṇara niṉṟatu. karuñcāṉṟāṉeṉat tokiyāyavaḻi karumeṉpatu kariyāṉeṉap pālkāṭṭi niṟṟalil puṇarkkap paṭātāyiṟṟu.
kolyāṉi eṉṟatu viṉittoki. atuvum kol leṉat toḻiṉmi uṇaraniṉṟatu. kolyāṉi eṉat tokiyāya vaḻi, koṉṟeṉak kālam kāṭṭi niṉṟamiyiṉ puṇarkkap paṭātāyiṟṟu.
ōroṉṟu eṉa itu, tammuṟ ṟām vanta eṇ. itu niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum aṉmiyiṟ puṇarkkap paṭātāyiṟṟu. itu tāṉē ōroṉṟākak koṭu eṉpuḻip puṇarkkappaṭum.
‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭiyātaṉa uṇṭāṉ, kariyaṉ eṉpaṉa. ivi uṇ eṉavum karumi eṉavum pirittu niṟuttiyavaḻi āṉum aṉṉum kuṟittuvarukiḷavi yaṉmiyāṉ vantu puṇarāmiyiṉ, puṇarkkappaṭāvāyiṉa.
‘tōṉṟi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, koḷḷeṉakkoṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻik koḷḷeṉpataṉi eṉa eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarkkappaṭāmi koḷka.
‘mey oruṅkiyalum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻic ceykiyum kālamum pālum tōṟṟi niṟkumāṟu pirittup puṇarkkappaṭāmi koḷka. 	    (76)
483. 	kiḷanta valla ceyyuḷuṭ ṭirinavum
	vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
	viḷampiya viyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
	vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉa roḻukkal
	naṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, iv vatikārap puṟaṉaṭi yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum - muṉ eṭuttōtiṉa allātaṉa ceyyuḷiṭattu vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa vaṟṟiyum, vaḻaṅku iyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum - vaḻakku naṭattumiṭattu maruvutaloṭu vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavaṟṟiyum, viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭa tōṉṟiṉ - muṉ coṉṉa muṭipiyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ, nalmati nāṭṭattu vaḻaṅku iyal maruṅkiṉ uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal - nallaṟiviṉatu ārāycciyāṉ vaḻakku iyalumiṭattu avaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟupāṭukaḷi yaṟintu naṭattuka, eṉmaṉār pulavar - eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: naṟavaṅkaṇṇi, ‘kaḷḷiyaṅkāṭu’ (akam. 97), ‘puṉṉiyaṅ kāṉal’ (akam. 80), ‘poṉṉantikiri’, (puṟam. 365) āraṅkaṇṇi, kāṉalam peruntuṟi (aiṅkuṟu. 158) eṉa ivi vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
“vērpiṇi vetirattuk kālporu naralici” (naṟ. 62) eṉpatu rakaravīṟu attuppeṟṟu muṭintatu. ‘muḷavumā’, (akam. 182) piṇavunāy eṉpaṉa, alvaḻikkaṇ meṉkaṇattuk kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉikeṭṭu ukaram peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
‘añcevi niṟiya mantiram kūṟi’ eṉpatu akam eṉṉum nilimoḻi cevi eṉṉum varumoḻiyoṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu.
‘āyiṭi’ eṉpatu avveṉṉum vakaravīṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu.
taṭavuttiri, taṭavuttōḷ eṉpaṉa uriccol muṭipu.
arumaruntaṉṉāṉ eṉaṟpālatu arumaruntāṉ eṉa marūuvāy muṭintatu. cōṇāṭu malāṭu eṉpaṉavum atu.
potiyi leṉpatum atu. piṟavum aṉṉa. 	(77)
oṉpatāvatu - kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
eḻuttatikāram iḷampūraṇaruri muṟṟuppeṟṟatu.
piṉṉiṇippukaḷ
tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟap peṟutaṟkuriya kāraṇam
tam kālattil nikaḻnta kaṭalkōḷāl tamiḻakattiṉ teṉ pālaminta peru nilapparappum tamiḻ nūlkaḷ palavum aḻintu paṭṭa pēriḻappiṉiyuṇarntu maṉaṅkavaṉṟa āciriyar tol kāppiyaṉār, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ kalvi vaḷarcciyiṟ karuttuṭiya rāyt tamiḻ moḻikkut toṇṭu ceyya eṇṇiṉār. kumarināṭṭiṉ teṉpāl teṉmaturit talic caṅkattiṟ cāṉṟōr palarum pōṟṟi vaḷartta tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum mūṉṟu tuṟikaḷilum vaḷarccipeṟṟut tikaḻntatu. muttamiḻt tuṟiyilum virinta pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟavē avaṟṟiṉ amitiyi viḷakkum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ pala tōṉṟuvaṉavāyiṉa. iṅṅaṉam virintu paranta tamiḻ nūṟparappiṉ amitiyik kumarināṭu kaṭal vāyppaṭṭa piṉṉar vāḻnta makkaḷ aṟiyum āṟṟalaṟṟavarāyiṉar. vaṭanāṭṭiṉarāṟ pēcappaṭum āriya moḻiyum tamiḻ nāṭṭil ciṟitu ciṟitāka iṭampeṟuvatāyiṟṟu. iru moḻikaḷukkumuriya iyalpukaḷuḷ oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟaṉōṭu viravum vakiyil teṉtamiḻ makkaḷum vaṭavarum aḷavaḷāvum niliyēṟpaṭṭatu. muttamiḻuḷ oṉṟaṟ kuriya iyalpukaḷ ēṉiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ iyitturikkappaṭuvaṉa vāyiṉa. potumakkaḷ tam moḻit tiṟattiyum poruṭṭiṟattiyum pakuttuṇarum āṟṟalaṟṟa varāyiṉar. inniliyil tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappilpiṉi ellārkkum viḷaṅka eṭutturikkum iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇa nūloṉṟu iṉṟiyamiyātatāyiṟṟu.1-6-1928
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 44-45

tolkāppiyaṉār kālam
ippoḻutu kiṭittuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷellāvaṟṟiṟkum mikamikat toṉmi vāyntatu. tolkāppiyamākum. eṭṭut toki nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟākiya puṟanāṉūṟṟiṟ kālattāl mikap paḻiya pāṭalkaḷum tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ coṟ poruḷamitiyiṉi ārāyuṅkāl iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlākiya tolkāppiyam caṅkat toki nūlkaḷellāvaṟṟiṟkum kālattāl muṟpaṭṭateṉpatu naṉku pulaṉām.
kayavāku eṉṉum peyaruṭiya vēntar iruvar ilaṅkiyil āṭci purintuḷḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ mutaṟkayavākuviṉ kālam ki. pi. 171 - 193 eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar tuṇipu. ceṅkuṭṭuva ṉeṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṉ, kaṇṇakiyārkkut tiruvuruvamittuk kōyileṭuttuk kaṭavuṇ maṅkalañ ceyta nāḷil, ilaṅki vēntaṉākiya kayavāku vañci nakarattiṟku vantu kaṇṇakiyāri vaḻipaṭṭu varampeṟṟuc ceṉṟāṉ eṉac ceṅkuṭṭuvaṟkut tampiyākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tāmiyaṟṟiya cilappatikārattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. eṉavē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kālam ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉpatu teḷivātal kāṇalām.1 iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, avar kālattum avarkku muṉṉum vāḻnta caṅkap pulavarkaḷum teyvap pulavar iyaṟṟiya tirukkuṟaḷiluḷḷa coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum karuttukkaḷiyum avvāṟē eṭuttāṇṭuḷ ḷārkaḷ. ākavē tirukkuṟaḷāciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār kālam ki. mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭeṉak koḷḷutal ēṟpuṭiyatākum.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār
nūlvarici - 10, pak. 87-88

tolkāppiyam nutaliya poruḷ
vaṇpukaḻmūvar taṇpoḻil varippilē vaḻaṅkum centamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ulakavaḻakkiyum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum mūvakiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum muṟippaṭa ārāyntu ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷiyellām tolkāppiyaṉār tām iyaṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūliṉ kaṇṇē tokuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. innūl cūttiram, ōttu, paṭalam eṉṉum mūṉṟuṟuppuk kaḷiyuṭiya piṇṭamāka amintuḷatu. āciriyappāvip pōṉṟa yāppilaminta nūṟpā cūttirameṉappaṭum. āciriyappāvukku aṭivariyaṟiyuṇṭu. innūṟpāvukku aṭivariyaṟiyilli. aṭivariyaṟi illāta ceyyuḷvaki āṟu eṉpar tolkāppiyar. avaṟṟuḷ nūṟpāvum oṉṟu. kaṇṇāṭiyiṉakattē etirppaṭṭa poruḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkumāṟu pōṉṟu paṭitta aḷavilēyē ārāyāmaṟ poruḷ eḷitil viḷaṅka iyaṟṟappaṭuvatē nūṟpāvākiya cūttiramākum.
avaṟṟuḷ,
cūttirantāṉē
āṭiniḻaliṉ aṟiyat tōṉṟi
nāṭuta liṉṟip poruḷnaṉi viḷaṅka
yāppiṉuṭ ṭōṉṟa yāttamip patuvē(162)
eṉavarum ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiram cūttirattiyalpiṉi naṉku viḷakkutal kāṇka. otta iṉattaṉavākiya maṇikaḷi oru māliyākak kōttamippatupōṉṟu oriṉamāka varum poruḷkaḷi orucēra iyitturittaṟku iṭamāka amivatu ōttu eṉappaṭum. itaṉi iyal eṉṟa peyarālum vaḻaṅkutaluṇṭu. palvēṟu vakippaṭavarum poruḷellāvaṟṟiṟkum vēṟu vēṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvatāy aviyellāvaṟṟiyum taṉṉuḷḷē yaṭakki niṟpatu paṭalam eṉappaṭum. cūttiram, ōttu, paṭalam eṉṉum im mūṉṟuṟup piṉiyum aṭakki niṟpatu piṇṭam eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu mūṉṟuṟuppaṭakkiya piṇṭamāka amintatē ittol kāppiyamākum. itaṉkaṇṇuḷḷa eḻuttatikāram, collatikāram, poruḷatikāram eṉpaṉa paṭalam eṉṉum uṟuppukkaḷām. paṭalattiṉ uḷḷuṟuppāka amintavi ōttukkaḷ. ōttiṉ uḷḷuṟuppāka viḷaṅkuvaṉa cūttiraṅkaḷ, cūttiramākiya uṟuppoṉṟēyuṭiyanūl iṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyal. cūttiram, ōttu ākiya iraṇṭuṟuppuṭiya nūl paṉṉirupaṭalam, cūttiram, ōttu. paṭalam ākiya mūṉṟuṟuppum oruṅkuṭiyanūl tolkāppiyam. immūvaki nūlkaḷiyum muṟiyē ciṟunūl, iṭinūl, perunūl eṉavum vaḻaṅkutaluṇṭu.	
	(tol-cey-iḷam - 165)
tolkāppiyam eṉṉum innūl eḻuttu, col poruḷ ākiya mūṉṟatikāraṅkaḷiyuṭiyatāy ovvō ratikāraṅ kaḷukkum oṉpatoṉpatu iyalkaḷāka irupat tēḻiyalkaḷāl iyaṉṟatu. innūṟ cūttiraṅkaḷ 1595-eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, 1611-eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum vakuttu uriyeḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 162-163




1. tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ
"eḻutteṉap paṭupa,
  akaramutal
  ṉakara viṟuvāy muppa ḵteṉpa
  cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē."1
"avitām,
  kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
  āyta meṉṟa
  muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa,"2
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṉmarapuc cūttiraṅkaḷ tamiḻeḻut tukkaḷ ivi yeṉavum, ittuṇiya veṉavum uṇarttu kiṉṟaṉa. 'eḻutteṉappaṭupa' eṉṉum ittalic cūttirattāṉum, ivviyaliṟ piṉvarum pala cūttiraṅkaḷāṉum tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ muṟi neṭuṅkaṇakkil vaḻaṅkivarum muṟiyē yāmeṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. atu virivañci viḷakkātu viṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. akaram mutal oḷakāram iṟutiyākavuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttukkaḷum, kakaram mutal ṉakaram iṟutiyākavuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟu cārpeḻut tukkaḷum tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷāmeṉka. eḻuttukkaḷi uruveḻuttu, uṇarveḻuttu, oliyeḻuttu, taṉmiyeḻuttu eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum pakuttuk koṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvārumuḷar. iḷampūraṇarum, nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum, 'taṉmiyum vaṭivum namakkuṇart talarumiyiṉ āciriyar kūṟiṟṟilar' eṉa iṅṅaṉamurippatu taṉmi yeḻutteṉa ōrpirivu koḷḷutalum uṇṭeṉ pataṟkup paḻañcāṉṟākum. veḷippaṭiyākavuḷḷa oliyeḻuttu, vaṭiveḻuttu eṉpaṉavē iṅku ārāyccikkuriyaṉa.
eḻuttu eṉṉum peyariṉ kāraṇam
cuvāmināta tēcikar tamatu ilakkaṇak kotturiyil eḻutteṉṉum peyar paṟṟi yeḻutuvatu piṉvarumāṟu:- "eḻutteṉṉun toḻiṟ peyar apporuḷi viṭṭup  pālpakāvaḵṟiṇip poruṭpotup peyarāy, ap poruḷi viṭṭu ōviya mutaliyaṉa pōlaṉṟi akaram ṉakaram mutaliyaṉa vaṭivi yuṇarttuñ ciṟappup peyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu oliyi yuṇarttum ākupeyarāy, apporuḷiviṭṭu avvoliyiṉa tilakkaṇatti yuṇarttum irumaṭiyākupeyarāy, apporuḷiviṭṭu avvilakkaṇatti yuṇarttum nūliṉiyuṇarttum mummaṭiyāku peyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṟṟeḻuttu.  iṅṅaṉa maṟivitta teḻuttu eṉak karuma karuttāviyum karuvik karuttāviyum uṇarttum nāṉmaṭi yākupeyarāy niṉṟu palaporuḷ paṭṭatu kāṇka".
itaṉāṉē, eḻutteṉṉuñ col toḻiṟ peyareṉpatum, atu mutaṟkaṇ eḻutaluṟum vaṭivi yuṇarttip piṉpu oliyi yuṇarttaluṟṟa teṉpatum, avar karuttātal peṟappaṭum. yāpparuṅkala virutti mēṟkōṭ cūttiramoṉṟu ‘eḻutappaṭutaliṉeḻuttē' eṉṟu  kūṟuvatum, naṉṉūl urikkaṇ ‘eḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻutteṉak koḷka' eṉṟu mayili nātar kūṟuvatum eḻutteṉpatu eḻutu eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟanta ceyappaṭu poruṭpeyar eṉṉum karuttuṭiyaṉavām. civañāṉa muṉivar tolkāppiyac cūttira viruttiyil eḻutap paṭuvateṉṉum poruṭkaṇ eḻuteṉṉu mutaṉiliyuric colliṉ muṉṉarc ceyappaṭu poruṇmi yuṇarttum aikāravikuti puṇarntu..... avvikāraṅ keṭṭuk keṭṭa vaḻit takaramiraṭṭittu eḻutteṉa muṭintatu' eṉṟu kūṟuvatu ataṉit teḷivākkukiṟatu. maṟṟum avar, ‘ivvāṟaṉṟi eḻuteṉṉu mutaṉili eḻutteṉattāṉē tirintu niṉṟa teṉṟum, aḵtāku peyarāṟ ceyappaṭu poruḷiyuṇarttiṟ ṟeṉṟuṅ kūṟuvārumuḷar; atu poruntātu, eṉṟurippatu cuvāmināta tēcikar kūṟṟi maṟuppatu pōṉṟuḷatu. eṉiṉum, ivaraṉivarum eḻutteṉpataṟku eḻutu eṉpatē mutaṉiliyeṉak koṇṭaṉar. iṉṉōr karuttiṉpaṭi eḻutteṉpatu eḻutappaṭum vaṭiviyē mutaṟkaṭ kuṟittatākum. aṅṅaṉa māyiṉ, oliyiyuṇarntu kōṭaṟkuk karuviyākavē vaṭivu vēṇṭappaṭṭateṉpatu ellārkkum uṭampāṭākaliṉ vaṭivākiya eḻuttuṇṭātaṟku muṉpu oliyik kuṟippatoru peyar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyattuyāṇṭum eḻutteṉṉum peyarē payiṉṟu vantuḷatu. ‘ōraḷa picikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttu' ‘īraḷa picikku neṭṭeḻuttu' eṉṟāṟpōl varuvaṉa palavum oliyiyē ciṟappākak kuṟittavum, vaṭiviṉic ciṟappākak kuṟikkaluṟum ‘uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē' ‘meyyi ṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal' eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷil eḻutteṉṉum peyar kāṇappaṭāmiyum cintikkaṟpālaṉa.
ivvaṟṟāl eḻutteṉṉum peyar moḻik karuviyām oliyik kuṟippa toṉṟākavē tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭuḷāreṉak karututal poruttamuṭitteṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. tolkāppiya urikārar yārum ippeyarp poruḷi viḷakkiṟṟilarāyiṉum 'ivveḻutteṉappaṭṭa ōciyi aruveṉpār aṟiyātār; ataṉi uruveṉṟē kōṭum' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatu oruvāṟu ikkaruttiṟkuc cārpākavuḷatu. iṉi, āciriyar ‘ellā veḻuttum' eṉṉum piṟappiyaṟ cūttirattu eḻuttolikaḷiṉ nilivēṟupāṭukaḷi yuṇarttuṅkāl ‘eḻutaru vaḷi'yil eṉavum, ‘akatteḻu vaḷiyici' eṉavum, ‘eḻuntupuṟat ticikkum meyterivaḷi yici' eṉavum, vaḷiyiciyiṉ eḻucciyi avaṟṟukkuk kāraṇamākak kōṭaliṉ eḻu eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu eḻutteṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiṟṟākak koḷvatu porutta muṭitteṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. atu piṉpu avveḻuttiṉi yuṇartaṟkuk karuviyāka vēṇṭappaṭṭa vaṭiviṉiyum kuṟittatākal vēṇṭum.
uyir mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ kāraṇam
iṉi, akaramutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭukkum uyir eṉṟum, kakara mutaliya patiṉeṭṭukkum mey eṉṟum kuṟiyiṭṭiruppatu paṇṭittamiḻariṉ uṇmi nūluṇarvukkuttakka cāṉṟākum. naṉṉūl uriyil, mayilinātar ‘āvi, uyir, mey, uṭampu eṉpaṉa iṭukuṟip potuppeyar' eṉṟu kūṟiyatu poruttamil kūṟṟām. ‘am mutalīrā ṟāvi' eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ cūttiravurik
kaṇ ‘āviyum meyyum pōṟaliṉ ivviruvakiyeḻuttiṟkum āvi meyyeṉpaṉa uvamavāku peyarāyk kāraṇap potup peyarāyiṉa' eṉṟu caṅkara namaccivāyappulavar kūṟuvatum, ‘oḷakāra viṟuvāyp, paṉṉīreḻuttu muyireṉa moḻipa' eṉṉun tolkāppiyar cūttira vurikkaṇ ‘ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiyatōr kuṟi; meypatiṉeṭṭiṉiyum iyakkittāṉ aruvāy vaṭiviṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ uyirāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum, ‘ṉakāra viṟuvāyp, patiṉeṇṇeḻuttu meyyeṉa moḻipa' eṉṉuñ cūttiravurikkaṇ ‘ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi, eṉṉi, paṉṉīruyirkkuntāṉ iṭaṅkoṭuttu avaṟṟāṉ iyaṅkuntaṉmi peṟṟa uṭampāy niṟṟaliṉ' eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urippaṉavum uyir meyyeṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷi viḷakkuvaṉavām. kuṟṟiya
likaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpaṉa ikara ukaraṅkaḷiṉ kuṟukkamāyiṉum avi muṉ oru māttiriyāy niṉṟu piṉ ari māttiriyāyk kuṟukiṉa eṉaṟkiṭaṉiṉṟi, iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭuñ cārntu iyaṟkiyāy arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟṟalāṉum appeyark kuriyavāyiṉa. āytam eṉpataṉ peyark kāraṇattip paḻiya uriyāḷar yārum uḷḷavāṟu pulappaṭuttiṉārallar.
