'āytameṉṟa ōcitāṉ aṭuppuk kūṭṭuppōl mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟeṉpatu uṇarttaṟku āytameṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meṉṟār' eṉat tolkāppiyavuriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. īṇṭu muppuḷḷi yeṉak kūṟiṉ avvaṭivu ......iṅṅaṉamō, aṉṟi uṅṅaṉamō, vēṟeṅṅaṉamō eḻutappaṭum eṉa aiyam nikaḻumākaliṉ, atu nikaḻāmip poruṭṭu 'muppāṟpuḷḷi' eṉa āciriyar kūṟi vittaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ‘aṭuppuk kūṭṭup pōla' eṉa uvamaṅkāṭṭi ataṉi viḷakkiṉar. āytameṉṉum peyarkku avar kāraṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟilar. piṟkālattē naṉṉūṟku uriyeḻutap pukuntār cilar nacciṉārkkiṉiyariṉ karuttiṉi yaṟiya māṭṭārāy ataṉit tiriyakkoṇṭu, ‘aṭuppuk kūṭṭup pōla oruvaki āyuta vaṭivāka mūṉṟu puḷḷi vittu eḻutappaṭutalāl āytameṉṟu peyar. āytam - āyutam;  iṅkē pōrk karuviyākiya kēṭakameṉṉum paṭiyum, camittaṟ karuviyākiya aṭuppum.' eṉṟu tammaṉam pōṉavāṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam oru neṟippāṭumiṉṟit taṉittamiḻc collākiya āytam eṉpataṉip paṭikkalap potumi yuṇarttum āyutam eṉṉum vaṭacolleṉak koṇṭu nalintu poruḷ kūṟuvatu ārāyccikkum aṟivukkum ciṟitum ottataṉṟu. āyiṉ, āytameṉpataṉ poruḷtāṉ yātō eṉiṉ, āytal eṉpatu nuṇukutal eṉṉum poruṭ ṭākaliṉ nuṇukiya ōciyuṭiyatu eṉpatu poruḷātal vēṇṭum. āytal apporuṭṭātali
"ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
  āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam"3
eṉṉum uriyiyal cūttirattāṉaṟika.
 mutal cārpu
iṉi, uyir, mey eṉpavaṟṟi mutaleḻutteṉṟum, kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliyavaṟṟic cārpeḻutteṉṟum piṉṉu ḷōr kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyaṉār akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppataṉiyum eḻutteṉṟataṉṟi, mutaleḻut teṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar. kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliya mūṉṟaṉiyum cārntu varuvaṉa eṉakkūṟi, aviyum eḻuttukkaḷōṭu oru nikaraṉa eṉṟār. eṉiṉum 'mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ' eṉac cārpiṟṟōṟṟamum eḻut teṉṉum peyarāl āḷappaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppattumūṉṟu eṉak kūṟātu ‘muppaḵteṉpa.....mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē' eṉak kūṟiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ammūṉṟum taṉittēṉum, cāriyiyoṭu poruntiyēṉum varutaliṉṟi, moḻiyic cārntē varutalākiya ciṟappiṉmiyip paṟṟi eṉka. iṅṅaṉam ciṟappum ciṟappiṉmiyum paṟṟi vēṟu pirittukkūṟuvatu āciriyark kiyaṟki yeṉpataṉi, ‘colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉiyeṉ ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟinticiṉōrē' eṉavum, ‘iṭiccoṟ kiḷaviyum uriccoṟkiḷaviyum avaṟṟu vaḻimaruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa', eṉavum coṟkaḷip pirittōtutaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyalākum. kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliya mūṉṟum uyirum meyyumpōla attuṇic ciṟappuṭiyaṉa valla veṉpatē āciriyar karuttātal ‘cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟu' eṉa mutaliṟ kūṟiyataṉiyē ‘cārntuvariṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat', tērntuveḷippaṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum, eṉap piṟappiyaluḷ valiyuṟuttik kūṟalāṉum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. avvāṟu kūṟiṉārāyiṉum, mutaṟ cūttirattu ‘eḻutteṉappaṭupa ...... muppaḵteṉpa......mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē' eṉa, mūṉṟu mallāviṭattu muppatuñ ciṟantaṉa eṉpataṉāl, mūṉṟumē muppatiṉuñ ciṟantaṉa eṉapporuḷ kōṭalum amiyumālō eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉam poruḷ koḷḷāmikkaṉṟē aṭutta cūttirattu avaṟṟi ‘eḻuttōraṉṉa eṉa ōtiṉārāyiṟṟeṉpatu. iṉṟēl ‘eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉpatu poruḷil kūṟṟāmāṟu kāṇka. ivvuṇmi ‘eḻuttōraṉṉa' veṉa vēṇṭā kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, muṉ ‘eṉappaṭupa' eṉṟa ciṟappu ammūṉṟaṟkuṅ koḷḷak kiṭantamiyiṉ, atu vilakkutal peṟutu meṉpatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarālum, iṅṅaṉamē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarālum viḷakkappeṟṟiruttaluṅ kāṇka. aṟṟāyiṉum, cārpiṟṟōṟṟa meṉpaṉa mūṉṟumē evaṟṟiṉuñ ciṟappuṭiyaṉa veṉṟu kāṇkiṉṟāmeṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ atu putiyatōrārāyc ciyiṉ payaṉeṉṟē koḷḷappaṭum. yāmum avvārāycciyiṉ payaṉi iḻattaṟku oruppaṭuvēmallēm. tamiḻp paṟṟuṭiyār palarum aṉṉar eṉiṉum, ataṉit tolkāppiyaṉār karuttāka vittu nalintu poruḷ kōṭaṟkum, putiya ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāl uḷatām perumiyi ārāycci yāḷarkkaṉṟit tolkāppiyaṉārk kaḷittaṟkum karututal periyatōriḻukkāmeṉka.
iṉi, tolkāppiyaṉār cārpiṟṟōṟṟam mūṉṟeṉak kūṟiyiruppavum, naṉṉūlār cārpeḻuttu patteṉpārāyiṉar.
‘uyirmey āytam uyiraḷapu oṟṟaḷapu- 
 aḵkiya i u ai oḷa maḵkāṉ -
 taṉinili pattuñ cārpeḻuttākum’4
eṉpatu naṉṉūl.
yāpparuṅkalamuṭiyār uyirmey, aḷapeṭi, ai oḷa makāraṅkaḷiṉ kuṟukkam ivaṟṟit taṉittaṉi eḻutteṉa vēṇṭiṉarāyiṉum āytakkuṟukkameṉa oṉṟu koṇṭilar. eṉiṉum, yāpparuṅkalavirutti mēṟkōṭ cūttiraṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil āytak kuṟukkamum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ilakkaṇa viḷakkamuṭiyār cārpeḻuttu oṉpateṉak koṇṭaṉar. āytak kuṟukkaṅ koṇṭilar. ataṉ urikārar, tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiṟṟilarākaliṉ āytak kuṟukkameṉa oṉṟu iṉṟeṉṟiyampiṉar. iṅṅaṉam cārpeḻuttukkaḷ paṟṟik karuttu vēṟupaṭuvārellām tolkāppiyaṉāratu āṇi kaṭippiṭittalip perumiyākak koḷḷum oru peṟṟiyarākaliṉ, yāmum tolkāppiyaratu karuttiṉi ārāyntu kāṇaṟpālam.
tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam, uyiraḷapeṭi, makarakkuṟukkam, uyirmey eṉpavaṟṟi nūṉ marapiṟ potuvakiyālum, moḻimarapiṟ ciṟappu vaki yālum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ mēlum ciṟappākak kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ kuṟukumeṉpataṉi moḻi marapilum, oṟṟaḷa peṭiyic ceyyuḷiyalilum kūṟiyuḷḷār. āytaṅ kuṟukumeṉa yāṇṭuṅ kūṟiṟṟilar. iṅṅaṉam piṟar cārpeḻutteṉak koṇṭa oṉpatu tolkāppiyarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum, avar cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟēyeṉṟum, avaṟṟuṭaṉāka eḻuttukkaḷ muppattumūṉṟē yeṉṟum variyaṟi ceytu koṇṭār. ataṉ kāraṇam eṉṉi yeṉpatē iṅku ārāytaṟkuriyatu. uyirmeyyi ōreḻuttākak koḷḷutal poruntāteṉac civañāṉa muṉivar tamatu tolkāppiyac cūttira viruttiyil naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār. atu,
"iṉi im mūṉṟumēyaṉṟi uyirmey mutaliyavaṟṟiyum cārpeḻutteṉpāru muḷarālōveṉiṉ, āl eṉpuḻi uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉāṟpōla, lā eṉpuḻiyum meymuṉṉum uyir piṉṉum niṉṟumayaṅkiṉa vēyallatu, uyirumeyyumākiya tantaṉmi tirintu vēṟākāmikku 'meyyōṭiyiyiṉu muyiriya ṟiriyā' eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷē cāṉṟākalāṉ, uyirmeyyākiya kālattum kuṟiṉmi neṭiṉmi yeṉṉum uyirttaṉmiyum, vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi yeṉṉum meyttaṉmiyum tammiyalpiṟ ṟirivu paṭāmiyāṉum, uṭaṉ mēluyir vantoṉṟutal poṉmaṇi pōla iyalpu puṇarcciyā
meṉpavākalāṉum, oṟṟumi nayattāṉ uyirmey yeṉpataṉit takara ñāḻal pōla ummit toki nilikkaḷattup piṟanta aṉmoḻittoki yeṉpārkkut takaramum ñāḻaluṅ kūṭiya cāntu piṉṉart takaramu ñāḻalu mākātavāṟu pōla meyyuyir nīṅkiṟṟaṉṉuruvātal poruntāmiyāṉum, tuṇaṅki yeṉpatu meymmutal uyirīṟu meymmayakkameṉavum varaku eṉpatu uyirttoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukara meṉavum koḷvataṉṟi, uyirmey mutal uyirmeyyīṟu, uyirmeymayakkam, uyirmeyt toṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram. eṉak koḷḷāmiyiṉ oṟṟumi nayampaṟṟi yoṉṟeṉpataṉāloru payaṉiṉmi
yāṉum, meykaḷellām uyirvantoṉṟātu taṉittu niṉṟa vaḻiyē māttiri koḷḷavum eḻutteṉṉavum paṭumeṉpatu avaṟṟiyal pākaliṉ atupaṟṟiyoṉṟeṉal vēṇṭāmi
yāṉum, avviyal paṟintu kōṭaṟkut taṉittu niṉṟameyyi yoṟṟeṉavum puḷḷiyeṉavum uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyi uyirmeyyeṉavum ciṟappup peyariṭṭāḷutal āciriyar karuttākalāṉum, uyirōṭu kūṭiyaviṭattu vari vaṭivuvēṟu
paṭutaliṉ atupaṟṟip 'puḷḷiyillāvellā meyyum' eṉa meymēl vittuc cūttirañ ceytu vaṭiveḻuttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāraṉṟi oliyeḻuttilakkaṇam vēṟupaṭak kūṟāmi
yāṉum - atu poruntāteṉa maṟukka" eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam uyirmey ōreḻuttākāteṉpataṟkuc civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟiya kāraṇaṅkaḷellām mikka valiyuṭiyaṉa veṉpatil aiyamilli. eṉiṉum, kā mutaliyaṉa ōreḻuttoru moḻiyeṉak koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, ceyyuḷiyalil, aci koḷḷumiṭattum piṟāṇṭum uyir meyyi ōreḻutteṉavē koḷḷavēṇṭi iruttalāṉum uyirmeyyi oruvāṟṟāl ōreḻuttākak koḷḷutalum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārkkuṭaṉ pāṭeṉpatu peṟṟām. ataṉāṟṟāṉ piṉṉūlāciriyarkaḷ uyirmeyyic cārpeḻuttuḷoṉṟākak koṇṭaṉarāvar. eṉiṉum, eḻuttiṉ taṉmiyi nōkkuḻi ataṉkaṇ uyirum meyyum vēṟuvēṟu pulappaṭa niṟṟalāṉum' uyirmeyyeṉa oṉṟu koḷḷātu kā eṉpatu k ā eṉa eḻutappaṭiṉum, eḻututaṟkum payilutaṟkum ciṟitu varuttamāvataṉṟi vēṟoriḻukkumiṉmi yāṉum tolkāppiyaṉār uyirmeyyi vēṟeḻuttāka vitteṇṇiṟṟilar. iṅṅaṉamē vaṭanūlār mutalāyiṉāruñ uyirum meyyuñ cērntataṉi ōreḻuttāka vitteṇ ṇāmiyum kāṇka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1. 	tolkāppiyam - eḻuttatikāram - nūṉmarapu - nūṟpā - 1
2. 	mēlatu - nūṟpā - 2
3. 	tolkāppiyam - collatikāram - uriyiyal - nūṟpā - 34
4. 	naṉṉūl - eḻuttatikāram - eḻuttiyal - nūṟpā - 5.

- nāvalar nāṭṭār tamiḻurikaḷ -16, 
pak. 43-50



2. tolkāppiya ārāycci
nūlāciriyar varalāṟu
1. tolkāppiyar jamatakkiṉi mintareṉpatum akattiyar māṇākkareṉpatum poruntumā eṉṉum ārāycci.
aṟivu cāṉṟa āṉṟōrkaḷē!
ivvārāycciyurikkup poruḷākavuḷḷa tolkāp
piyattil,
“aviyaṭakkilē ariṟavat teriyiṉ
  vallā kūṟiṉum vakuttaṉar koṇmiṉēṉ
  ṟellā māntarkkum vaḻimoḻin taṉṟē”
eṉa aviyaṭakkattiṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, yāṉum ammuṟi paṟṟi eṉ urikaḷiluḷḷa kuṟikaḷi nuṅkaḷ cīriya matiyāṟ ceppañ ceytu koḷkaveṉa vēṇṭi, eṉatu ārāycciyit toṭaṅkukiṉṟēṉ. ippoḻutu namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum kālattāl muntiyatum,  tamiḻiyal aṉittiyum muṟṟaveṭuttuk kūṟuvatum tolkāppiyamē eṉpatu tamiḻvallār aṉivarkkum oppa muṭinta karuttākum. innūliṉi naṉku ārāytalāṉē tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ iyalpukaḷiyum, tamiḻakattiṉ paḻiya nilimikaḷiyum oruvāṟu kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉpatil aiyamilli. yāṉ ivvārāycciyi naṭāttuḻip  paṇṭi nūl, uriyāciriyaṉmār koṇṭa koḷkikaḷukku orōvaḻi māṟākac cellutaluṅ kūṭum aṉṟi, itukāṟum yāṉ koṇṭirunta karuttukku muraṇākavum cila iruttal kūṭum. ārāycciyiṉ peṟṟi ituveṉavaṟinta aṟivuṭiyār ivi kuṟittu eṉṉi muṉiyāreṉṉum tuṇivuṭiyēṉ.
innūliṉ āciriyarākiya tolkāppiyar camatakkiṉi muṉivarkku mintar eṉṟum, akattiyaṉārkku māṇākkar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟār. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiyataṟku ātaravu, uriyāciriya rellāruḷḷum piṉ vantavarākiya āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, tolkāppiyap pāyiravuriyil, “tēva
rellāruṅ kūṭi yām cēraviruttaliṉ mēruntāḻntu teṉṟiciyuyarntatu, itaṟku akattiyaṉārē āṇṭiruttaṟ
kuriyareṉṟu avari vēṇṭikkoḷḷa, avarum teṉṟicikkaṭ pōtukiṉṟavar kaṅkiyāruḻic ceṉṟu kāviriyāri vāṅkik koṇṭu, piṉṉar yamataṅkiyāruḻic ceṉṟu avar makaṉār tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyāri vāṅkik koṇṭu, pulattiyaṉāruḻic ceṉṟu avaruṭaṉ piṟanta kumariyār  ulōpāmuttiriyāri avar koṭuppa nīrēṟṟu irīip, peyarntu... potiyiliṉkaṇiruntu, irāvaṇaṉik kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu, irākkatari āṇṭu iyaṅkāmi vilakki, tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyārākiya tolkāppiyaṉāri nōkki ‘nī ceṉṟu kumariyārik koṇṭu varuka’  eṉak kūṟiṉar” eṉa urittirukkum katiyaṉṟi vēṟilli.
akattiyaṉār eṉap peyariya tamiḻāciriyar oruvar paṇṭu viḷaṅkiyiruntaṉareṉpataṟkum, muttamiḻkkum ilakkaṇamāka avarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatoru virinta nūl akattiyameṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ tikaḻntateṉpataṟkum aḷaviṟanta cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi nāṉ eḻutiya ‘akattiyar’ eṉṉum uri nūlil virivākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. īṇṭuc cilavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟēṉ.
iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uriyil muccaṅka varalāṟu kūṟu
miṭattu, taliccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷum iṭiccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷum akattiyaṉār mutalil vittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. akattiyam muccaṅkattārkkum nūlāyiruntateṉavum atiṟkūṟap peṟṟuḷatu. piṉ vanta uriyāḷarkaḷ palarum akattiyaṉārip paṟṟiyum akattiyattip paṟṟiyum āṇṭāṇṭuk kuṟippiṭ ṭiruppatuṭaṉ, akattiyameṉac ciṟcila nūṟpākkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
“taraviṉṟākit tāḻici peṟṟum”
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiravuriyil ‘iṉi ivvāṟu vanta koccakaṅkaḷiyellām oru variyaṟip paṭuttup pāttōṟum iṉañcērttip paṇṇiṟkut tiṟam
pōlap piṉṉuḷḷa. āciriyar aṭakkuvar.  ataṉi akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum uṇarntu avartam karuttaṟinta āciriyar avvāṟaṭakkāmikkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar’ eṉa nacciṉārk kiṉiyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. avvāciriyar kūṟuṅ kāraṇam iviyeṉa avar piṉpu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavi yellām pērāciriya ruriyāka viruttaliṉ, akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum uṇarntavareṉa avarāṟ cuṭṭappeṟṟavar pērāciriyarē yeṉpatu tuṇipu. “āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ’ eṉṉum ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiravuriyil ‘iḵtu eṟṟāṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ, itaṉ mutaṉūl ceyta āciriyaṉ akattiyaṉār collumāṟṟāṟ peṟutum eṉṟavāṟu” eṉap pērāciriyar tāmē kūṟiyiruppatum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.
itiliruntu kaṭiccaṅka kālattiṉ piṉ neṭunāḷ vari akattiyam vaḻaṅki vantateṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟalākum. eṉiṉum ataṟku yārum uri kaṇṭāreṉat teriyāmiyiṉ, uri
yāḷarkaḷ kālattil annūl muḻuvuruvuṭaṉ kāṇak kūṭātavāṟu citintu pōyiṟṟeṉṟu karututal cālum. iṉi, taliccaṅkap pulavarāyiruntu tamiḻilakkaṇam iyaṟṟiya ivvakattiyarum, muṉivar palaruḷḷum ciṟantavarāka vittup purāṇavitikācaṅkaḷ kūṟum akattiyarum, irukku vētappatikaṅ kaḷiṟ palavaṟṟi iyaṟṟiya akattiyarum oruvareṉṟō, vevvēṟāvar eṉṟō aṟutiyiṭṭurittal mika aritākum. akattiyar eṉpatu ōr kuṭumpap peyarē eṉṟum, ap peyaruṭiyār palar iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum, mutalil vintamaliyiṉ vaṭakkil ōr akattiyarum, irāmāyaṇa kālattil kōtāvariyāṟṟaṅkariyil ōr akattiyarum, pārata kālattil potiyiṉ maliyil maṟṟōr akattiyarum iruntuḷāreṉṟum oru cārār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟoru cārār akattiyar peyariṉar palareṉak kūṟuvatuṭaṉ taliccaṅkattu akattiyar iruntāreṉpatu tavaṟu eṉṟum, nakkīraṉārkku ilakkaṇam aṟivuṟuttuvarākat tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam kūṟukiṉṟa akattiyaroruvar kaṭiccaṅka nāḷil iruntārāvareṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl avartam kūṟṟukku yātoru ātāramum kāṭṭappaṭavilli. orē peyaruṭiya palar orupeṟṟiyē nikaraṟṟa perumi vāyn
tiruntaṉar eṉpatu putumiyākat tōṟṟiṉum aṅṅaṉam nikaḻtal kūṭāteṉpatilli. eṉiṉum avar oruvarallar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu iṉṟiyamiyātataṉṟō? enta nūlkaḷiṉ vāyilāka akattiyar eṉṉum peyari nām aṟikiṉṟōmō anta nūlkaḷaṉittum akattiyari oruvarākavē vittuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. appeyariṉar palareṉpataṟkut tiṉiyaḷavu cāṉṟum kāṇappaṭavilli. vintattiṟku vaṭakkilirunta akattiyariyum potiyil akattiyariyum oruvarākavē kanta purāṇam mutaliyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kōtāvari maruṅkirunta akattiyarum potiyilakattiyarum vēṟallareṉpatu irāmāyaṇattāl aṟiyappaṭum.
“nīṇṭa tamiḻālulaki nēmiyiṉaḷantāṉ”
“taḻaṟpuri cuṭarkkaṭavuḷ tantatamiḻ tantāṉ”
“eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟami ḻiyampiyici koṇṭāṉ”
eṉa akattiyap paṭalattuk kampar kūṟumāṟuṅ kāṇka. maṟṟum avar ‘vintameṉum viṇṭōy nākamatu nākamuṟa nākameṉa niṉṟa’ miyum, ‘īcaṉika rāyulaku cīrpeṟa virunta’ miyum kūṟutalum karutaṟpāṟṟu. akattiyar palareṉpār, muṉṉuḷḷa nūlāciriyar palarum peyarorumiyāl maruṇṭu akattiya roruvareṉak koṇṭu vaḻuvi viṭṭaṉareṉak kūṟuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭiyarākiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu evvāṟāyiṉum ākuka. irāma rāvaṇap pōrukku muṉṉarē akattiyar potiyiliṉ kaṇ iruntaṉa reṉpatiyum, avarē akattiyamiyaṟṟiya tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉpatiyum namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa ātaravukaḷaṉittum valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉat tilirunta kāntaṉ eṉṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉ aracar kulattik kaḷitaliyē nōṉpākak koṇṭa paracurāmaṉ taṉṉoṭu pōrkuṟittu varutaliyaṟintu akattiya muṉivaric caraṇaṭintāṉeṉṟu maṇimēkalikkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatum, akattiyar irāvaṇaṉi veṉṟu pōkkiyatākat tolkāppiya uriyilum maturik kāñciyuriyilum nacciṉākkiṉiyar kūṟuvatum pōlvaṉa vellām ikkarutti araṇ ceytu niṟṟal kāṇka.
iṉi, tolkāppiyaṉār akattiyarkku māṇākkar tāmō eṉpatu ārāytaṟkuriyatu. tamiḻurikaḷil muṟpaṭṭa
tākiya iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluriyāṉatu tolkāppiyari iṭiccaṅkattup pulavarākavum, tolkāppiyatti iṭic
caṅkattārkkum kaṭiccaṅkattārkkum nūlākavum kūṟuvataṉṟi avari akattiyaṉāroṭu toṭarpupaṭuttu yātum kūṟiṟṟilatu.
“maṉṉiya ciṟappiṉ vāṉōr vēṇṭat
  teṉmali yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaraṉ
  ṟaṉpāṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ tāviṉ ṟuṇarnta
  tuṉṉaruñ cīrttit tolkāppiyaṉ mutaṟ
  paṉṉiru pulavarum pāṅkuṟap pakarnta
  paṉṉiru paṭalam paḻippiṉ ṟuṇarntōṉ”
eṉa varum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālip pāyiramē mutaṉ mutalākat tolkāppiyari akattiyar māṇākkar eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē piṉvanta uriyāciriyar cilarum kūṟuvārāyiṉar. veṇpā mālikku mutaṉūlākiya paṉṉirupaṭalam kaḷaviyalurip pāyirattil aḷaviṉāṟ peyar peṟṟataṟku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭap paṭṭiruttaliṉ innūlkaḷ ki.pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟpaṭṭavi yalla veṉṟu karutal poruntum. eṉiṉum tolkāppiyar mutaṟ paṉṉiru pulavarum cērntiyaṟṟiyatu paṉṉiru paṭalam eṉpatil iḷampūraṇa vaṭi
kaḷum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum karuttoruppaṭātavarākiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇiyiyaluriyil iḷampūraṇar “akattiṇi ēḻākip puṟattiṇi paṉṉiraṇṭākil moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vittal eṉṉum tantiravuttikkum poruntātāki, miki paṭakkūṟal, taṉṉāṉoru poruḷ karutik kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamum payakkum eṉka” eṉṟum, “paṉṉiru paṭalattuḷ ‘taṉṉuṟu toḻilē vēntuṟu toḻileṉ-ṟaṉṉaviruvakittē veṭci’ eṉa iraṇṭu kūṟu paṭak kūṟiṉārāyiṉum... avar avvāṟu kūṟal mikipaṭak kūṟalām. ataṉāl paṉṉirupaṭalattuḷ veṭcippaṭalam tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉār eṉṟal poruntātu” eṉṟum, “paṉṉiru paṭalattuḷ karantikkaṇ puṇṇoṭu varutal mutalāka vēṟupaṭac cila tuṟi kūṟiṉārāl eṉiṉ, puṇpaṭuttal māṟṟōr ceyta maṟattuṟi yākaliṉ aḵtu ivarkku māṟākak kūṟutal mayaṅkakkūṟutalām; ēṉiyavum ivvāṟu mayaṅkak kūṟalum, kuṉṟak kūṟalum, mikipaṭak kūṟalum āyavāṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉ perukumākaliṉ uyttuṇarntu kaṇṭu koḷka” eṉṟum iṅṅaṉam palaviṭattum paṉṉiru paṭalatti maṟuttiruppatuṭaṉ, veṭcippaṭalam tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyataṉṟeṉat tuṇintum urittuḷḷār. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum paṉṉiru paṭalak koḷkiyip palaviṭattu maṟuttiruppatuṭaṉ, ‘koṭuppōrētti’ eṉṉum cūttiravuriyil “tattam putu nūl vaḻikaḷāṟ puṟa nāṉūṟṟiṟkut tuṟi kūṟiṉārēṉum, akattiyamum tolkāppiyamumē tokikaḷukku nūlākaliṉ avar cūttirap poruḷākat tuṟikūṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟuṇarka” eṉavum urittuḷḷār. ivaṟṟiliruntu paṉṉiru paṭalam eṉpatu piṟkālattu yārō oruvarāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟu, akattiyar māṇākkar paṉṉiruvarālum iyaṟṟappeṟṟateṉak kaṭṭiyurikkappaṭṭateṉpatē avarkaḷ karuttātal peṟappaṭum.  ‘viṉiyiṉīṅki’ eṉṉum marapiyaṟ cūttiravuriyiṟ pērāciriyar “paṉṉiru paṭalattup puṉinturi vakiyāṟ pāyirac cūttirattuḷ urikkappaṭṭatu” eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal ūṉṟiyuṇariṉ avarkaruttum ituveṉpatu pōtarum. ivvāṟṟāl akattiyarkku māṇākkar tolkāppiyar eṉṉum koḷkiyum valiyaṟṟoḻital kaṇkūṭām. tolkāppiyattilē vaṭacoṟkaḷi eṭuttāḷutaṟku viti kūṟiyiruppatuṭaṉ ampōtaraṅkam, kuñcaram, viciyaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ payiṉṟiruppatum, ikara aikāravīṟṟu tiṅkaṭ peyarum nāṭpeyarum niṉṟu puṇartaṟku vaḻivakuttiruppatum, vaḻi nūl vakiyuḷ moḻi peyarttiyaṟṟalum oṉṟāka vitittiruppatum, piṟavum ōrntuṇariṉ āriyamakkaḷiṉ kalappup peritum ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarē innūl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatāku meṉil poruttamām. irāmāyaṇa kālattil attuṇi mikutiyāka āriyakkalappu iruntirukka iṭamiṉmiyāl āciriyar akattiyaṉārkku neṭunāḷiṉ piṉpu tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyatākumeṉalē nēritu. ivvāṟṟāl tolkāppiya ṉār akattiyaṉārkku māṇākkarākkutal cellāteṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ākavē, avar camatakkiṉiyiṉ putalvar eṉpatum, akattiyarāl aḻittu varappeṟṟaṉa reṉpatum poruntāmi kūṟal vēṇṭā eṉka. tolkāppiyar akattiyaṉārkku uṭaṉ kālattiṉaralla rāyiṉum avaratu koḷkiyip pōṟṟiyō rarukiṉamiyāl avarkku māṇākkareṉap peṟṟaṉar eṉak karututal cālum. iṉi pārata nikaḻccikkup piṉpē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟa teṉpataṟku uṟutiyuṭiya cāṉṟu oṉṟu uḷḷatu. tolkāppiyattu uyir mayaṅkiyalil,
“paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kāli
  niliyiṉ ṟākum aiyeṉuyirē
  ākāram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.”
“koṭimuṉ variṉē aiyavaṇiṟpak
  kaṭiniliyiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti”
eṉṉuñcūttiraṅkaḷāl paṉi eṉpatu koṭi eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarttup paṉikkoṭiyeṉac ceyki ceytu muṭikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷil it toṭar payiṉṟiruntālaṉṟi āciriyar iṅṅaṉam vitikūṟā
reṉpatu tēṟṟam. toki nūlkaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum nampi mūttapirāṉākiya palarāmaṉ palaviṭattuk kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇalākum. avaṟkuk koṭi paṉiyeṉpatum avaṟṟuḷ kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu.
“putiyiruḷuṭukkip polampaṉik koṭiyōṟku”
eṉavum,
“niṉṉoṉ ṟuyarkoṭi paṉi”
eṉap paripāṭalilum
“aṭalvēn nāñciṟ paṉik koṭi yōṉum”
eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟilum,
“poṟpaṉi veḷḷiyi yuḷḷā toḻukiṉṉā”
eṉa iṉṉā nāṟpatilum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl palarāmaṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiya pārata nikaḻccikkup piṉpē innūl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatātal orutali. pārata kālattip paṟṟi ārāycci ceytuḷḷār palar. pāratap pōr naṭantatu ki.mu.1000-ām āṇṭil eṉṟu oruvarum, ki.mu.1100-ām āṇṭil eṉṟu maṟṟoruvarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ki.mu.1194-ām āṇṭil nikaḻntateṉṟu cittūr kōpāliyar ārāyn turikkiṉṟaṉar. cu.ū. tattar kūṟuṅkālam iṉṉuñ ciṟitu muṉṉarc celkiṉṟatu. ū.ē. vittiyā eṉpavar ki.mu. 3100 tāṉ pāratappōriṉ kālamāmeṉṟu pala cāṉṟukaḷāl niṟuvu kiṉṟaṉar. tuvāpara kaliyuka caktiyil pāratam nikaḻnta teṉṉum paramparik koḷki vittiyar avarkaḷāl vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷ koṇṭum niṟuvappaṭutaliṉ atuvē pāratap pōriṉ kālamākumeṉa yāmum koḷḷaṟpālam. iṉi, māpāratap pōriṉ piṉpu tolkāppiyam eppoḻutu tōṉṟiya teṉpatu ārāytaṟkuriyatu. ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ tamiḻ moḻi tiruntiya niliyiṭinta tiṉṟeṉṉum karuttuṭiya oruvar tolkāppiyam annūṟṟāṇṭi yaṭuttut tōṉṟiyatākum eṉpar. ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa jinēntiramē tolkāppiyappāyirattil ‘aintiram’ eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭateṉṉum karuttuṭiya orucārār aḵtu ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭiyil utitta tākum eṉpar. cātavākaṉaṉ eṉṉum āntiramaṉṉaṉukku amiccaṉāyirunta cārvavarmaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa ‘kātantiram’ eṉpatē tirāviṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭiṟṟu eṉak koṇṭa, maṟṟoru cārār innūl ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṉpu eḻuntatākumeṉpar.
iṅṅaṉam airōppiya āciriyar cilarālum intiya āciriyar cilarālum palavāṟākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vellām paḻaṅkatiyāyp pōṉamiyiṉ iṉi avaṟṟiṉ porun tāviyalpukaḷi eṭuttu viḷakkutal vēṇṭā. kaṭic caṅkattiṉ iṟutikkālamāṉatu ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkat tiṟkup piṟpaṭṭataṉṟu eṉpatu varalāṟṟaṟivuṭiya tamiḻ vallōr palarālum ippoḻutu naṉku tuṇiyappeṟṟuḷḷatu. kaṭiccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷē perum pakutiyiṉar ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭilum iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭilum iruntōrāvar. avarkaḷāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟa ceyyuṭkaḷil cakaṭam, camam, camaḻppu, ñamali, ñamaṉ, yavaṉar mutaliya coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu vantiruppavum, tolkāppiyaṉār,
“cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟōraṟṟē
  a ai oḷaveṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē”
“āe,
  o eṉum mūvuyir ñakārat turiya”
  āvō ṭallatu yakaram mutalātu”
eṉṉum cūttiraṅkaḷāl ca, ña, ya, mutaliya eḻuttukaḷ moḻikku mutalākā eṉa vilakkiyuḷḷār. maṟṟum avar,
“	nāṟcīr koṇṭa taṭiyeṉappaṭumē”
eṉṟu kūṟivittu, eḻuttuvariyaṟi koṇṭu annāṟ
cīraṭiyiyē kuṟaḷaṭi, cintaṭi, nēraṭi, neṭilaṭi, kaḻineṭilaṭi eṉa aivakippaṭutturittaṉar. itu kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi eṉappaṭum. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷilō ik kaṭṭaḷiyaṭikku ilakkiyam kāṇu
māṟilli. caṅkac ceyyuḷ palavaṟṟiyum ārāyntu tol
kāppiyattiṟku uri vakutta pērāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ‘āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ’ eṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyil, “ciṟappuṭiya kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuṭ payiṉṟu varal vēṇṭum piṟaveṉiṉ, innūl ceyta kālattil talic caṅkattārum iṭiccaṅkattārum kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi payiṉṟu varacceyyuḷ ceytāreṉpatu ic cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ peṟutum; piṉpu kaṭiccaṅkattārkku aḵtu aritākaliṉ cīrvakiyaṭi payilac ceyyuḷ ceytāreṉṟuṇarka” eṉa urittaṉar. iṅṅaṉam kaṭiccaṅka nāḷilēyē tolkāppiyar kūṟiyaṉa iṟantu
paṭṭaṉavum, kūṟātaṉa tōṉṟiyaṉavumāka uḷḷavi cālappala. avaṟṟi virippiṟ perukum. ivaṟṟiliruntu kaṭiccaṅka nāṭkuc cila pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyateṉal peṟappaṭum. kaṭiccaṅka nāḷiṉ muṉpē tolkāppiyar iruntāreṉak koṇṭāruḷ oru cārār, ilaṅkit tīviṉ varalāṟṟāṟ peṟappaṭum kaṭalkōḷ palavaṟṟuḷ ki.mu. 306l nikaḻnta mūṉṟāṅ kaṭal kōṭkuc ciṟitu muṉṉē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟateṉavum, alekcāntar eṉṉum kirēkka vēntaṉ intiyāviṉ mēṟpaṭiyeṭuttu vantapoḻutu uṭaṉpōnta kirēkka vāṉa nūlāciriyarāṟ koṇṭu varappaṭṭa ‘hōrā’ eṉṉum moḻi tolkāppiyattuḷ ‘ōri’ eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭutaliṉ atu ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappeṟṟateṉal pōtarum eṉavum kūṟuvar. maṟṟum oru cārār pāṇiṉiyāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟṟa vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇavāciriyar aṟupattu nālvaruḷ mutalvaṉākiya intiraṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ‘aintiram’ eṉṉum nūliṉic cuṭṭi ‘aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ” eṉap paṉampāraṉār pāyirattuḷ ōtutaliṉ, pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālamākiya ki.mu.350kku muṉpē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟatākum eṉpar. ōrieṉṉum tamiḻc collē ‘arici’ mutaliya coṟkaḷ pōṉṟu kirēkka moḻiyiṟ pukuntatāmeṉak karutaṟkup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ iruttalāṉum, tolkāppiyar pāṇiṉikkup piṉṉiruntavarāyiṉ vaṭamoḻiyil mikacciṟappuṭiyatākiya pāṇiṉīyatti naṉku kaṟṟu, ōr pakutiyiṟ piṟanta pala coṟkaḷi ārāyntu ataṉkaṇ aṭakkum annūṉmuṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭu tāmum tamatu nūliṉ kaṇ coṟkaḷi ārāyātu viṭārākalāṉum, pāṇiṉīyam niṟinta eṉṉātu ‘aintiram niṟinta’ eṉak kūṟiyiruppataṉāl avar pāṇiṉīyatti uṇarntavarallar eṉpatu peṟappaṭutalāṉum tolkāppiyar pāṇiṉikku muṉṉiruntārāvar eṉṉum koḷkiyē valiyuṟā niṟkum. pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 300 eṉṟum, 350-eṉṟum, ki.mu.700kku nīṇṭanāḷiṉ muṉṉākum eṉṟum carittira aṟiñar palar palavāṟu kūṟā niṟpar es. kiruṣṇa pēl vālkar eṉṉum aṟiñar tamatu ‘vaṭamoḻi’ ilakkaṇavoḻuṅkukaḷ’ eṉṉum nūlilē’ pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup pintiyataṉṟeṉa ārāyntu niṟuviyuḷḷār. pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālam etuvāyiṉum tolkāppiyaṉār avarkku muṉṉiruntārāvareṉpatu, kaṭippiṭikka tolkāppiyar kālatti ārāytaṟkuriya maṟṟoru cātaṉam kumariyāṟu kaṭalkoḷḷappaṭuvataṟku muṉpu avar iruntāreṉpatu. kalittoki, cilappatikāram, kaḷaviyaluri mutaliya tamiḻnūl, urikaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil nikaḻnta kaṭal kōḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilappatikāravuriyil aṭiyārkku nallār kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṟku nāṟpattoṉpatu tamiḻnāṭukaḷ iruntu kaṭalkoḷḷappaṭṭateṉak kūṟukiṉṟār. erṉasṭ hekkal, skāṭ eliyaṭ mutaliya pēraṟiñarkaḷāṟ kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟa kumarikkaṇṭa (lemūriyā) aḻivu paṉṉūṟāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu nikaḻntatākal vēṇṭum. ‘eliyaṭ’ paṇṭitar kūṟum aintu periya piraḷayaṅkaḷil ki.mu. 9564-il nikaḻnta aintām piraḷayattāl ēḻēḻ tamiḻnāṭukaḷ kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu karututal poruntum. eṉiṉum kumariyāṟum, ataṉ teṟkē ciṟu nilappakutiyum eñciyiruntu piṉṉoru kaṭal kōḷāl aḻintirukka vēṇṭum. kaḷaviyaluriyāṉatu ‘akkālattuppōlum pāṇṭiya ṉāṭṭik kaṭalkoṇṭatu’ eṉa iṭiccaṅkattiṟutiyil nikaḻntatākak karutiyurikkiṉṟa kaṭalkōḷāl kumariyāṟu aḻintirukka vēṇṭumātaliṉ ataṟku muṉpē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟatāmeṉka. paṉampāraṉār kumariyāṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku elliyākak kūṟiyiruppatum, ‘kaṭal koḷvataṉ muṉpu piṟanāṭu muṇmiyiṉ ṟātaṟkum elli kūṟappaṭṭatu’ eṉa iḷampūraṇar urittiruppatum pōlvaṉa itaṉi valiyuṟuttāniṟkum. iṉi akkaṭalkōḷ ekkālattu nikaḻntatō eṉiṉ, kūṟuval. yūtavētattiṉ ātiyākamattilē nōvāviṉ kālattil ōr piraḷayam uṇṭāṉa varalāṟu viritturikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu kimu. 2344il nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu yūtanūlāciriyarkaḷ kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. pāpilōṉiyarkkuḷḷum ōr kaṭal kōṭ ceyti vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉ varalāṟu ki.mu.2000-il avarkaḷāl veṭṭappaṭṭa kalveṭ ṭoṉṟilē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ilaṅkit tīviṉ varalāṟṟilum ki.mu.2387il ōr kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntami kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. manuvaracaṉ kālattil ōr piraḷayam nikaḻntamiyic catapata pirāmaṇam terivikkiṉṟatu. appiraḷayattōṭu toṭarpuṟṟa manuvaracaṉ maliyamalimēl tavam purintu koṇṭirun tāṉeṉa maccapurāṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu. pākavata purāṇamāṉatu kirutamāliyāṟṟaṅkariyilē tirāviṭa aracaṉāṉa cattiyavirataṉ eṉpāṉ tavañceytu koṇṭirunta
poḻutu aṅku mīṉ vaṭivuṭaṉ vanta oru teyvam aṅkē nikaḻap pōkum kaṭalkōḷi avaṉukku muṉṉarē aṟivittu, avvāṟē atu nikaḻntapoḻutu ōr marakkalattil avaṉiyum avaṉic cārntōriyum ēṟṟuvittuk koṇṭupōy uyvitta varalāṟṟi viritturikkiṉṟatu. yūtarkaḷiṉ ākamattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṭalkōḷ varalāṟṟōṭu pākavatapurāṇattiṉ varalāṟu peritum ottiruttal karutaṟpālatu. iṅṅaṉam vevvēṟu nāṭṭiṉar kūṟum piraḷayam varalāṟṟālum kālattālum peritum ottiruttalāṉum, atu tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻntateṉpataṟkup purāṇaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi paḻiya tamiḻnūl urikaḷum, nilanūl uṇmiyum cāṉṟu pakartalāṉum iṟṟikkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 4200-āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu tamiḻakattiṉ teṟkē oru kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntu,  ataṉāṟ kumariyāṟum, ataṉic cārnta nilappakutiyum aḻintaṉa veṉṟum, tolkāppiyam ataṟku muṉṉarē iyaṟṟappeṟṟa tākumeṉṟum koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. orukkāl ikkaṭal kōḷāṟ kumariyāṟu aḻiyātiruntiruppiṉ, ilaṅki varalāṟṟilē ki.mu.504il nikaḻntatākak kūṟappaṭum iraṇṭāṅ kaṭalkōḷiṉ muṉpāvatu tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟap peṟṟatākal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟṟāl ilaṅki varalāṟu kūṟum iraṇṭāṅ kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻnta ki.mu.504ām āṇṭukkum, pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālamākiya ki.mu.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum muṉṉum, pārata kālamākiya ki.mu.3100kkup piṉṉum uḷḷa kālap pakutiyoṉṟilē tolkāppiyar iruntāreṉpatu koḷḷaṟpāṟṟu.
tolkāppiyaratu kalvip perumiyum, 
nūlamipput tiṟaṉum :
tolkāppiyap pāyiramāṉatu tolkāppiyarit tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ārāyntavar eṉṟum, muntu nūl kaṇṭavar eṉṟum, aintiram niṟintavar  eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvāciriyarkku muṉpē tamiḻil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ pala iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu ivar tāmē ‘eṉpa’ eṉṟum, ‘eṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉṟum ivvāṟākat tollāciriyar matampaṭap palaviṭattuṅ kūṟic cellutalāl aṟiyappaṭum. muntunūl eṉṟatu akattiyam oṉṟaṉiyē yeṉac cilar koṇṭaṉar. aḵtoṉṟē yāyiṉ ōriṭattēṉum ataṉi vitantōtātu yāṇṭum ‘eṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉṟum, ‘kaṭiniliyiṉṟē āciriyarkku’ eṉṟum potuppaṭak kūṟic cellārākaliṉ, ivarkku muṉpē pala nūlkaḷ iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, avaṟṟiyē ‘muntu nūl’ eṉap pāyiramum kūṟiṟṟeṉavum kōṭal vēṇṭum. muntu nūlāvaṉa akattiyamum, māpurāṇamum, pūtapurāṇamum, ici nuṇukkamum eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟiṉar. appoḻu tiruntavi iṉṉiṉṉa nūlkaḷeṉa aṟutiyiṭṭurittal cālāteṉiṉum akattiyam uḷḷiṭṭa pala nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa veṉpatu tēṟṟam. ivar avaṟṟiyum, aṟṟināḷ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷiyum naṉkārāyntavar eṉap pāyiram kūṟuvatum, aviyaṉittiyum muṟṟavaṟintavar eṉakkarutaṟkuriya kuṟippukkaḷ tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatum ivaratu kalviyiṉ akalattiyum āḻattiyum, naṉku pulappaṭuttuvaṉa vām. eṭuttukkāṭṭākap piṉvarum kuṟippukkaḷ cila nōkkaṟpālaṉa. moḻi marapiṉ kaṇṇē moḻikku iṟutiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷi uṇarttumiṭattu ‘uccakāram iru mōḻikkurittē’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl cakaravukaratti īṟṟiluṭiya muṟṟiyalukaramoḻi, tamiḻil iraṇṭē uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum,
‘uppakāram oṉṟeṉa moḻiya 
 iruvayiṉiliyum poruṭṭā kummē’
eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl pakaravukaratti īṟṟiluṭiya muṟṟukara moḻi oṉṟēyuṇṭeṉṟum aḵtoṉṟumē taṉviṉip poruḷum piṟaviṉipporuḷum uṭittākumeṉṟum kūṟiṉar. piṉṉum nakaravoṟṟi īṟākavuṭiya col uccakāra moḻipōl iraṇṭēyeṉṟum, ñakaravoṟṟi yīṟāka
vuṭiya col uppakāramoḻipōl oṉṟēyeṉṟum, āṉāl itu iruporuṭpaṭāteṉṟum kūṟiṉar. maṟṟum nilam nilaṉ, kalam kalaṉ eṉpapōla makarattōṭu mayaṅkuta
lillāta ṉakaravīṟṟu aḵṟiṇic coṟkaḷ oṉpatu uḷḷaṉa
veṉṟum, tokimarapiṉ kaṇṇē aḷavup peyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum mutalākavuḷḷaṉa kacatapanamavau eṉṉum oṉpateḻuttumē eṉṟum variyaṟuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivi
pōlvaṉavum, uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya palatiṟappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiyum eñcāteṭuttu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarttuc ceyki ceytu muṭippatum, vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ poruḷ vikaṟpaṅkaḷiyum avi mayaṅkivarumāṟṟiyum mika viritturippatum, iḷamippeyarum āṇpāṟpeyarum peṇpāṟpeyarum ākiya marapup peyarkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum eṭuttu avi iṉṉiṉṉa  vaṟṟukku uriyaveṉa uṇarttuvatum piṟavum tamiḻ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ coṟporuṭ parappu muḻutum ivvāciriyarāl naṉku vaṭittaṟiyap peṟṟaṉa eṉpati uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉiyeṉat teḷḷitiṉ viḷakkuvaṉavātal kāṇka.
iṉi ivvāciriyartāmē cūttirattiyalpeṉak kūṟiya cilvakiyeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭātalum nuṇmiyoṭu
puṇarnta oṇmittātalum pōlvaṉavellām oruṅkamin
taṉavākat tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ viḷaṅkuvatum, avaṟṟiṉ
kaṇ coṟkaḷ poṉpaṇiyāmāṟu pōla ivar karutiya
vāṟellām tirintu aḻaku ceyvatum pōlvaṉavaṟṟi nōkkiṉ ivar vaḻivaḻiyākat tamiḻilē ūṟivantatoru kuṭiyiṉ kaṭṭōṉṟi, uṇṇuñcōṟum parukunīrum tiṉṉum veṟṟiliyumellām tamiḻākavē koṇṭu vaḷarntu talimiyeytiya cāṉṟōrāvar eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
“mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum”
“ovvu maṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭiyē”
“ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum”
“uppakāramoṭu ñakāriyumaṟṟē
  apporuḷiraṭṭātivaṇiyāṉa
eṉṉum cūttiraṅkaḷiluḷḷa mutalā, mutalum, alaṅkaṭi, civaṇum, ñakāri, ivaṇi yeṉṉum colvaḻakku
kaḷiyum, innūṟpākkaḷiṉ tiṭpa nuṭpaṅkaḷiyum nōkkuṅ
kaḷ. iṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyaṟ cūttiram cilavaṉṟi naṉṉūl ciṉṉūl toṉṉūl mutalāya piṟkālattu ennūliṉ kaṇṇum 
oru cūttirattāṉum innūṟpākkaḷiṉ aṇimiyilum niṟṟaṟ
kuriya taṉṟāyiṉ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉāratu tamiḻp pulamiyiṉ viḻuppatti eṅṅaṉam aḷaviṭṭurittal kūṭum? iṉi ivar vaṭamoḻiyilum perum pulami vāyntavareṉpatu ‘aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉeṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi’ eṉṉum pāyirak kūṟṟāṉē tuṇiyappaṭum.
“akatteḻu vaḷiyici yariṟapa nāṭi
  aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittē”
eṉa ivar kūṟuvatu arumaṟiyiṉkaṇ ivarukkuḷḷa payiṟciyip pulappaṭuttā niṟkum.
iṉi ivvāciriyar innūli amikkum tiṟappāṭukaḷ nōkkaṟpālaṉa. eḻuttum collum poruḷumākiya mūṉṟaṉiyum mūṉṟu atikārattāṟ kūṟaluṟṟa ivar ovvōratikārattiyum oṉpatu oṉpatu iyalkaḷāka vakuttuk koṇṭuḷḷār. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr atikāramum iyalvakiyāl ōraḷaviṉavāka vakuttiruppatu tām kūṟakkarutiya poruḷkaḷi ōrelliyuḷ aṭakka muyaṉṟu iṭarppaṭuva tākumeṉac cilar karutavuṅkūṭum. eṉiṉum avviyalkaḷ yāvum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavum, avvavvatikāraṅkaḷōṭu iyipuḷ ḷaṉavumātal nuṇṇaṟivāṉ ārāyvārkkup pulaṉākā niṟkum, āyiṉ ōratikāratti oṉpāṉ iyaliṟ kuṟiyavō mikavō vakuttamittal ollātō eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉañ ceytal ollumāyiṉum oru nikarāṉa variyaṟiyuṭimiyiṉ payaṉkaruti aṅṅaṉam iyalkaḷi variyaṟi ceytu, poruḷaḷavukkēṟpac cūttiraṅkaḷi mikuttum kuṟittum iṭarppāṭiṉṟi yāttamittuḷḷāreṉal vēṇṭum. paṇṭiyāciriyarkaḷ tām karutiya poruḷkaḷi eñcāmaṟ kūṟuvatil nōkkuṭiyarāyiruntamipōṉṟē avi piṟkālattil tiripiṉṟi niṉṟu nilavavum, kaṟpōruḷḷattil eḷitiṟpukuntu akalātirukkavum uriya muṟikaḷi iyalumaḷavu kikkoṇṭu avaṟṟik kūṟum karuttuṭiyārāvar. pāṇiṉiyum tamatu nūliṉ eṭṭu attiyāyaṅkaḷiyum nannāṉku pātaṅkaḷāka vakuttuḷḷaṉar. tirukkuṟaḷ, tēvāram, tiruvāymoḻi mutaliya tamiḻ maṟikaḷum, āriya vētap pakutiyākiya aitarēyap pirāmmaṇam mutaliyaṉavum, caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil patiṟṟuppattu mutalāyiṉavum ovvōraḷaviṉa vāka iyaṉṟiruttalum kāṇka. iṉi, ivvāciriyar meyppāṭṭiyaliṉ kaṇ eṇvaki meyppāṭum nikaḻuṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷi uṇarttumiṭattu,
“kalvi taṟukaṇ icimi koṭiyeṉac
  collappaṭṭa perumitam nāṉkē”
“celvam pulaṉē puṇarvu viṉiyāṭṭeṉ
  ṟallal nītta vuvaki nāṉkē”
eṉṟiṅṅaṉam ovvoru meyppāṭṭukkum nannāṉku kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatum, uvamaviyalilē viṉi, payaṉ, mey, uru eṉṉum nālvaki uvamattiṟkum urupukaḷ taṉittaṉi evveṭṭāka variyaṟutturippatum, peyariyalilē, 
“avaṉivaṉ uvaṉeṉa varuum peyarum
  avaḷivaḷ uvaḷeṉa varūum peyarum
  avarivar uvareṉa varūum peyarum
  yāṉ yām nāmeṉa varūum peyarum
  yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāvar eṉṉum  
  āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappatiṉintum
  pālaṟi vanta uyartiṇip peyarē” 
eṉavum,
“atuvitu utuveṉa varūum peyarum
  avimuta lākiya āytap peyarum
  aviyivi uviyeṉa varūum peyarum
  avimuta lākiya vakarap peyarum
  yātuyā yāvi eṉṉum peyarum  
  āvayiṉ mūṉṟōṭappatiṉintum
  pālaṟi vanta aḵṟiṇip peyarē”
eṉavum ōriṉamāṉa mummūṉṟu  peyarkaḷi ovvōraṭiyil niṟutti aḻakum teḷivum poruntak kūṟuvatum pōlvaṉa kaṟpōruḷḷattil avi naṉku patiyumāṟu ceyya mēṟ koṇṭa muṟikaḷām eṉka. iṉi ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil potuvum ciṟappum mutaliya vitikaḷ oṉṟaṉiyoṉṟu pōṟṟiyum maṟuttum cellum iyalpiṉavākaliṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaṟpār nūliṉ oru pakuti kaṟṟu amiyātu nūṉmuḻutuṅ kaṟkum kaṭappāṭuṭiyarāvar. akkaṭappāṭ ṭiṉiṉṟum avar vilakāmip poruṭṭu ivar nūṟpākkaḷi amittirukkun tiṟaṉ mika viyattaṟkuriyatu. iruḷ eṉṉuñ col niṉṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyil varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟavantavar ‘iruḷeṉkiḷavi veyiliyal niliyum’ eṉṟār. veyil eṉṉuñ coṟku aṅṅaṉam viti kūṟumiṭattō ‘veyileṉkiḷavi maḻiyiyal niliyum’ eṉṟār. maṟṟu, maḻi eṉṉuñ coṟku viti kūṟuṅkāl ‘maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiyal niliyum’ eṉṟār. vaḷi eṉṉuñ collukku vitiyum ‘vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum - avviyal niliyal cevviteṉpa’ eṉa ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa cūttirattōṭumāṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār. accūttirantāṉ ‘paṉiyeṉa varūum kālavēṟṟumikkattum iṉṉum cāriyi yākum’ eṉpatu. iṅṅaṉam uyir mayaṅkiyal 39-ām cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya vitiyi 161-ām cūttiraṅkaṭkuppiṉ puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaliluḷḷa ‘iruḷeṉ kiḷavi’ eṉṉum cūttiraṅkāṟum toṭarntu varacceytiruttaliṉ ivi yaṉittiyum oruṅku kaṟṟuṇarntālaṉṟi iruḷ, veyil, maḻi, vaḷi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ attuccāriyi yāvatu iṉcāriyi yāvatu peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉpataṉi eṅṅaṉam aṟital cālum. ivi vevvēṟu uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya coṟka ḷātaliṉ ivaṟṟi oruvaḻik koṇarntu oruṅkumuṭittalum poruntāmi kāṇka. ivvāṟu muṭippaṉa maṟṟum palavuḷa. iṅṅaṉam cūttiraṅkaḷik kōvippaṭutti mutalum muṭivum māṟukōḷiṉṟi ivvāciriyar amittirukkum muṟiyi uṇaralāṟṟāta cilar tammi ilakkaṇam vallārākat tāmē matittuk koṇṭu, ‘tolkāppiyattuḷ iccūttiram īṇṭiruttal poruntātu, āṇṭu iruttal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu tamakkut tōṟṟiyavā ṟellām urittal ettuṇip periyatōr aṟiyāmi yākum.
tolkāppiyattiṟku mutaṉūl aintirameṉpār
kūṟṟup pōliyātal :
tirukkurukūrc cuppiramaṇiya tīkkitar tām iyaṟṟiya tamiḻp pirayōka vivēkavuriyil “ēḻiyaṉ muṟiya tetir muka vēṟṟumi - vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉ-ṟōtiya pulavaṉu muṉṉoru vakiyā - ṉintiraṉeṭṭām vēṟṟumi yeṉṟaṉaṉ’ eṉa akattiyattiṉ kaṇṇum, ‘aintira niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ’ eṉat tolkāppiyap pāyirattiṉ kaṇṇum varutaliṉ ivikaṭku mutaṉūl pāṇiṉiyamum aintiramumā
meṉka. akattiya niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṉāmiyāṉum, ‘kaṭiniliyiṉṟē yāciriyarkku’ eṉap potuppaṭa vōtiya tallatu peyareṭuttōtāmiyāṉum atuvē poruḷeṉka. piṉṉūl ceytār tattam pāyirattuḷ vēṇṭiya vāṟōtiṉār; atupaṟṟi urikārarum avarurittāṅkurittār; atu  poruḷaṉmiyaṟika” eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. ivar akattiyattiṟku mutaṉūl pāṇiṉiyam eṉak kūṟiyatu pōliyuri yāmeṉa ‘akattiyar’ eṉṉum urinūlil virittu viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. tolkāppiyattiṟku aintiram mutaṉūl eṉṟatu poruntumā eṉpatu īṇṭu ārāytaṟkuriyatu. ettaṉiyō pala nūlāciriyar uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ koḷkikku muṟṟilum māṟākat tām koṇṭa ittuṇiviṟku ivar kāṭṭiya ātāram tol
kāppiyap pāyirattil “aintira niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ” eṉak kūṟiyiruppatēyākum. tolkāppiyar tamakku muṉṉuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷēyaṉṟi aintiraviyākaraṇamum kaṟṟuvallavareṉa avar perumi kūṟa vēṇṭiyē pāyirattuḷ avvāṟurikkap paṭṭatu. ataṉāl aintiratti mutaṉūlākak koṇṭaṉa reṉpatu  eṭṭuṇiyum peṟappaṭuvatiṉṟu. pāyirattil, ‘tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakattu vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi’ eṉpatum, ‘centami ḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa veṇṇi’ eṉpatum nūl iyaṟṟiṉamiyi vicēṭittu niṟṟalum, ‘aintiram niṟinta’ eṉpatu ākkiyōṉ peyari vicēṭittu niṟṟalum nōkkātu tīkkitar iṅṅaṉam vaḻuvuvārāyiṉar. avar piṟitōriṭattil ‘ yām innūluṭ perumpāṉmiyum taṟcamam taṟpavaṅkaḷāṟ kūṟiṉām. ipperumpāṉmiyuṅ kūṟiyatu yātu paṟṟiyeṉiṉ, vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻ moḻikkum ilakkaṇa moṉṟeṉpa taṟiyātu camñāpētattālum pāṭi vēṟṟumiyālum ikaḻntu vēṟeṉpāri nōkkiyeṉka’ eṉak kūṟiyiruppati nōkkuṅkāl, vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻukkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉak koṇṭa tiripuṇarcciyum avar ciṟitum ōrātu tolkāppiyattiṟku aintiram mutaṉūleṉak kūṟak kāraṇamā yiruntateṉal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, cuppiramaṇiyatīkkitarēyaṉṟic cuvāmināta tēci
karum tamatu ilakkaṇak kottil 
“vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi yeṉumiru moḻiyiṉum
  ilakkaṇa moṉṟē yeṉṟē yeṇṇuka”
eṉṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar. tamiḻiṉum vaṭamoḻiyiṉum nirampiya pulamiyuṭiyarākiya ivviruvarum iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyatu mikunta viyappiṟkuriyatu. ivarkaḷ tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻikkumuḷḷa vēṟṟumiyi aṟiyātārallar eṉpatu, cuppiramaṇiya tīkkitar tamatu pirayōka vivēkattu 46-ām ceyyuḷuriyil ;‘itu vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻmoḻikkum pētam kōṭi kūṟiṭṭu oru kūṟuṇṭō iṉṟōveṉpatu kūṟukiṉṟatu. tiṇi
uṇarttum viṉivikutiyum, āṇpāl peṇpāluṇarttum viṉivikutiyum vaṭamoḻikkilli. tamiḻmoḻikkup piratamā
vipattiyum, iliṅkattirayamumilli’ eṉa urittalāṉum, iṅṅaṉamē cuvāmināta tēcikarum ‘irutiṇiyum āṇpāl peṇpāl viṉiyīṟum vaṭamoḻikkilli. mūṉṟiliṅkamum, mutalīṟṟu vēṟṟumikaṭ kurupukaḷum tamiḻiṟkilli. mēlum āṇṭāṇṭuk kāṭṭutum’ eṉa ōriṭatturittu, maṟṟōriṭattu,
‘cilapeyareccamuñ cila viṉittokiyum
  iruvakiyuvamiyum vaṇṇac ciṉiccolum
  paṇput tokiyeṉap pakarum vaṭanūl’
eṉac cūttirañ ceytu, ‘pakarum vaṭa nūl eṉavē tamiḻiṟ kaṭāteṉka’ eṉavum urittalāṉum iṉitu viḷaṅkum. ivvāṟu ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷi naṉku uṇarntu vittē ivarkaḷ irumoḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉak kūṟiyatu vaṭamoḻiyiṉ mēluḷḷa paṟṟu mikutiyāṉō, vēṟe
taṉāṉō eṉpatu terintilatu. iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyilum piṟa moḻikaḷilum ilakkaṇam eṉpatu eḻuttuc collamitiyē yākum. vaṭanūlār ilakkaṇattic cattanūl eṉa vaḻaṅkutaluṅ kāṇka. tamiḻilakkaṇamō eḻuttuñ collumākiya oliyaḷavil aṭaṅkuvataṉṟu; makkaḷ uṟutip poruḷeytu
taṟkuriya oḻukalāṟṟiṉiyellām variyaṟi ceytuṇarttum poruṭpakutiyi uṭiyatu. poruḷilakkaṇam vēṟem moḻiyilumiṉṟit tamiḻil māttiramē yuḷḷateṉpatu aṉivarālum naṉkaṟiyappaṭṭatu. tamiḻāṉatu piṟa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷik kāṭṭiṉum poruḷilakkaṇattiyē ciṟantatākavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākavum koṇṭuḷḷateṉpatu 
‘eḻuttum collum yāppum ārāyvatu poru
 ḷatikārattiṉ poruṭṭaṉṟē, poruḷatikāram 
 peṟēmēyeṉiṉ ivi peṟṟum peṟṟilēm’ 
eṉṉum kaḷaviyaluriyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. ivvāṟākavum, irumoḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉpatum, tolkāppiyat
tiṟku mutaṉūl aintiram eṉpatum poruntumāṟu eṅṅaṉam? poruḷilakkaṇatti viṭuttuc collilakkaṇam oṉṟē koṇṭu aṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉārāvareṉiṉ, ataṉkaṇṇum irumoḻikkum kōṭipētamuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu avarē kūṟiṉami mēlē kāṭṭappeṟṟatu. tolkāppiyar aintiratti mutaṉūlākak koṇṭaṉa reṉṉum pirayōkavivēka nūlār koḷkiyi irumoḻikkaṭalum nilikaṇṭuṇarnta civañāṉamuṉivar tamatu pāyiraviruttiyuḷ maṟuttirukkum pakuti īṇṭu aṟintu makiḻaṟpālatu. atu, ‘aintiram nōkkit tokut
tāṉeṉiṉ, tamiḻ moḻip puṇarccikkaṭpaṭum ceykikaḷum, ‘kuṟiyīṭukaḷum, viṉikkuṟippu viṉittoki mutaliya cila collilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi mutaliya coṟpākupāṭukaḷum, akampuṟam eṉṉum poruṭpāku
pāṭukaḷum, kuṟiñci veṭci mutaliya tiṇippākupāṭukaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷum, veṇpā mutaliya ceyyuḷilakkaṇamum, iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭāmiyāṉum, iviyellām tāmē paṭittuk koṇṭu ceytāreṉiṉ muntu nūl kaṇṭu eṉpataṉōṭu muraṇutalāṉum muṟkālattu mutaṉūl akattiyam eṉpatūum, ataṉ vaḻittākiya tol
kāppiyam ataṉ vaḻinūleṉpatūum tuṇiyappaṭumeṉka’ eṉpatu. itiṟkuṟippiṭap peṟṟaṉavum piṟavumākiya tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷ piṉṉarum viḷakkappaṭum. ivvāṟṟāl tol
kāppiyattiṟku mutaṉūl aintirameṉpār kūṟṟuc ciṟitum poruttamillāta pōliyuriyāyoḻital kāṇka.
tolkāppiyattiṉ pākupāṭu
tolkāppiyam eṉṉum nūl cūttiram, iyal, atikāram (paṭalam) eṉṉum mūṉṟu uṟuppukkaḷi aṭakkiyatōr piṇṭamākum.  innūṟpaṭalaṅkaḷ eḻuttatikāram, collati
kāram, poruḷatikāram eṉa mūṉṟām. ivvatikāraṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟum oṉpatu iyalkaḷi uṭiyaṉa. eḻuttati
kārattiṉ iyalkaḷ nūṉ marapu, moḻi marapu, piṟappiyal, puṇariyal, toki marapu, urupiyal, uyir mayaṅkiyal, puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal, kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal eṉpaṉa. nūṉmarapāṉatu nūṟku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavākiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarum, muṟiyum, tokiyum, pākupāṭum, aḷavum, uruvum, mey
yeḻuttukkaḷ piṟa meyyōṭum tammōṭum mayaṅkumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
moḻimarapāṉatu kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa cārpeḻuttukkaḷ oru moḻik kaṇṇum puṇar
moḻiyakattum varumāṟum, ōreḻuttoru moḻi mutaliya moḻip pākupāṭum, makarak kuṟukkamum, aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum, moḻikku mutalām eḻuttukkaḷum, īṟām eḻuttukkaḷum piṟavum uṇarttukiṟatu. piṟappiyalāṉatu uṭampiṉakattu eḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkum nilikkaḷaṅkaḷum, uṟuppukkaḷum, avaṟṟāṉ avi vēṟu vēṟu piṟakkumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. puṇariyalāṉatu meyyum, uyirum iṟutiyum mutalumākavuḷḷa coṟkaḷ niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyumāy alvaḻi vēṟṟumip poruḷil iyalpum tiripumeytip potu vakiyāṟ puṇarumāṟum, peyaruṭaṉ ai mutaliya vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷum, iṉ mutaliya cāriyikaḷum puṇarumāṟum, eḻuttukkaḷuṭaṉ kāram mutaliya cāriyikaḷ puṇarumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. toki
marapāṉatu mēl uyir mayaṅkiyal mutaliyavaṟṟil īṟukaṭōṟum virintu muṭiyum puṇarcci vitikaḷit tokuttu muṭikkiṉṟatu. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumiyum mēl vēṟṟumikkuk kūṟum muṭivu peṟātu tiripeyti niṟkumāṟṟiyum iḵtu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
urupiyalāṉatu peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷuṭaṉ puṇarumpoḻutu cāriyip pēṟeytumāṟṟi virittuṇarttu kiṟatu. uyirmayaṅkiyalāṉatu uyirīṟṟu moḻikaḷ niṉṟu perum
pāṉmi vaṉkaṇattōṭum, ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇaṅ
kaḷōṭum alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum puṇarumāṟṟi virittuṇarttukiṉṟatu. puḷḷimayaṅkiyalāṉatu meyyīṟṟu moḻikaḷ niṉṟu perumpāṉmi vaṉkaṇattōṭum, ciṟupāṉmi ēṉikkaṇaṅkaḷōṭum puṇarumāṟṟi virit
tuṇarttukiṉṟatu. kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalāṉatu kuṟṟiya
lukarattiṉ vakiyum, avi niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇaru
māṟum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. oṉṟu mutalākiya kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇup peyarkaḷ niṉṟu eṇṇup peyarōṭum piṟa peyar
kaḷōṭum puṇarum muṟimiyi itu virittuṇarttu
kiṉṟatu. eḻuttatikārattil niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu
kiḷaviyumāka vittup puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ iviyeṉ
pataṉiyum iḵtuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
collatikārattiṉ iyalkaḷ kiḷaviyākkam, vēṟṟumi
yiyal, vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal, viḷimarapu, peyariyal, viṉi
yiyal, iṭiyiyal, uriyiyal, eccaviyal eṉpaṉa. kiḷaviyākka
māṉatu collum poruḷum irutiṇi aimpālākap pākupaṭumāṟum, cila tiṇi pāl iṭam marapu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaḻānili, vaḻuvamitikaḷum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. vēṟṟumi
yiyalāṉatu eṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum mutaliyaṉa kūṟi, viḷiyoḻinta ēṉi vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ poruḷ vikaṟpaṅkaḷi virittuṇarttukiṉṟatu. vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiya lāṉatu vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ tamporuḷiṟ ṟīrātu piṟavaṟṟiṉ poruḷiṟ ceṉṟu mayaṅkum poruṇ mayakkamum, tamporuḷiṟ ṟīrntu piṟavaṟṟiṉ poruḷiṟ ceṉṟu mayaṅkum urupu mayakkamum, viṉi nikaḻcciyiṉ eṭṭuk kāraṇaṅkaḷum, āku peyariṉ vakikaḷum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. viḷimarapāṉatu viḷiyiṉ potuvilakkaṇamum, uyartiṇip peyar, aḵṟiṇip peyar, viravup peyarkaḷil ivvīṟu ivvāṟu viḷiyēṟkumeṉpatum, viḷiyēlāp peyarkaḷ iviyeṉpatum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. peyariyalāṉatu nālvakic coṟkumuriya cila potuvilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, peyarccolliṉ pākupāṭum, ilakkaṇamum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. viṉiyiyalāṉatu viṉic colliṉ pākupāṭum, ilakkaṇamum, kālavaḻānili vaḻuvamitikaḷum, oru cār marapu vaḻuvamitiyum uṇarttu kiṉṟatu. iṭiyiya lāṉatu iṭiccolliṉ pākupāṭum, ‘maṇ’ mutaliya iṭiccoṟkaḷ poruḷ kuṟikkumāṟum, iraṭṭittu niṉṟu aci niliyāvaṉavum poruḷ kuṟippaṉavum iviyeṉpatum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. uriyiyalāṉatu iciyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟi oru col pala poruṭku urimiyātalum pala col oru poruṭku urimiyātalum uṭiya uric coṟkaḷ palavum, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷum, coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ uṇarumāṟum, ataṉi yuṇarttu muṟimiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. eccaviyalāṉatu muṟkūṟiya eṭṭiyaluḷḷum uṇarttutaṟku iṭamiṉṟi eñci niṉṟa collilakkaṇaṅkaḷit tokuttuṇarttukiṉṟatu. ceyyuṭkuriya iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭa col eṉpavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, valikkum vaḻivalittal mutaliya ceyyuḷ vikāraṅkaḷum, niraṉiṟi, cuṇṇam, aṭimaṟi, moḻimāṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷ kōḷkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, vēṟṟumit toki mutaliya aṟuvakit tokikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, pirinili mutaliya pattu vaki eccaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ muṭivukaḷum, cila viṉic coṟkaḷ aciniliyā māṟum, cila vaḻānili vaḻuvamitikaḷum, piṟavum iḵtu uṇarttukiṉṟateṉka.
poruḷatikārattiṉ iyalkaḷ akattiṇiyiyal, puṟat
tiṇiyiyal, kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal, poruḷiyal, meyppāṭṭiyal, uvamaviyal, ceyyuḷiyal, marapiyal eṉpaṉa. akattiṇi yiyalāṉatu akattiṇi ēḻaṉ peyarum, akaṉintiṇiyiṉ mutal karu uripporuḷkaḷum, akapporuṭṭali makkaḷāvār ivareṉpatum, ōtaṟpirivu mutaliya pirivukaḷiṉ variyaṟikaḷum, naṟṟāy, tōḻi, kaṇṭōr, talivaṉ eṉpavarkkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭaṅkaḷum, uḷḷuṟiyuvamamum, kikkiḷi perun tiṇikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. puṟattiṇiyiyalāṉatu  puṟattiṇi ēḻaṉ peyarum ilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṉ pakutiyākiya tuṟikaḷum uṇarttu kiṉṟatu. pāṭāṇṭiṇiyil orupakutiyākak kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu vakikaḷi uṇarttukiṉṟatu. kaḷaviyalāṉatu kaḷavu iṉṉateṉpatum, talimakkaḷ etirppāṭṭiṉ kāraṇamum, kāṭci mutaliyaṉa nikaḻumāṟum, iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum kaḷavuk kūṭṭam nāṉkum, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭa vakikaḷum, eṇvaki maṇamum akattiṇiyēḻaṉuḷ iṉṉavāṟu aṭaṅku
meṉpatum, kaḷaveṉṉum kikōḷil talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi, cevili eṉpōrkkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭaṅkaḷum, kaḷavu veḷippaṭumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. kaṟpiyalāṉatu kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyi maṇattalākiya kaṟpiṉ ilakkaṇamum, muṉivar karaṇam yāttavāṟum, kaṟpeṉṉum kikōḷil talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi, cevili, aṟivar, kūttar, pārppār mutaliyōrkkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭaṅkaḷum, ōtaṟ pirivu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kālavariyaṟiyum, talivaṉum taliviyum tuṟavaṟam nikaḻtti vīṭupeṟumāṟum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. poruḷiyalāṉatu akapporuṭkuriya vaḻuvamitikaḷ palavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. meyppāṭṭiyalāṉatu akattiṟkum puṟat
tiṟkum potuvākiya eṇvaki meyppāṭukaḷum, avi ovvoṉṟu nāṉkāmāṟum, avi pōl vēṟu muppattiraṇṭu meyppāṭukaḷum, akapporuṭkēyuriya meyppāṭṭiṉ vaki
kaḷum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. uvamaviyalāṉatu uvamattiṉ ilakkaṇamum, vakiyum, nilikkaḷaṉum, cila uvama vikaṟpaṅkaḷum, uvamavurupukaḷum, talivi mutalāyiṉār iṉṉiṉṉaviṭattu uḷḷuṟiyuvamam kūṟaṟkuriyāreṉpatum, piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. ceyyuḷiyalāṉatu nallicip pulavar ceyyuṭkaḷil taṉinilic ceyyuṭku uṟuppāka varum māttiri mutalāya irupattāṟum, perumpāṉmiyum toṭar nilic ceyyuṭku uṟuppāka varum ammi mutaliya eṭṭu vaṉappum, kūṟu mukattāṟ ceyyuḷilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu. aṭivariyillāta aṟuvakic ceyyuḷum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. marapiyalāṉatu iḷamip peyar, āṇpāṟ peyar, peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷil iṉṉiṉṉavi iṉṉiṉṉavaṟṟukku uriyaveṉpatum, ōraṟivuyir mutaliya uyir vakikaḷum, pul, maram eṉpavaṟṟuḷ varum marapup peyarkaḷum, antaṇar mutaliyōrkkuriya cila muṟimikaḷum, iruvaki nūl, kāṇṭiki, uri eṉpavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, nūṟkuṟṟam pattum, utti muppattiraṇṭum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyataṟku nīṇṭa nāḷiṉ muṉpē tamiḻ moḻiyum, tamiḻarum uyariya niliyil iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpataṟku atiṟ kāṇappaṭum ātaravukaḷ.
tolkāppiyattilē palaviṭattum ‘eṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉṟu muṉṉi yāciriyarkaḷiṉ tuṇivu kūṟappaṭutali nōkkiṉ ataṟku muṉpē tamiḻilakkaṇa nūl pala iruntiruttal tēṟṟam. ‘nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē’ ‘nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē’ eṉa varupavi pōlvaṉavaṟṟāl muṉṉi nūlāciriyarkaḷiṉ perumiyum pulaṉākum. ‘tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu’ eṉavum, ‘tōleṉa moḻipa toṉmoḻip pulavar’ eṉavum varuvaṉa tolkāppiyam tōṉṟutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē tamiḻ moḻiyum tamiḻāciriyarum ciṟantiruntami kāṭṭum. iṅkē kuṟikkap peṟṟōr ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷeṉpataṉiyum, ōr moḻiyil viḻumiya ilakkaṇan tōṉṟa vēṇṭumāyiṉ ataṟku muṉpē vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ciṟantiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum uṇmiyiṉiyum koṇṭu nōkkiṉ. tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu toṭakkam kaṇṭuṇaralākāta attuṇip paḻamiyuṭiyateṉpatu peṟappaṭum. tolkāppiyattiṟ payiṉṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, atu kūṟum ceyyuṭkaḷ, poruḷkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟi uyttuṇariṉum avi attakiya tiruntiya nili yeytutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum uṇmi pulaṉākum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat toṇṇūṟu, toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarkaḷik kāṇka. oṉpatu pattu toṇṇūṟu eṉavum, oṉpatu nūṟu toḷḷāyiram eṉavum āyiṉamikku āciriyar kūṟiyirukkum tiripu viti moḻi nūliyaṟkiyōṭu poruntu vataṉṟāyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟaravu uṇaralākāvāṟu avarkku neṭu nāḷiṉ muṉpē accoṟkaḷ annilimi yeyti viṭṭaṉa eṉpatu pulaṉāmaṉṟō? tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ paḻiya uyariya nili ivvārāyccikaḷiṉ aṟutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭumākaliṉ, īṇṭu iraṇṭu kuṟippukkaḷ māttiram eṭuttukkāṭṭuval. ‘pōnti vēmpē ār eṉa varūum, māperun tāṉiyar malinta pūvum’ ‘vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ’ eṉṉun toṭarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarkaḷiyum, avarkaḷuṭiya māli, tāṉi, nilam eṉpavaṟṟiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. mūvēntar ‘vaṇpukaḻ mūvar’ eṉavum, tamiḻnilam ‘taṇpoḻil varippu’ eṉavum pukaḻnturikkappaṭṭiruttal cintikkaṟpālatu. vaṇpukaḻuṭiya reṉavē ataṟkuk kāraṇamāya koṭi aḷi ceṅkōl kuṭiyōmpal mutaliya iṟimāṭciyaṉittum uṭiya reṉpatum peṟappaṭum. oru nāṭṭiṉ araciyal tiruntiya niliyil iruppatu annāṭṭiṉatum, annāṭṭu makkaḷatum uyarvik kāṭṭum kuṟiyākum. ataṉāṟṟāṉ,
“āṅkami veytiyak kaṇṇum payamiṉṟē
  vēntami villāta nāṭu”
eṉat teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum kūṟiṉar. cēkkiḻār, periyapurāṇattilē cōṇāṭṭiṉ ciṟappukaḷik kūṟivantu, muṭivil,
“naṟṟamiḻ varippi ṉīṅku nāmpukaḻ tirunā ṭeṉṟum
  poṟṟaṭan tōḷāl viyam potukkaṭin tiṉitu kākkum
  koṟṟava ṉanapāyaṉ poṟ kuṭiniḻaṟ kuḷirvarutēṟā
  maṟṟataṉ perumi nammāl varampuṟa viḷampa lāmō”
eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkum ceyyuḷiṉ karuttum īṇṭu aṟintu makiḻtaṟkuriyatu. vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa vētam mutalāya kali
kaḷiyum, tamiḻ nūlkaḷaṉittiyum naṉkuṇarnta parimēlaḻakar tirukkuṟaḷuriyilē ‘cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉṟāṟpōlap paṭippuk kālantoṭaṅki mēmpaṭṭu varuṅkuṭi’ eṉac ciṟappittōtiya mūvēntar pukaḻ kūṟappaṭavē tolkāp
piyarkku muṉpu toṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uyarvum tāṉē peṟappaṭum eṉka. iṉi, oru nāṭṭiṉ uyarvellām annāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ kuṇavoḻukkac ciṟappēyākum. makkaḷuḷḷē makkaḷiṉ paṇpu aṟiyappaṭiṉ atu koṇṭē ēṉiyar paṇpum aṟiyappaṭṭatākum eṉpa. paṇṭu tamiḻ nāṭṭu makaḷir paṇpu eṅṅaṉam iruntateṉpatu
“uyiriṉum ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉum
  ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpuc ciṟan taṉṟeṉat
  tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu”
eṉat tolkāppiyarum kūṟuvataṉāṟ pulappaṭum. piṟa paṇpukaḷum piṉṉark kāṭṭappeṟum. iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiliruntē tolkāppiyan tōṉṟutaṟku neṭunāḷiṉ muṉṉarē tamiḻum tamiḻarum uyarnili yeytiyiruntami aṅki nelliyeṉa naṉku viḷaṅkum.
eḻuttārāycciyum collārāycciyum
tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tokiyum muṟiyum uruvamum:
“eḻutteṉappaṭupa
  akaramutala
  ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵ teṉpa
  cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē”
eṉṉum tolkāppiya mutaṟ cūttirattāl tamiḻeḻuttuk
kaḷiṉ toki muppattu mūṉṟu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. akara mutal oḷakāram īṟākavuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭum uyireḻut
tukkaḷ. kakara mutal ṉakaram īṟākavuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭum mey
yeḻuttukkaḷ. immuppateḻuttukkaḷiyum mutaleḻut
teṉpa. kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṉum mūṉṟum cārpeḻuttukkaḷ. immūṉṟum uyirpōlat taṉittum meypōla akarattōṭu kūṭiyum iyaṅkutaliṉṟi oru moḻiyic cārntē varum iyalpiṉavākaliṉ cārpeḻut
teṉappaṭṭaṉa. itu paṟṟiyē ivi tōṉṟu māṟṟi āciriyar moḻi marapiṉkaṇ kūṟiṉāreṉka. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpaṉa ikara ukaraṅkaḷiṉ kuṟukkamākaliṉ ivaṟṟi vēṟu kūṟa vēṇṭiya teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ivi muṉṉar oru māttiri yāy niṉṟu piṉṉar oru kāraṇampaṟṟi ari māttiriyākātu eṉṟum oru māttiriyāyē niṟkum oru taṉmiyiṉavākaliṉ ivaṟṟi vēṟu eḻuttukkaḷāka āciriyar vēṇṭiṉāreṉka. ivaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟṟikaram, kuṟṟukaram eṉṟu peyar kūṟiyoḻiyātu, ‘iyal’ eṉpatu cērttu ‘kuṟṟiya likaram’, ‘kuṟṟiyalukaram’ eṉak kuṟippiṭṭiruttalum karutaṟpālatu. uyirmey, uyiraḷapeṭi, oṟṟaḷapeṭi, aikārak kuṟukkam, oḷakārak kuṟukkam, makarak kuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam eṉpavaṟṟi viṭuttuc cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟeṉṟatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ kūṟuval. ‘āl’ eṉpuḻi uyirmuṉṉum meypiṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉāṟpōl ‘lā’ eṉpuḻiyum meymuṉṉum, uyir piṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉavē yallatu’ uyirum meyyumākiya tam taṉmi tirintu vēṟāyiṉa valla: “meyyōṭiyiyiṉum uyiriyal tiriyā” eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷum ataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. tiṉi eṉpatu mey mutal uyirīṟṟuc col eṉavum, varaku eṉpatu uyirttoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉavum koḷvataṉṟi, uyir mey mutal uyirmey īṟṟuc col eṉavum, uyir meyt toṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukara meṉavum koḷḷāmiyuṅ kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl uyir mey ōreḻuttākāmi peṟappaṭum. aḷapeṭiyiraṇṭum oru kāraṇam paṟṟi nīṇṭolippaṉavē ākaliṉ avi vēṟeḻuttākā. aikārak kuṟukkam mutaliyaṉavum iyaṟkiyākak kuṟukiyicippaṉavalla vākaliṉ ōreḻuttākā. aṅṅaṉamākaliṉ, piṉṉuḷōr koṇṭa āytak kuṟukkameṉpatoḻittu ēṉiya vellāvaṟṟiyum āciriyar eṭuttōtiṉarāyiṉum avaṟṟi vēṟeḻuttākak koṇṭu cārpeḻutteṉa vēṇṭiṉārallar. vaṭanūlārum uyirmey, uyiraḷapeṭi, aikārak kuṟukkam mutaliyavaṟṟi vēṟeḻutteṉak koḷḷāmi
yuṇarka. piṉvanta naṉṉūlār mutalāyiṉār eḻuttiṉiyalpi nōkkātu vēṟek kāraṇattāṉō avaṟṟiyum cārpeḻut
teṉpārāyiṉar. iṉi oru cārār teṅku, iñci, vaṇṭu, pantu, cempiyaṉ eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōla melleḻutti aṭuttuḷḷa valleḻuttukkaḷiṉ ōcikkum, cekutta, pacitta eṉpavaṟṟil iyalpāka meṉmiyāy olikkum valleḻuttōci mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum vēṟu taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷ illāmiyāl tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkāṉatu eḻuttuk kuṟipāṭuṭiyatu eṉpar. kakaram mutaliya valleḻuttukkaḷ taṉittum, piṟaveḻuttuk
kaḷōṭu kūṭi olikkum oliyum vevvēṟu iyalpiṉavāyiṉ piṉṉatu iyaṟkiyoliyaṉṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. mel
leḻuttōṭu kūṭātē vēṟupaṭa olippatuṇṭāyiṉ atu ataṉip pēcum makkaḷiṉ cōrvu mutaliyavaṟṟāl uṇṭā
vatēyām. avvoli evviṭattum oru peṟṟiyē nikaḻ
vatākak koḷḷutal amiyātu. iṅṅaṉam piṟaveḻuttuk
kaḷiṉ cērkkiyāleḻum ceyaṟkiyolikkum, cōrvu mutaliyavaṟṟāleḻum pōliyolikkum taṉittaṉi eḻuttukkaḷ amippatāyiṉ nāṭkunāḷ eḻuttukkaḷ varampiṉṟip palki marapunili tirintu muṉ muṉṉuḷḷa vaḻakkum ceyyuḷumellām poruḷaṟiya vārāvāyk kaḻiyum eṉpataṉi aṟital vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum teṅku, iñci mutaliyavaṟṟiluḷḷa olikaḷ vēṟeḻuttukkaḷ vēṇṭāmalē yātoru iṭarppāṭumiṉṟi iṉitiṉ iyaṉṟu varavum avaṟṟukku vēṟu eḻuttukkaḷ vakukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu poruttamākumō? tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku vakutta mutalāciriyar tamatu pēraṟiviṉ tiṟattāl tamiḻiluḷḷa olikaḷiṉ peṟṟiyi yellām nuṉittuṇarntu eḻuttukkaḷ vakutta aṭivi ōrntuṇarātu piṟar tamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟellām eḻuttukkaḷi mikuttalum kuṟittalum ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvatu periyatōrētamākumeṉka. tamiḻ meyyeḻuttukkaḷ vali, meli, iṭi yeṉa mūṉṟu iṉamāka, avi ovvoṉṟum orēyaḷavāka avvāṟu eḻuttukkaḷ peṟṟiruppatum, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiyalum, piṟavum nōkkiṉ avi paranta ārāycciyum ciṟanta kāraṇamum koṇṭu aṅṅaṉam vakukkap peṟṟaṉavā meṉpatu pōtarum.
iṉi, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ kiṭakki muṟi ippoḻutu pōṉṟē tolkāppiyar kālattum, ataṟku muṉṉum iruntatu. aṅṅaṉam iruntateṉpatu tolkāppiyattu nūṉmarapilum piṟappiyalilumuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi aṇukavittu nōkkiṉ iṉitu pulappaṭum. accūttiraṅkaḷ ‘eṉpa’ ‘eṉamoḻipa’ eṉa āciriyar tuṇivāka urittaliṉ tolkāppiyarkku muṉṉum aṅṅaṉamiruntami tēṟṟam. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku ammuṟiyāl eppoḻutu yārāl vakukkap peṟṟateṉa aṟutiyiṭutal aritu. eṉiṉum, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟi vippum vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟivippum irumoḻiyiṉum ciṟappeḻuttoḻiya nōkkiṉ yātum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi amintiruttaliṉ, tamiḻ vaṭa moḻi iraṇṭum naṉkuṇarnta āciriyaroruvarāl oru moḻiyi nōkki maṟṟoru moḻikkaṇṇum ammuṟi yāṉē eḻuttukkaḷ amikkap peṟṟaṉavāmeṉṟu karututal poruntum. eḻuttukkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟu vaḻaṅkutaṟkaṇṇum ‘pakāram makāram’ eṉṟāṟpōla irumoḻiyilum oṟṟumiyuḷatu. vaṭamoḻiyilē ‘talakāram’ eṉṟum, tamiḻilē ‘laḷaḵkāṉ’ eṉṟum iraṇṭeḻuttukkuc cāriyi yoṉṟē varutalum ciṟupāṉmiyuṇṭu.
vaṭamoḻiyi nōkkiyē tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ muṟip
paṭuttap peṟṟaṉavāyiṉ āciriyar akattiyaṉārāl tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku aṅṅaṉam vakukkap peṟṟatāmeṉak karututalum iḻukkaṉṟu. āṉāl vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttukkaḷ tolkāp
piyarkkum akattiyaṉārkkum muṉṉarum ammuṟiyāl vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉaveṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭiṉaṉṟi vaṭamoḻi nōkkit tamiḻeḻuttu muṟi koḷḷap peṟṟateṉal aṭātu. vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷi uṇarttum mākēccura cūttiram eṉpavaṟṟil uyireḻuttukkaḷiṉ kiṭakki muṟi māttiram ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkumāṟē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. mutalum īṟuṅ kūṭṭip pirattiyākarittu ‘ac’ eṉavum, ‘hal’ eṉavum vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷ peyariṭappaṭṭiruppatu pōlaṉṟi, tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ avaṟṟiṉiyalpāl uyir, mey eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉ ottiruttal kaṇṭu uyir eṉavum, mey eṉavum peyariṭap
paṭṭiruttali nōkkiṉ piṟarik kāṭṭiṉum viḻumiya tattuva uṇarvu cāṉṟōrāl tamiḻneṭuṅkaṇakku vakukkappeṟṟa
tāmeṉal pōtarum ākaliṉ tamiḻi nōkkiyē vaṭaveḻuttu muṟi tōṉṟiṟṟāmeṉak karututalum ēṟpuṭittē. ivaṟṟuḷ yātu uṟutiyuṭitteṉpatu iru moḻiyiṉum vallunarāl ārāyntu veḷiyiṭaṟ pālatu. iṉi, tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kiṭakki muṟikkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ tolkāppiya mutaṟ cūttira viruttiyil civañāṉa muṉivarāl naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
iṉi, ivveḻuttukkaḷ oli vaṭivum vari vaṭivum eṉa iruvaki vaṭivuṭiyaṉa. cilar eḻuttukkaḷi uṇarveḻuttu, oliyeḻuttu, taṉmiyeḻuttu, uruveḻuttu eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum pākupaṭutturippar. ivaṟṟuḷ uṇarveḻut
tāvatu civākamaṅkaḷil uṇarttappaṭum cūkkumi, picanti, mattimi, vikari eṉṉum nālvaki vākkuḷ mattimit tāṉattu uṇarcciyaḷavāka irukkum eḻutteṉṟum, oli
yeḻuttāvatu taṉ cevikkē kēṭkum vikariyeṉṟum. taṉmi eḻuttāvatu piṟar cevikkuk kēṭkum oliyuṭiya vikari
yeṉṟum, uruveḻuttāvatu cevippulaṉām oliyeḻutti aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāka ōviyam pōl eḻutappaṭuvatāmeṉṟum koḷka. ivaṟṟuḷ uṇarvu, oli, taṉmi eṉṉum mūṉṟum oliyeṉṟē koḷḷaṟpālaṉa. eṉavē eḻuttukkaḷ oliyeḻuttu eṉavum, uruveḻuttu eṉavum iru vakiyākum. oliyeḻuttāvatu uyiriṉ muyaṟciyāl untiyiluḷḷa utāṉaṉ eṉṉuṅ kāṟṟu neñcu, miṭaṟu, tali eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu nilipeṟṟu avaṟṟuṭaṉ pal, itaḻ, nā, mūkku, aṇṇam eṉṉum uṟuppukkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭutalāl vēṟu vēṟu oliyākat tōṉṟuvatām. tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ yāvum iṅṅaṉam piṟakkumāṟu tolkāppiyattilē piṟappiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvolivaṭivētāṉ nūlkaḷiṟ perumpālum eḻutteṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatākum. uruveḻut tāvatu kīṟṟum puḷḷiyuṅ koṇṭu kaṭpulaṉākat tōṉṟumāṟu eḻutappaṭuvatām. oliyeḻuttiṉṟi itaṟku nilipēṟiṉmi yāṉum, iḵtiṉṟiyē oliyeḻuttiṟku iyakkamuṇṭākalāṉum, ōreḻuttolikkē vevvēṟu moḻiyāḷar vevvēṟu vaṭivamittuk koḷvarākalāṉum iḵtu attuṇic ciṟappuṭittaṉṟu. ataṉāṟṟāṉ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkum vaṭivu kūṟātu cilavaṟṟukkuc ciṟupāṉmi vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. nūṉ marapilē meykaḷum, ekara okaraṅkaḷum puḷḷipeṟum eṉṟum, makarattiṟku uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvākum eṉṟum, āytatti muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppatum, meyyum uyirum kūṭiyaviṭattu avi tiriyātum tirintu niṟkumeṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyiruppatu maṉṟi, vēṟu vaṭivu kūṟappaṭāmi kāṇka. cilar kuṟṟiyalikara, kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaṭkum puḷḷiyuṇṭeṉpar. tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ cila avvāṟu karututaṟkum iṭañceytu niṟṟalē avar aṅṅaṉam kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamākum. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟiṟkup puḷḷi uṇṭeṉṟu tuṇinturikkat tolkāppiyattilē pōtiya cāṉṟilli. āciriyar iṅṅaṉam ciṟupāṉmiyē vaṭivu kūṟiṉārāyiṉum, kūṟiyavaṟṟiliruntē akkālattil tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ vari vaṭivil eḻutap peṟṟu vantaṉa eṉṉum uṇmi peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. oliyeḻutti nōkka vaṭiveḻuttuc ciṟappilateṉṟu muṉ kūṟappaṭṭatāyiṉum vaṭiveḻuttiṉ payaṉ ciṟiteṉṟu karutalākātu. moḻi vaḷarccikkum, ammoḻiyip pēcum makkaḷiṉ nākarika vaḷarccikkum vaṭiveḻuttuc ciṟanta karuviyākum. tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ oli vaṭivum, vari vaṭivum ‘eḻuttu’ eṉṉum oru peyarālēyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iccol ‘eḻu’ eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟantiruppiṉ olivaṭivik kuṟippatu poruntum. aṉṟēl eḻuttu variyaṟiyum avi eḻutappaṭutalum oruṅku tōṟṟiya kāraṇattāl vari vaṭivik kuṟikkum appeyarē oli vaṭiviṟkum āyiṟṟeṉṟu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyattilē ‘antaratteḻutiya eḻuttiṉmāṉa’ eṉṉun toṭaril ‘eḻutiya’ eṉṉum viṉi koṭukkap paṭṭiruttaliṉ eḻutu eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiliruntu eḻuttu eṉṉum peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpatē peritum poruttamām.
iṉi, tamiḻ vaṭiveḻuttukkaḷiṉ varalāṟākap piṟar kūṟiyiruppaṉa cila ārāytaṟkuriyaṉa. moḻi nūl ārāycci
yāḷarkaḷ paṇṭu kalveṭṭukkaḷilum ceppup paṭṭayaṅ
kaḷilum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷukku vaṭṭeḻuttu eṉṟu peyar koṭuttu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. airōppiyap pulavarkaḷuḷḷē ‘ṭākṭar kālṭuval’, ‘ṭākṭar pūlar’ mutaliya cilar tamiḻ vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ intiya nāṭu muḻutum vaḻaṅkiya pirāmi eṉṉum acōka eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu vantaṉavām eṉpar. tamiḻ ekara okaraṅkaṭkum ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭkum vaṭivu vēṟṟumiyillāmi avi paḻiya vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu vantaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum eṉṟu ‘kālṭuval’ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. misṭar i. tāmas ‘ṭākṭar ris ṭēviṭs’, ‘ṭākṭar parṉal’ eṉṉum airōppiya āciriyarkaḷ intiyāvil vaḻaṅkum eḻuttukkaḷ evvitattilum āriyaruṭiyaṉavallaveṉṟum, ki.mu. 7 allatu 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ varttakarkaḷāl intiyāvil vaḻaṅkac ceytaṉavākumeṉṟum kiṭittuḷḷa ellā ātaravukaḷum uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉkiṉṟaṉar. intu āriya ilakkaṇak kārarkaḷiṉ varukikku muṉpē tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku tiruntiya niliyil iruntateṉṟum, tamiḻ vaṭṭeḻuttu acōka eḻuttiṉiṉṟum vantataṉṟeṉṟum avarkaḷ uṟutiyāka moḻikiṉṟaṉar. ikkoḷkiyē tiru. cuvāmi vētācalam avarkaḷāl tamatu nūṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟil valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. tiru.em. cīṉivāciyaṅkāravarkaḷ ‘tamiḻārāyccikaḷ’ eṉṉum tam nūliṉ kaṇṇē vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻarkkuriyaṉavē yaṉṟi vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya acōka eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tiripallaveṉṟum, kālṭuvel kūṟukiṟapaṭiyaṉṟi ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷōṭu vēṟṟumi teriya ekara okaraṅkaḷukkup puḷḷiyuṇṭeṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟutalālum, pa,ma, eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷ pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷilum vaṭṭeḻuttuk kaḷilum vēṟupaṭṭiruttalālum kālṭuvel mutalāyiṉōr koḷki poruntātu eṉṟum, ki.mu. 15 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē tamiḻarkaḷ piṉīṣiyar, ekiptiyarkaḷuṭaṉ vāṇikattāl kalantiruttalāl ekiptiyaruṭiya eḻuttu muṟiyit tamiḻar annāḷilēyē koṇarntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, intiya āriyar ivveḻuttu muṟiyit tamiḻariṭamiruntu peṟṟiruttal kūṭumeṉṟum kūṟi, tāmas,ṭēviṭs, parṉal eṉpavarkaḷiṉ koḷkiyē valiyuṭittāmeṉa vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. tiru.pā.vē. māṇikka nāyakkar avarkaḷ tamatu ‘tamiḻ maṟi viḷakkam’ eṉṉum nūlilē, vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ eṉpaṉa pallava maṉṉar kālattil cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷē vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟum, avi acōka ṉuṭiya ‘pāli’ moḻi aṟintavarkaḷum, tamiḻ naṉku aṟiyāta varkaḷumāṉa kaṟṟaccarkaḷāl poṟikkappaṭṭamiyiṉ ciṟcila tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷukkup piratiyākap pāli eḻuttukkaḷik kalantu poṟittu viṭṭaṉareṉṟum, pallavar kālattukku muṉṉum piṉṉum uḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷukkum vaṭṭeḻuttukkum toṭarpu illiyeṉṟum, ippoḻu
tuḷḷa tamiḻiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ ki.mu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muntiyatākiya tolkāppiyak kālantoṭaṅki oru peṟṟiyē uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyattiṟku muṉpu toṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ vaṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉum peyarkku uriyavāyiṉum allavāyiṉum avi acōkaṉuṭiya pāli eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu uṇṭāṉaviyalla veṉpatu tēṟṟam. ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭaṅkiyē vaḻaṅki vanta pāli (pirāmi) eḻuttiṉiṉṟu ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpu toṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa veṉṟu kūṟuvatu eṅṅaṉam poruntum? iṉi, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ pirāmi acōka eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu vantaṉavallavāyiṉum, avi tamiḻarālēyē mutalil uṇṭākkap peṟṟaṉavō allatu cila airōppiya āciriyar mutalāyiṉār karutumāṟu vēṟṟu nāṭṭavarākiya ekiptiyariṭamiruntu aṟintu koḷḷap peṟṟaṉavō eṉpatu tuṇinturikkaṟpālataṉṟu. tamiḻar vēṟṟunāṭṭavariṭamiruntē eḻuttiṉi aṟintāreṉpataṟku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ illāta variyil tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻar tāmākavē aṟintu uṇṭākkiyaṉa veṉṟu koḷvatē muṟiyākum.
iṉi, uyirmeykaḷiṉ vaṭivu vēṟṟumi kuṟittut tol
kāppiya urikārarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppaṉa īṇṭu aṟitaṟkuriyaṉa. ‘uruvutirintuyirttal mēlum kīḻum vilaṅku peṟuvaṉa vilaṅku peṟṟuyirttalum, kōṭu peṟuvaṉa kōṭu peṟṟuyirttalum, puḷḷi peṟuvaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟuyirttalum, puḷḷiyum kōṭum uṭaṉē peṟuvaṉa uṭaṉ peṟṟuyirttalum eṉakkoḷka’ eṉa iḷampūraṇar kūṟiṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya
taṉōṭu mēlum itaṉi viḷakki ‘ki, kī’ mutaliyaṉa mēl vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ‘ku, kū’ mutaliyaṉa kīḻ vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ‘ke, kē’ mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa. ‘kā, ṅā’ mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa. arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyi ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyi vaḷitteḻutiṉār. ‘ko, kō, ṅo, ṅō’ mutaliyaṉa puḷḷiyum kōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟaṉa eṉa urittār. ivarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam vaṭivu kāṭṭiyatu tamakku muṉṉirunta kalveṭṭukkaḷik koṇṭō piṟa ātaravukaḷik koṇṭō ātal vēṇṭum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiyiruppati liruntu ‘kā’ mutaliyaṉa avar kālattiṟku muṉpu arukiṟ puḷḷi peṟṟum, avar kālattil kāl peṟṟum iyaṅkiyami pulaṉākum. ‘ke, kē’ mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatāl muṉpu nēr kōṭāka iruntatē piṉpu vaḷitteḻutappeṟṟatu eṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷ ‘ki ku’ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivu vēṟṟumi kāṭṭātu viṭṭataṉ kāraṇam pulappaṭavilli. ki mutaliyaṉa muṉpu irukōṭu peṟumeṉak koḷka. ivi ivvāṟākavē kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ paḻiya vaṭivu iṉṉum ārāyntu kāṇaṟpālatu.
iṉi, peyar, viṉi, iṭi, uri eṉat tamiḻccol nālvakippaṭum. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
“col leṉappaṭuva, peyarē viṉiyeṉ
  ṟāyiraṇṭeṉpa aṟinticiṉōrē”
“iṭic coṟ kiḷaviyum uric coṟkiḷaviyum
  avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa”
eṉak kūṟiyiruppatu peyar viṉikaḷiṉ ciṟappiyum, iṭi urikaḷiṉ ciṟappiṉmiyiyum kāṭṭum. peyar viṉikaḷi
yiṉṟi iṭiurikaṭku iyakkamiṉmi uṇarka. eṉiṉum avar,
“ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟit taṉavē” 
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu ‘acinili’ mutaliya iṭic coṟkaḷum poruḷuṇarccikku oruvāṟu tuṇiyāvaṉa eṉpati viḷakkum. peyarc collāvatu irutiṇi aimpāṟ poruḷi uṇarttum collām. payaṉili koḷḷaṟkum urupēṟṟaṟkum urittātalum, kālantōṟṟāmiyum peyarc colliṉ ilakkaṇamākum. uyartiṇip peyar, aḵṟiṇip peyar, viravup peyar eṉap peyarccol mūvakippaṭum. viṉic collāvatu poruḷiṉ puṭi peyarcciyākiya toḻili uṇarttum collām. toḻil nikaḻccikku viṉi mutal, karuvi, iṭam, kālam eṉpaṉa iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. vēṟṟumi koḷḷāmiyum, kālam tōṟṟalum viṉic colliṉ ilakkaṇamākum. kālamum ceyalum kuṟippiṟ peṟappaṭuvaṉa kuṟippu viṉiyum, veḷippaṭiyāka vuḷḷaṉa terinili viṉiyum ākum. 
iviyum uyartiṇi viṉi, aḵṟiṇi viṉi, potu viṉi eṉa mūvakippaṭum. iviyellām mūṉṟu kālamum, mūṉṟu iṭamum, aintu pālum paṟṟiyum, muṟṟu, peyareccam, viṉiyeccam eṉpaṉa paṟṟiyum palavākap pākupaṭumāṟuṅ kāṇka.
iṭic collāvatu peyarum viṉiyum iṭamāka avaṟṟic cārntu tōṉṟum collām. iṭi eṉpatu iṭam eṉṉum poruṭṭu. iṉi, peyar viṉikaṭku muṉṉum piṉṉum varumāyiṉum perumpālum iṭiyē varutaliṉ iṭiccol eṉappaṭṭatu eṉpārum, peyarccol viṉiccoṟkaḷumākātu avaṟṟiṉ vēṟumākātu iṭi nikaratāy niṟṟaliṉ iṭiccollāyiṟṟu eṉpārum uḷar. iṭiccol cāriyiyum, viṉiyurupum, vēṟṟumiyurupum, aciniliyum, iciniliyum, kuṟippiṟ poruḷuṇarttuvatum, uvamavurupum eṉa eḻuvakippaṭum. kuṟippiṟ poruḷuṇarttutal ‘maṉ’ mutaliyaṉa ‘kaḻivu’ mutaliya poruḷiyuṇarttutal. naṉṉūlār poruḷuṇarttuvatum kuṟippum eṉa iraṇṭākak koṇṭu iṭiccolli eṇvakiyākkuvar. uriccol lāvatu ici, kuṟippu, paṇpu eṉpavaṟṟi uṇarttum col. iḵtu oru poruḷuṇarttum oru collum, oru poruḷuṇarttum pala collum, pala poruḷuṇarttum oru collum eṉa mūvakippaṭum. naṉṉūlār ‘piṅkala mutalā nallō ruric coliṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu koṇṭu collellām uric collē eṉpar pirayōka vivēka nūlār. avar,
“veḷippaṭu collē kiḷattal vēṇṭā
  veḷippaṭa vārā vuriccoṉmēṉa”
eṉṉum cūttirattiyum tamakkātaravākak koṇṭu ‘veḷip
paṭavārāvuriccol’ eṉṟamiyāl ēṉiyaṉa veḷippaṭa vanta uriccolleṉpatu peṟṟām eṉpar. aṅṅaṉam kūṟuvatu, colleṉappaṭuvaṉa peyarum viṉiyumākiya iraṇṭumē eṉṟu avaṟṟic ciṟappittuk kūṟi iṭiccollum uric
collum avaṟṟic cārntu tōṉṟumeṉak kūṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukkum, ēṉiyāciriyaṉmār karuttukkum māṟākaliṉ pōliyeṉṟotukkaṟpāṟṟu.
iṉi uriccol eṉṉum peyarpporuḷ uṇart
tuvār ici kuṟippu paṇpu eṉṉum poruṭku uriya collā
kaliṉ uriccollāyiṟṟu eṉpārum, ceyyuṭkuriya collā
kaliṉ uriccol eṉpārum, poruṭku urimi pūṇṭu varutaliṉ uriccol eṉpārum, poruṭkup paṇpu urimi pūṇṭu niṟṟaliṉ ataṉi uṇarttuñ col uriccol eṉappaṭṭateṉpārum āka urikārar pala tiṟattavarāvar. vaṭa
nūlāciriyar ‘tātu’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa coṟkaḷē ivi
yeṉpar teyvac ciliyār. ikkaruttu orucār porutta
muṭittākum eṉka.
iṉi tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu mikka iṉimiyuṭiya teṉpatu palarum aṟintatoṉṟu. paṇṭināḷ toṭṭu vanta pulavar perumakkaḷ palarum itaṉ cuviyi nukarntu viyantu itaṉik kuṟikkumiṭamellām centamiḻ, pintamiḻ, vaṇṭamiḻ, oṇṭamiḻ, taṇṭamiḻ, meṉṟamiḻ, iṉṟamiḻ, tīntamiḻ, cuvittamiḻ, naṟuntamiḻ, ceḻuntamiḻ, naṟṟamiḻ, coṟṟamiḻ eṉṟiṅṅaṉamāka  aṭikoṭuttu vaḻaṅki varuvāyiṉar.
‘oṇṭīntamiḻ’ eṉa māṇikkavācakarum, ‘tēṉuṟi tamiḻ’ eṉak kallāṭarum, ‘cevviya maturañ cērnta naṟṟamiḻ’ eṉṟu tañcivāṇaṉ kōviyuṭiyārum kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam palarum iṉimiyuṭiyateṉap pārāṭṭutaṟkut tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ iṉiya ōci nalamum, iyaṟki neṟi vaḻuvāta cantiyamitiyum peritum kāraṇamātal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷellām urappalum kaṉittalum vēṇṭātu eḷiya muyaṟciyāl eḻuvaṉa. avaṟṟuḷḷum cilaveḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalil vārāmiyum, cilaveḻuttukkaḷ iṟutiyil vārāmiyum, cilaveḻuttuṭaṉ cilaveḻuttu mayaṅki vārāmiyum nōkkiṉ tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ ākkappāṭu arumuyaṟciyiṉṟi kūṟat takkavāṟu iyaṟki muṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉaveṉpatu pulappaṭum. taṉi meykaḷum, uyir meykaḷum ṭakaram mutaliyaṉavum mutalil variṉum, valloṟṟukkaḷ iṟutiyil variṉum, mayaṅkāveṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ mayaṅkumāṟu niṟpiṉum avi meṉmic cevvi yuṭiya nāviṟku varuttam viḷittal kaṇkūṭu. māṇavarkaḷ prapu, ṭampam, tvok, utpatti eṉpavaṟṟi ōtik kāṇka.
iṉi iccoṟkaḷē iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticic col, vaṭacol eṉa nālvakippaṭum. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
“iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭacoleṉ
  ṟaṉittē ceyyu ḷīṭṭac collē”
eṉṉum eccaviyaṟ cūttirattāl tamiḻcceyyuṭkaḷukkuriyaṉa innālvakic collum eṉṟami kāṇka. peyar viṉi iṭi uri eṉṉum nāṉkum iṅṅaṉam pākupaṭum eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar karuttākum. peyar viṉi iṭi uri eṉpaṉa iyaṟcolliṉ pākupāṭu eṉpatum, tiricollum ticic
collum vaṭacollum perumpāṉmi peyarāyum ciṟupāṉmi viṉiyākavum varum eṉpatum cēṉāvariyar, teyvac ciliyār, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṉṉum uriyāḷar kaḷiṉ karuttām. ivarkkuḷḷum ciṟitu vēṟṟumiyuṇṭu. ataṉi uri nōkki yuṇarka. iyaṟcollāvatu, centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkuṭaṉ poruntit tam poruḷ vaḻuvāmal icikkum collām. eṉavē yāvarum eḷitil poruḷuṇarum centamiḻ nāṭṭuc col iyaṟcol eṉappaṭum eṉka. itaṉi, centamiḻ nilattum koṭuntamiḻ nilattum oppa vaḻaṅkum colleṉṟum, iyalpākiya colleṉṟum kūṟuvar. itaṉic ceñcol eṉiṉum amiyum eṉpar teyvacciliyār. tiri collāvatu iyaṟcolliṉ vēṟupaṭa varuñcol. oru poruḷ kuṟitta pala collākiyum pala poruḷ kuṟitta oru collākiyum aritiṟ poruḷuṇarappaṭum collē tiricol eṉka. iyaṟcollē tirintu tiri collākum eṉpatum, kiḷi kiḷḷi yeṉṟāṉatu uṟupputtirintatum, tatti eṉṟāṉatu muḻuvatum tirintatum ām eṉpatum cēṉāvariyar karuttu. iḷampūraṇar karuttum itupōlumeṉak koḷḷak kiṭakkiṟatu. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar colliṉ tirivi oruvāṟu koṇṭārāyiṉum, muḻuvatum tirittal eṉpataṉip 
piṟa coṟkoṇarntu tirittal eṉpar. iyaṟ colliṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvateṉpatē piṟa āciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttākum. kiḷi eṉṉum collē tatti eṉṟum, mali eṉṉum collē veṟpu eṉṟum tirintaṉa veṉpatu tamiḻiyalukku muṟṟilum māṟākiyāl cēṉāvariyar uri koḷḷaṟpālataṉṟākum. ticic collāvatu centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻntu kiṭanta paṉṉiru nilattumuḷḷār karutiya poruḷiyuṭiyatākum. ellā nāṭṭiṟkum potuvātaliṉṟik koṭuntamiḻ nāṭu ovvoṉṟaṟkēyuriya col ticic collām eṉka. vaṭa collāvatu vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu tamiḻiṟ koḷḷappaṭuñ col. aḵtu iru moḻikkum potuvāṉa eḻuttālāyatum, vaṭamoḻikkēyuriya eḻuttuc citintu varuvatum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. itaṟku āriyac coṟ pōlum eṉak kūṟiya iḷampūraṇaruri poruntuvataṉṟu. iṉi centamiḻ nilam yātu, centamiḻic  cērnta paṉṉiru nilam yāvi eṉa ārāytal vēṇṭum. iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, nacci
ṉārkkiṉiyar mūvarum centamiḻ nilamāvatu viyi yāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum maruta yāṟṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkum uḷḷa nilam eṉpar. ivarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam koṇ
ṭamikku yātum ātāram kāṭṭavilli. nāmum kaṇṭa
tilli. ivvuriyāḷarkaḷ kālattiṟku ivvariyaṟi poruttamuṭittup pōlum! kaṭiccaṅka nāḷilum, piṉvanta cēkkiḻār, kampar mutalāṉōr nāṭkaḷilum pāṇṭināṭē centamiḻ nāṭeṉak koḷḷap peṟṟateṉṟu ‘kapilar’ eṉṉum uri nūlil pala cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. āciriyar tolkāppiyar nāḷilē kiḻakkum mēṟkum kaṭalum, vaṭakkum teṟkum vēṅkaṭamum, kumariyum elliyāka vuṭiya nilamuḻutum centamiḻ nāṭātal vēṇṭum.
“vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
  āyiṭit tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
  vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ  
  eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi”
eṉappāyirattum,
“vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
  nāṟpeya relli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum
  yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉac ceyyuḷiyaliṉum varutal itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum. eṉavē paṉṉiru nilam eṉpaṉa itaṟkup puṟampākavuḷḷa tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷātal vēṇṭum. iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar mutalāyiṉār poṅkar nāṭu, oḷināṭu, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu, kuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, paṉṟi nāṭu, kaṟkā nāṭu, cītanāṭu, pūḻi nāṭu, maliyamāṉāṭu, aruvānāṭu, aruvāvaṭatali eṉpaṉa paṉṉiru nilam eṉpar. itu piṟkālattiṟkup poruntumāyiṉum tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkup poruntuvatākātu. aṟṟi nāḷil intiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭa pakutiyil āriyamaṉṟip piṟa moḻikaḷum, teṉ pakutiyil tamiḻaṉṟip piṟa moḻikaḷum vaḻaṅkiyiruppiṉ āriyattiṟkē vaṭamoḻi eṉṟum, tamiḻukkē teṉmoḻi eṉṟum peyar vaḻakkuṇṭākak kāraṇamilli. aṉṟiyum, ciṅkaḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku mutaliyaṉa ap
poḻutu taṉi moḻikaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppiṉ tolkāppiyar ticic coṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟumiṭattil ammoḻikaḷi viṭuttu, ‘centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum taṅkuṟippiṉa’ eṉṟu māttiram kūṟutal poruttamuṭittaṉṟu. ātaliṉ vaṭakkē āriyattiṉ citivukaḷum, teṟkē tamiḻiṉ citivukaḷum taṉi moḻikaḷiṉ nilimiyi aṭiyāmalē akkālattil vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉṟu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. yāṉ kūṟuvatē teyvac ciliyārkkum vēṟucilarkkum karutteṉpatu avaruriyāl aṟiyappaṭum. ‘vēṅkaṭamaliyiṉ teṟkum, kumariyiṉ vaṭakkum, kuṇa kaṭaliṉ mēṟkum, kuṭa kaṭaliṉ kiḻakkumākiya nilam centamiḻ nilam eṉpa’ ‘paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṉkarippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum, ciyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamum , tuḷuvamum kuṭakamum kuṉṟakamum, kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum vaṭukum teliṅkum kaliṅkamum eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum’ eṉṉum avvurippakutikaḷ kāṇka. itaṟku mēṟkōḷākaavvuriyiṟ koḷḷap peṟṟatoru cūttirappakuti naṉṉūl mayili nātar uriyilum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. atu, 
“kaṉṉit teṉkarik kaṭpaḻan tīvam
  ciṅkaḷam kollam kūviḷa meṉṉum
  elliyiṉ puṟattavum īḻam pallavam
  kaṉṉaṭam vaṭuku kaliṅkam teliṅkam
  koṅkaṇam tuḷuvam kuṭakam kuṉṟam
  eṉpaṉa kuṭapāl irupuṟac ciyat
  tuṭaṉuṟipu paḻakun tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷum
  muṭiyuṭi mūvarum iṭunila vāṭci
  aracumēm paṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭikaḷ
  patiṉmarum uṭaṉirup piruvarum paṭitta
  paṉṉiru ticiyiṟ coṉṉaya muṭiyavum”
eṉpatu; itaṉ piṉ ‘eṉṟār akattiyaṉār’ eṉṟu kūṟiyirut
taliṉ iḵtu akattiyac cūttiramē eṉak koḷḷap peṟṟami pulaṉākum. ippaṉṉiru nilaṅkaḷum muṭiyuṭi vēntar mūvariṉ āṇi vaḻi kuṟunila maṉṉar patiṉmarālum ēṉi iruvarālum āḷap peṟṟaṉa veṉa iccūttiram kūṟuvatum karutaṟpālatu.
kallāta ciṟuvar pēcciluḷḷa cantiyum, tamiḻilakkaṇaṅ
kūṟum cantiyum oṉṟēyākaliṉ iyaṟkiyiṉ vaḻuvāmalē 
tamiḻ nūlōr cantikaḷi amittuḷḷaṉar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. utāraṇamāka māvili eṉa vakaravuṭampaṭumey peṟuvatum, vāḻiyili eṉa yakaravuṭampaṭumey peṟuvatum, maravēr eṉa makaravoṟṟukkeṭuvatum, vāḻikkāy eṉak kakaravoṟṟu mikuvatum, puḷiyaṅkāy eṉa amcāriyi peṟṟu makaram ṅakaramāyt tirivatum tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟumāṟē ciṟuvar pēccil amintiruttal kāṇka. poruḷuṇarttu muṟi
yilum tamiḻc coṟkaḷ mikac ciṟappuṭiyaṉa. tamiḻiluḷḷa viṉic coṟkaḷ tiṇi, pāl, eṇ, iṭam, kālam, ceyal eṉpavaṟṟi oruṅku viḷakkutal pōl vēṟu emmoḻic coṟkaḷ viḷakkuvaṉavākum? paṭittāṉ eṉṉum viṉi
muṟṟilē pakutiyāṟ ceyalum, iṭiniliyāṟ kālamum, vikutiyāl tiṇi, pāl, eṇ. iṭaṅkaḷum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutal kāṇka. tiru. celvak kēcavarāya mutaliyār em.ē. avarkaḷ tām eḻutiya ‘tamiḻ’ eṉṉuṅ kaṭṭuriyil āṅkilat
tōṭu tamiḻi oppiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyiruppataṉ ōr pakuti piṉ varuvatu. ‘nām vantilaṉ, ceṉṟilaṉ eṉa oru collāy vaḻaṅkum etirmaṟi muṟṟukkaḷi āṅkilattil urippataṟku mūṉṟu coṟkaḷ vēṇṭum. teriniliyum kuṟippumāṉa ovvoru viṉiyālaṇiyum peyariyum āṅkilattil pala coṟkaḷiṉṟi amikkalākātu. ‘vantāy’kku eṉpati vantava
ṉākiya uṉakku eṉap poruḷ paṭuvatāy nāṉku coṟkaḷāl amikka vēṇṭum. aṭiyēṉukku eṉṉum kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyari amikka āṟu coṟkaḷ vēṇṭum. nammuṭiya peyarecca viṉiyeccaṅkaḷum coṟ curuṅkutaṟku anukūlamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. vanta maṉitaṉ eṉpati amikka avarkaḷukku nāṉku coṟkaḷ vēṇṭum. avaṉ vantu pōṉāṉ eṉṉum oru vākkiyatti avarkaḷ iraṇṭu vākkiyamiṉṟi urikka iyalātu. avarkaḷukku vēṟṟumit
toki, viṉit toki, uvamit toki, aṉmoḻit tokikaḷ illi.’ itiliruntu tamiḻc coṟkaḷum coṟ
ṟoṭarkaḷum curuṅkiya vuruvil virinta poruḷik koṇṭirukkum ciṟappu naṉku pulaṉākum.
collamiti, puṇarcci, vēṟṟumi mutaliyavaṟṟil tamiḻum vaṭamoḻiyum vevvēṟu iyalpiṉavātal:
ulakil vaḻaṅkum ellā moḻikkum āriya moḻiyē tāyāku meṉṟum, ammuṟiyē tamiḻum āriyattiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyatāku meṉṟum koḷḷum ārāyntu tuṇiyāta koḷkiyiṉiyuṭiyār cilar ciṉṉāḷ muṉpu toṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar, moḻiyārāycci eṉpatu putiya kali muṟikaḷil oṉṟāki moḻikaḷiṉ uṇmit taṉmiyi viḷakki varum innāḷil akkoḷki arukiviṭṭatēṉum uṇmiyuṇarātār cilar iṉṉumiruttaliṉ avar poruṭ
ṭāka vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻikaḷiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa iyalpiṉi viḷakkutal kaṭaṉākiṉṟatu. moḻi nūṟpulavarkaḷ moḻikaḷi avvavaṟṟiṉ paṇpukaḷ koṇṭu vevvēṟu iṉam allatu kuḻuuvākap pākupaṭuttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. pakutikaḷē coṟkaḷāka allatu collellām ōraciyēyākavuḷḷa iṉam (aiḷaṭiṭayavipē) oṉṟu. pakutiyuṭaṉ vikuti mutaliyaṉa oṭṭiniṉṟu, ataṉāṟ colliṉ urupu tiriyātirukkum iṉam (hapapaṭaraviyēviena) oṉṟu. vikuti mutaliyaṉa kūṭutalāl   colliṉ uruvantirintum māṟutalaṭiyum iṉam (aikēṭanuviṭiyēṭa)  oṉṟu. moḻikaḷ palavum im mūṉṟiṉattilē aṭaṅkāniṟkum. cīṉam mutaliyaṉa mutal iṉattiyum, tamiḻ mutaliyaṉa iraṇṭāvatu iṉittiyum, vaṭamoḻi mutaliyaṉa mūṉṟāvatu iṉattiyum cērntavi. tamiḻāṉatu  ciṟupāṉmi mutal iṉattiyum mūṉṟām iṉattiyum ottirukkumāyiṉum perumpāṉmi ovvāmi
yuṭiyatu. moḻikaḷ oru nilimiyiliruntu maṟṟoru nilimiyi oru kālattil eytu meṉpataṟkuriya iyipum cāṉṟum iṉmiyāl immūviṉamum ekkālattum mūvēṟu taṉittaṉmikaḷi uṭiyaṉavēyākum. inta aṭippaṭiyāṉa vēṟupāṭṭiliruntē tamiḻum vaṭamoḻiyum oṉṟiyoṉṟu cārntirāta taṉimoḻikaḷ eṉpatu naṉku pulappaṭum. maṟṟu ivviru moḻikaḷumilakkaṇa vakiyāl eṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum kāṇaṟpālatu.
tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻikkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumikaḷik kūṟuṅkāl ivviru moḻikkum oṟṟumiyē illiyeṉak karutuvatākak koḷḷalākātu. tiruttamuṟṟa moḻikaḷ pala
vaṟṟilum potuvāka amintirukkak kūṭiya paṇpukaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. uyir mey, peyar viṉi, āṇpāl, peṇpāl, orumi, paṉmi, eḻuvāy, payaṉili, mūviṭam, mukkālam pōlvaṉa ellā moḻikaṭkum potuviyalpukaḷām. tamiḻ
vāṇarum vaṭamoḻivāṇarum neṭuṅkālamākak kalantu vāḻtalāl akkalappup paṟṟiyum ivviru moḻik kaṇṇum otta paṇpukaḷ cila amital iyaṟkiyē. moḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟukku avaṟṟiṉ potuviyalkaḷiṉum ciṟappiyalpukaḷē mikka valimiyuṭiyaṉa vākum. ākaliṉ tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalkaḷ iviyeṉpatu kāṭṭappaṭiṉ atukoṇṭu aḵtu ōr uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyāmeṉpatu pōtarum.
muṉpu, tolkāppiyattiṟku mutaṉūl aintiram  eṉpār
kūṟṟi maṟutturitta vaḻiyum civañāṉa muṉivar tol
kāppiyap pāyiraviruttiyalil tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷi viḷakki ataṉi maṟutturittamiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭiya vaḻiyum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ māṇpukaḷi viḷakka nērnta vaḻiyum tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷ pala pōntaṉa. mēlē potu vakiyāṟ kūṟiyaṉavum, kūṟātaṉavumāya cila ciṟappiyalpukaḷ iṅkē viḷakkappaṭum.
āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷi uyartiṇi eṉṟum, ēṉit tāḻnta uyirkaḷiyum uyirillāvaṟṟiyum aḵṟiṇi eṉṟum pākupāṭu ceytiruppatu tamiḻilaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻi mutaliya vēṟemmoḻiyilum illi. eḻuttukaṭku uyir, mey eṉap peyariṭṭiruppatu pōṉṟu iḵtum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tattuva vuṇarvu mēmpāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟanta cāṉṟākum. pāl vakuppu muṟiyilum tamiḻum vaṭamoḻiyum muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭavi. tamiḻ muṟiyāṉatu poruṇōkkattāl āṇi āṇ eṉṟum peṇṇip peṇ eṉṟum kūṟum ciṟappuṭiyatu. vaṭamoḻi muṟiyō coṉṉōkkaṅkoṇṭu āṇip peṇṇākavum, peṇṇi āṇākavum, iraṇṭiyum aliyākavum, uyirillāta poruṭkaḷiyum āṇ peṇṇāka
vum piṟaḻak kūṟum kuṟipāṭuṭiyatu. oru poruḷē col vēṟṟumiyāl āṇākavum, peṇṇākavum aliyākavum māṟuvatum oru poruḷik kuṟitta oru collē aṭi
moḻiyāka varuṅkāl āṇ peṇ aliyāka māṟuvatum ākiya kuṟikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷaṉa. ulakattup poru
ḷellām cāttuvikam irācatam tāmatam eṉṉum kuṇaṅ
kaḷōṭu kalantaṉavākaliṉ cāttuvikamuḷḷatu āṇākavum, irācatamuḷḷatu peṇṇākavum, tāmatamuḷḷatu aliyākavum, oru poruḷē akkuṇaṅkaḷiṉ mikuti kuṟi oppup paṟṟi āṇ peṇ aliyākavum kūṟappaṭum eṉṉum vaṭa nūlār kūṟṟum ārāyuṅkāl uṇmiyuṭitteṉat tōṉṟavilli. takkiṇam eṉṉum ticip peyarāṉatu ataṉāl vicēṭikkap paṭṭataṟ kēṟpat takkiṇa tēcam eṉpuḻi āṇpālum, takkiṇāmūrtti eṉpuḻip peṇpālum ākumeṉiṉ, tēcam cāttuvikamākavum mūrtti irācatamākavum kōṭaṟkuriya iyiputāṉ eṉṉiyō? tamiḻ nūlkaḷilē ‘teṉṟalañ celvaṉ’ ‘nāṇeṉṉu nallāḷ’ eṉṟiṅṅaṉam uyiril poruḷum kuṇamum mutalāyiṉ āṇpālākavum peṇ pālākavum kūṟappaṭutal vaṭanūl muṟiyaṉṟō veṉiṉ, ivi uruvaka mātaliṉ vaṭanūl muṟiyāmāṟu yāṅṅaṉam eṉka. ‘kaṅkiyāḷ’ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum virittu uruvakamākkutal vēṇṭum. ciṟupāṉmi vaṭanūṉmuṟi taḻuviṟṟumām. 
tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷum nūlkaḷum
ceyyuḷ eṉpatit tolkāppiyar potup peyarākak koṇṭirukkiṟār. pāṭṭukkum urikkum cūttirattukkum akkālattil ceyyuḷeṉṟē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu vantatu. pāṭṭu eṉpataṟkup pā eṉappaṭuvatē ciṟappup peyarākum. avar uṟuppukkaḷ 34 eṉavum pirittirukkiṟār. ceyyuḷ yāvum poruḷ ilakkaṇattukku ilakkiyamāṉavi. māttiri eṉpatum avvuṟuppukaḷil oṉṟu. ceyyuḷil toki nilic ceyyuḷ eṉṟum toṭarnilic ceyyuḷeṉṟum pākupāṭukaḷ uṇṭu. ‘puṟanāṉūṟu’ toki nilic ceyyuḷākum. ‘cīvaka cintāmaṇi’ toṭarnilic ceyyuḷākum. tolkāppiyattil kūṟiyuḷḷa acikkum, maṟṟa nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa acikkum vittiyācamirukkiṟatu. tolkāppiyar ‘nāṟ cīr koṇṭatu aṭiyeṉappaṭumē’ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.ittaṉi eḻuttukkaḷ mutal ittaṉi eḻuttuvariyuḷḷatu iṉṉiṉṉa aṭiyeṉṟum avar vakuttirukkiṟār. 4 mutal 6 vari uḷḷatik kuṟaḷaṭiyeṉṟum, 7 mutal 9 variyuḷḷati cintaṭiyeṉṟum,   10 mutal 14 variyuḷḷati nēraṭiyeṉṟum, 15 mutal 19 variyuḷḷati neṭilaṭiyeṉṟum 18 mutal20 variyuḷḷati kaḻi neṭil eṉṟum pirittirukkiṟār. itutāṉ kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi eṉappaṭuvatu. inta kaṭṭaḷiyaṭi eṉpatu kaṭiccaṅka kālattilēyē illātu pōyviṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. itaṉip pērāciriyar mutaliyōr taṅkaḷ uriyil viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. 
oru kālattil vaḻakkattiliruntu piṟaku maṟinta oru kāriyattip piṟkālattilum vaḻakkiliruppatu pōlak kūṟuvatu kūṭāteṉa āciriyar ‘marapu’ eṉṟa nuṇukkattālē kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṟār. utāraṇamāka taḻi eṉpatu muṟkālattil kiyuṟiyākak koṇṭu koṭukkum oruvita taḻiyālāṉa uṭi. ippōtu anta taḻi koṭukkum vaḻakkamilli. ippaṭiyirukka ikkālattilum taḻi koṭuttatāka eṅkāvatu colluvōmāṉāl atu tavaṟākum. tūraviruntu oruvar pāṭukiyil atu ettaṉmiyāṉa pāveṉak kaṇṭaṟivatē pā eṉṟa uṟuppiṉ laṣaṇameṉac collappaṭukiṟatu. pā eṉṟa col uṟuppiṉ peyarāka viruppattōṭu ceyyuḷiṉ peyarākavu mirukkiṟatu. pākkaḷi āṟuvakiyākap pākupāṭu ceytirukkiṟār. ceyyuḷil iruvaki uṇṭeṉavum, avaṟṟil oṉṟu aṭi variyaṟi uḷḷateṉṟum, maṟṟoṉṟu aṭivariyaṟiyaṟṟateṉṟum kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. nūl eṉpatu aṭivariyaṟi illātatu. paṇṭināḷil nūl eṉṟatu ilakkaṇam eṉṟa oṉṟi maṭṭum kuṟittatu. ippōtu nām ellāvaṟṟiyum nūleṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟōm. akkālattil ceyyuḷ eṉṟa peyarāl maṟṟavi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.
nūlukku aṭivariyaṟi illātatu pōlavē cūttirattukkum aṭivariyilli, urikkum illi.
